-- GENERATED standalone hub: all six game modules included locally.
--[[
	SOLOS HUB v3.25  (main LocalScript + one script per game)
	Tabs: Dashboard | Player | ESP | Visuals | Players | Teleports | World | Tools | Commands | Settings
	* The X button fully UNLOADS the hub (every loop, ESP object, effect and edit is reverted).
	* Press the menu key (RightShift by default) to hide / show without unloading.
	* Re-executing the script unloads the previous copy automatically.

	v3.27: Player Body Part Editor - local size, joint offsets, rotation, and reset controls.
	v3.26: Build A Boat added to the game list (module: Bab.txt).
	v3.25: main UI rework - sidebar categories (Combat / Movement / Visuals / Player / Teleports / Misc / Settings), every
	       category and game is a panel with a left sub-nav + feature cards, gradient active tab, page header with icon +
	       subtitle, window 760 wide. Old tabs live on as sub-pages (Tabs["Teleports"] etc. still work for modules).
	v3.24: key window, loading screen and game picker rebuilt to the new reference (brand title, ringed logo, icon fields,
	       gradient Verify button, Owner pill, tip line, game rows with tags + Select arrow). SolosIcons.png is 9x8 cells of 112px (1008x896).
	v3.23: card-style sections, flat divided rows with optional description line, accent toggles, purple-navy default palette,
	       dashboard stat cards with icons, every game rebuilt on dropdowns + collapsible groups.
	v3.22: UI rework - every game is ONE sidebar tab with sub-tabs (Hub.GamePanel), "UNIVERSAL" divider for the global tabs,
	       Dropdown + Collapse components, player picker inside the MM2 Attack tab, glow icon pack (icons/ folder in the repo)
	       replaces the broken text close/minimize glyphs.
	v3.21: games moved out into their own scripts (mm2/arsenal/pf/lt2/doomspire.lua). The picker downloads the chosen one
	       from Hub.ModuleURLs and loadstrings it. Universal stays here.
	v3.20: every game except Universal is labelled Work In Progress (picker, sidebar header, toast).
	v3.19: Lumber Tycoon 2 module (auto chop, bring/sell logs, tree ESP, teleports, claim plot).
	v3.18: Phantom Forces module (low-profile aim assist, triggerbot, ESP, radar).
	v3.17: Arsenal module (aimbot, triggerbot, hitbox, ESP, weapon tweaks); more MM2 + Doomspire features.
	v3.15: server key system - Premium/Owner keys are checked by the Cloudflare Worker (VERIFY_URL in the key window); no keys are stored in this script.
	v3.1: each tab is wrapped in a do ... end block so the script stays under Luau's
	200-local-variable limit (that limit was why the old version never showed anything).
	v3.2: every tab now has its own icon.
	v3.3: ~60 new features. Players tab now has a search bar + profile pictures.
	      New code is kept inside the existing do ... end blocks (only 1 new top-level local: Thumb).
	v3.14: UI sound effects - drop .mp3 / .ogg files into workspace/SolosHub/sounds/ to change the sound of
	       buttons, toggles, sliders, tabs, hover, notifications and errors (Settings > UI Sounds).
	       Join / leave alerts have their own sound switch (off by default) so they don't spam the notify/error sounds.
	       Key window: Show / Hide button masks your key (saved, hidden by default) so it never leaks on stream or video.
	v3.10: action features - air walk, wall climb, glide, face camera, click-to-walk, auto retreat, quick slots,
	       waypoint tour, stick behind, middle-click select, friends, proximity alert, object/prompt ESP,
	       camera offset, cinematic camera, screenshot mode, go-to-nearest, join a player's server.
	v3.9: sidebar background image, emoji tab icons (switchable), smarter team detection for ESP,
	      fixed tab buttons getting stuck highlighted.
	v3.8: all saved data now lives in workspace/SolosHub/ (auto-created, old files are migrated).
	      Put images in workspace/SolosHub/themes/ and pick one as the GUI background (Theme tab).
	v3.7: Void Hold (Player tab) - parks your character in the void without dying, camera stays put.
	v3.6: global search bar (tabs + features) that jumps to and highlights the feature (Ctrl+F).
	v3.12: removed X-Ray, Hide Players, View Extras, Camera Tools, Team Detection panel, Object ESP, Extra Info,
	      Extra Character Options, Inventory & Emotes, Automation, Go To Nearest (and their commands).
	v3.11: Hub Chat tab - private chat between Solos Hub users in the same server (opt-in relay).
	v3.5: collapsible sections, grouped sidebar, hover effects, new tabs:
	      Servers, Chat, Macros, Explorer, Toolbox, Theme (+ Dashboard favorites).
	v3.4: join by Job ID, server browser, saved servers, previous-server rejoin,
	      redesigned key window + loading screen.
]]

-- ====================================================
-- DATA FOLDER  (v3.8): workspace/SolosHub/  +  themes/  +  sounds/ (v3.14)
-- ====================================================
local Hub = { dir = "SolosHub", themes = "SolosHub/themes", sounds = "SolosHub/sounds" }
Hub.ok = (makefolder and isfolder and writefile and readfile and isfile) and true or false
function Hub.path(name)
	if Hub.ok then return Hub.dir .. "/" .. name end
	return "SolosHub_" .. name -- executor can't make folders: fall back to prefixed files
end
if Hub.ok then
	pcall(function()
		if not isfolder(Hub.dir) then makefolder(Hub.dir) end
		if not isfolder(Hub.themes) then makefolder(Hub.themes) end
		if not isfolder(Hub.sounds) then makefolder(Hub.sounds) end
	end)
	-- move files saved by older versions into the folder
	for old, new in pairs({
		["SolosHub_Config.json"] = "config.json", ["SolosHub_Key.txt"] = "key.txt",
		["SolosHub_Servers.json"] = "servers.json", ["SolosHub_LastServer.json"] = "last_server.json",
		["SolosHub_Macros.json"] = "macros.json", ["SolosHub_Notes.txt"] = "notes.txt",
	}) do
		pcall(function()
			if isfile(old) and not isfile(Hub.path(new)) then writefile(Hub.path(new), readfile(old)) end
		end)
	end
	pcall(function()
		local readme = Hub.themes .. "/README.txt"
		if not isfile(readme) then
			writefile(readme, "Solos Hub themes folder\n\nDrop .png / .jpg / .jpeg / .webp images in here.\nThen open the hub > Theme tab > Background Image > Refresh Image List > Apply.\n")
		end
	end)
	pcall(function()
		local readme = Hub.sounds .. "/README.txt"
		if not isfile(readme) then
			writefile(readme, "Solos Hub sounds folder\n\nDrop .mp3 or .ogg files in here and name them after the event they should play for:\n\n  click.mp3       buttons (also: button)\n  toggle.mp3      toggle switches (or split: toggle_on / toggle_off)\n  slider.mp3      sliders while you drag (also: slide)\n  tab.mp3         switching tabs (also: page)\n  hover.mp3       hovering a button\n  notify.mp3      notifications (also: notification, toast)\n  error.mp3       errors / warnings / denied actions (also: deny, warn)\n\nThen open the hub > Settings > UI Sounds > Rescan Sounds Folder.\nAn event with no file stays silent. Keep sounds short (under a second) so they don't overlap.\n")
		end
	end)
end

local function new(class, props, parent)
	local o = Instance.new(class)
	for k, v in pairs(props) do o[k] = v end
	if parent then o.Parent = parent end
	return o
end
local function round(o, r) return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 8) }, o) end

-- ====================================================
-- UI SOUND EFFECTS  (v3.14): workspace/SolosHub/sounds/
-- Events: click, toggle_on, toggle_off, slider, tab, hover, notify, error.
-- Files are matched by name (see the README the hub writes into the folder). No file = silent.
-- ====================================================
Hub.sfx = { enabled = true, volume = 0.6, joinLeave = false, files = {}, assets = {}, pool = {}, last = {}, saving = false }
Hub.sfx.names = {
	click = { "click", "button" },
	toggle_on = { "toggle_on", "on", "toggle", "switch" },
	toggle_off = { "toggle_off", "off", "toggle", "switch" },
	slider = { "slider", "slide" },
	tab = { "tab", "page" },
	hover = { "hover" },
	notify = { "notify", "notification", "toast" },
	error = { "error", "deny", "warn" },
}
Hub.sfx.order = { "click", "toggle_on", "toggle_off", "slider", "tab", "hover", "notify", "error" }

function Hub.sfxScan()
	local s = Hub.sfx
	table.clear(s.files)
	table.clear(s.assets)
	for _, snd in pairs(s.pool) do pcall(function() snd:Destroy() end) end
	table.clear(s.pool)
	if not (Hub.ok and listfiles) then return 0 end
	local ok, list = pcall(listfiles, Hub.sounds)
	local n = 0
	if ok and type(list) == "table" then
		for _, p in ipairs(list) do
			local name = tostring(p):match("[^/\\]+$") or ""
			local base, ext = name:match("^(.+)%.(%w+)$")
			if base and (ext:lower() == "mp3" or ext:lower() == "ogg") then
				s.files[base:lower()] = name
				n = n + 1
			end
		end
	end
	return n
end

function Hub.sfxFile(ev)
	local aliases = Hub.sfx.names[ev]
	if not aliases then return nil end
	for _, a in ipairs(aliases) do
		local f = Hub.sfx.files[a]
		if f then return f end
	end
	return nil
end

function Hub.play(ev, pitch)
	local s = Hub.sfx
	if not s.enabled or Unloading or s.volume <= 0 then return end
	local name = Hub.sfxFile(ev)
	if not name then return end
	local now = os.clock()
	if s.last[ev] and now - s.last[ev] < (ev == "slider" and 0.06 or 0.03) then return end
	s.last[ev] = now
	local path = Hub.sounds .. "/" .. name
	local asset = s.assets[path]
	if asset == nil then
		local gca = getcustomasset or getsynasset
		local ok, a = false, nil
		if gca then ok, a = pcall(gca, path) end
		asset = (ok and a) or false
		s.assets[path] = asset
	end
	if not asset then return end
	local snd = s.pool[ev]
	if not snd or not snd.Parent then
		snd = new("Sound", { Name = "SolosSfx_" .. ev, SoundId = asset }, Gui)
		s.pool[ev] = snd
	end
	snd.Volume = s.volume
	snd.PlaybackSpeed = pitch or 1
	snd.TimePosition = 0
	snd:Play()
end

function Hub.sfxSave()
	local s = Hub.sfx
	if s.saving or not Hub.ok then return end
	s.saving = true
	task.delay(0.6, function()
		s.saving = false
		pcall(function() writefile(Hub.path("sounds.json"), HttpService:JSONEncode({ enabled = s.enabled, volume = s.volume, joinLeave = s.joinLeave })) end)
	end)
end

function Hub.sfxTest()
	task.spawn(function()
		for _, ev in ipairs({ "click", "toggle_on", "toggle_off", "slider", "tab", "notify", "error" }) do
			if Unloading then return end
			Hub.play(ev)
			task.wait(0.45)
		end
	end)
end

if Hub.ok and isfile(Hub.path("sounds.json")) then
	local ok, d = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(Hub.path("sounds.json"))) end)
	if ok and type(d) == "table" then
		if type(d.enabled) == "boolean" then Hub.sfx.enabled = d.enabled end
		if type(d.volume) == "number" then Hub.sfx.volume = math.clamp(d.volume, 0, 1) end
		if type(d.joinLeave) == "boolean" then Hub.sfx.joinLeave = d.joinLeave end
	end
end
Hub.sfxScan()
onUnload(function()
	for _, snd in pairs(Hub.sfx.pool) do pcall(function() snd:Destroy() end) end
end)

local ToastHolder = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 1, -20), Position = UDim2.new(1, -270, 0, 10), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 50 }, Gui)
new("UIListLayout", { VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom, Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, ToastHolder)

local function notify(text, color, silent)
	if not S.Notify or Unloading then return end
	if not silent then Hub.play((color == T.Bad or color == T.Warn) and "error" or "notify") end
	local t = new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), BackgroundColor3 = T.Side, Text = text, TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 12, ZIndex = 50 }, ToastHolder)
	round(t, 8)
	new("UIStroke", { Color = color or T.Accent, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, t)
	task.delay(S.ToastTime or 2.5, function() if t.Parent then t:Destroy() end end)
end

local function copyText(text)
	local clip = setclipboard or toclipboard or set_clipboard or (Clipboard and Clipboard.set)
	if clip then
		local ok = pcall(clip, text)
		if ok then notify("Copied: " .. (#text > 28 and (text:sub(1, 28) .. "...") or text)) else notify("Clipboard failed", T.Warn) end
	else
		notify("Clipboard not supported here", T.Warn)
	end
end

local function Label(parent, props)
	local l = new("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, TextWrapped = true,
		TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(185, 190, 195), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0),
	})
	for k, v in pairs(props) do l[k] = v end
	l.Parent = parent
	return l
end

local function Row(parent, h)
	local flat = parent and parent:GetAttribute("SolosCard")
	local f = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h or 36), BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BackgroundTransparency = flat and 1 or 0, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, parent)
	if flat then
		-- inside a card: flat row with a thin divider under it
		f:SetAttribute("SolosRow", true)
		new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, -1), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, f)
	else
		round(f, 8)
	end
	return f
end

-- title (+ optional grey description line) on the left of a row; rightPad = space kept free for the control
function Hub.titled(row, text, rightPad, desc)
	local TS = game:GetService("TextService")
	local base = row.Size.Y.Offset
	local title, d
	if desc then
		title = Label(row, { Text = text, Size = UDim2.new(1, -rightPad, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 7), Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, TextColor3 = T.Text, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top })
		d = Label(row, { Text = desc, Size = UDim2.new(1, -rightPad, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 24), TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top })
		d:SetAttribute("SolosDesc", true)
	else
		title = Label(row, { Text = text, Size = UDim2.new(1, -rightPad, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, TextColor3 = T.Text })
	end
	-- long names / descriptions take as many lines as they need and the row grows with them
	local function fit()
		local w = row.AbsoluteSize.X - rightPad - 14
		if w < 60 then return end
		local th = TS:GetTextSize(text, 12, Enum.Font.GothamMedium, Vector2.new(w, 1000)).Y
		if desc then
			local dh = TS:GetTextSize(desc, 10, Enum.Font.Gotham, Vector2.new(w, 1000)).Y
			title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -rightPad, 0, th)
			d.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 8 + th)
			d.Size = UDim2.new(1, -rightPad, 0, dh)
			row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.max(base, 8 + th + dh + 8))
		else
			row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.max(base, th + 16))
		end
	end
	bind(row:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"), fit)
	task.defer(fit)
end

-- label on the left, coloured value pill on the right (info cards like "Round Info"). obj.Set(text, color)
function Hub.Stat(parent, label, value, color)
	local row = Row(parent, 34)
	Label(row, { Text = label, Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0), TextColor3 = T.Sub })
	local pill = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 22), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, BackgroundColor3 = T.Btn, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, row)
	round(pill, 6)
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10) }, pill)
	local val = new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = value or "-", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = color or T.Text }, pill)
	local obj = {}
	function obj.Set(text, col) val.Text = text val.TextColor3 = col or T.Text end
	return obj
end

local function rankSuffix(minRank)
	if not minRank then return "" end
	return "  [" .. (minRank == "Owner" and "Owner" or "Premium") .. "]"
end
local function denyRank(minRank)
	if quiet then return end
	notify("Requires " .. minRank, T.Warn)
end

		Bombs = "attack", Raids = "aimbot", Fighting = "attack",
	},
}
-- get(name, cb): cb(asset, rectOffset, rectSize) runs (maybe later, async) once the sheet is on disk.
-- name can be a tab name or an icon name. Set Image = asset, ImageRectOffset = rectOffset, ImageRectSize = rectSize.
function Hub.Icons.get(name, cb)
	local I = Hub.Icons
	name = I.Map[name] or name
	local idx = I.Atlas[name]
	if not idx then return end
	local off = Vector2.new((idx % I.cols) * I.cell, math.floor(idx / I.cols) * I.cell)
	local size = Vector2.new(I.cell, I.cell)
	if I.sheet then cb(I.sheet, off, size) return end
	if I.sheet == false then return end
	local waiter = function(a) cb(a, off, size) end
	if I.pending then table.insert(I.pending, waiter) return end
	I.pending = { waiter }
	task.spawn(function()
		local asset, reason
		local okp, perr = pcall(function()
			local getAsset = getcustomasset or getsynasset
			if not (getAsset and writefile and readfile and isfile) then
				reason = "this executor can't save files (needs writefile + getcustomasset)"
				return
			end
			local path = Hub.path("icons_v" .. I.ver .. ".png")
			-- only accept a sheet with the exact size this script expects: a stale / wrong icons file can never show garbled icons
			local function valid(d)
				if type(d) ~= "string" or #d < 60 or d:sub(2, 4) ~= "PNG" then return false end
				local w = d:byte(17) * 16777216 + d:byte(18) * 65536 + d:byte(19) * 256 + d:byte(20)
				local h = d:byte(21) * 16777216 + d:byte(22) * 65536 + d:byte(23) * 256 + d:byte(24)
				return w == I.cols * I.cell and h == I.rows * I.cell
			end
			if not (isfile(path) and valid(readfile(path))) then
				local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request)
				local url = I.url .. "?v=" .. I.ver
				local body, code
				if req then
					local res = req({ Url = url, Method = "GET" })
					code = res and res.StatusCode
					if res and (code == nil or code == 200) then body = res.Body end
				else
					body = game:HttpGet(url)
				end
				if not valid(body) then
					if code and code ~= 200 then
						reason = "download failed (HTTP " .. tostring(code) .. ") - upload SolosIcons.png to the repo root"
					else
						reason = "SolosIcons.png is missing, not a PNG, or not " .. (I.cols * I.cell) .. "x" .. (I.rows * I.cell) .. " - upload the new one"
					end
					return
				end
				writefile(path, body)
			end
			asset = getAsset(path)
			if not asset or asset == "" then
				reason = "getcustomasset returned nothing"
				asset = nil
			end
		end)
		if not okp then reason = tostring(perr) end
		local list = I.pending
		I.pending = nil
		I.sheet = asset or false
		if asset then
			for _, f in ipairs(list or {}) do pcall(f, asset) end
		else
			I.err = reason or "unknown error"
			warn("[SolosHub] icons not loaded: " .. I.err)
			task.delay(4, function() if Running then notify("Icons not loaded: " .. I.err, T.Warn) end end)
		end
	end)
end
-- puts an icon on a button/label: shows `fallback` text until the image is ready (and forever if it can't load)
function Hub.Icons.button(btn, name, fallback, size, hoverTint)
	btn.Text = fallback
	local img = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -size / 2, 0.5, -size / 2), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false, ZIndex = btn.ZIndex + 1 }, btn)
	Hub.Icons.get(name, function(a, o, s) if img.Parent then img.Image = a img.ImageRectOffset = o img.ImageRectSize = s img.Visible = true btn.Text = "" end end)
	if hoverTint then
		bind(btn.MouseEnter, function() img.ImageColor3 = hoverTint end)
		bind(btn.MouseLeave, function() img.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) end)
	end
	return img
end

local Main = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 760, 0, 500), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -380, 0.5, -250), BackgroundColor3 = T.Bg, BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true, Visible = false }, Gui)

Label(Side, { Text = "Better Features. Better Experience.", Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 12), Position = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 25), TextSize = 9, TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })

local NavScroll = new("ScrollingFrame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -134), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 79), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 2, ScrollBarImageColor3 = T.Hover, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new() }, Side)
new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 5), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, NavScroll)
new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10) }, NavScroll)

local Footer = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 55), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -55), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, Side)

end)
Label(Footer, { Text = LocalPlayer.DisplayName, Size = UDim2.new(1, -65, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0.5, -14), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextColor3 = T.Text })
local TagLabel = Label(Footer, { Text = rank, Size = UDim2.new(1, -65, 0, 12), Position = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0.5, 2), TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = T.Sub })
new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 0), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, Footer)

local TopBar = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 45), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 10 }, Main)
local PageIcon = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(186, 10), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false, ZIndex = 11 }, TopBar)
local PageTitle = Label(TopBar, { Text = "", Size = UDim2.new(1, -480, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 222, 0, 5), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 16, TextColor3 = T.Text, ZIndex = 11 })
local PageSub = Label(TopBar, { Text = "", Size = UDim2.new(1, -480, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 222, 0, 26), TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Sub, ZIndex = 11, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
Hub.PageSubs = { Dashboard = "View server info and manage your features." }
-- title / subtitle / icon for the page in the top bar (panels use their current sub-page's subtitle)
function Hub.setHeader(name)
	local pn = S.Panels and S.Panels[name]
	PageTitle.Text = name
	local sub = Hub.PageSubs[name] or ""
	local key = Hub.Icons.Map[name]
	if pn then
		sub = (pn.Current and pn.Subtitles[pn.Current]) or pn.Default or ""
		key = pn.Icon
	end
	PageSub.Text = sub
	PageIcon.Visible = false
	if key then
		Hub.Icons.get(key, function(a, o, s)
			if PageIcon.Parent and S.CurTab == name then PageIcon.Image = a PageIcon.ImageRectOffset = o PageIcon.ImageRectSize = s PageIcon.Visible = true end
		end)
	end
end
local MinLogo = Label(TopBar, { Text = "Solos Hub", Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 0), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 15, TextColor3 = T.Text, Visible = false, ZIndex = 12 })
local MinBtn = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 45), Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "—", TextColor3 = T.Sub, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, ZIndex = 13 }, TopBar)
local CloseBtn = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 45), Position = UDim2.new(1, -35, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "✕", TextColor3 = T.Sub, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 13, ZIndex = 13 }, TopBar)

Hub.Icons.button(MinBtn, "minimize_l", "-", 18)
Hub.Icons.button(CloseBtn, "close_l", "X", 18, Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 120))

new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -170, 0, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0, 170, 0, 45), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, Main)
local Content = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -170, 1, -45), Position = UDim2.new(0, 170, 0, 45), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, Main)
local Grip = new("ImageButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, -14), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = "rbxassetid://6031302927", ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 72, 78), ZIndex = 20 }, Main)

do
	local isMin, normalSize = false, Main.Size
	bind(MinBtn.MouseButton1Click, function()
		Hub.play("click")
		isMin = not isMin
		if isMin then
			normalSize = Main.Size
			Side.Visible, Content.Visible, Grip.Visible, PageTitle.Visible, MinLogo.Visible = false, false, false, false, true
			PageSub.Visible, PageIcon.Visible = false, false
			TweenService:Create(Main, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Size = UDim2.new(0, normalSize.X.Offset, 0, 45) }):Play()
		else
			local tw = TweenService:Create(Main, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Size = normalSize })
			tw:Play()
			tw.Completed:Wait()
			if not isMin then
				Side.Visible, Content.Visible, Grip.Visible, PageTitle.Visible, MinLogo.Visible = true, true, true, true, false
				PageSub.Visible = true
				Hub.setHeader(S.CurTab)
			end
		end
	end)
	-- X = full unload (kills every loop, ESP object and effect)
	bind(CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("click") Unload() end)

	local dragging, dragInput, dragStart, startPos
	bind(TopBar.InputBegan, function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging, dragStart, startPos = true, input.Position, Main.Position
			input.Changed:Connect(function() if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end end)
		end
	end)
	bind(TopBar.InputChanged, function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragInput = input end
	end)
	local resizing, resizeStart, startSize
	bind(Grip.InputBegan, function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			resizing, resizeStart, startSize = true, input.Position, Main.Size
			input.Changed:Connect(function() if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then resizing = false end end)
		end
	end)
	bind(UIS.InputChanged, function(input)
		if dragging and input == dragInput then
			local d = input.Position - dragStart
			Main.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
		elseif resizing and not isMin and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			local d = input.Position - resizeStart
			Main.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(640, startSize.X.Offset + d.X), 0, math.max(340, startSize.Y.Offset + d.Y))
		end
	end)
end

-- ====================================================
-- TABS + COMPONENTS
-- ====================================================
local Tabs, TabButtons = {}, {}

local function selectTab(name)
	-- an old tab name that now lives inside a category panel: open that panel's sub-page
	local own = S.TabOwner and S.TabOwner[name]
	if own and not (S.Panels and S.Panels[name]) then
		own.panel.Select(own.sub)
		name = own.panel.Title
	end
	for n, p in pairs(Tabs) do
		if not p:GetAttribute("SolosSub") then p.Visible = (n == name) end
	end
	for title, pn in pairs(S.Panels or {}) do pn.Page.Visible = (title == name) end
	for n, b in pairs(TabButtons) do
		local on = (n == name)
		b.Btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		b.Btn.BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover
		b.Pill.Visible = on
		if on then b.Grad.Color = ColorSequence.new(T.Accent, T.Accent:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.28)) end
		b.Ind.Visible = false
		b.Lbl.TextColor3 = on and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or T.Sub
		b.Icon.ImageTransparency = on and 0 or 0.35
		b.Glyph.TextTransparency = on and 0 or 0.3
	end
	S.CurTab = name
	Hub.setHeader(name)
end
local function AddTab(name, icon)
	local page = new("ScrollingFrame", { Name = name, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = T.Hover, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), Visible = false }, Content)
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 14), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, page)
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 18), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 18), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 12), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 18) }, page)
	Tabs[name] = page

	local btn = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = name, TextColor3 = T.Sub, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 13, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, AutoButtonColor = false }, NavScroll)
	round(btn, 8)
	btn.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 38) }, btn)
	local ic = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.new(0, -29, 0.5, -10), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = icon, ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), ImageTransparency = 0.4, Visible = false }, btn)
	local gl = new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.new(0, -29, 0.5, -10), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "•", TextSize = 14, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextColor3 = T.Sub }, btn)
	local ind = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, -36, 0.5, -8), BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false }, btn)
	round(ind, 2)
	-- active tab = accent gradient pill. The pill is its own frame behind a separate text label: a UIGradient on the button
	-- itself would tint the button's text with the same colour as the pill and make the name invisible.
	local pill = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 38, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, -38, 0, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 0 }, btn)
	round(pill, 8)
	local grad = new("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(T.Accent, T.Accent) }, pill)
	local lbl = new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = name, TextColor3 = T.Sub, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 13, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, ZIndex = 2 }, btn)
	btn.TextTransparency = 1 -- the label above draws the name; Btn.Text stays for anything that reads it
	TabButtons[name] = { Btn = btn, Icon = ic, Ind = ind, Glyph = gl, Grad = grad, Pill = pill, Lbl = lbl }
	if Hub.Icons.Map[name] then
		Hub.Icons.get(name, function(a, o, s) if ic.Parent then ic.Image = a ic.ImageRectOffset = o ic.ImageRectSize = s ic.Visible = true gl.Visible = false end end)
	end
	bind(btn.MouseEnter, function() Hub.play("hover") if btn.BackgroundTransparency == 1 then btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6 end end)
	bind(btn.MouseLeave, function() if btn.BackgroundTransparency == 0.6 then btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end end)
	bind(btn.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("tab") selectTab(name) end)
	return page
end

local function Section(page, title, sub, icon)
	local f = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, page)
	round(f, 10)
	new("UIStroke", { Color = T.Hover, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, f)
	f:SetAttribute("SolosCard", true)
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 0), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, f)
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6) }, f)
	local head = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false }, f)
	local tx = 14
	if icon then
		local ic = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -10), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false }, head)
		Hub.Icons.get(icon, function(a, o, s) if ic.Parent then ic.Image = a ic.ImageRectOffset = o ic.ImageRectSize = s ic.Visible = true end end)
		tx = 42
	end
	Label(head, { Text = title, Size = UDim2.new(1, -(tx + 30), 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, tx, 0, 0), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Text })
	local arrow = Label(head, { Text = "▼", Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 0), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = T.Sub })
	local subLabel
	if sub and sub ~= "" then
		subLabel = Label(f, { Text = sub, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
		new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, tx) }, subLabel)
	end
	new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, f)
	local collapsed = false
	local function setCollapsed(v)
		collapsed = v
		arrow.Text = v and "▶" or "▼"
		for _, ch in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
			if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch ~= head and ch ~= subLabel then ch.Visible = (not v) and not ch:GetAttribute("SolosClosed") end
		end
	end
	bind(head.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("click") setCollapsed(not collapsed) end)
	table.insert(S.Sections, setCollapsed)
	S.SecMap[f] = setCollapsed
	return f
end

local function Toggle(parent, text, default, cb, minRank, id, desc)
	local row = Row(parent, desc and 46 or 36)
	Hub.titled(row, text .. rankSuffix(minRank), 70, desc)
	local sw = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0.5, -9), BackgroundColor3 = default and T.Accent or T.Off, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false }, row)
	round(sw, 9)
	sw:SetAttribute("SolosSwitch", true)
	local dot = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14), Position = default and UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, -7) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -7), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, sw)
	round(dot, 7)
	local state = default
	local obj = {}
	function obj.Set(v) -- visuals only
		state = v
		TweenService:Create(sw, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { BackgroundColor3 = v and T.Accent or T.Off }):Play()
		TweenService:Create(dot, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { Position = v and UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, -7) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -7) }):Play()
	end
	function obj.Get() return state end
	function obj.Fire(v) -- visuals + callback (+ rank gate). Returns false if denied.
		if minRank and v and not hasRank(minRank) then denyRank(minRank) return false end
		obj.Set(v)
		if cb then cb(v) end
		return true
	end
	-- sounds only play for real clicks (not for panic / config load / commands)
	bind(sw.MouseButton1Click, function()
		local want = not state
		if obj.Fire(want) == false then Hub.play("error") else Hub.play(want and "toggle_on" or "toggle_off") end
	end)
	if id then C[id] = obj end
	return obj
end

local function Slider(parent, text, min, max, default, cb, id)
	local long = game:GetService("TextService"):GetTextSize(text, 12, Enum.Font.Gotham, Vector2.new(1000, 20)).X > 122
	local row = Row(parent, long and 58 or 42)
	local hit
	if long then
		-- two-line layout: full-width name on top (value at the right), track underneath
		Label(row, { Text = text, Size = UDim2.new(1, -86, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 6), TextWrapped = false, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
		hit = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 28), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "" }, row)
	else
		Label(row, { Text = text, Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 1, 0) })
		hit = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -220, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0.5, -12), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "" }, row)
	end
	local track = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 6), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -3), BackgroundColor3 = T.Off, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, hit)
	round(track, 3)
	local pct = (default - min) / (max - min)
	local fill = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, track)
	round(fill, 3)
	local pill = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.new(pct, -7, 0.5, -7), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, track)
	round(pill, 7)
	local val = Label(row, { Text = tostring(default), Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, long and 0 or 1, long and 18 or 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, long and 6 or 0), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextWrapped = false, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 145, 150) })
	local current = default
	local obj = {}
	local function visual(v)
		v = math.clamp(math.floor(v + 0.5), min, max)
		current = v
		local s = (v - min) / (max - min)
		fill.Size = UDim2.new(s, 0, 1, 0)
		pill.Position = UDim2.new(s, -7, 0.5, -7)
		val.Text = tostring(v)
		return v
	end
	function obj.Set(v)
		v = visual(v)
		if cb then cb(v) end
	end
	function obj.SetSilent(v) visual(v) end -- moves the slider without firing the callback
	function obj.Get() return current end
	local sliding = false
	local function setFromX(x)
		local s = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
		local before = current
		obj.Set(min + s * (max - min))
		if current ~= before then Hub.play("slider", 0.85 + (current - min) / math.max(max - min, 1) * 0.5) end
	end
	bind(hit.InputBegan, function(i)
		if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			sliding = true
			setFromX(i.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	bind(UIS.InputChanged, function(i)
		if sliding and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then setFromX(i.Position.X) end
	end)
	bind(UIS.InputEnded, function(i)
		if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then sliding = false end
	end)
	if id then C[id] = obj end
	return obj
end

local function Button(parent, text, action, cb, minRank, desc)
	local row = Row(parent, desc and 46 or 36)
	Hub.titled(row, text .. rankSuffix(minRank), 150, desc)
	local b = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 26), Position = UDim2.new(1, -114, 0.5, -13), BackgroundColor3 = T.Btn, Text = action, TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11, AutoButtonColor = false }, row)
	round(b, 7)
	new("UIStroke", { Color = T.Accent, Transparency = 0.6, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, b)
	bind(b.MouseEnter, function() Hub.play("hover") b.BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover end)
	bind(b.MouseLeave, function() b.BackgroundColor3 = T.Btn end)
	bind(b.MouseButton1Click, function()
		if minRank and not hasRank(minRank) then Hub.play("error") denyRank(minRank) return end
		Hub.play("click")
		if cb then cb() end
	end)
	return b
end

local function Input(parent, text, placeholder, action, cb)
	local row = Row(parent, 36)
	Label(row, { Text = text, Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 1, 0) })
	local box = new("TextBox", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -262, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(0, 156, 0.5, -12), BackgroundColor3 = T.Btn, Text = "", PlaceholderText = placeholder, PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 95, 100), TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, ClearTextOnFocus = false, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, row)
	round(box, 6)
	local b = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0.5, -12), BackgroundColor3 = T.Btn, Text = action, TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11 }, row)
	round(b, 6)
	bind(b.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("click") if cb then cb(box.Text, false) end end)
	bind(box.FocusLost, function(enter) if enter and cb then cb(box.Text, true) end end)
	return box
end

local function Keybind(parent, text, default, cb, allowClear)
	local row = Row(parent, 36)
	Label(row, { Text = text, Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 1, 0) })
	local b = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(1, -94, 0.5, -12), BackgroundColor3 = T.Btn, Text = (default == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown) and "None" or default.Name, TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11 }, row)
	round(b, 6)
	local current, listening = default, false
	bind(b.MouseButton1Click, function()
		Hub.play("click")
		listening, BindingActive = true, true
		b.Text, b.TextColor3 = "Press key...", T.Accent
	end)
	bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i)
		if listening and i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			listening = false
			task.defer(function() BindingActive = false end)
			if i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
				-- cancelled, keep the old key
			elseif i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace and allowClear then
				current = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
				if cb then cb(current) end
			else
				current = i.KeyCode
				if cb then cb(current) end
			end
			b.Text, b.TextColor3 = (current == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown) and "None" or current.Name, T.Text
		end
	end)
end

local function InfoRow(parent, text)
	local row = Row(parent, 30)
	return Label(row, { Text = text })
end

-- avatar thumbnails (cached, async, shared by the player list / inspector card)
local Thumb = { cache = {}, pending = {} }
function Thumb.get(userId, cb)
	local hit = Thumb.cache[userId]
	if hit then cb(hit) return end
	local waiters = Thumb.pending[userId]
	if waiters then table.insert(waiters, cb) return end
	Thumb.pending[userId] = { cb }
	task.spawn(function()
		local ok, content = pcall(Players.GetUserThumbnailAsync, Players, userId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100)
		local list = Thumb.pending[userId]
		Thumb.pending[userId] = nil
		if ok and content then
			Thumb.cache[userId] = content
			for _, f in ipairs(list or {}) do pcall(f, content) end
		end
	end)
end

-- compact single-select dropdown (inline list, never clipped by a scroll frame). Get() = index, Name() = text.
-- cb(index, name) runs on every pick. Set(i, silent) changes it from code. Not saved to config (like the old cycle buttons).
local function Dropdown(parent, text, options, default, cb, minRank, desc)
	local row = Row(parent, desc and 46 or 36)
	Hub.titled(row, text .. rankSuffix(minRank), 170, desc)
	local pill = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 26), Position = UDim2.new(1, -154, 0.5, -13), BackgroundColor3 = T.Btn, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false }, row)
	round(pill, 7)
	new("UIStroke", { Color = T.Accent, Transparency = 0.6, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, pill)
	local val = Label(pill, { Text = "", Size = UDim2.new(1, -26, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Text, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
	local arrow = Label(pill, { Text = "▼", Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 0), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextSize = 8, TextColor3 = T.Sub })
	local list = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundColor3 = T.Bg, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false }, parent)
	round(list, 8)
	list:SetAttribute("SolosClosed", true)
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, list)
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 5), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 5), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 5), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 5) }, list)
	local opts, idx, btns, obj = options, math.clamp(default or 1, 1, math.max(#options, 1)), {}, {}
	local function paint()
		val.Text = opts[idx] or "-"
		for i, b in ipairs(btns) do
			b.BackgroundTransparency = (i == idx) and 0 or 1
			b.TextColor3 = (i == idx) and T.Text or T.Sub
		end
	end
	local function setOpen(v)
		list.Visible = v
		list:SetAttribute("SolosClosed", not v)
		arrow.Text = v and "▲" or "▼"
	end
	local function build()
		for _, b in ipairs(btns) do b:Destroy() end
		table.clear(btns)
		for i, name in ipairs(opts) do
			local b = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = name, TextColor3 = T.Sub, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, AutoButtonColor = false, LayoutOrder = i }, list)
			round(b, 6)
			new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10) }, b)
			bind(b.MouseButton1Click, function()
				Hub.play("click")
				idx = i
				paint()
				setOpen(false)
				if cb then cb(i, opts[i]) end
			end)
			table.insert(btns, b)
		end
		paint()
	end
	function obj.Get() return idx end
	function obj.Name() return opts[idx] end
	function obj.Set(i, silent)
		idx = math.clamp(i, 1, math.max(#opts, 1))
		paint()
		if not silent and cb then cb(idx, opts[idx]) end
	end
	function obj.SetOptions(newOpts, keep)
		opts = newOpts
		idx = keep and math.clamp(idx, 1, math.max(#opts, 1)) or 1
		build()
	end
	bind(pill.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("click") setOpen(not list.Visible) end)
	build()
	return obj
end

-- collapsible group for a list of toggles/sliders. Returns the content frame: use it as the parent of Toggle()/Slider().
-- The header shows "n of m on". Ids / config / commands of the toggles inside keep working.
local function Collapse(parent, title)
	local head = Row(parent, 36)
	Label(head, { Text = title, Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextColor3 = T.Text })
	local count = Label(head, { Text = "", Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -106, 0, 0), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = T.Sub })
	local arrow = Label(head, { Text = "▼", Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 0), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextSize = 8, TextColor3 = T.Sub })
	local hit = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 2 }, head)
	local content = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false }, parent)
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, content)
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10) }, content)
	content:SetAttribute("SolosGroup", true)
	content:SetAttribute("SolosCard", true)
	content:SetAttribute("SolosClosed", true)
	local function setOpen(v)
		content.Visible = v
		content:SetAttribute("SolosClosed", not v)
		arrow.Text = v and "▲" or "▼"
	end
	S.GroupOpen = S.GroupOpen or {}
	S.GroupOpen[content] = setOpen
	bind(hit.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("click") setOpen(not content.Visible) end)
	Hub.Groups = Hub.Groups or {}
	table.insert(Hub.Groups, { content = content, count = count })
	if not Hub._groupLoop then
		Hub._groupLoop = true
		task.spawn(function()
			while Running do
				for _, g in ipairs(Hub.Groups) do
					if g.content.Parent then
						local on, all = 0, 0
						for _, d in ipairs(g.content:GetDescendants()) do
							if d:GetAttribute("SolosSwitch") then
								all = all + 1
								if d.BackgroundColor3 == T.Accent then on = on + 1 end
							end
						end
						g.count.Text = all > 0 and (on .. " of " .. all .. " on") or ""
					end
				end
				task.wait(0.5)
			end
		end)
	end
	return content, setOpen
end

-- PANEL: one sidebar tab with a left sub-nav and a right pane of feature cards (layout from the reference).
-- Used by every game and by the Universal categories (Combat, Movement, Visuals, ...).
--   local P = Hub.GamePanel("Murder Mystery 2", "attack", "Round info, loot and attack tools.")
--   local RoundTab = P.Sub("Round", "round", "Live round info and role ESP.")   -- a page: Section(RoundTab, ...) as usual
function Hub.GamePanel(title, iconName, subtitle)
	Hub.Icons.Map[title] = iconName
	local page = AddTab(title)
	Tabs[title] = nil -- the panel itself is not a content page; its sub-pages are
	page.ScrollingEnabled = false
	page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
	page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
	for _, c in ipairs(page:GetChildren()) do
		if c:IsA("UIListLayout") or c:IsA("UIPadding") then c:Destroy() end
	end
	S.Panels = S.Panels or {}
	S.TabOwner = S.TabOwner or {}
	local panel = { Title = title, Icon = iconName, Page = page, Names = {}, Subs = {}, Items = {}, SubSrc = {}, Subtitles = {}, Default = subtitle or "", Current = nil }
	S.Panels[title] = panel
	local nav = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 142, 1, -24), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 12), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, page)
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, nav)
	local sep = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, -24), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(159, 12), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, page)
	local pane = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -160, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ClipsDescendants = true }, page)
	panel.Nav, panel.Pane = nav, pane

	-- the sub-nav only shows when there is more than one visible sub-page
	function panel.Refresh()
		local vis, first = 0, nil
		for _, n in ipairs(panel.Names) do
			if panel.Items[n].Btn.Visible then
				vis = vis + 1
				if not first then first = n end
			end
		end
		local multi = vis > 1
		nav.Visible, sep.Visible = multi, multi
		pane.Position = multi and UDim2.fromOffset(160, 0) or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
		pane.Size = multi and UDim2.new(1, -160, 1, 0) or UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		for _, f in pairs(panel.Subs) do
			local pad = f:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
			if pad then pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, multi and 4 or 18) end
		end
		local cur = panel.Current
		if first and not (cur and panel.Items[cur] and panel.Items[cur].Btn.Visible) then panel.Select(first) end
	end
	function panel.Select(name)
		if not panel.Subs[name] then return end
		panel.Current = name
		for n, f in pairs(panel.Subs) do f.Visible = (n == name) end
		for n, it in pairs(panel.Items) do
			local on = (n == name)
			it.Btn.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1
			it.Btn.TextColor3 = on and T.Text or T.Sub
			it.Stroke.Enabled = on
			it.Icon.ImageTransparency = on and 0 or 0.4
		end
		if S.CurTab == title then Hub.setHeader(title) end
	end
	local function addItem(name, icon)
		local btn = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = name, TextColor3 = T.Sub, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, AutoButtonColor = false, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, LayoutOrder = #panel.Names }, nav)
		round(btn, 8)
		local stroke = new("UIStroke", { Color = T.Accent, Transparency = 0.45, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Enabled = false }, btn)
		new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 34) }, btn)
		local ic = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, -24, 0.5, -8), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ImageTransparency = 0.4, Visible = false }, btn)
		if icon then Hub.Icons.get(icon, function(a, o, s) if ic.Parent then ic.Image = a ic.ImageRectOffset = o ic.ImageRectSize = s ic.Visible = true end end) end
		bind(btn.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("tab") panel.Select(name) end)
		bind(btn.MouseEnter, function() if btn.BackgroundTransparency == 1 then btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6 end end)
		bind(btn.MouseLeave, function() if btn.BackgroundTransparency == 0.6 then btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end end)
		return { Btn = btn, Icon = ic, Stroke = stroke }
	end
	local function newPage(name)
		local f = new("ScrollingFrame", { Name = name, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = T.Hover, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), Visible = false }, pane)
		new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 14), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, f)
		new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 18), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 16), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 12), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 18) }, f)
		return f
	end
	local function register(name, f, icon, subtitle, srcTab)
		f:SetAttribute("SolosSub", name)
		table.insert(panel.Names, name)
		panel.Subs[name] = f
		panel.Subtitles[name] = subtitle
		panel.SubSrc[name] = srcTab
		if srcTab then
			S.TabOwner[srcTab] = S.TabOwner[srcTab] or { panel = panel, sub = name }
			local ob = TabButtons[srcTab]
			if ob and not S.Panels[srcTab] then ob.Btn.Visible = false end -- the old sidebar button is replaced by the panel
		end
		panel.Items[name] = addItem(name, icon or Hub.Icons.Map[name])
		panel.Refresh()
		if not panel.Current then panel.Select(name) end
	end
	-- a brand new sub-page
	function panel.Sub(name, icon, subtitle)
		local f = newPage(name)
		register(name, f, icon, subtitle)
		return f
	end
	-- an existing tab page becomes a sub-page (srcTab = its old tab name, used by filterTabs + search)
	function panel.Adopt(name, existing, icon, subtitle, srcTab)
		existing.Parent = pane
		existing.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		existing.Position = UDim2.new()
		existing.Visible = false
		local pad = existing:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
		if pad then pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 18) pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 16) pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 12) end
		register(name, existing, icon, subtitle, srcTab)
		return existing
	end
	-- a new sub-page made from some of an existing page's cards (matched by card title)
	function panel.Take(name, from, titles, icon, subtitle, srcTab)
		local f = newPage(name)
		local want = {}
		for _, tl in ipairs(titles) do want[tl] = true end
		for _, ch in ipairs(from:GetChildren()) do
			if ch:IsA("Frame") and ch:GetAttribute("SolosCard") then
				local head = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextButton")
				local lbl = head and head:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
				if lbl and want[lbl.Text] then ch.Parent = f end
			end
		end
		register(name, f, icon, subtitle, srcTab)
		return f
	end
	return panel
end

-- like GamePanel, but for a Universal category that replaces an existing sidebar tab of the same name
function Hub.MergePanel(title, iconName, subtitle)
	local oldPage, oldBtn = Tabs[title], TabButtons[title]
	local panel = Hub.GamePanel(title, iconName, subtitle)
	Tabs[title] = oldPage
	if oldBtn then oldBtn.Btn:Destroy() end
	panel.Universal = true
	return panel
end

-- searchable player list with profile pictures
local function PlayerList(parent, height)
	local searchRow = Row(parent, 34)
	local search = new("TextBox", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -92, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -12), BackgroundColor3 = T.Btn, Text = "", PlaceholderText = "Search display name or @username...", PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 95, 100), TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, ClearTextOnFocus = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left }, searchRow)
	round(search, 6)
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8) }, search)
	local clearBtn = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(1, -74, 0.5, -12), BackgroundColor3 = T.Btn, Text = "Clear", TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11 }, searchRow)
	round(clearBtn, 6)
	local countHolder = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, parent)
	local countLbl = Label(countHolder, { Text = "", Position = UDim2.new(), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Sub })

	local list = new("ScrollingFrame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height), BackgroundColor3 = T.Bg, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new() }, parent)
	round(list, 8)
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, list)
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4) }, list)

	S.ListRefresh = S.ListRefresh or {}
	local function refreshAll() for _, f in ipairs(S.ListRefresh) do pcall(f) end end
	local buttons, first = {}, nil
	local function refresh()
		for _, b in pairs(buttons) do b:Destroy() end
		table.clear(buttons)
		if S.Target and not S.Target.Parent then S.Target = nil end
		local q = (search.Text:lower():match("^%s*@?(.-)%s*$")) or ""
		local pool, total = {}, 0
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= LocalPlayer then
				total = total + 1
				if q == "" or p.Name:lower():find(q, 1, true) or p.DisplayName:lower():find(q, 1, true) then
					table.insert(pool, p)
				end
			end
		end
		table.sort(pool, function(a, b) return a.DisplayName:lower() < b.DisplayName:lower() end)
		first = pool[1]
		for i, p in ipairs(pool) do
			local sel = (S.Target == p)
			local b = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38), BackgroundColor3 = sel and T.Accent or T.Hover, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, LayoutOrder = i }, list)
			round(b, 6)
			local img = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30), Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -15), BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BorderSizePixel = 0, Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/GuiImagePlaceholder.png" }, b)
			round(img, 15)
			Thumb.get(p.UserId, function(content) if img.Parent then img.Image = content end end)
			Label(b, { Text = p.DisplayName, Size = UDim2.new(1, -52, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 4), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.Text, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
			Label(b, { Text = "@" .. p.Name, Size = UDim2.new(1, -52, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 20), TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = sel and T.Text or T.Sub, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
			b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Hub.play("click") S.Target = p refreshAll() end)
			table.insert(buttons, b)
		end
		if #pool == 0 then
			countLbl.Text = (total == 0) and "No other players in this server" or ("No matches for \"" .. search.Text .. "\"")
		else
			countLbl.Text = string.format("Showing %d of %d players  -  press Enter to pick the first match", #pool, total)
		end
		if S.OnTargetChanged then pcall(S.OnTargetChanged) end
	end
	bind(search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), refresh)
	bind(search.FocusLost, function(enter)
		if enter and first then S.Target = first refreshAll() end
	end)
	bind(clearBtn.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("click") search.Text = "" end)
	bind(Players.PlayerAdded, function() task.defer(refresh) end)
	bind(Players.PlayerRemoving, function() task.delay(0.15, refresh) end)
	table.insert(S.ListRefresh, refresh)
	refresh()
	return refresh
end

-- ====================================================
-- CHARACTER HELPERS
-- ====================================================
local function getChar() return LocalPlayer.Character end
local function getHum() local c = LocalPlayer.Character return c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") end
local function getRoot() local c = LocalPlayer.Character return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end
local function cam() return Workspace.CurrentCamera end

local function findPlayer(q)
	if not q or q == "" then return nil end
	q = q:lower()
	if q == "me" then return LocalPlayer end
	if q == "random" then
		local pool = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= LocalPlayer then table.insert(pool, p) end end
		return pool[math.random(math.max(#pool, 1))]
	end
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p.Name:lower():sub(1, #q) == q or p.DisplayName:lower():sub(1, #q) == q then return p end
	end
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p.Name:lower():find(q, 1, true) or p.DisplayName:lower():find(q, 1, true) then return p end
	end
	return nil
end

local function nearestPlayer()
	local root = getRoot()
	if not root then return nil end
	local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p ~= LocalPlayer then
			local r = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if r then
				local d = (r.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
				if d < bestD then best, bestD = p, d end
			end
		end
	end
	return best, bestD
end

-- ====================================================
-- FEATURE LOGIC
-- ====================================================
local origSpeed, origJump, origJumpHeight, origUseJump = 16, 50, 7.2, false
local speedApplied, jumpApplied = false, false
local origGravity = Workspace.Gravity
local origFov = cam().FieldOfView
local origMaxZoom = LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance
local origClock = Lighting.ClockTime
local origLighting = {
	Brightness = Lighting.Brightness, FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
	GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows, Ambient = Lighting.Ambient, OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
}
local lastSafe, deathCF, prevTpCF = nil, nil, nil
local noclipParts, flingParts = weak(), weak()

local function doTeleport(cf)
	local r = getRoot()
	if not r then notify("No character", T.Warn) return false end
	prevTpCF = r.CFrame
	if S.TweenTP then
		TweenService:Create(r, TweenInfo.new(S.TweenTime or 1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { CFrame = cf }):Play()
	else
		r.CFrame = cf
	end
	return true
end

-- main movement loop
bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
	local hum, root = getHum(), getRoot()
	if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
	-- walk speed / sprint
	local ws
	if S.Speed then ws = S.SpeedValue end
	if S.Sprint and not UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() and UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then ws = math.max(ws or 0, S.SprintValue) end
	if ws then
		if not speedApplied then origSpeed = hum.WalkSpeed speedApplied = true end
		hum.WalkSpeed = ws
	elseif speedApplied then
		hum.WalkSpeed = origSpeed
		speedApplied = false
	end
	-- jump power
	if S.Jump then
		if not jumpApplied then
			origJump, origJumpHeight, origUseJump = hum.JumpPower, hum.JumpHeight, hum.UseJumpPower
			jumpApplied = true
		end
		hum.UseJumpPower = true
		hum.JumpPower = S.JumpValue
	elseif jumpApplied then
		hum.JumpPower = origJump
		hum.JumpHeight = origJumpHeight
		hum.UseJumpPower = origUseJump
		jumpApplied = false
	end
	-- bunny hop
	if S.Bhop and not UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() and UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) and hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
		hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
	end
	if root then
		if S.Spin then root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(S.SpinSpeed), 0) end
		if S.Freeze and not root.Anchored then root.Anchored = true end
	end
end)
onUnload(function()
	local h, r = getHum(), getRoot()
	if h then
		if speedApplied then h.WalkSpeed = origSpeed end
		if jumpApplied then
			h.JumpPower = origJump
			h.JumpHeight = origJumpHeight
			h.UseJumpPower = origUseJump
		end
	end
	if r then r.Anchored = false end
end)

-- noclip + anti-fling
local function restoreNoclip()
	for p in pairs(noclipParts) do if p.Parent then p.CanCollide = true end end
	table.clear(noclipParts)
end
local function restoreFling()
	for p in pairs(flingParts) do if p.Parent then p.CanCollide = true end end
	table.clear(flingParts)
end
bind(RunService.Stepped, function()
	if S.Noclip and LocalPlayer.Character then
		for _, p in ipairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
			if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.CanCollide then p.CanCollide = false noclipParts[p] = true end
		end
	end
	if S.AntiFling then
		for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if pl ~= LocalPlayer and pl.Character then
				for _, d in ipairs(pl.Character:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.CanCollide then d.CanCollide = false flingParts[d] = true end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)
onUnload(restoreNoclip)
onUnload(restoreFling)

bind(UIS.JumpRequest, function()
	if S.InfJump then
		local h = getHum()
		if h then h:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
	end
end)
bind(LocalPlayer.Idled, function()
	if S.AntiAfk then
		VirtualUser:CaptureController()
		VirtualUser:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
	end
end)

-- fly
local flyBV, flyBG
local function stopFly()
	if flyBV then flyBV:Destroy() flyBV = nil end
	if flyBG then flyBG:Destroy() flyBG = nil end
	local h = getHum()
	if h then h.PlatformStand = false end
end
local function startFly()
	local root, hum = getRoot(), getHum()
	if not root or not hum then S.Fly = false if C.fly then C.fly.Set(false) end return end
	stopFly()
	flyBV = new("BodyVelocity", { MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9), Velocity = Vector3.zero }, root)
	flyBG = new("BodyGyro", { MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9), P = 9e4, CFrame = root.CFrame }, root)
	hum.PlatformStand = true
end
local function onFly(v)
	S.Fly = v
	if v then startFly() else stopFly() end
end
bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
	if S.Fly and flyBV and flyBG then
		local c = cam()
		local dir = Vector3.zero
		if not UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then dir += c.CFrame.LookVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then dir -= c.CFrame.LookVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then dir += c.CFrame.RightVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then dir -= c.CFrame.RightVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then dir += Vector3.yAxis end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then dir -= Vector3.yAxis end
		end
		flyBV.Velocity = dir.Magnitude > 0 and dir.Unit * S.FlySpeed or Vector3.zero
		flyBG.CFrame = c.CFrame
	end
end)
onUnload(stopFly)

-- character hooks (respawn handling, death position)
local function hookChar(char)
	task.spawn(function()
		local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
		if hum then
			hum.Died:Connect(function()
				local r = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if r then deathCF = r.CFrame end
			end)
		end
	end)
end
if LocalPlayer.Character then hookChar(LocalPlayer.Character) end
bind(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function(char)
	flyBV, flyBG = nil, nil
	speedApplied, jumpApplied = false, false
	hookChar(char)
	task.spawn(function()
		local root = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
		if not root or not Running then return end
		task.wait(0.15)
		if S.DeathReturn and deathCF then
			root.CFrame = deathCF
			deathCF = nil
			notify("Returned to death location")
		end
		if S.Fly then task.wait(0.4) if S.Fly and Running then startFly() end end
	end)
end)

-- spectate
local function stopSpectate()
	S.Spectating = false
	local h = getHum()
	if h then cam().CameraSubject = h end
end
local function spectate(p)
	local h = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if h then cam().CameraSubject = h S.Spectating = true notify("Spectating " .. p.DisplayName)
	else notify("Target has no character", T.Warn) end
end
onUnload(stopSpectate)

local function teleportToPlayer(p)
	local theirs = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if theirs and doTeleport(theirs.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)) then notify("Teleported to " .. p.DisplayName)
	elseif not theirs then notify("Target has no character", T.Warn) end
end

-- freecam
local fc = { look = false, yaw = 0, pitch = 0, pos = Vector3.zero, prevType = nil }
local function setFreecam(v)
	S.Freecam = v
	local c = cam()
	if v then
		fc.prevType = c.CameraType
		local look = c.CFrame.LookVector
		fc.pitch = math.asin(math.clamp(look.Y, -1, 1))
		fc.yaw = math.atan2(-look.X, -look.Z)
		fc.pos = c.CFrame.Position
		c.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
		local r = getRoot()
		if r then r.Anchored = true end
		notify("Freecam: hold Right Mouse to look, WASD + Space/Ctrl, Shift = fast")
	else
		fc.look = false
		UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
		c.CameraType = fc.prevType or Enum.CameraType.Custom
		local r = getRoot()
		if r and not S.Freeze then r.Anchored = false end
		local h = getHum()
		if h then c.CameraSubject = h end
	end
end
bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i)
	if S.Freecam and i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
		fc.look = true
		UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCurrentPosition
	end
end)
bind(UIS.InputEnded, function(i)
	if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 and fc.look then
		fc.look = false
		UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
	end
end)
bind(UIS.InputChanged, function(i)
	if S.Freecam and fc.look and i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
		fc.yaw = fc.yaw - i.Delta.X * 0.003
		fc.pitch = math.clamp(fc.pitch - i.Delta.Y * 0.003, -1.5, 1.5)
	end
end)
bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function(dt)
	if S.Freecam then
		local rot = CFrame.fromOrientation(fc.pitch, fc.yaw, 0)
		local dir = Vector3.zero
		if not UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then dir += rot.LookVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then dir -= rot.LookVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then dir += rot.RightVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then dir -= rot.RightVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then dir += Vector3.yAxis end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then dir -= Vector3.yAxis end
		end
		local sp = S.FreecamSpeed * (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) and 3 or 1)
		if dir.Magnitude > 0 then fc.pos = fc.pos + dir.Unit * sp * dt end
		cam().CFrame = CFrame.new(fc.pos) * rot
	end
end)
onUnload(function() if S.Freecam then setFreecam(false) end end)


-- click teleport / click delete
local Mouse = LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
local deleted = {}
local function undoDelete()
	local d = table.remove(deleted)
	if d and d.part then d.part.Parent = d.parent notify("Restored " .. d.part.Name) else notify("Nothing to undo", T.Warn) end
end
local function restoreAllDeleted()
	for i = #deleted, 1, -1 do
		local d = deleted[i]
		if d.part then d.part.Parent = d.parent end
	end
	table.clear(deleted)
end
bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(input, gp)
	if gp or input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
	if S.ClickTP and UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) and Mouse.Target then
		doTeleport(CFrame.new(Mouse.Hit.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)))
	elseif S.ClickDelete and UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftAlt) then
		local t = Mouse.Target
		if t and not t:IsA("Terrain") and not (LocalPlayer.Character and t:IsDescendantOf(LocalPlayer.Character)) then
			local m = t:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			if not (m and m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")) then
				table.insert(deleted, { part = t, parent = t.Parent })
				if #deleted > 300 then table.remove(deleted, 1) end
				t.Parent = nil
				notify("Deleted " .. t.Name .. " (undo in Tools)")
			end
		end
	end
end)
onUnload(restoreAllDeleted)

-- world effects
bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
	if S.Fullbright then
		Lighting.Brightness, Lighting.FogEnd, Lighting.GlobalShadows = 2, 1e6, false
		Lighting.Ambient, Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(178, 178, 178), Color3.fromRGB(178, 178, 178)
	end
	if S.NoShadows then Lighting.GlobalShadows = false end
	if S.NoFog then Lighting.FogEnd = 1e6 end
	if S.LockTime then Lighting.ClockTime = S.TimeValue end
end)
local function restoreLighting() for k, v in pairs(origLighting) do Lighting[k] = v end end
onUnload(function()
	if mods.Light then restoreLighting() end
	if mods.Time then Lighting.ClockTime = origClock end
	if mods.Gravity then Workspace.Gravity = origGravity end
	if mods.Fov then cam().FieldOfView = origFov end
	if mods.Zoom then LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = origMaxZoom end
end)

local origQuality
local function setLowGfx(on)
	pcall(function()
		local r = settings().Rendering
		if on then origQuality = origQuality or r.QualityLevel r.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
		elseif origQuality then r.QualityLevel = origQuality end
	end)
end
onUnload(function() if S.LowGfx then setLowGfx(false) end end)

local particlesOff = weak()
local function isEmitter(d)
	return d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Trail") or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("Smoke") or d:IsA("Fire") or d:IsA("Sparkles")
end
local function setParticles(on)
	if on then
		for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if isEmitter(d) and d.Enabled then
				particlesOff[d] = true
				d.Enabled = false
			end
		end
	else
		for d in pairs(particlesOff) do if d.Parent then d.Enabled = true end end
		table.clear(particlesOff)
	end
end
onUnload(function() setParticles(false) end)

-- post-processing effects (blur, bloom, color correction, sun rays, depth of field)
local effectsOff = weak()
local function setEffects(off)
	if off then
		local function scan(root)
			for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("PostEffect") and d.Enabled then effectsOff[d] = true d.Enabled = false end
			end
		end
		scan(Lighting)
		local c = cam()
		if c then scan(c) end
	else
		for d in pairs(effectsOff) do if d.Parent then d.Enabled = true end end
		table.clear(effectsOff)
	end
end
onUnload(function() setEffects(false) end)

-- keep particle removal working on objects that appear later
bind(Workspace.DescendantAdded, function(d)
	if S.NoParticles and isEmitter(d) and d.Enabled then
		particlesOff[d] = true
		d.Enabled = false
	end
end)

-- crosshair
local Cross = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false }, EspGui)
local crossBars = {}
for i = 1, 4 do
	crossBars[i] = new("Frame", { BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5) }, Cross)
end
local function updateCross()
	local len, gap, th = S.CrossSize, S.CrossGap, S.CrossThick or 2
	local off = gap + len / 2
	crossBars[1].Size, crossBars[1].Position = UDim2.fromOffset(th, len), UDim2.fromOffset(0, -off)
	crossBars[2].Size, crossBars[2].Position = UDim2.fromOffset(th, len), UDim2.fromOffset(0, off)
	crossBars[3].Size, crossBars[3].Position = UDim2.fromOffset(len, th), UDim2.fromOffset(-off, 0)
	crossBars[4].Size, crossBars[4].Position = UDim2.fromOffset(len, th), UDim2.fromOffset(off, 0)
	for _, b in ipairs(crossBars) do b.BackgroundColor3 = S.CrossColor end
	Cross.Visible = S.Crosshair
end
updateCross()

-- server helpers
local function serverHop(smallest)
	local ok, res = pcall(function()
		local url = ("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"):format(game.PlaceId)
		return HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet(url))
	end)
	if not ok or type(res) ~= "table" or not res.data then notify("Server hop isn't available here", T.Warn) return end
	local pool = {}
	for _, s in ipairs(res.data) do
		if s.id ~= game.JobId and s.playing and s.maxPlayers and s.playing < s.maxPlayers then table.insert(pool, s) end
	end
	if #pool == 0 then notify("No other servers found", T.Warn) return end
	local pick
	if smallest then
		table.sort(pool, function(a, b) return a.playing < b.playing end)
		pick = pool[1]
	else
		pick = pool[math.random(#pool)]
	end
	notify("Hopping servers...", T.Good)
	TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, pick.id, LocalPlayer)
end

-- auto rejoin when kicked / disconnected (needs CoreGui access, executor only)
local autoRejoin, rejoinHooked = false, false
task.spawn(function()
	local ok, overlay = pcall(function()
		local gui = game:GetService("CoreGui"):WaitForChild("RobloxPromptGui", 10)
		return gui and gui:WaitForChild("promptOverlay", 10)
	end)
	if ok and overlay and Running then
		rejoinHooked = true
		bind(overlay.ChildAdded, function(ch)
			if autoRejoin and ch.Name == "ErrorPrompt" then
				notify("Disconnected - rejoining...", T.Warn)
				task.wait(1)
				pcall(function() TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer) end)
			end
		end)
	end
end)

-- fps cap (executor only)
local origFps = getfpscap and getfpscap() or nil
local fpsChanged = false
onUnload(function() if fpsChanged and origFps and setfpscap then pcall(setfpscap, origFps) end end)

-- join / leave alerts
bind(Players.PlayerAdded, function(p) if S.Alerts then notify(p.DisplayName .. " joined", T.Good, not Hub.sfx.joinLeave) end end)
bind(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(p) if S.Alerts then notify(p.DisplayName .. " left", T.Warn, not Hub.sfx.joinLeave) end end)

-- config save / load (executor file functions)
local CONFIG_FILE = Hub.path("config.json")
local CONFIG_SKIP = { fly = true, freecam = true, freeze = true, follow = true, orbit = true, autotool = true, repeatcmd = true, rec = true, hover = true, voidhold = true, stick = true, tour = true, cine = true }
local function saveConfig()
	if not (writefile and readfile and isfile) then notify("File saving isn't supported here", T.Warn) return end
	local data = { toggles = {}, values = {} }
	for id, obj in pairs(C) do
		if not CONFIG_SKIP[id] then
			local v = obj.Get()
			if type(v) == "boolean" then data.toggles[id] = v
			elseif type(v) == "number" then data.values[id] = v end
		end
	end
	local ok = pcall(function() writefile(CONFIG_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(data)) end)
	notify(ok and "Config saved" or "Couldn't save config", ok and T.Good or T.Bad)
end
local function loadConfig()
	if not (writefile and readfile and isfile) then notify("File saving isn't supported here", T.Warn) return end
	if not isfile(CONFIG_FILE) then notify("No saved config found", T.Warn) return end
	local ok, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(CONFIG_FILE)) end)
	if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" then notify("Config file is unreadable", T.Bad) return end
	quiet = true
	for id, v in pairs(data.values or {}) do
		local o = C[id]
		if o and type(v) == "number" and o.SetSilent then
			pcall(o.Set, v)
		end
	end
	for id, v in pairs(data.toggles or {}) do
		local o = C[id]
		if o and type(v) == "boolean" and not CONFIG_SKIP[id] and o.Get() ~= v then
			pcall(o.Fire, v)
		end
	end
	quiet = false
	notify("Config loaded", T.Good)
end

-- ====================================================
-- ESP
-- ====================================================
-- team detection that also works in games that don't use Roblox Teams (v3.9)
local function teamKeyOf(p)
	if p.Team ~= nil then return "t:" .. p.Team.Name end
	if not p.Neutral then return "c:" .. tostring(p.TeamColor) end
	local ch = p.Character
	local ls = p:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
	for _, n in ipairs({ "Team", "team", "TeamName", "Side", "Faction" }) do
		local a = p:GetAttribute(n)
		if a == nil and ch then a = ch:GetAttribute(n) end
		if a ~= nil then return "a:" .. tostring(a) end
		local v = p:FindFirstChild(n)
		if not (v and v:IsA("ValueBase")) then v = ls and ls:FindFirstChild(n) end
		if v and v:IsA("ValueBase") then return "v:" .. tostring(v.Value) end
	end
	return nil
end
S.TeamKey = teamKeyOf
S.SameTeam = function(p)
	local mine, theirs = teamKeyOf(LocalPlayer), teamKeyOf(p)
	return mine ~= nil and theirs ~= nil and mine == theirs
end
S.TeamColorOf = function(p)
	if p.Team ~= nil then return p.Team.TeamColor.Color end
	if not p.Neutral then return p.TeamColor.Color end
	return nil
end

local Esp = {}
local function buildCard(plr, o)
	local gui = new("BillboardGui", { Name = "SolosCard", Size = UDim2.fromOffset(176, 58), StudsOffsetWorldSpace = Vector3.new(0, 3.4, 0), AlwaysOnTop = true, ResetOnSpawn = false, LightInfluence = 0, Enabled = false, MaxDistance = S.ESP.MaxDist }, PlayerGui)
	local frame = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = T.Side, BackgroundTransparency = 0.08, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, gui)
	round(frame, 8)
	o.CardStroke = new("UIStroke", { Color = S.ESP.Color, Thickness = 1.5, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, frame)
	o.CardImg = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 44), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 7), BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BorderSizePixel = 0, Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/GuiImagePlaceholder.png" }, frame)
	round(o.CardImg, 6)
	Label(frame, { Text = plr.DisplayName, Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 6), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.Text, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
	Label(frame, { Text = "@" .. plr.Name, Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 23), TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
	local bar = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0, 5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 58, 1, -14), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, frame)
	round(bar, 3)
	o.CardFill = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, bar)
	round(o.CardFill, 3)
	o.Card = gui
	Thumb.get(plr.UserId, function(content) if o.CardImg and o.CardImg.Parent then o.CardImg.Image = content end end)
end

local function makeEsp(plr)
	if Esp[plr] then return end
	local o = {}
	o.Box = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false }, EspGui)
