	o.Stroke = new("UIStroke", { Thickness = 1.5, Color = S.ESP.Color, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, o.Box)
	o.Name = new("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 14), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 12, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4, Visible = false }, EspGui)
	o.Info = new("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 14), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 214, 220), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4, Visible = false }, EspGui)
	o.HealthBack = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false }, EspGui)
	o.HealthFill = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, o.HealthBack)
	o.Tracer = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundColor3 = S.ESP.Color, Visible = false }, EspGui)
	Esp[plr] = o
end
local function hideEsp(o)
	o.Box.Visible, o.Name.Visible, o.Info.Visible, o.HealthBack.Visible, o.Tracer.Visible = false, false, false, false, false
	if o.Highlight then o.Highlight.Enabled = false end
	if o.Card then o.Card.Enabled = false end
end
local function destroyEsp(plr)
	local o = Esp[plr]
	if not o then return end
	for _, v in pairs(o) do if typeof(v) == "Instance" then pcall(function() v:Destroy() end) end end
	Esp[plr] = nil
end
for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= LocalPlayer then makeEsp(p) end end
bind(Players.PlayerAdded, function(p) if p ~= LocalPlayer then makeEsp(p) end end)
bind(Players.PlayerRemoving, destroyEsp)

bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
	local E = S.ESP
	if not E.Enabled then
		if mods.EspOn then
			for _, o in pairs(Esp) do hideEsp(o) end
			mods.EspOn = false
		end
		return
	end
	mods.EspOn = true
	local c = cam()
	local myRoot = getRoot()
	local baseCol = E.Rainbow and Color3.fromHSV((os.clock() * 0.25) % 1, 0.85, 1) or E.Color
	for plr, o in pairs(Esp) do
		local char = plr.Character
		local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local show = root ~= nil and hum ~= nil and hum.Health > 0
		if show and E.TeamCheck and S.SameTeam(plr) then show = false end
		local dist = 0
		if show then
			dist = (root.Position - (myRoot and myRoot.Position or c.CFrame.Position)).Magnitude
			if dist > E.MaxDist then show = false end
		end
		if not show then hideEsp(o) continue end

		-- color: friend > selected player > team color > rainbow / chosen color
		local col = baseCol
		if E.TeamColors and not E.Rainbow then local tc = S.TeamColorOf(plr) if tc then col = tc end end
		if E.HighlightTarget and plr == S.Target then col = T.Warn end
		if E.Friends and E.FriendCache and E.FriendCache[plr] then col = T.Good end

		-- chams (work even when off screen)
		if E.Chams then
			if not o.Highlight or o.Highlight.Parent ~= char then
				if o.Highlight then o.Highlight:Destroy() end
				o.Highlight = new("Highlight", { FillTransparency = 0.6, OutlineTransparency = 0, DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop }, char)
			end
			o.Highlight.FillColor, o.Highlight.OutlineColor, o.Highlight.Enabled = col, col, true
			o.Highlight.FillTransparency = E.FillTrans or 0.6
		elseif o.Highlight then
			o.Highlight.Enabled = false
		end

		-- player card above the head (pfp + display name + username)
		if E.Cards then
			if not o.Card then buildCard(plr, o) end
			local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or root
			if o.Card.Adornee ~= head then o.Card.Adornee = head end
			o.Card.MaxDistance = E.MaxDist
			o.Card.Enabled = true
			o.CardStroke.Color = col
			local hp = math.clamp(hum.Health / math.max(hum.MaxHealth, 1), 0, 1)
			o.CardFill.Size = UDim2.new(hp, 0, 1, 0)
			o.CardFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(hp * 0.33, 1, 1)
		elseif o.Card then
			o.Card.Enabled = false
		end

		local rootPos, onScreen = c:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)
		if not onScreen then
			o.Box.Visible, o.Name.Visible, o.Info.Visible, o.HealthBack.Visible, o.Tracer.Visible = false, false, false, false, false
			continue
		end
		local top = c:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
		local bot = c:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0))
		local h = math.max(math.abs(bot.Y - top.Y), 8)
		local w = h * 0.55
		local x, y = rootPos.X - w / 2, top.Y

		o.Box.Visible = E.Boxes
		o.Box.Position, o.Box.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
		o.Stroke.Color = col
		o.Stroke.Thickness = E.Thick or 1.5

		o.Name.Visible = E.Names
		o.Name.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(rootPos.X, y - 2)
		o.Name.Text = plr.DisplayName
		o.Name.TextSize = E.TextSize or 12
		o.Info.TextSize = (E.TextSize or 12) - 1

		local parts = {}
		if E.Distance then table.insert(parts, string.format("%d studs", math.floor(dist))) end
		if E.Health then table.insert(parts, string.format("%d HP", math.floor(hum.Health))) end
		if E.Tool then
			local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
			if tool then table.insert(parts, tool.Name) end
		end
		o.Info.Visible = #parts > 0
		o.Info.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(rootPos.X, y + h + 2)
		o.Info.Text = table.concat(parts, "  |  ")

		local pct = math.clamp(hum.Health / math.max(hum.MaxHealth, 1), 0, 1)
		o.HealthBack.Visible = E.Health
		o.HealthBack.Position, o.HealthBack.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(x - 6, y), UDim2.fromOffset(3, h)
		o.HealthFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, pct, 0)
		o.HealthFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(pct * 0.33, 1, 1)

		if E.Tracers then
			local vp = c.ViewportSize
			local oy = (E.TracerOrigin == 1 and vp.Y) or (E.TracerOrigin == 2 and vp.Y / 2) or 0
			local from, to = Vector2.new(vp.X / 2, oy), Vector2.new(rootPos.X, y + h)
			local d = to - from
			o.Tracer.Visible = true
			o.Tracer.BackgroundColor3 = col
			o.Tracer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((from.X + to.X) / 2, (from.Y + to.Y) / 2)
			o.Tracer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, d.Magnitude)
			o.Tracer.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X)) - 90
		else
			o.Tracer.Visible = false
		end
	end
end)
onUnload(function()
	for plr in pairs(Esp) do destroyEsp(plr) end
	for _, o in ipairs(PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do if o.Name == "SolosCard" then o:Destroy() end end
end)

-- ====================================================
-- SLOW TICK (0.25s): follow, anti-void, inspector, hide players, position
-- ====================================================
local slowAcc = 0
local function slowTick()
	local hum, root = getHum(), getRoot()
	-- anti-void / safe position
	if root and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
		local voidY = Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
		if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air and root.Position.Y > voidY + 150 then lastSafe = root.CFrame end
		if S.AntiVoid and lastSafe and root.Position.Y < voidY + 60 then
			root.CFrame = lastSafe + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			notify("Anti-Void: pulled you back", T.Warn)
		end
		if PosLabel then PosLabel.Text = string.format("Position: %.1f, %.1f, %.1f", root.Position.X, root.Position.Y, root.Position.Z) end
	end
	-- follow target
	if S.Follow then
		local t = S.Target
		local tr = t and t.Character and t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if tr and hum then hum:MoveTo(tr.Position) end
	end
	-- inspector
	if Insp.Display then
		local t = S.Target
		if t and t.Parent then
			local th = t.Character and t.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local tr = t.Character and t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			Insp.Display.Text = "Display: " .. t.DisplayName
			Insp.User.Text = "Username: @" .. t.Name
			Insp.Id.Text = "User ID: " .. t.UserId
			Insp.Age.Text = "Account age: " .. t.AccountAge .. " days"
			Insp.Team.Text = "Team: " .. (t.Team and t.Team.Name or "None")
			Insp.Health.Text = "Health: " .. (th and (math.floor(th.Health) .. " / " .. math.floor(th.MaxHealth)) or "-")
			Insp.Dist.Text = "Distance: " .. ((tr and root) and (math.floor((tr.Position - root.Position).Magnitude) .. " studs") or "-")
		else
			for _, l in pairs(Insp) do l.Text = l.Text:match("^[^:]+:") .. " -" end
		end
	end
end
bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
	slowAcc = slowAcc + dt
	if slowAcc >= 0.25 then
		slowAcc = 0
		pcall(slowTick)
	end
end)

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: DASHBOARD
-- ====================================================
do
	local Home = AddTab("Dashboard", ICONS.Dashboard)
	local Grid = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 140), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, Home)
	new("UIGridLayout", { CellSize = UDim2.new(0.5, -6, 0, 64), CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, Grid)
	local function StatCard(title, iconName)
		local c = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, Grid)
		round(c, 10)
		new("UIStroke", { Color = T.Hover, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, c)
		local ico = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 38), Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.5, -19), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, c)
		round(ico, 19)
		local img = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -11, 0.5, -11), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false }, ico)
		Hub.Icons.get(iconName, function(a, o, s) if img.Parent then img.Image = a img.ImageRectOffset = o img.ImageRectSize = s img.Visible = true end end)
		Label(c, { Text = title, Size = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 12), TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Sub })
		return Label(c, { Text = "-", Size = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 30), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 16, TextColor3 = T.Text })
	end
	local PlayersStat = StatCard("Server Players", "players")
	local RuntimeStat = StatCard("Session Runtime", "time")
	local PingStat = StatCard("Ping", "active")
	local FpsStat = StatCard("FPS", "speed")

	local Disc = Section(Home, "Community", "Join the Solos Hub Discord for updates and support", "discord")
	Button(Disc, "Join Discord", "Join", function() Hub.joinDiscord() end)
	Button(Disc, "Copy Discord Link", "Copy", function() Hub.copyDiscord() end)

	local Quick = Section(Home, "Quick Actions", "Common one-click tools", "active")
	Button(Quick, "Reset Character", "Respawn", function() local h = getHum() if h then h.Health = 0 end end)
	Button(Quick, "Rejoin Server", "Rejoin", function() TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer) end)
	Button(Quick, "Copy Place ID", "Copy", function() copyText(tostring(game.PlaceId)) end)
	Button(Quick, "Copy Server Job ID", "Copy", function() copyText(game.JobId) end)
	Button(Quick, "Stop All Movement Hacks", "Disable", function()
		for _, id in ipairs({ "speed", "jump", "sprint", "noclip", "infjump", "bhop", "fly", "spin", "freeze" }) do
			if C[id] then C[id].Fire(false) end
		end
		notify("Movement features disabled", T.Warn)
	end)
	-- NEW: panic + copy-my-info
	S.Panic = function()
		local n = 0
		for id, o in pairs(C) do
			if o.Fire and id ~= "antiafk" and o.Get() == true then
				o.Fire(false)
				n = n + 1
			end
		end
		notify("Panic: turned off " .. n .. " features", T.Warn)
	end
	Button(Quick, "Panic: Turn Everything Off", "Panic", function() S.Panic() end)
	Button(Quick, "Copy My User ID", "Copy", function() copyText(tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId)) end)
	Button(Quick, "Copy My Username", "Copy", function() copyText(LocalPlayer.Name) end)

	local Info = Section(Home, "Server Info", "Details about this session", "info")
	InfoRow(Info, "Game: " .. game.Name)
	InfoRow(Info, "Place ID: " .. tostring(game.PlaceId))
	local JobLabel = InfoRow(Info, "Job ID: " .. (game.JobId ~= "" and game.JobId or "Studio / private"))
	JobLabel.TextSize = 10

	-- NEW: live stats
	local Live = Section(Home, "Live Stats", "Updates twice a second", "speed")
	local MemRow = InfoRow(Live, "Memory: -")
	local ClockRow = InfoRow(Live, "Local time: -")
	local UpRow = InfoRow(Live, "Server uptime: -")
	local SpdRow = InfoRow(Live, "Your speed: -")
	local HpRow = InfoRow(Live, "Your health: -")

	local fps = 60
	bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt) fps = fps * 0.9 + (1 / math.max(dt, 0.001)) * 0.1 end)
	local function getPing()
		local ok, v = pcall(function() return Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue() end)
		if ok and v then return math.floor(v) end
		return math.floor(LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() * 2000)
	end
	local startClock = os.clock()
	task.spawn(function()
		while Running do
			pcall(function()
				local t = math.floor(os.clock() - startClock)
				PlayersStat.Text = #Players:GetPlayers() .. " / " .. Players.MaxPlayers
				RuntimeStat.Text = string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", t // 3600, (t % 3600) // 60, t % 60)
				PingStat.Text = getPing() .. " ms"
				FpsStat.Text = math.floor(fps + 0.5) .. " FPS"
				MemRow.Text = string.format("Memory: %d MB", math.floor(Stats:GetTotalMemoryUsageMb()))
				ClockRow.Text = "Local time: " .. os.date("%I:%M:%S %p")
				local up = math.floor(Workspace.DistributedGameTime)
				UpRow.Text = string.format("Server uptime: %02d:%02d:%02d", up // 3600, (up % 3600) // 60, up % 60)
				local r, h = getRoot(), getHum()
				SpdRow.Text = r and string.format("Your speed: %.1f studs/s", r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude) or "Your speed: -"
				HpRow.Text = h and string.format("Your health: %d / %d", math.floor(h.Health), math.floor(h.MaxHealth)) or "Your health: -"
			end)
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)
end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: PLAYER
-- ====================================================
do
	local PlayerTab = AddTab("Player", ICONS.Player)
	local Move = Section(PlayerTab, "Movement", "Local character modifiers")
	Toggle(Move, "Enable Walk Speed", false, function(v) S.Speed = v end, nil, "speed")
	Slider(Move, "Walk Speed", 16, 200, 16, function(v) S.SpeedValue = v end, "speedvalue")
	Toggle(Move, "Enable Jump Power", false, function(v) S.Jump = v end, nil, "jump")
	Slider(Move, "Jump Power", 50, 300, 50, function(v) S.JumpValue = v end, "jumpvalue")
	Toggle(Move, "Sprint (hold Left Shift)", false, function(v) S.Sprint = v end, nil, "sprint")
	Slider(Move, "Sprint Speed", 20, 200, 40, function(v) S.SprintValue = v end, "sprintvalue")
	Toggle(Move, "Noclip", false, function(v) S.Noclip = v if not v then restoreNoclip() end end, nil, "noclip")
	Keybind(Move, "Noclip Hotkey  (Backspace = none)", S.NoclipKey, function(k) S.NoclipKey = k end, true)
	Toggle(Move, "Infinite Jump", false, function(v) S.InfJump = v end, nil, "infjump")
	Toggle(Move, "Bunny Hop (hold Space)", false, function(v) S.Bhop = v end, nil, "bhop")

	local FlySec = Section(PlayerTab, "Flight", "WASD to move, Space = up, Ctrl = down")
	Toggle(FlySec, "Fly", false, onFly, "Premium User", "fly")
	Slider(FlySec, "Fly Speed", 10, 250, 60, function(v) S.FlySpeed = v end, "flyspeed")
	Keybind(FlySec, "Fly Hotkey", S.FlyKey, function(k) S.FlyKey = k end)

	local CharSec = Section(PlayerTab, "Character", "Modify how your character behaves")
	Toggle(CharSec, "Spin", false, function(v) S.Spin = v end, nil, "spin")
	Slider(CharSec, "Spin Speed", 1, 60, 10, function(v) S.SpinSpeed = v end, "spinspeed")
	Toggle(CharSec, "Freeze Character", false, function(v)
		S.Freeze = v
		local r = getRoot()
		if r and not v and not S.Freecam then r.Anchored = false end
	end, nil, "freeze")
	Toggle(CharSec, "Anti-Void (pull back if you fall)", false, function(v) S.AntiVoid = v end, nil, "antivoid")
	Toggle(CharSec, "Respawn At Death Location", false, function(v) S.DeathReturn = v end, nil, "deathreturn")
	Toggle(CharSec, "Anti-Fling (others can't collide)", false, function(v) S.AntiFling = v if not v then restoreFling() end end, "Owner", "antifling")
	Button(CharSec, "Sit Down", "Sit", function() local h = getHum() if h then h.Sit = true end end)
	Button(CharSec, "Heal To Full (local)", "Heal", function() local h = getHum() if h then h.Health = h.MaxHealth end end)

	-- BODY EDITOR BEGIN: edits only the current LocalPlayer character.
	do
		local body = { enabled = false, parts = {}, selected = nil, originals = {}, values = {}, sliders = {} }
		local sec = Section(PlayerTab, "Body Part Editor", "Experiment with your own R6/R15 character. Other players may not see local edits.")
		local info = InfoRow(sec, "Disabled - choose a body part, then enable editing")
		local picker, enabledToggle
		local outline = new("SelectionBox", { LineThickness = 0.04, Color3 = T.Accent, SurfaceTransparency = 1, Visible = false }, Gui)
		local function defaults()
			return { sx = 100, sy = 100, sz = 100, x = 0, y = 0, z = 0, rx = 0, ry = 0, rz = 0 }
		end
		local function owned(part)
			local char = LocalPlayer.Character
			return char and part and part.Parent == char and part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart"
		end
		local function sync()
			local values = body.values[body.selected] or defaults()
			for key, slider in pairs(body.sliders) do slider.SetSilent(values[key]) end
			outline.Adornee = body.enabled and owned(body.selected) and body.selected or nil
			outline.Visible = outline.Adornee ~= nil
		end
		local function restore(part)
			local original = body.originals[part]
			if not original then body.values[part] = nil return true end
			local ok = pcall(function()
				if part.Parent == original.char then
					part.Size = original.size
					for _, joint in ipairs(original.joints) do
						if joint.motor:IsDescendantOf(original.char) and joint.motor.Part1 == part then
							joint.motor.C0 = joint.c0
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			if ok then body.originals[part], body.values[part] = nil, nil end
			return ok
		end
		local function restoreAll()
			local pending = {}
			for part in pairs(body.originals) do table.insert(pending, part) end
			local failed = 0
			for _, part in ipairs(pending) do if not restore(part) then failed = failed + 1 end end
			sync()
			return failed
		end
		local function apply()
			local part, char = body.selected, LocalPlayer.Character
			if not body.enabled then info.Text = "Enable editing before adjusting a body part" sync() return end
			if not owned(part) then info.Text = "No editable body part - refresh after spawning" return end
			local original = body.originals[part]
			if not original then
				original = { char = char, size = part.Size, joints = {} }
				for _, joint in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
					if joint:IsA("Motor6D") and joint.Part1 == part and joint.Part0 and joint.Part0:IsDescendantOf(char) then
						table.insert(original.joints, { motor = joint, c0 = joint.C0 })
					end
				end
				body.originals[part] = original
			end
			local v = body.values[part] or defaults()
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				part.Size = Vector3.new(math.max(0.05, original.size.X * v.sx / 100), math.max(0.05, original.size.Y * v.sy / 100), math.max(0.05, original.size.Z * v.sz / 100))
				local offset = CFrame.new(v.x / 10, v.y / 10, v.z / 10) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(v.rx), math.rad(v.ry), math.rad(v.rz))
				for _, joint in ipairs(original.joints) do
					if joint.motor:IsDescendantOf(char) and joint.motor.Part1 == part then joint.motor.C0 = joint.c0 * offset end
				end
			end)
			if not ok then
				restore(part)
				info.Text = "Could not apply edit: " .. tostring(err)
			else
				info.Text = part.Name .. (#original.joints > 0 and " - local size / joint offsets applied" or " - size applied; no Motor6D for movement")
			end
			sync()
		end
		local function selectPart(index)
			body.selected = body.parts[index]
			sync()
			info.Text = body.selected and (body.selected.Name .. " - offsets are relative to its original joint") or "No body parts found - refresh after spawning"
		end
		local function refresh()
			local previous = body.selected
			body.parts = {}
			local char = LocalPlayer.Character
			if char then
				for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do if owned(part) then table.insert(body.parts, part) end end
			end
			table.sort(body.parts, function(a, b) return a.Name < b.Name end)
			local names, selected = {}, 1
			for i, part in ipairs(body.parts) do names[i] = part.Name if part == previous then selected = i end end
			if #names == 0 then names[1] = "No character" end
			picker.SetOptions(names, false)
			picker.Set(selected, true)
			selectPart(selected)
		end
		enabledToggle = Toggle(sec, "Enable Body Editing", false, function(value)
			body.enabled = value
			if value then refresh() if body.selected then info.Text = "Ready - select a part and adjust the sliders" end
			else
				local failed = restoreAll()
				info.Text = failed == 0 and "Disabled - all body edits restored" or "Some edits could not reset - try Reset All again"
			end
			sync()
		end)
		picker = Dropdown(sec, "Body Part", { "No character" }, 1, selectPart)
		Button(sec, "Refresh Body Parts", "Refresh", refresh)
		local scale = Collapse(sec, "Size - percent of original")
		local move = Collapse(sec, "Position - 10 units = 1 stud")
		local rotate = Collapse(sec, "Rotation - degrees")
		local function control(parent, label, key, low, high, initial)
			body.sliders[key] = Slider(parent, label, low, high, initial, function(value)
				if not body.enabled or not owned(body.selected) then sync() info.Text = "Enable editing and select your body part first" return end
				local v = body.values[body.selected] or defaults()
				body.values[body.selected] = v
				v[key] = math.clamp(value, low, high)
				apply()
			end)
		end
		control(scale, "Width %", "sx", 25, 300, 100)
		control(scale, "Height %", "sy", 25, 300, 100)
		control(scale, "Depth %", "sz", 25, 300, 100)
		control(move, "Offset X", "x", -50, 50, 0)
		control(move, "Offset Y", "y", -50, 50, 0)
		control(move, "Offset Z", "z", -50, 50, 0)
		control(rotate, "Rotate X", "rx", -180, 180, 0)
		control(rotate, "Rotate Y", "ry", -180, 180, 0)
		control(rotate, "Rotate Z", "rz", -180, 180, 0)
		Button(sec, "Reset Selected Body Part", "Reset", function()
			if body.selected then
				local ok = restore(body.selected)
				info.Text = ok and "Selected part restored" or "Could not restore selected part - try again"
				sync()
			end
		end)
		Button(sec, "Reset All Body Parts", "Reset All", function()
			info.Text = restoreAll() == 0 and "All body parts restored" or "Some parts could not reset - try again"
		end)
		bind(LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving, function()
			body.enabled = false
			restoreAll()
			body.selected, body.parts = nil, {}
			enabledToggle.Set(false)
			sync()
			info.Text = "Character changed - refresh body parts after spawning"
		end)
		bind(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function()
			-- Edits are never transferred to a new rig automatically.
			body.enabled = false
			restoreAll()
			enabledToggle.Set(false)
			refresh()
		end)
		onUnload(function()
			body.enabled = false
			restoreAll()
			outline:Destroy()
		end)
		refresh()
	end
	-- BODY EDITOR END

	local Misc = Section(PlayerTab, "Utility", "Quality-of-life options")
	Toggle(Misc, "Anti-AFK", true, function(v) S.AntiAfk = v end, nil, "antiafk")


	-- NEW (v3.7): Void Hold ----------------------------------------------------------
	do
		local vh = { on = false, origCF = nil, hold = nil, camCF = nil, camType = nil }
		S.VoidDist, S.VoidBelow, S.VoidKey = 20000, false, Enum.KeyCode.Unknown

		local function restoreCam()
			local c = cam()
			c.CameraType = vh.camType or Enum.CameraType.Custom
			local h = getHum()
			if h then c.CameraSubject = h end
		end
		local function enable()
			local r, h, c = getRoot(), getHum(), cam()
			if not r or not h or h.Health <= 0 then notify("No character to hold", T.Warn) return false end
			if C.fly and C.fly.Get() then C.fly.Fire(false) end
			if C.freecam and C.freecam.Get() then C.freecam.Fire(false) end
			vh.origCF = r.CFrame
			vh.camCF, vh.camType = c.CFrame, c.CameraType
			local pos
			if S.VoidBelow then
				-- below the map but safely above the kill height
				pos = Vector3.new(r.Position.X, Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight + 80, r.Position.Z)
			else
				-- far away from everything, same height
				pos = r.Position + Vector3.new(S.VoidDist, 0, 0)
			end
			vh.hold = CFrame.new(pos)
			c.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
			c.CFrame = vh.camCF
			r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			r.CFrame = vh.hold
			r.Anchored = true
			vh.on = true
			notify("Void Hold ON - camera locked in place", T.Good)
			return true
		end
		local function disable()
			vh.on = false
			local r = getRoot()
			if r then
				if not S.Freeze then r.Anchored = false end
				if vh.origCF then r.CFrame = vh.origCF end
				r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			restoreCam()
			notify("Void Hold OFF - returned", T.Sub)
		end
		-- character died / respawned: just let go (no teleport back)
		local function release()
			vh.on = false
			if C.voidhold then C.voidhold.Set(false) end
			restoreCam()
		end

		bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
			if not vh.on then return end
			local r, h = getRoot(), getHum()
			if not r or not h or h.Health <= 0 then release() return end
			if (r.Position - vh.hold.Position).Magnitude > 5 then r.CFrame = vh.hold end
			if not r.Anchored then r.Anchored = true end
			r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		end)
		bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
			if vh.on then
				local c = cam()
				c.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
				c.CFrame = vh.camCF
			end
		end)
		bind(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function() if vh.on then release() end end)
		bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
			if gp or BindingActive or not Unlocked or i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
			if S.VoidKey ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and i.KeyCode == S.VoidKey and C.voidhold then
				C.voidhold.Fire(not C.voidhold.Get())
			end
		end)
		onUnload(function() if vh.on then disable() end end)

		local Void = Section(PlayerTab, "Void Hold", "Park your character in the void without dying - your camera stays where it was")
		Toggle(Void, "Void Hold", false, function(v)
			if v then
				if not enable() then C.voidhold.Set(false) end
			elseif vh.on then
				disable()
			end
		end, nil, "voidhold")
		Slider(Void, "Void Distance", 1000, 100000, 20000, function(v) S.VoidDist = v end, "voiddist")
		Toggle(Void, "Below-Map Void (just above kill height)", false, function(v) S.VoidBelow = v end, nil, "voidbelow")
		Keybind(Void, "Void Hold Hotkey  (Backspace = none)", S.VoidKey, function(k) S.VoidKey = k end, true)
		Button(Void, "Return To Original Spot", "Return", function()
			if vh.on then C.voidhold.Fire(false) else notify("Void Hold isn't on", T.Warn) end
		end)
	end


	-- NEW (v3.10): movement tools with a real use -------------------------------------------
	do
		local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
		rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		S.AirUp, S.ClimbSpeed, S.GlideSpeed, S.RetreatPct = 25, 40, 12, 30
		local platform, level = nil, 0
		local retreatCF, retreatCool = nil, 0

		local function killPlatform() if platform then platform:Destroy() platform = nil end end
		local function footOffset(h, r)
			if h.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then return 3 end
			return h.HipHeight + r.Size.Y / 2
		end

		bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
			local r, h, ch = getRoot(), getHum(), getChar()
			if not r or not h or h.Health <= 0 then return end
			local typing = UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() ~= nil

			-- air walk: invisible platform that follows you; Space raises it, Ctrl lowers it
			if S.AirWalk then
				if not platform or not platform.Parent then
					platform = new("Part", { Name = "SolosPlatform", Size = Vector3.new(8, 1, 8), Anchored = true, CanCollide = true, Transparency = 1 }, Workspace)
				end
				if not typing then
					if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then level = level + S.AirUp * dt end
					if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then level = level - S.AirUp * dt end
				end
				platform.CFrame = CFrame.new(r.Position.X, level - 0.5, r.Position.Z)
			end

			-- wall climb: walk into a wall holding W to go up it
			if S.WallClimb and not typing and UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
				rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { ch }
				if Workspace:Raycast(r.Position, r.CFrame.LookVector * 3, rayParams) then
					r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, S.ClimbSpeed, 0)
				end
			end

			-- glide: hold Space while falling to slow your descent
			if S.Glide and not typing and UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) and h.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air then
				local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity
				if v.Y < -S.GlideSpeed then r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, -S.GlideSpeed, v.Z) end
			end

			-- auto retreat: jump back to a safe spot when health gets low
			if S.Retreat and retreatCF and os.clock() > retreatCool and (h.Health / math.max(h.MaxHealth, 1)) * 100 <= S.RetreatPct then
				retreatCool = os.clock() + 5
				doTeleport(retreatCF)
				notify("Low health - retreated to your safe spot", T.Warn)
			end
		end)

		-- face camera (shift-lock style)
		bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
			if not S.FaceCam or S.Freecam then return end
			local h, r = getHum(), getRoot()
			if h and r and h.Health > 0 then
				if h.AutoRotate then h.AutoRotate = false end
				local look = cam().CFrame.LookVector
				local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
				if flat.Magnitude > 0.01 then r.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(r.Position, r.Position + flat) end
			end
		end)

		-- shift + click to walk
		bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
			if gp or not S.ClickWalk or i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) and Mouse.Target then
				local h = getHum()
				if h then h:MoveTo(Mouse.Hit.Position) end
			end
		end)
		onUnload(function()
			killPlatform()
			local h = getHum()
			if h and S.FaceCam then h.AutoRotate = true end
		end)

		local Sec = Section(PlayerTab, "Movement Tools", "Walk on air, climb walls, glide, face the camera, click to walk, auto retreat")
		Toggle(Sec, "Air Walk  (Space up / Ctrl down)", false, function(v)
			S.AirWalk = v
			if v then
				local r, h = getRoot(), getHum()
				if not r or not h then S.AirWalk = false C.airwalk.Set(false) return end
				level = r.Position.Y - footOffset(h, r)
			else
				killPlatform()
			end
		end, nil, "airwalk")
		Slider(Sec, "Air Walk Rise Speed", 5, 100, 25, function(v) S.AirUp = v end, "airup")
		Toggle(Sec, "Wall Climb  (walk into a wall with W)", false, function(v) S.WallClimb = v end, nil, "wallclimb")
		Slider(Sec, "Climb Speed", 10, 120, 40, function(v) S.ClimbSpeed = v end, "climbspeed")
		Toggle(Sec, "Glide  (hold Space while falling)", false, function(v) S.Glide = v end, nil, "glide")
		Slider(Sec, "Glide Fall Speed", 3, 60, 12, function(v) S.GlideSpeed = v end, "glidespeed")
		Toggle(Sec, "Always Face Camera", false, function(v)
			S.FaceCam = v
			if not v then local h = getHum() if h then h.AutoRotate = true end end
		end, nil, "facecam")
		Toggle(Sec, "Shift + Click To Walk", false, function(v) S.ClickWalk = v end, nil, "clickwalk")
		Button(Sec, "Set Retreat Spot (here)", "Set", function()
			local r = getRoot()
			if r then retreatCF = r.CFrame notify("Retreat spot saved", T.Good) end
		end)
		Toggle(Sec, "Auto Retreat When Health Is Low", false, function(v)
			if v and not retreatCF then notify("Set a retreat spot first", T.Warn) end
			S.Retreat = v
		end, nil, "retreat")
		Slider(Sec, "Retreat Below HP %", 5, 90, 30, function(v) S.RetreatPct = v end, "retreatpct")
	end

end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: ESP
-- ====================================================
do
	local EspTab = AddTab("ESP", ICONS.Esp)
	local EspMain = Section(EspTab, "Player ESP", "Overlays drawn over other players")
	Toggle(EspMain, "Enable ESP", false, function(v) S.ESP.Enabled = v end, nil, "esp")
	local EspOv = Collapse(EspMain, "Overlays")
	Toggle(EspOv, "Boxes", true, function(v) S.ESP.Boxes = v end, nil, "boxes")
	Toggle(EspOv, "Names", true, function(v) S.ESP.Names = v end, nil, "names")
	Toggle(EspOv, "Health Bars", true, function(v) S.ESP.Health = v end, nil, "health")
	Toggle(EspOv, "Distance / HP Text", true, function(v) S.ESP.Distance = v end, nil, "distance")
	Toggle(EspOv, "Held Tool Name", false, function(v) S.ESP.Tool = v end, nil, "esptool")
	Toggle(EspOv, "Tracers", false, function(v) S.ESP.Tracers = v end, "Premium User", "tracers")
	Toggle(EspOv, "Chams (see through walls)", false, function(v) S.ESP.Chams = v end, "Premium User", "chams")

	local CardSec = Section(EspTab, "Player Cards", "Small card above each head: avatar, display name, username, health")
	Toggle(CardSec, "Player Cards", false, function(v) S.ESP.Cards = v end, "Premium User", "cards")

	local EspSet = Section(EspTab, "ESP Settings", "Filters and colors")
	local EspFilt = Collapse(EspSet, "Filters")
	Toggle(EspFilt, "Hide Teammates", false, function(v) S.ESP.TeamCheck = v end, nil, "teamcheck")
	Toggle(EspFilt, "Use Team Colors", false, function(v) S.ESP.TeamColors = v end, nil, "teamcolors")
	Toggle(EspFilt, "Highlight Selected Player", false, function(v) S.ESP.HighlightTarget = v end, nil, "esptarget")
	Toggle(EspFilt, "Rainbow Color", false, function(v) S.ESP.Rainbow = v end, "Premium User", "rainbow")
	Slider(EspSet, "Max Distance", 100, 5000, 1500, function(v) S.ESP.MaxDist = v end, "maxdist")
	local colorNames = {}
	for i, c in ipairs(ColorPresets) do colorNames[i] = c[1] end
	Dropdown(EspSet, "ESP Color", colorNames, 1, function(i) S.ESP.Color = ColorPresets[i][2] end)
	Dropdown(EspSet, "Tracer Origin", TRACER_ORIGINS, S.ESP.TracerOrigin or 1, function(i) S.ESP.TracerOrigin = i end)

	-- NEW: friend cache -----------------------------------------------------
	local fcache = {}
	S.ESP.FriendCache = fcache
	local function checkFriend(p)
		if p == LocalPlayer then return end
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, res = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(p.UserId) end)
			if ok then fcache[p] = res end
		end)
	end
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do checkFriend(p) end
	bind(Players.PlayerAdded, checkFriend)
	bind(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(p) fcache[p] = nil end)

	-- NEW: head dots + off-screen arrows (own overlay objects) ---------------
	local extras = {}
	local function getExtra(plr)
		local o = extras[plr]
		if o then return o end
		o = {}
		o.Dot = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false }, EspGui)
		round(o.Dot, 4)
		o.Arrow = new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "▲", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 18, Visible = false }, EspGui)
		extras[plr] = o
		return o
	end
	local function hideExtra(o) o.Dot.Visible = false o.Arrow.Visible = false end
	bind(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(p)
		local o = extras[p]
		if o then
			o.Dot:Destroy()
			o.Arrow:Destroy()
			extras[p] = nil
		end
	end)
	bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
		local E = S.ESP
		if not (E.Enabled and (E.HeadDots or E.Arrows)) then
			for _, o in pairs(extras) do hideExtra(o) end
			return
		end
		local c = cam()
		local vp = c.ViewportSize
		local center = Vector2.new(vp.X / 2, vp.Y / 2)
		local radius = math.min(vp.X, vp.Y) / 2 - 40
		local baseCol = E.Rainbow and Color3.fromHSV((os.clock() * 0.25) % 1, 0.85, 1) or E.Color
		local myRoot = getRoot()
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
				local o = getExtra(plr)
				local char = plr.Character
				local head = char and (char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
				local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local show = head ~= nil and hum ~= nil and hum.Health > 0
				if show and E.TeamCheck and S.SameTeam(plr) then show = false end
				if show and myRoot and (head.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude > E.MaxDist then show = false end
				if not show then
					hideExtra(o)
				else
					local col = baseCol
					if E.TeamColors and not E.Rainbow then local tc = S.TeamColorOf(plr) if tc then col = tc end end
					if E.Friends and fcache[plr] then col = T.Good end
					local pos, onScreen = c:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position)
					o.Dot.Visible = E.HeadDots == true and onScreen
					o.Dot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(pos.X, pos.Y)
					o.Dot.BackgroundColor3 = col
					if E.Arrows and not onScreen then
						local rel = c.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(head.Position)
						local dir = Vector2.new(rel.X, -rel.Y)
						if dir.Magnitude < 1e-3 then dir = Vector2.new(0, 1) end
						dir = dir.Unit
						local p2 = center + dir * radius
						o.Arrow.Visible = true
						o.Arrow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(p2.X, p2.Y)
						o.Arrow.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dir.X, -dir.Y))
						o.Arrow.TextColor3 = col
					else
						o.Arrow.Visible = false
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	local EspPlus = Section(EspTab, "ESP Extras", "Head dots, off-screen arrows and style options")
	Toggle(EspPlus, "Head Dots", false, function(v) S.ESP.HeadDots = v end, nil, "headdots")
	Toggle(EspPlus, "Off-Screen Arrows", false, function(v) S.ESP.Arrows = v end, nil, "arrows")
	Toggle(EspPlus, "Highlight Friends (green)", false, function(v) S.ESP.Friends = v end, nil, "espfriends")
	Slider(EspPlus, "Chams Fill %", 0, 100, 60, function(v) S.ESP.FillTrans = v / 100 end, "fillamount")
	Slider(EspPlus, "Box Thickness", 1, 5, 2, function(v) S.ESP.Thick = v end, "boxthick")
	Slider(EspPlus, "ESP Text Size", 9, 20, 12, function(v) S.ESP.TextSize = v end, "textsize")

end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: VISUALS
-- ====================================================
do
	local VisTab = AddTab("Visuals", ICONS.Visuals)
	local CamSec = Section(VisTab, "Camera", "Field of view, zoom and freecam")
	Slider(CamSec, "Field Of View", 30, 120, math.floor(origFov), function(v) cam().FieldOfView = v mods.Fov = true end, "fov")
	Slider(CamSec, "Max Zoom Distance", 10, 1000, math.clamp(math.floor(origMaxZoom), 10, 1000), function(v) LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = v mods.Zoom = true end, "zoom")
	Toggle(CamSec, "Freecam", false, setFreecam, "Premium User", "freecam")
	Slider(CamSec, "Freecam Speed", 10, 300, 60, function(v) S.FreecamSpeed = v end, "freecamspeed")
	Button(CamSec, "Reset Camera", "Reset", function()
		if C.freecam.Get() then C.freecam.Fire(false) end
		stopSpectate()
		cam().FieldOfView = origFov
		LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = origMaxZoom
		C.fov.SetSilent(origFov)
		C.zoom.SetSilent(origMaxZoom)
		notify("Camera reset")
	end)

	local CrossSec = Section(VisTab, "Crosshair", "Custom screen-center crosshair")
	Toggle(CrossSec, "Show Crosshair", false, function(v) S.Crosshair = v updateCross() end, nil, "crosshair")
	Slider(CrossSec, "Size", 4, 40, 10, function(v) S.CrossSize = v updateCross() end, "crosssize")
	Slider(CrossSec, "Gap", 0, 20, 4, function(v) S.CrossGap = v updateCross() end, "crossgap")
	local crossColorIdx, crossColorBtn = 1, nil
	crossColorBtn = Button(CrossSec, "Crosshair Color", "Blue", function()
		crossColorIdx = crossColorIdx % #ColorPresets + 1
		S.CrossColor = ColorPresets[crossColorIdx][2]
		crossColorBtn.Text = ColorPresets[crossColorIdx][1]
		updateCross()
	end)



	-- NEW: crosshair extras --------------------------------------------------
	S.CrossThick = 2
	local cdot = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false }, Cross)
	local rainbowCross = false
	bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
		if rainbowCross then
			local col = Color3.fromHSV((os.clock() * 0.4) % 1, 0.9, 1)
			for _, b in ipairs(crossBars) do b.BackgroundColor3 = col end
		end
		cdot.BackgroundColor3 = crossBars[1].BackgroundColor3
	end)
	Toggle(CrossSec, "Center Dot", false, function(v) cdot.Visible = v end, nil, "crossdot")
	Toggle(CrossSec, "Rainbow Crosshair", false, function(v) rainbowCross = v if not v then updateCross() end end, nil, "crossrainbow")
	Slider(CrossSec, "Thickness", 1, 8, 2, function(v) S.CrossThick = v updateCross() end, "crossthick")

end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: PLAYERS
-- ====================================================
do
	local PlayersTab = AddTab("Players", ICONS.Players)
	local ListSec = Section(PlayersTab, "Player Select", "Search by name, click a profile to select")
	refreshPlayerList = PlayerList(ListSec, 190)

	local InspSec = Section(PlayersTab, "Inspector", "Live info about the selected player")
	Insp.Display = InfoRow(InspSec, "Display: -")
	Insp.User = InfoRow(InspSec, "Username: -")
	Insp.Id = InfoRow(InspSec, "User ID: -")
	Insp.Age = InfoRow(InspSec, "Account age: -")
	Insp.Team = InfoRow(InspSec, "Team: -")
	Insp.Health = InfoRow(InspSec, "Health: -")
	Insp.Dist = InfoRow(InspSec, "Distance: -")

	local function withTarget(fn)
		return function()
			if S.Target and S.Target.Parent then fn(S.Target) else notify("Select a player first", T.Warn) end
		end
	end
	local function cycleTarget(step)
		local pool = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= LocalPlayer then table.insert(pool, p) end end
		if #pool == 0 then notify("No other players", T.Warn) return end
		local idx = table.find(pool, S.Target) or 0
		idx = ((idx - 1 + step) % #pool) + 1
		S.Target = pool[idx]
		refreshPlayerList()
		spectate(S.Target)
	end

	local Act = Section(PlayersTab, "Actions", "Everything here runs on your own client")
	Button(Act, "Teleport To Player", "Teleport", withTarget(teleportToPlayer))
	Button(Act, "Spectate Player", "Spectate", withTarget(spectate))
	Button(Act, "Spectate Next Player", "Next >", function() cycleTarget(1) end)
	Button(Act, "Spectate Previous Player", "< Prev", function() cycleTarget(-1) end)
	Button(Act, "Stop Spectating", "Stop", function() stopSpectate() end)
	Toggle(Act, "Follow Player (walk to them)", false, function(v)
		S.Follow = v
		if not v then local h, r = getHum(), getRoot() if h and r then h:MoveTo(r.Position) end end
	end, nil, "follow")
	Button(Act, "Select Nearest Player", "Select", function()
		local p, d = nearestPlayer()
		if p then
			S.Target = p
			refreshPlayerList()
			notify("Selected " .. p.DisplayName .. " (" .. math.floor(d) .. " studs)")
		else
			notify("No players nearby", T.Warn)
		end
	end)
	Button(Act, "Teleport To Nearest Player", "Teleport", function()
		local p = nearestPlayer()
		if p then teleportToPlayer(p) else notify("No players nearby", T.Warn) end
	end)
	Button(Act, "Copy Username", "Copy", withTarget(function(p) copyText(p.Name) end))
	Button(Act, "Copy Display Name", "Copy", withTarget(function(p) copyText(p.DisplayName) end))
	Button(Act, "Copy User ID", "Copy", withTarget(function(p) copyText(tostring(p.UserId)) end))
	Button(Act, "Copy Profile Link", "Copy", withTarget(function(p) copyText("https://www.roblox.com/users/" .. p.UserId .. "/profile") end))
	Button(Act, "Copy All Usernames", "Copy", function()
		local names = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do table.insert(names, p.Name) end
		copyText(table.concat(names, ", "))
	end)
	Button(Act, "Teleport To Random Player", "Random", function()
		local p = findPlayer("random")
		if p and p ~= LocalPlayer then teleportToPlayer(p) else notify("No other players", T.Warn) end
	end)

	-- NEW: selected-player profile card (big avatar) -------------------------
	local SelSec = Section(PlayersTab, "Selected Player", "Profile picture and quick info")
	local card = Row(SelSec, 64)
	local PLACEHOLDER = "rbxasset://textures/ui/GuiImagePlaceholder.png"
	local bigImg = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 48), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 8), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BorderSizePixel = 0, Image = PLACEHOLDER }, card)
	round(bigImg, 24)
	local selName = Label(card, { Text = "No player selected", Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(68, 10), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Text })
	local selUser = Label(card, { Text = "Search or click a player above", Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(68, 32), TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Sub })
	local lastShown = false
	local function updateCard()
		local t = S.Target
		if t == lastShown then return end
		lastShown = t
		if t and t.Parent then
			selName.Text = t.DisplayName
			selUser.Text = "@" .. t.Name .. "   |   ID " .. t.UserId
			bigImg.Image = PLACEHOLDER
			Thumb.get(t.UserId, function(content) if S.Target == t then bigImg.Image = content end end)
		else
			selName.Text = "No player selected"
			selUser.Text = "Search or click a player above"
			bigImg.Image = PLACEHOLDER
		end
	end
	S.OnTargetChanged = updateCard
	updateCard()


	task.spawn(function()
		while Running do
			pcall(updateCard)
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)

	-- NEW: more actions ------------------------------------------------------
	local orbit, orbitR, orbitSpeed, orbitAng = false, 12, 4, 0
	bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
		if not orbit then return end
		local t, r = S.Target, getRoot()
		local tr = t and t.Character and t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if r and tr then
			orbitAng = orbitAng + dt * orbitSpeed
			local p = tr.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(orbitAng) * orbitR, 0, math.sin(orbitAng) * orbitR)
			r.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(p, tr.Position)
		end
	end)
	local Act2 = Section(PlayersTab, "More Actions", "Extra tools for the selected player")
	Button(Act2, "Teleport Behind Player", "Behind", withTarget(function(p)
		local tr = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if tr then doTeleport(tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 5)) notify("Teleported behind " .. p.DisplayName) else notify("Target has no character", T.Warn) end
	end))
	Button(Act2, "Teleport Above Player", "Above", withTarget(function(p)
		local tr = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if tr then doTeleport(tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 8, 0)) notify("Teleported above " .. p.DisplayName) else notify("Target has no character", T.Warn) end
	end))
	Button(Act2, "Face Player", "Face", withTarget(function(p)
		local tr = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local r = getRoot()
		if tr and r then r.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(r.Position, Vector3.new(tr.Position.X, r.Position.Y, tr.Position.Z)) end
	end))
	Toggle(Act2, "Orbit Player", false, function(v) orbit = v end, nil, "orbit")
	Slider(Act2, "Orbit Radius", 4, 60, 12, function(v) orbitR = v end, "orbitradius")
	Slider(Act2, "Orbit Speed", 1, 20, 4, function(v) orbitSpeed = v end, "orbitspeed")
	Button(Act2, "Select Farthest Player", "Select", function()
		local root = getRoot()
		if not root then return end
		local best, bd = nil, -1
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local r = p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if r then
				local d = (r.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
				if d > bd then best, bd = p, d end
			end
		end
		if best then
			S.Target = best
			refreshPlayerList()
			notify("Selected " .. best.DisplayName .. " (" .. math.floor(bd) .. " studs)")
		else
			notify("No players found", T.Warn)
		end
	end)
	Button(Act2, "Select Random Player", "Select", function()
		local p = findPlayer("random")
		if p and p ~= LocalPlayer then S.Target = p refreshPlayerList() notify("Selected " .. p.DisplayName) else notify("No other players", T.Warn) end
	end)
	Button(Act2, "Clear Selection", "Clear", function() S.Target = nil refreshPlayerList() end)
	Button(Act2, "Copy Avatar Image Link", "Copy", withTarget(function(p)
		copyText("https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId=" .. p.UserId .. "&width=420&height=420&format=png")
	end))
	Button(Act2, "Copy Player Summary", "Copy", withTarget(function(p)
		copyText(p.DisplayName .. " (@" .. p.Name .. ") id " .. p.UserId .. " age " .. p.AccountAge .. "d")
	end))

	-- section order: list, selected card, inspector, actions, more actions, player tools
	ListSec.LayoutOrder, SelSec.LayoutOrder, InspSec.LayoutOrder = 1, 2, 3
	Act.LayoutOrder, Act2.LayoutOrder = 4, 5
	

	-- NEW (v3.10): stick behind, middle-click select, friends, proximity alert, hotkeys ------------
	do
		local stickDist, alertR, nearSet = 6, 60, {}
		S.NearestKey, S.SelectKey = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, Enum.KeyCode.Unknown

		local function isFriend(p)
			local fc = S.ESP.FriendCache
			return fc and fc[p] == true
		end
		local function friends()
			local list = {}
			for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= LocalPlayer and isFriend(p) then table.insert(list, p) end end
			return list
		end

		bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
			if not S.Stick then return end
			local t, r = S.Target, getRoot()
			local tr = t and t.Character and t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if r and tr then
				local pos = (tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, stickDist)).Position
				r.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + tr.CFrame.LookVector)
				r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
		end)
		bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
			if gp then return end
			if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3 and S.MidSelect then
				local t = Mouse.Target
				local m = t and t:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
				local p = m and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m)
				if p and p ~= LocalPlayer then
					S.Target = p
					refreshPlayerList()
					notify("Selected " .. p.DisplayName, T.Good)
				else
					notify("No player under the cursor", T.Warn)
				end
			elseif i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and Unlocked and not BindingActive then
				if S.NearestKey ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and i.KeyCode == S.NearestKey then
					local p = nearestPlayer()
					if p then teleportToPlayer(p) end
				elseif S.SelectKey ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and i.KeyCode == S.SelectKey then
					local p, d = nearestPlayer()
					if p then
						S.Target = p
						refreshPlayerList()
						notify("Selected " .. p.DisplayName .. " (" .. math.floor(d) .. " studs)")
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		task.spawn(function()
			while Running do
				if S.ProxAlert then
					local r = getRoot()
					if r then
						for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
							if p ~= LocalPlayer then
								local pr = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								local dist = pr and (pr.Position - r.Position).Magnitude
								if dist and dist <= alertR then
									if not nearSet[p] then
										nearSet[p] = true
										notify(p.DisplayName .. " is nearby (" .. math.floor(dist) .. " studs)", T.Warn)
									end
								else
									nearSet[p] = nil
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
				task.wait(0.5)
			end
		end)

		local Sec = Section(PlayersTab, "Player Tools", "Stick to players, pick them in the world, friends, alerts and hotkeys")
		Toggle(Sec, "Stick Behind Selected Player", false, function(v) S.Stick = v end, nil, "stick")
		Slider(Sec, "Stick Distance", 3, 40, 6, function(v) stickDist = v end, "stickdist")
		Toggle(Sec, "Middle-Click To Select Player", false, function(v) S.MidSelect = v end, nil, "midselect")
		Button(Sec, "Teleport To Nearest Friend", "Teleport", function()
			local root = getRoot()
			if not root then return end
			local best, bd = nil, math.huge
			for _, p in ipairs(friends()) do
				local r = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if r then
					local d = (r.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
					if d < bd then best, bd = p, d end
				end
			end
			if best then teleportToPlayer(best) else notify("No friends found in this server", T.Warn) end
		end)
		local friendIdx = 0
		Button(Sec, "Select Next Friend", "Next", function()
			local list = friends()
			if #list == 0 then notify("No friends found in this server", T.Warn) return end
			friendIdx = friendIdx % #list + 1
			S.Target = list[friendIdx]
			refreshPlayerList()
			notify("Selected " .. S.Target.DisplayName, T.Good)
		end)
		Toggle(Sec, "Proximity Alert (someone gets close)", false, function(v) S.ProxAlert = v table.clear(nearSet) end, nil, "proxalert")
		Slider(Sec, "Alert Radius", 10, 300, 60, function(v) alertR = v end, "alertradius")
		Keybind(Sec, "Teleport To Nearest Hotkey", S.NearestKey, function(k) S.NearestKey = k end, true)
		Keybind(Sec, "Select Nearest Hotkey", S.SelectKey, function(k) S.SelectKey = k end, true)
	end

end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: TELEPORTS
-- ====================================================
do
	local TpTab = AddTab("Teleports", ICONS.Teleports)
	local TpQuick = Section(TpTab, "Teleport Tools", "Move yourself around the map")
	PosLabel = InfoRow(TpQuick, "Position: -")
	Toggle(TpQuick, "Ctrl + Click Teleport", false, function(v) S.ClickTP = v end, nil, "clicktp")
	Button(TpQuick, "Teleport To Spawn", "Spawn", function()
		local sp = Workspace:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpawnLocation", true)
		if sp then doTeleport(sp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)) notify("Teleported to spawn") else notify("No spawn found", T.Warn) end
	end)
	Button(TpQuick, "Return To Previous Position", "Back", function()
		if prevTpCF then doTeleport(prevTpCF) else notify("No previous position", T.Warn) end
	end)
	Button(TpQuick, "Copy Current Position", "Copy", function()
		local r = getRoot()
		if r then copyText(string.format("%.1f, %.1f, %.1f", r.Position.X, r.Position.Y, r.Position.Z)) end
	end)
	Input(TpQuick, "Teleport To Coordinates", "x, y, z", "Go", function(text)
		local x, y, z = text:match("(-?[%d%.]+)[,%s]+(-?[%d%.]+)[,%s]+(-?[%d%.]+)")
		x, y, z = tonumber(x), tonumber(y), tonumber(z)
		if x and y and z then doTeleport(CFrame.new(x, y, z)) notify("Teleported") else notify("Use format: x, y, z", T.Warn) end
	end)
	Input(TpQuick, "Teleport To Player", "name or display", "Go", function(text)
		local p = findPlayer(text)
		if p and p ~= LocalPlayer then teleportToPlayer(p) else notify("Player not found", T.Warn) end
	end)

	local WpSec = Section(TpTab, "Waypoints", "Save spots and jump back to them (this session)")
	WP = {}
	local wpHolder = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, wpHolder)
	function rebuildWaypoints()
		for _, ch in ipairs(wpHolder:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("Frame") then ch:Destroy() end end
		for i, wp in ipairs(WP) do
			local row = Row(wpHolder, 34)
			Label(row, { Text = wp.name .. "  (" .. math.floor(wp.cf.X) .. ", " .. math.floor(wp.cf.Y) .. ", " .. math.floor(wp.cf.Z) .. ")", Size = UDim2.new(1, -140, 1, 0), TextSize = 11 })
			local go = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -108, 0.5, -11), BackgroundColor3 = T.Btn, Text = "Go", TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11 }, row)
			round(go, 6)
			local del = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0.5, -11), BackgroundColor3 = T.Bad, Text = "Del", TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11 }, row)
			round(del, 6)
			go.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Hub.play("click") doTeleport(wp.cf) end)
			del.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Hub.play("click") table.remove(WP, i) rebuildWaypoints() end)
		end
	end
	local function addWaypoint(name)
		local r = getRoot()
		if not r then notify("No character", T.Warn) return end
		name = (name or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if name == "" then name = "Waypoint " .. (#WP + 1) end
		table.insert(WP, { name = name, cf = r.CFrame })
		rebuildWaypoints()
		notify("Saved " .. name, T.Good)
	end
	Button(WpSec, "Save Current Position", "Save", function() addWaypoint("") end)
	local nameBox
	nameBox = Input(WpSec, "Save Waypoint As", "waypoint name", "Save", function(text)
		addWaypoint(text)
		nameBox.Text = ""
	end)
	Button(WpSec, "Clear All Waypoints", "Clear", function() table.clear(WP) rebuildWaypoints() end)
	wpHolder.Parent = WpSec

	-- NEW: teleport extras ---------------------------------------------------
	S.TweenTime, S.UpAmount = 1, 50
	local TpPlus = Section(TpTab, "Teleport Extras", "Smooth teleports, offsets and waypoint helpers")
	Toggle(TpPlus, "Smooth Teleport (tween, not snap)", false, function(v) S.TweenTP = v end, nil, "tweentp")
	Slider(TpPlus, "Tween Time (x0.1s)", 2, 50, 10, function(v) S.TweenTime = v / 10 end, "tweentime")
	Slider(TpPlus, "Offset Distance", 5, 500, 50, function(v) S.UpAmount = v end, "upamount")
	Button(TpPlus, "Teleport Up", "Up", function() local r = getRoot() if r then doTeleport(r.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, S.UpAmount, 0)) end end)
	Button(TpPlus, "Teleport Down", "Down", function() local r = getRoot() if r then doTeleport(r.CFrame - Vector3.new(0, S.UpAmount, 0)) end end)
	Button(TpPlus, "Teleport Forward", "Forward", function() local r = getRoot() if r then doTeleport(r.CFrame + r.CFrame.LookVector * S.UpAmount) end end)
	local spawnIdx = 0
	Button(TpPlus, "Cycle Spawn Points", "Next", function()
		local list = {}
		for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("SpawnLocation") then table.insert(list, d) end end
		if #list == 0 then notify("No spawns found", T.Warn) return end
		spawnIdx = spawnIdx % #list + 1
		doTeleport(list[spawnIdx].CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))
		notify("Spawn " .. spawnIdx .. " / " .. #list)
	end)
	Button(TpPlus, "Copy Full CFrame", "Copy", function()
		local r = getRoot()
		if r then copyText("CFrame.new(" .. tostring(r.CFrame) .. ")") end
	end)
	local wpIdx = 0
	Button(TpPlus, "Teleport To Next Waypoint", "Next", function()
		if #WP == 0 then notify("No waypoints saved", T.Warn) return end
		wpIdx = wpIdx % #WP + 1
		doTeleport(WP[wpIdx].cf)
		notify("Waypoint: " .. WP[wpIdx].name)
	end)
	Button(TpPlus, "Copy All Waypoints", "Copy", function()
		if #WP == 0 then notify("No waypoints saved", T.Warn) return end
		local lines = {}
		for _, wp in ipairs(WP) do
			table.insert(lines, string.format("%s: %.1f, %.1f, %.1f", wp.name, wp.cf.X, wp.cf.Y, wp.cf.Z))
		end
		copyText(table.concat(lines, "\n"))
	end)
	local deathWp, deathCount = false, 0
	local function hookDeathWp(char)
		local h = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
		if h then
			h.Died:Connect(function()
				if deathWp and Running then
					local r = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if r then
						deathCount = deathCount + 1
						table.insert(WP, { name = "Death " .. deathCount, cf = r.CFrame })
						rebuildWaypoints()
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	if LocalPlayer.Character then task.spawn(hookDeathWp, LocalPlayer.Character) end
	bind(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function(ch) task.spawn(hookDeathWp, ch) end)
	Toggle(TpPlus, "Auto-Save Death Locations", false, function(v) deathWp = v end, nil, "deathwp")

	-- NEW (v3.10): quick slots, waypoint tour, teleport-to-mouse key ------------------------------
	do
		local slots = {}
		local slotKeys = { Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, Enum.KeyCode.Unknown }
		S.TourSecs, S.TpMouseKey = 3, Enum.KeyCode.Unknown

		local Sec = Section(TpTab, "Quick Slots", "Three instant save / teleport slots with optional hotkeys")
		for i = 1, 3 do
			Button(Sec, "Save Slot " .. i .. " (here)", "Save", function()
				local r = getRoot()
				if r then slots[i] = r.CFrame notify("Slot " .. i .. " saved", T.Good) end
			end)
			Button(Sec, "Teleport To Slot " .. i, "Go", function()
				if slots[i] then doTeleport(slots[i]) else notify("Slot " .. i .. " is empty", T.Warn) end
			end)
			Keybind(Sec, "Slot " .. i .. " Hotkey  (Backspace = none)", slotKeys[i], function(k) slotKeys[i] = k end, true)
		end
		bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(inp, gp)
			if gp or BindingActive or not Unlocked or inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
			for i = 1, 3 do
				if slotKeys[i] ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and inp.KeyCode == slotKeys[i] and slots[i] then doTeleport(slots[i]) end
			end
			if S.TpMouseKey ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and inp.KeyCode == S.TpMouseKey and Mouse.Target then
				doTeleport(CFrame.new(Mouse.Hit.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)))
			end
		end)

		local Tour = Section(TpTab, "Waypoint Tour & Hotkeys", "Cycle through your waypoints automatically; teleport to your cursor with a key")
		Toggle(Tour, "Auto Tour Waypoints", false, function(v) S.Tour = v end, nil, "tour")
		Slider(Tour, "Seconds Per Stop", 1, 30, 3, function(v) S.TourSecs = v end, "toursecs")
		Keybind(Tour, "Teleport To Cursor Hotkey", S.TpMouseKey, function(k) S.TpMouseKey = k end, true)
		task.spawn(function()
			local idx = 0
			while Running do
				if S.Tour and #WP > 0 then
					idx = idx % #WP + 1
					doTeleport(WP[idx].cf)
					local t0 = os.clock()
					while Running and S.Tour and os.clock() - t0 < S.TourSecs do task.wait(0.1) end
				else
					task.wait(0.3)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: WORLD
-- ====================================================
do
	local WorldTab = AddTab("World", ICONS.World)
	local Env = Section(WorldTab, "Lighting", "Local lighting changes")
	Toggle(Env, "Fullbright", false, function(v)
		S.Fullbright = v
		if v then mods.Light = true else restoreLighting() end
	end, nil, "fullbright")
	Toggle(Env, "Remove Shadows", false, function(v)
		S.NoShadows = v
		if v then mods.Light = true else Lighting.GlobalShadows = origLighting.GlobalShadows end
	end, nil, "noshadows")
	Toggle(Env, "Remove Fog", false, function(v)
		S.NoFog = v
		if v then mods.Light = true else Lighting.FogEnd = origLighting.FogEnd end
	end, nil, "nofog")
	Toggle(Env, "Remove Blur / Bloom / Effects", false, function(v) S.NoEffects = v setEffects(v) end, nil, "noeffects")
	Slider(Env, "Time Of Day", 0, 24, math.floor(Lighting.ClockTime), function(v) S.TimeValue = v Lighting.ClockTime = v mods.Time = true end, "time")
	Toggle(Env, "Lock Time Of Day", false, function(v) S.LockTime = v if v then mods.Time = true end end, nil, "locktime")

	local Phys = Section(WorldTab, "Physics", "Workspace settings (local)")
	Slider(Phys, "Gravity", 0, 400, math.floor(origGravity), function(v) Workspace.Gravity = v mods.Gravity = true end, "gravity")
	Button(Phys, "Reset World Settings", "Reset", function()
		for _, id in ipairs({ "fullbright", "noshadows", "nofog", "noeffects", "locktime" }) do if C[id] then C[id].Fire(false) end end
		if mods.Light then restoreLighting() end
		Lighting.ClockTime = origClock
		Workspace.Gravity = origGravity
		C.gravity.SetSilent(origGravity)
		C.time.SetSilent(origClock)
		mods.Gravity, mods.Time = false, false
		notify("World settings restored")
	end)

	-- NEW: gravity presets ---------------------------------------------------
	Button(Phys, "Moon Gravity", "Apply", function() Workspace.Gravity = 33 mods.Gravity = true C.gravity.SetSilent(33) end)
	Button(Phys, "Heavy Gravity", "Apply", function() Workspace.Gravity = 400 mods.Gravity = true C.gravity.SetSilent(400) end)
	Button(Phys, "Normal Gravity", "Apply", function() Workspace.Gravity = origGravity mods.Gravity = true C.gravity.SetSilent(origGravity) end)

	-- NEW: extra lighting ----------------------------------------------------
	local origExposure = Lighting.ExposureCompensation
	local expDirty = false
	local atmoOrig = weak()
	local function setAtmo(off)
		for _, d in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
			if d:IsA("Atmosphere") then
				if off then
					if not atmoOrig[d] then atmoOrig[d] = { d.Density, d.Haze } end
					d.Density, d.Haze = 0, 0
				elseif atmoOrig[d] then
					d.Density, d.Haze = atmoOrig[d][1], atmoOrig[d][2]
					atmoOrig[d] = nil
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local cloudsOrig = nil
	local function setClouds(off)
		local cl = Workspace.Terrain:FindFirstChildOfClass("Clouds")
		if not cl then
			if off then notify("No clouds in this game", T.Warn) end
			return
		end
		if off then
			if cloudsOrig == nil then cloudsOrig = cl.Enabled end
			cl.Enabled = false
		elseif cloudsOrig ~= nil then
			cl.Enabled = cloudsOrig
			cloudsOrig = nil
		end
	end
	onUnload(function()
		setAtmo(false)
		setClouds(false)
		if expDirty then Lighting.ExposureCompensation = origExposure end
	end)
	bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
		if S.Cycle and not S.LockTime then
			Lighting.ClockTime = (Lighting.ClockTime + dt * (S.CycleSpeed or 30) / 60) % 24
		end
	end)

	local Lit = Section(WorldTab, "Extra Lighting", "Brightness, exposure, haze, clouds and day cycle")
	Slider(Lit, "Brightness", 0, 10, math.clamp(math.floor(Lighting.Brightness), 0, 10), function(v) Lighting.Brightness = v mods.Light = true end, "brightness")
	Slider(Lit, "Exposure (x0.1)", -30, 30, math.clamp(math.floor(origExposure * 10 + 0.5), -30, 30), function(v) Lighting.ExposureCompensation = v / 10 expDirty = true end, "exposure")
	local ambIdx, ambBtn = 0, nil
	ambBtn = Button(Lit, "Ambient Tint", "Default", function()
		ambIdx = (ambIdx + 1) % (#ColorPresets + 1)
		if ambIdx == 0 then
			Lighting.Ambient = origLighting.Ambient
			ambBtn.Text = "Default"
		else
			Lighting.Ambient = ColorPresets[ambIdx][2]
			ambBtn.Text = ColorPresets[ambIdx][1]
			mods.Light = true
		end
	end)
	Toggle(Lit, "Remove Atmosphere / Haze", false, setAtmo, nil, "atmo")
	Toggle(Lit, "Remove Clouds", false, setClouds, nil, "clouds")
	Toggle(Lit, "Day / Night Cycle", false, function(v) S.Cycle = v if v then mods.Time = true end end, nil, "cycle")
	Slider(Lit, "Cycle Speed", 1, 120, 30, function(v) S.CycleSpeed = v end, "cyclespeed")
	Button(Lit, "Reset Extra Lighting", "Reset", function()
		for _, id in ipairs({ "atmo", "clouds", "cycle" }) do if C[id] then C[id].Fire(false) end end
		Lighting.ExposureCompensation = origExposure
		Lighting.Ambient = origLighting.Ambient
		Lighting.Brightness = origLighting.Brightness
		ambIdx = 0
		ambBtn.Text = "Default"
		C.exposure.SetSilent(math.clamp(math.floor(origExposure * 10 + 0.5), -30, 30))
		C.brightness.SetSilent(math.clamp(math.floor(origLighting.Brightness), 0, 10))
		notify("Extra lighting restored")
	end)
end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: TOOLS
-- ====================================================
do
	local ToolsTab = AddTab("Tools", ICONS.Tools)
	local ServerTab = AddTab("Servers", ICONS.Servers)
	do
		local SrvInfo = Section(ServerTab, "Server Info", "Details about the server you are in")
		InfoRow(SrvInfo, "Place version: " .. tostring(game.PlaceVersion))
		InfoRow(SrvInfo, "Server type: " .. ((game.PrivateServerId ~= "" and "Private / VIP") or "Public"))
		local srvPl = InfoRow(SrvInfo, "Players: -")
		local srvUp = InfoRow(SrvInfo, "Uptime: -")
		local srvPing = InfoRow(SrvInfo, "Ping: -")
		task.spawn(function()
			while Running do
				pcall(function()
					srvPl.Text = "Players: " .. #Players:GetPlayers() .. " / " .. Players.MaxPlayers
					local up = math.floor(Workspace.DistributedGameTime)
					srvUp.Text = string.format("Uptime: %02d:%02d:%02d", up // 3600, (up % 3600) // 60, up % 60)
					local ok, p = pcall(function() return Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue() end)
					srvPing.Text = "Ping: " .. ((ok and p) and math.floor(p) or math.floor(LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() * 2000)) .. " ms"
				end)
				task.wait(1)
			end
		end)
	end
	local ServerSec = Section(ServerTab, "Server", "Moving between servers")
	Button(ServerSec, "Rejoin Server", "Rejoin", function() TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer) end)
	Button(ServerSec, "Server Hop", "Hop", function() serverHop(false) end, "Premium User")
	Button(ServerSec, "Hop To Smallest Server", "Hop", function() serverHop(true) end, "Premium User")
	Button(ServerSec, "Copy Game Link", "Copy", function() copyText("https://www.roblox.com/games/" .. game.PlaceId) end)
	Toggle(ServerSec, "Join / Leave Alerts", false, function(v) S.Alerts = v end, nil, "alerts")
	Toggle(ServerSec, "Auto Rejoin If Kicked", false, function(v)
		autoRejoin = v
		if v and not rejoinHooked then notify("Auto rejoin may not work in this environment", T.Warn) end
	end, nil, "autorejoin")

	local PerfSec = Section(ToolsTab, "Performance", "Make the game run smoother")
	Toggle(PerfSec, "Low Graphics Mode", false, function(v) S.LowGfx = v setLowGfx(v) end, nil, "lowgfx")
	Toggle(PerfSec, "Disable Particles / Trails", false, function(v) S.NoParticles = v setParticles(v) end, nil, "noparticles")
	if setfpscap then
		Slider(PerfSec, "FPS Cap", 30, 360, math.clamp(math.floor(origFps or 60), 30, 360), function(v) fpsChanged = true pcall(setfpscap, v) end, "fpscap")
	else
		InfoRow(PerfSec, "FPS cap not supported by this environment")
	end


	local DelSec = Section(ToolsTab, "Click Delete", "Alt + Click removes a part for YOU only (undo anytime)")
	Toggle(DelSec, "Click Delete (Alt + Click)", false, function(v) S.ClickDelete = v end, "Owner", "clickdelete")
	Button(DelSec, "Undo Last Delete", "Undo", undoDelete)
	Button(DelSec, "Restore All Deleted", "Restore", function() restoreAllDeleted() notify("Restored everything") end)


	-- NEW: server extras -----------------------------------------------------
	local SrvX = Section(ServerTab, "Server Extras", "Rejoin the same server and share it")
	Button(SrvX, "Rejoin Same Server", "Rejoin", function()
		pcall(function() TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer) end)
	end)
	Button(SrvX, "Copy Join Script", "Copy", function()
		copyText(('game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(%d, "%s", game.Players.LocalPlayer)'):format(game.PlaceId, game.JobId))
	end)
	Button(SrvX, "Copy Player List With IDs", "Copy", function()
		local lines = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do table.insert(lines, p.Name .. " (" .. p.UserId .. ")") end
		copyText(table.concat(lines, "\n"))
	end)

	-- NEW: hide textures + mute ----------------------------------------------
	local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")
	local texOff, muted = weak(), weak()
	local function setTextures(off)
		if off then
			for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("Decal") and d.Transparency < 1 then texOff[d] = d.Transparency d.Transparency = 1 end
			end
		else
			for d, orig in pairs(texOff) do if d.Parent then d.Transparency = orig end end
			table.clear(texOff)
		end
	end
	local function setMute(on)
		if on then
			for _, root in ipairs({ Workspace, SoundService }) do
				for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("Sound") and d.Volume > 0 then muted[d] = d.Volume d.Volume = 0 end
				end
			end
		else
			for d, v in pairs(muted) do if d.Parent then d.Volume = v end end
			table.clear(muted)
		end
	end
	bind(Workspace.DescendantAdded, function(d)
		if S.NoTex and d:IsA("Decal") and d.Transparency < 1 then texOff[d] = d.Transparency d.Transparency = 1 end
		if S.Mute and d:IsA("Sound") and d.Volume > 0 then muted[d] = d.Volume d.Volume = 0 end
	end)
	onUnload(function() setTextures(false) setMute(false) end)
	Toggle(PerfSec, "Hide Textures & Decals (FPS boost)", false, function(v) S.NoTex = v setTextures(v) end, nil, "notex")
	Toggle(PerfSec, "Mute Game Sounds", false, function(v) S.Mute = v setMute(v) end, nil, "mute")

	-- NEW (v3.4): join by Job ID, server browser, saved servers ---------------------
	do
		local SAVE_FILE, LAST_FILE = Hub.path("servers.json"), Hub.path("last_server.json")
		local canFile = (writefile and readfile and isfile) and true or false
		local saved, prev = {}, nil

		local function readJson(path)
			if not (canFile and isfile(path)) then return nil end
			local ok, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path)) end)
			return ok and data or nil
		end
		local function writeJson(path, data)
			if canFile then pcall(function() writefile(path, HttpService:JSONEncode(data)) end) end
		end

		local list = readJson(SAVE_FILE)
		if type(list) == "table" then
			for _, e in ipairs(list) do
				if type(e) == "table" and e.job and e.place then table.insert(saved, e) end
			end
		end
		-- remember which server we were in last time (so "Join Previous Server" works)
		local last = readJson(LAST_FILE)
		if type(last) == "table" and last.job then
			if last.job ~= game.JobId then prev = { job = last.job, place = last.place } else prev = last.prev end
		end
		if game.JobId ~= "" then writeJson(LAST_FILE, { job = game.JobId, place = game.PlaceId, prev = prev }) end

		local function cleanId(s) return (tostring(s or ""):gsub("[%s\"']", "")) end
		local function validJob(s)
			return s:match("^%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x$") ~= nil
		end
		local function joinJob(job, place)
			job = cleanId(job)
			place = tonumber(place) or game.PlaceId
			if not validJob(job) then notify("That doesn't look like a valid Job ID", T.Warn) return false end
			if job == game.JobId then notify("You're already in that server", T.Warn) return false end
			notify("Joining server...", T.Good)
			local ok, err = pcall(function() TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(place, job, LocalPlayer) end)
			if not ok then notify("Teleport failed: " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 40), T.Bad) return false end
			return true
		end
		local function joinPlace(id)
			local n = tonumber(cleanId(id))
			if not n then notify("Enter a numeric Place ID", T.Warn) return end
			notify("Teleporting to place " .. n .. "...", T.Good)
			pcall(function() TeleportService:Teleport(n, LocalPlayer) end)
		end
		S.JoinJob, S.JoinPlace = joinJob, joinPlace
		bind(TeleportService.TeleportInitFailed, function(_, result, msg)
			notify("Teleport failed: " .. tostring(msg or result):sub(1, 50), T.Bad)
		end)

		-- join section ------------------------------------------------------------
		local Join = Section(ServerTab, "Join Server", "Teleport to a specific server by its Job ID")
		local jobBox
		jobBox = Input(Join, "Job ID", "paste server job id", "Join", function(text) joinJob(text) end)
		local getClip = getclipboard or fromclipboard
		if getClip then
			Button(Join, "Join Job ID From Clipboard", "Paste & Join", function()
				local ok, text = pcall(getClip)
				if ok and text then jobBox.Text = cleanId(text) joinJob(text) else notify("Couldn't read clipboard", T.Warn) end
			end)
		end
		Input(Join, "Join Place ID", "other game place id", "Go", function(text) joinPlace(text) end)
		Button(Join, "Join Previous Server", "Go Back", function()
			if prev and prev.job then joinJob(prev.job, prev.place) else notify("No previous server recorded yet", T.Warn) end
		end)
		Button(Join, "Copy Join Link (browser)", "Copy", function()
			copyText("roblox://experiences/start?placeId=" .. game.PlaceId .. "&gameInstanceId=" .. game.JobId)
		end)
		Button(Join, "Copy My Server's Job ID", "Copy", function() copyText(game.JobId) end)

		-- saved servers -----------------------------------------------------------
		local Saved = Section(ServerTab, "Saved Servers", "Bookmark servers and jump back to them" .. (canFile and " (kept between sessions)" or " (this session only)"))
		local holder = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
		new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, holder)
		local rebuildSaved
		local function miniBtn(row, text, x, color, cb)
			local b = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, x, 0.5, -11), BackgroundColor3 = color or T.Btn, Text = text, TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11 }, row)
			round(b, 6)
			b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Hub.play("click") cb() end)
		end
		function rebuildSaved()
			for _, ch in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("Frame") then ch:Destroy() end end
			for i, e in ipairs(saved) do
				local row = Row(holder, 34)
				row.LayoutOrder = i
				Label(row, { Text = e.name .. "  (" .. e.job:sub(1, 8) .. ")", Size = UDim2.new(1, -170, 1, 0), TextSize = 11, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
				miniBtn(row, "Join", -160, T.Accent, function() joinJob(e.job, e.place) end)
				miniBtn(row, "Copy", -108, T.Btn, function() copyText(e.job) end)
				miniBtn(row, "Del", -56, T.Bad, function()
					table.remove(saved, i)
					writeJson(SAVE_FILE, saved)
					rebuildSaved()
				end)
			end
		end
		local function saveServer(name)
			if game.JobId == "" then notify("No server id here (Studio?)", T.Warn) return end
			for _, e in ipairs(saved) do if e.job == game.JobId then notify("This server is already saved", T.Warn) return end end
			name = (name or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
			if name == "" then name = "Server " .. (#saved + 1) end
			table.insert(saved, { name = name, job = game.JobId, place = game.PlaceId })
			writeJson(SAVE_FILE, saved)
			rebuildSaved()
			notify("Saved " .. name, T.Good)
		end
		S.SaveServer = saveServer
		Button(Saved, "Save Current Server", "Save", function() saveServer("") end)
		local saveBox
		saveBox = Input(Saved, "Save Server As", "bookmark name", "Save", function(text)
			saveServer(text)
			saveBox.Text = ""
		end)
		Button(Saved, "Clear All Saved Servers", "Clear", function()
			table.clear(saved)
			writeJson(SAVE_FILE, saved)
			rebuildSaved()
		end)
		holder.Parent = Saved
		rebuildSaved()

		-- server browser ----------------------------------------------------------
		local Browse = Section(ServerTab, "Server Browser", "Public servers for this game (needs HTTP support)")
		local function fetchServers(order)
			local ok, res = pcall(function()
				local url = ("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=%s&limit=100"):format(game.PlaceId, order)
				return HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet(url))
			end)
			if not ok or type(res) ~= "table" or not res.data then return nil end
			return res.data
		end
		local srvList = new("ScrollingFrame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 210), BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new() })
		round(srvList, 8)
		new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, srvList)
		new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4) }, srvList)
		local fetching = false
		local function refreshServers(order)
			if fetching then return end
			fetching = true
			notify("Fetching servers...")
			task.spawn(function()
				local data = fetchServers(order)
				fetching = false
				if not Running then return end
				for _, ch in ipairs(srvList:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("Frame") then ch:Destroy() end end
				if not data then notify("Server list isn't available here", T.Warn) return end
				for i, s in ipairs(data) do
					local here = (s.id == game.JobId)
					local full = (s.playing or 0) >= (s.maxPlayers or 0)
					local row = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = i }, srvList)
					round(row, 6)
					local info = string.format("%d / %d players   |   %s ms   |   %s fps", s.playing or 0, s.maxPlayers or 0, tostring(s.ping and math.floor(s.ping) or "?"), tostring(s.fps and math.floor(s.fps) or "?"))
					Label(row, { Text = info .. (here and "   (you)" or (full and "   (full)" or "")), Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 1, 0), TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = here and T.Good or T.Text })
					miniBtn(row, "Join", -108, T.Accent, function() joinJob(s.id) end)
					miniBtn(row, "Copy", -58, T.Btn, function() copyText(s.id) end)
				end
				notify("Found " .. #data .. " servers", T.Good)
			end)
		end
		S.RefreshServers = refreshServers
		Button(Browse, "Refresh: Most Players First", "Refresh", function() refreshServers("Desc") end)
		Button(Browse, "Refresh: Fewest Players First", "Refresh", function() refreshServers("Asc") end)
		srvList.Parent = Browse
		Button(Browse, "Hop To Fullest Open Server", "Hop", function()
			task.spawn(function()
