				local data = fetchServers("Desc")
				if not data then notify("Server list isn't available here", T.Warn) return end
				for _, s in ipairs(data) do
					if s.id ~= game.JobId and (s.playing or 0) < (s.maxPlayers or 0) then joinJob(s.id) return end
				end
				notify("No open servers found", T.Warn)
			end)
		end, "Premium User")
	end


	-- NEW (v3.10): go to nearest interactable + join a player's server ---------------------------
	do
		local Join = Section(ServerTab, "Join A Player's Server", "Follow a friend into their game (they must allow it)")
		Input(Join, "Username", "roblox username", "Join", function(text)
			text = text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
			if text == "" then notify("Type a username first", T.Warn) return end
			task.spawn(function()
				local ok, uid = pcall(Players.GetUserIdFromNameAsync, Players, text)
				if not ok or not uid then notify("User not found", T.Warn) return end
				local ok2, _, _, placeId, jobId = pcall(TeleportService.GetPlayerPlaceInstanceAsync, TeleportService, uid)
				if ok2 and jobId and S.JoinJob then
					S.JoinJob(jobId, placeId)
				else
					notify("Couldn't locate them (must be an online friend who allows joining)", T.Warn)
				end
			end)
		end)
	end

end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: COMMANDS
-- ====================================================
do
	local CmdTab = AddTab("Commands", ICONS.Commands)
	local CmdSec = Section(CmdTab, "Command Bar", "Type a command and press Enter (Up/Down = history). Try: cmds")
	local cmdBox
	local LogSec = Section(CmdTab, "Output", "Results of your commands")
	local LogFrame = new("ScrollingFrame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 190), BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new() }, LogSec)
	round(LogFrame, 8)
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, LogFrame)
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8) }, LogFrame)
	local logCount = 0
	local function logCmd(text, color)
		logCount = logCount + 1
		local l = new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text, TextColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(185, 190, 195), Font = Enum.Font.Code, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextWrapped = true, LayoutOrder = logCount }, LogFrame)
		local kids = {}
		for _, ch in ipairs(LogFrame:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then table.insert(kids, ch) end end
		if #kids > 80 then kids[1]:Destroy() end
		task.defer(function() LogFrame.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 1e6) end)
		return l
	end

	local Commands = {}
	local function addCmd(names, usage, fn)
		for _, n in ipairs(names) do Commands[n] = { usage = usage, fn = fn } end
	end
	local function flip(id, args, label)
		local tg = C[id]
		if not tg then return end
		local v
		if args[1] then
			local a = args[1]:lower()
			v = (a == "on" or a == "true" or a == "1" or a == "yes")
		else
			v = not tg.Get()
		end
		if tg.Fire(v) == false then
			logCmd((label or id) .. " needs a higher rank", T.Warn)
			return
		end
		logCmd((label or id) .. ": " .. (tg.Get() and "ON" or "OFF"), tg.Get() and T.Good or T.Sub)
	end
	local function num(id, args, label, enableId)
		local n = tonumber(args[1])
		if not n then logCmd("Usage: " .. label .. " <number>", T.Warn) return end
		C[id].Set(n)
		if enableId and C[enableId] then C[enableId].Fire(true) end
		logCmd(label .. " set to " .. C[id].Get(), T.Good)
	end
	local function needPlayer(args)
		local p = findPlayer(args[1])
		if not p then logCmd("Player not found: " .. tostring(args[1]), T.Bad) end
		return p
	end
	local function pickWaypoint(args)
		local q = table.concat(args, " "):lower()
		if q == "" then return nil end
		local n = tonumber(q)
		if n and WP[n] then return n end
		for i, wp in ipairs(WP) do if wp.name:lower():sub(1, #q) == q then return i end end
		return nil
	end

	addCmd({ "speed", "ws" }, "speed <n>", function(a) num("speedvalue", a, "speed", "speed") end)
	addCmd({ "jump", "jp" }, "jump <n>", function(a) num("jumpvalue", a, "jump", "jump") end)
	addCmd({ "sprint" }, "sprint [on/off]", function(a) flip("sprint", a, "sprint") end)
	addCmd({ "noclip", "nc" }, "noclip [on/off]", function(a) flip("noclip", a, "noclip") end)
	addCmd({ "fly" }, "fly [on/off]", function(a) flip("fly", a, "fly") end)
	addCmd({ "flyspeed", "fs" }, "flyspeed <n>", function(a) num("flyspeed", a, "fly speed") end)
	addCmd({ "infjump", "ij" }, "infjump [on/off]", function(a) flip("infjump", a, "infinite jump") end)
	addCmd({ "bhop" }, "bhop [on/off]", function(a) flip("bhop", a, "bunny hop") end)
	addCmd({ "spin" }, "spin [on/off]", function(a) flip("spin", a, "spin") end)
	addCmd({ "freeze" }, "freeze [on/off]", function(a) flip("freeze", a, "freeze") end)
	addCmd({ "antivoid" }, "antivoid [on/off]", function(a) flip("antivoid", a, "anti-void") end)
	addCmd({ "deathreturn", "dr" }, "deathreturn [on/off]", function(a) flip("deathreturn", a, "death return") end)
	addCmd({ "esp" }, "esp [on/off]", function(a) flip("esp", a, "ESP") end)
	addCmd({ "boxes" }, "boxes [on/off]", function(a) flip("boxes", a, "boxes") end)
	addCmd({ "names" }, "names [on/off]", function(a) flip("names", a, "names") end)
	addCmd({ "tracers" }, "tracers [on/off]", function(a) flip("tracers", a, "tracers") end)
	addCmd({ "chams" }, "chams [on/off]", function(a) flip("chams", a, "chams") end)
	addCmd({ "cards" }, "cards [on/off]", function(a) flip("cards", a, "player cards") end)
	addCmd({ "fullbright", "fb" }, "fullbright [on/off]", function(a) flip("fullbright", a, "fullbright") end)
	addCmd({ "freecam", "fc" }, "freecam [on/off]", function(a) flip("freecam", a, "freecam") end)
	addCmd({ "crosshair", "ch" }, "crosshair [on/off]", function(a) flip("crosshair", a, "crosshair") end)
	addCmd({ "antiafk" }, "antiafk [on/off]", function(a) flip("antiafk", a, "anti-AFK") end)
	addCmd({ "alerts" }, "alerts [on/off]", function(a) flip("alerts", a, "join/leave alerts") end)
	addCmd({ "lowgfx" }, "lowgfx [on/off]", function(a) flip("lowgfx", a, "low graphics") end)
	addCmd({ "particles" }, "particles [on/off]", function(a) flip("noparticles", a, "particle removal") end)
	addCmd({ "effects" }, "effects [on/off]", function(a) flip("noeffects", a, "effect removal") end)
	addCmd({ "autorejoin" }, "autorejoin [on/off]", function(a) flip("autorejoin", a, "auto rejoin") end)
	addCmd({ "fov" }, "fov <n>", function(a) num("fov", a, "FOV") end)
	addCmd({ "gravity", "grav" }, "gravity <n>", function(a) num("gravity", a, "gravity") end)
	addCmd({ "time" }, "time <0-24>", function(a) num("time", a, "time of day") end)
	addCmd({ "zoom" }, "zoom <n>", function(a) num("zoom", a, "max zoom") end)
	addCmd({ "fps" }, "fps <n>", function(a)
		if not C.fpscap then logCmd("FPS cap not supported here", T.Warn) return end
		num("fpscap", a, "fps cap")
	end)
	addCmd({ "tp", "goto" }, "tp <player>", function(a)
		local p = needPlayer(a)
		if p and p ~= LocalPlayer then teleportToPlayer(p) logCmd("Teleported to " .. p.DisplayName, T.Good) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "tpn" }, "tpn", function()
		local p = nearestPlayer()
		if p then teleportToPlayer(p) logCmd("Teleported to " .. p.DisplayName, T.Good) else logCmd("No players nearby", T.Warn) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "nearest" }, "nearest", function()
		local p, d = nearestPlayer()
		if p then S.Target = p refreshPlayerList() logCmd("Nearest: " .. p.DisplayName .. " (" .. math.floor(d) .. " studs)", T.Good) else logCmd("No players nearby", T.Warn) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "view", "spectate" }, "view <player>", function(a)
		local p = needPlayer(a)
		if p then S.Target = p refreshPlayerList() spectate(p) logCmd("Viewing " .. p.DisplayName, T.Good) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "unview" }, "unview", function() stopSpectate() logCmd("Stopped spectating") end)
	addCmd({ "follow" }, "follow <player>", function(a)
		local p = needPlayer(a)
		if p and p ~= LocalPlayer then S.Target = p refreshPlayerList() C.follow.Fire(true) logCmd("Following " .. p.DisplayName, T.Good) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "unfollow" }, "unfollow", function() C.follow.Fire(false) logCmd("Stopped following") end)
	addCmd({ "info" }, "info <player>", function(a)
		local p = needPlayer(a)
		if p then
			logCmd(p.DisplayName .. " (@" .. p.Name .. ")  id " .. p.UserId .. "  age " .. p.AccountAge .. "d  team " .. (p.Team and p.Team.Name or "none"))
		end
	end)
	addCmd({ "players", "plrs" }, "players", function()
		local names = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do table.insert(names, p.Name) end
		logCmd(#names .. " players: " .. table.concat(names, ", "))
	end)
	addCmd({ "savepos", "save" }, "savepos [name]", function(a)
		local r = getRoot()
		if r then
			local name = table.concat(a, " ")
			if name == "" then name = "Waypoint " .. (#WP + 1) end
			table.insert(WP, { name = name, cf = r.CFrame })
			rebuildWaypoints()
			logCmd("Saved " .. name, T.Good)
		end
	end)
	addCmd({ "wp" }, "wp <number|name>", function(a)
		local i = pickWaypoint(a)
		if i then doTeleport(WP[i].cf) logCmd("Teleported to " .. WP[i].name, T.Good) else logCmd("Waypoint not found", T.Warn) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "delwp" }, "delwp <number|name>", function(a)
		local i = pickWaypoint(a)
		if i then local n = WP[i].name table.remove(WP, i) rebuildWaypoints() logCmd("Deleted " .. n, T.Good) else logCmd("Waypoint not found", T.Warn) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "copypos" }, "copypos", function()
		local r = getRoot()
		if r then copyText(string.format("%.1f, %.1f, %.1f", r.Position.X, r.Position.Y, r.Position.Z)) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "back" }, "back", function()
		if prevTpCF then doTeleport(prevTpCF) logCmd("Went back", T.Good) else logCmd("No previous position", T.Warn) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "spawn" }, "spawn", function()
		local sp = Workspace:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpawnLocation", true)
		if sp then doTeleport(sp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)) logCmd("Teleported to spawn", T.Good) else logCmd("No spawn found", T.Warn) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "undo" }, "undo", function() undoDelete() end)
	addCmd({ "sit" }, "sit", function() local h = getHum() if h then h.Sit = true end end)
	addCmd({ "heal" }, "heal", function() local h = getHum() if h then h.Health = h.MaxHealth logCmd("Healed (local)", T.Good) end end)
	addCmd({ "rejoin", "rj" }, "rejoin", function() TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer) end)
	addCmd({ "hop" }, "hop", function()
		if hasRank("Premium User") then serverHop(false) else logCmd("Server hop requires Premium", T.Warn) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "reset", "re" }, "reset", function() local h = getHum() if h then h.Health = 0 end end)
	addCmd({ "rank" }, "rank", function() logCmd("Rank: " .. rank) end)
	addCmd({ "saveconfig" }, "saveconfig", function() saveConfig() end)
	addCmd({ "loadconfig" }, "loadconfig", function() loadConfig() end)
	addCmd({ "hide" }, "hide", function() Main.Visible = false logCmd("Menu hidden (press " .. S.MenuKey.Name .. ")") end)
	addCmd({ "clear", "cls" }, "clear", function() for _, ch in ipairs(LogFrame:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then ch:Destroy() end end end)
	addCmd({ "unload", "exit" }, "unload", function() Unload() end)
	addCmd({ "cmds", "help" }, "cmds", function()
		local seen, usages = {}, {}
		for _, c in pairs(Commands) do if not seen[c.usage] then seen[c.usage] = true table.insert(usages, c.usage) end end
		table.sort(usages)
		local chunk = {}
		for i, u in ipairs(usages) do
			table.insert(chunk, u)
			if #chunk == 4 or i == #usages then logCmd(table.concat(chunk, "   |   ")) chunk = {} end
		end
	end)

	local history, histIdx = {}, 0
	local function runCommand(line)
		line = line:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if line == "" then return end
		if history[#history] ~= line then table.insert(history, line) end
		if #history > 50 then table.remove(history, 1) end
		histIdx = #history + 1
		local parts = {}
		for w in line:gmatch("%S+") do table.insert(parts, w) end
		local name = table.remove(parts, 1):lower()
		logCmd("> " .. line, T.Accent)
		local c = Commands[name]
		if not c then logCmd("Unknown command: " .. name .. "  (type cmds)", T.Bad) return end
		local ok, err = pcall(c.fn, parts)
		if not ok then logCmd("Error: " .. tostring(err), T.Bad) end
	end
	cmdBox = Input(CmdSec, "Command", "e.g.  speed 80", "Run", function(text, enter)
		runCommand(text)
		cmdBox.Text = ""
		if enter then
			task.delay(0.05, function()
				if Running and Main.Visible and cmdBox.Parent then cmdBox:CaptureFocus() end
			end)
		end
	end)
	bind(cmdBox.InputBegan, function(i)
		if not cmdBox:IsFocused() or #history == 0 then return end
		if i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Up then
			histIdx = math.max(1, histIdx - 1)
			cmdBox.Text = history[histIdx] or ""
			cmdBox.CursorPosition = #cmdBox.Text + 1
		elseif i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Down then
			histIdx = math.min(#history + 1, histIdx + 1)
			cmdBox.Text = history[histIdx] or ""
			cmdBox.CursorPosition = #cmdBox.Text + 1
		end
	end)

	-- NEW: ~30 more commands -------------------------------------------------
	addCmd({ "headdots" }, "headdots [on/off]", function(a) flip("headdots", a, "head dots") end)
	addCmd({ "arrows" }, "arrows [on/off]", function(a) flip("arrows", a, "off-screen arrows") end)
	addCmd({ "orbit" }, "orbit <player>", function(a)
		local p = needPlayer(a)
		if p and p ~= LocalPlayer then S.Target = p refreshPlayerList() C.orbit.Fire(true) logCmd("Orbiting " .. p.DisplayName, T.Good) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "unorbit" }, "unorbit", function() C.orbit.Fire(false) logCmd("Stopped orbiting") end)
	addCmd({ "up" }, "up [studs]", function(a)
		local r = getRoot()
		if r then doTeleport(r.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, tonumber(a[1]) or S.UpAmount or 50, 0)) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "down" }, "down [studs]", function(a)
		local r = getRoot()
		if r then doTeleport(r.CFrame - Vector3.new(0, tonumber(a[1]) or S.UpAmount or 50, 0)) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "tweentp" }, "tweentp [on/off]", function(a) flip("tweentp", a, "smooth teleport") end)
	addCmd({ "brightness", "br" }, "brightness <0-10>", function(a) num("brightness", a, "brightness") end)
	addCmd({ "exposure" }, "exposure <-30..30>", function(a) num("exposure", a, "exposure") end)
	addCmd({ "cycle" }, "cycle [on/off]", function(a) flip("cycle", a, "day/night cycle") end)
	addCmd({ "atmosphere", "haze" }, "atmosphere [on/off]", function(a) flip("atmo", a, "atmosphere removal") end)
	addCmd({ "clouds" }, "clouds [on/off]", function(a) flip("clouds", a, "cloud removal") end)
	addCmd({ "mute" }, "mute [on/off]", function(a) flip("mute", a, "mute") end)
	addCmd({ "notex" }, "notex [on/off]", function(a) flip("notex", a, "texture removal") end)
	addCmd({ "rejoinsame", "rjs" }, "rejoinsame", function()
		pcall(function() TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer) end)
	end)
	addCmd({ "joinscript" }, "joinscript", function()
		copyText(('game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(%d, "%s", game.Players.LocalPlayer)'):format(game.PlaceId, game.JobId))
	end)
	addCmd({ "panic" }, "panic", function() if S.Panic then S.Panic() end end)
	addCmd({ "scale" }, "scale <60-140>", function(a) num("uiscale", a, "UI scale") end)
	addCmd({ "search", "find" }, "search <text>", function(a)
		local q = table.concat(a, " "):lower()
		if q == "" then logCmd("Usage: search <text>", T.Warn) return end
		local hits = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= LocalPlayer and (p.Name:lower():find(q, 1, true) or p.DisplayName:lower():find(q, 1, true)) then table.insert(hits, p) end
		end
		if #hits == 0 then logCmd("No players match '" .. q .. "'", T.Warn) return end
		S.Target = hits[1]
		refreshPlayerList()
		local names = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(hits) do table.insert(names, p.DisplayName .. " (@" .. p.Name .. ")") end
		logCmd(#hits .. " match(es): " .. table.concat(names, ", "), T.Good)
	end)
	addCmd({ "friends" }, "friends", function()
		local names = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= LocalPlayer then
				local ok, res = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(p.UserId) end)
				if ok and res then table.insert(names, p.Name) end
			end
		end
		logCmd(#names > 0 and ("Friends here: " .. table.concat(names, ", ")) or "No friends in this server")
	end)
	addCmd({ "!!", "last" }, "!! (repeat last)", function()
		local prev = history[#history - 1]
		if prev then runCommand(prev) else logCmd("No earlier command", T.Warn) end
	end)

	-- NEW (v3.4): server commands
	addCmd({ "joinjob", "jj" }, "joinjob <jobId>", function(a)
		if S.JoinJob and S.JoinJob(a[1]) then logCmd("Joining server " .. tostring(a[1]):sub(1, 8) .. "...", T.Good) else logCmd("Usage: joinjob <jobId>", T.Warn) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "joinplace", "jpl" }, "joinplace <placeId>", function(a)
		if S.JoinPlace then S.JoinPlace(a[1]) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "saveserver" }, "saveserver [name]", function(a)
		if S.SaveServer then S.SaveServer(table.concat(a, " ")) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "servers" }, "servers", function()
		if S.RefreshServers then S.RefreshServers("Desc") logCmd("Fetching servers (see Tools > Server Browser)") end
	end)

	-- NEW (v3.5): commands for the new tabs
	S.Run = runCommand
	addCmd({ "say" }, "say <message>", function(a) if S.SendChat then S.SendChat(table.concat(a, " ")) end end)
	addCmd({ "calc" }, "calc <expression>", function(a)
		if not S.Calc then return end
		local ok, v = pcall(S.Calc, table.concat(a, " "))
		logCmd(ok and ("= " .. tostring(v)) or ("Error: " .. tostring(v)), ok and T.Good or T.Bad)
	end)
	-- NEW (v3.10): commands for the action features
	addCmd({ "airwalk", "aw" }, "airwalk [on/off]", function(a) flip("airwalk", a, "air walk") end)
	addCmd({ "wallclimb", "climb" }, "wallclimb [on/off]", function(a) flip("wallclimb", a, "wall climb") end)
	addCmd({ "glide" }, "glide [on/off]", function(a) flip("glide", a, "glide") end)
	addCmd({ "facecam" }, "facecam [on/off]", function(a) flip("facecam", a, "face camera") end)
	addCmd({ "clickwalk" }, "clickwalk [on/off]", function(a) flip("clickwalk", a, "click to walk") end)
	addCmd({ "retreat" }, "retreat [on/off]", function(a) flip("retreat", a, "auto retreat") end)
	addCmd({ "stick" }, "stick <player>", function(a)
		local p = needPlayer(a)
		if p and p ~= LocalPlayer then S.Target = p refreshPlayerList() C.stick.Fire(true) logCmd("Sticking behind " .. p.DisplayName, T.Good) end
	end)
	addCmd({ "unstick" }, "unstick", function() C.stick.Fire(false) logCmd("Stopped sticking") end)
	addCmd({ "tour" }, "tour [on/off]", function(a) flip("tour", a, "waypoint tour") end)
	addCmd({ "proxalert" }, "proxalert [on/off]", function(a) flip("proxalert", a, "proximity alert") end)

	addCmd({ "voidhold", "void" }, "voidhold [on/off]", function(a) flip("voidhold", a, "void hold") end)
	addCmd({ "hover" }, "hover [on/off]", function(a) flip("hover", a, "hover inspector") end)
	addCmd({ "instaprompt", "ip" }, "instaprompt [on/off]", function(a) flip("instaprompt", a, "instant prompts") end)
	addCmd({ "record" }, "record [on/off]", function(a) flip("rec", a, "path recording") end)
	addCmd({ "playpath" }, "playpath", function() if S.PlayPath then S.PlayPath() end end)
	addCmd({ "stoppath" }, "stoppath", function() if S.StopPath then S.StopPath() end end)

	-- NEW: live suggestions + log tools --------------------------------------
	local sugRow = InfoRow(CmdSec, "Suggestions: start typing a command")
	bind(cmdBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function()
		local w = cmdBox.Text:match("^%s*(%S*)"):lower()
		if w == "" then sugRow.Text = "Suggestions: start typing a command" return end
		local hits = {}
		for n in pairs(Commands) do if n:sub(1, #w) == w then table.insert(hits, n) end end
		table.sort(hits)
		sugRow.Text = (#hits > 0) and ("Suggestions: " .. table.concat(hits, ", ", 1, math.min(#hits, 8))) or "No matching command"
	end)
	Button(CmdSec, "Re-run Last Command", "Run", function()
		if history[#history] then runCommand(history[#history]) else logCmd("No history yet", T.Warn) end
	end)
	Button(CmdSec, "Show All Commands", "List", function() runCommand("cmds") end)
	Button(CmdSec, "Copy Output Log", "Copy", function()
		local labels = {}
		for _, ch in ipairs(LogFrame:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then table.insert(labels, ch) end end
		table.sort(labels, function(a, b) return a.LayoutOrder < b.LayoutOrder end)
		local lines = {}
		for _, l in ipairs(labels) do table.insert(lines, l.Text) end
		copyText(table.concat(lines, "\n"))
	end)
	Button(CmdSec, "Clear Command History", "Clear", function()
		table.clear(history)
		histIdx = 1
		logCmd("History cleared", T.Sub)
	end)

	logCmd("Type 'cmds' to list every command.", T.Sub)
end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: SETTINGS
-- ====================================================
do
	local SetTab = AddTab("Settings", ICONS.Settings)
	local Ui = Section(SetTab, "Interface", "Menu behavior")
	Keybind(Ui, "Menu Toggle Key", S.MenuKey, function(k) S.MenuKey = k end)
	Toggle(Ui, "Show Notifications", true, function(v) S.Notify = v end)
	local Acc = Section(SetTab, "Account", "License information")
	RankLabel = InfoRow(Acc, "Rank: " .. rank)
	Button(Acc, "Copy Discord Link", "Copy", function() Hub.copyDiscord() end)
	Button(Acc, "Join Discord", "Join", function() Hub.joinDiscord() end)

	local Cfg = Section(SetTab, "Config", "Save and restore your toggles and sliders")
	if writefile and readfile and isfile then
		Button(Cfg, "Save Config", "Save", saveConfig)
		Button(Cfg, "Load Config", "Load", loadConfig)
	else
		InfoRow(Cfg, "Config files not supported by this environment")
	end

	local Help = Section(SetTab, "Controls", "Handy shortcuts")
	InfoRow(Help, "Menu key hides / shows the hub (X unloads everything)")
	InfoRow(Help, "Fly hotkey toggles flight (default Q)")
	InfoRow(Help, "Ctrl + Click = teleport  |  Alt + Click = delete part")
	InfoRow(Help, "Hold Left Shift = sprint  |  Right Mouse = freecam look")
	InfoRow(Help, "F = dash  |  Hold Z = zoom  (both rebindable)")
	local Danger = Section(SetTab, "Danger Zone", "Unload stops everything and restores your game")
	Button(Danger, "Unload Hub", "Unload", function() Unload() end)

	-- NEW: appearance + HUD ---------------------------------------------------
	local uiScale = new("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, Main)
	local hud = new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(270, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10), BackgroundColor3 = T.Side, BackgroundTransparency = 0.15, TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 12, Visible = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = "" }, EspGui)
	round(hud, 6)
	new("UIStroke", { Color = T.Accent, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, hud)
	local hudFps = 60
	bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function(dt) hudFps = hudFps * 0.9 + (1 / math.max(dt, 0.001)) * 0.1 end)
	task.spawn(function()
		while Running do
			if hud.Visible then
				pcall(function()
					local ok, ping = pcall(function() return Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue() end)
					ping = (ok and ping) and math.floor(ping) or math.floor(LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() * 2000)
					hud.Text = string.format("Solos Hub  |  %d FPS  |  %d ms  |  %s", math.floor(hudFps + 0.5), ping, os.date("%I:%M %p"))
				end)
			end
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)

	local Look = Section(SetTab, "Appearance & HUD", "Window scale, opacity, notifications and overlays")
	Slider(Look, "UI Scale %", 60, 140, 100, function(v) uiScale.Scale = v / 100 end, "uiscale")
	Slider(Look, "Window Opacity %", 30, 100, 100, function(v) Main.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - v / 100 end, "uiopacity")
	Slider(Look, "Toast Seconds", 1, 8, 3, function(v) S.ToastTime = v end, "toasttime")
	Toggle(Look, "Watermark HUD (FPS / ping / time)", false, function(v) hud.Visible = v end, nil, "hud")
	Button(Look, "Reset Menu Position", "Center", function()
		local sc = uiScale.Scale
		Main.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -Main.Size.X.Offset * sc / 2, 0.5, -Main.Size.Y.Offset * sc / 2)
	end)

	-- NEW (v3.14): UI sound effects ----------------------------------------------------------------
	do
		local canSfx = (Hub.ok and listfiles and (getcustomasset or getsynasset)) and true or false
		local Sec = Section(SetTab, "UI Sounds", "Drop .mp3 / .ogg files into workspace/" .. Hub.sounds .. " to replace button, toggle and slider sounds")
		if not canSfx then InfoRow(Sec, "This executor can't list files / load custom audio - sounds are unavailable") end
		Toggle(Sec, "Enable UI Sounds", Hub.sfx.enabled, function(v) Hub.sfx.enabled = v Hub.sfxSave() end)
		Toggle(Sec, "Sounds For Join / Leave Alerts", Hub.sfx.joinLeave, function(v) Hub.sfx.joinLeave = v Hub.sfxSave() end)
		Slider(Sec, "Master Volume %", 0, 100, math.floor(Hub.sfx.volume * 100 + 0.5), function(v) Hub.sfx.volume = v / 100 Hub.sfxSave() end)
		local LABELS = { click = "Buttons", toggle_on = "Toggle on", toggle_off = "Toggle off", slider = "Sliders", tab = "Tabs", hover = "Button hover", notify = "Notifications", error = "Errors / warnings" }
		local rows = {}
		for _, ev in ipairs(Hub.sfx.order) do rows[ev] = InfoRow(Sec, LABELS[ev] .. ": -") end
		local function refreshRows()
			for _, ev in ipairs(Hub.sfx.order) do
				local f = Hub.sfxFile(ev)
				rows[ev].Text = LABELS[ev] .. ": " .. (f or "silent (no file)")
				rows[ev].TextColor3 = f and T.Good or T.Sub
			end
		end
		refreshRows()
		Button(Sec, "Rescan Sounds Folder", "Rescan", function()
			local n = Hub.sfxScan()
			refreshRows()
			notify(n .. " sound file(s) found", n > 0 and T.Good or T.Warn)
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Test Sounds (plays each one)", "Play", function() Hub.sfxTest() end)
		Button(Sec, "Copy Sounds Folder Path", "Copy", function() copyText(Hub.sounds) end)
	end

	-- NEW: panic key ----------------------------------------------------------
	S.PanicKey = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
	Keybind(Ui, "Panic Key  (Backspace = none)", S.PanicKey, function(k) S.PanicKey = k end, true)
	bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
		if gp or BindingActive or not Unlocked or i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
		if S.PanicKey ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and i.KeyCode == S.PanicKey and S.Panic then S.Panic() end
	end)

	-- NEW: auto-save on unload / auto-load on start ---------------------------
	if writefile and readfile and isfile then
		Toggle(Cfg, "Auto-Save On Unload / Auto-Load On Start", false, function(v) S.AutoSave = v end, nil, "autosave")
		onUnload(function() if S.AutoSave then saveConfig() end end)
		task.spawn(function()
			while Running and not Unlocked do task.wait(0.5) end
			if not Running then return end
			task.wait(1)
			local ok, data = pcall(function() return isfile(CONFIG_FILE) and HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(CONFIG_FILE)) end)
			if ok and type(data) == "table" and data.toggles and data.toggles.autosave == true then loadConfig() end
		end)
	end

	-- NEW (v3.8): saved data folder
	do
		local Data = Section(SetTab, "Saved Data Folder", "Everything the hub saves lives in one folder")
		InfoRow(Data, "Folder: workspace/" .. Hub.dir .. (Hub.ok and "" or "   (folders unsupported - using file prefix)"))
		InfoRow(Data, "Files: config, key, servers, macros, notes, theme  |  Images: " .. Hub.themes .. "  |  Sounds: " .. Hub.sounds)
		Button(Data, "Copy Folder Path", "Copy", function() copyText(Hub.dir) end)
		Button(Data, "Forget Saved Key", "Forget", function()
			local p = Hub.path("key.txt")
			if delfile and isfile and isfile(p) then
				pcall(delfile, p)
				notify("Saved key deleted", T.Good)
			else
				notify("No saved key found", T.Warn)
			end
		end)
		local armed = false
		Button(Data, "Delete All Saved Data (press twice)", "Delete", function()
			if not armed then
				armed = true
				notify("Press again within 4 seconds to delete all saved data", T.Warn)
				task.delay(4, function() armed = false end)
				return
			end
			armed = false
			local n = 0
			for _, nm in ipairs({ "config.json", "key.txt", "servers.json", "last_server.json", "macros.json", "notes.txt", "theme.json", "ui.json", "chatlog.txt", "sounds.json", "keyprefs.json" }) do
				local p = Hub.path(nm)
				if delfile and isfile and isfile(p) then pcall(delfile, p) n = n + 1 end
			end
			notify("Deleted " .. n .. " file(s) (images and sound files are kept)", T.Good)
		end)
	end

end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: CHAT  (v3.5)
-- ====================================================
do
	local ChatTab = AddTab("Chat", ICONS.Chat)
	local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
	local logs, nextN, filterText, keywords = {}, 0, "", {}
	S.ChatStamps, S.ChatMention, S.ChatJoins = true, true, false
	local useNew = false
	pcall(function() useNew = (TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService) end)

	local Feed = Section(ChatTab, "Chat Log", "Messages from players in this server")
	local feedFrame = new("ScrollingFrame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 230), BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new() }, Feed)
	round(feedFrame, 8)
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, feedFrame)
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8) }, feedFrame)

	local function lineText(e) return (S.ChatStamps and ("[" .. e.t .. "] ") or "") .. e.name .. ": " .. e.text end
	local function matches(e)
		local f = filterText:lower()
		return f == "" or e.name:lower():find(f, 1, true) ~= nil or e.text:lower():find(f, 1, true) ~= nil
	end
	local function addLabel(e)
		new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = lineText(e), TextColor3 = e.system and T.Sub or (e.mention and T.Warn or Color3.fromRGB(200, 205, 210)), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextWrapped = true, LayoutOrder = e.n }, feedFrame)
		task.defer(function() if feedFrame.Parent then feedFrame.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 1e6) end end)
	end
	local function rebuild()
		for _, ch in ipairs(feedFrame:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then ch:Destroy() end end
		for _, e in ipairs(logs) do if matches(e) then addLabel(e) end end
	end
	local function push(e)
		table.insert(logs, e)
		if #logs > 300 then
			table.remove(logs, 1)
			rebuild()
		elseif matches(e) then
			addLabel(e)
		end
	end
	local function system(text)
		nextN = nextN + 1
		push({ n = nextN, t = os.date("%H:%M:%S"), name = "*", text = text, system = true })
	end
	local function onMessage(plr, text)
		nextN = nextN + 1
		local e = { n = nextN, t = os.date("%H:%M:%S"), name = plr.DisplayName, text = text, mention = false }
		local low = text:lower()
		if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
			if S.ChatMention and (low:find(LocalPlayer.Name:lower(), 1, true) or low:find(LocalPlayer.DisplayName:lower(), 1, true)) then
				e.mention = true
				notify("Mentioned by " .. plr.DisplayName, T.Warn)
			end
			for _, k in ipairs(keywords) do
				if low:find(k, 1, true) then
					e.mention = true
					notify("Keyword '" .. k .. "' from " .. plr.DisplayName, T.Warn)
					break
				end
			end
		end
		push(e)
	end
	if useNew then
		bind(TextChatService.MessageReceived, function(msg)
			local src = msg.TextSource
			local plr = src and Players:GetPlayerByUserId(src.UserId)
			if plr then onMessage(plr, msg.Text) end
		end)
	else
		local function hook(p) bind(p.Chatted, function(m) onMessage(p, m) end) end
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do hook(p) end
		bind(Players.PlayerAdded, hook)
	end
	bind(Players.PlayerAdded, function(p) if S.ChatJoins then system(p.DisplayName .. " joined") end end)
	bind(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(p) if S.ChatJoins then system(p.DisplayName .. " left") end end)

	local function sendChat(text)
		text = (text or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if text == "" then notify("Type a message first", T.Warn) return end
		local ok = false
		pcall(function()
			if useNew then
				local chans = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
				local ch = chans and chans:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
				if ch then ch:SendAsync(text) ok = true end
			else
				local ev = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
				local say = ev and ev:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")
				if say then say:FireServer(text, "All") ok = true end
			end
		end)
		if not ok then notify("Couldn't send - chat isn't available here", T.Warn) end
	end
	S.SendChat = sendChat

	local Ctl = Section(ChatTab, "Log Controls", "Filter, timestamps and exports")
	local fbox
	fbox = Input(Ctl, "Filter Messages", "name or text", "Clear", function() fbox.Text = "" end)
	bind(fbox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function() filterText = fbox.Text rebuild() end)
	Toggle(Ctl, "Show Timestamps", true, function(v) S.ChatStamps = v rebuild() end, nil, "chatstamps")
	Toggle(Ctl, "Log Joins / Leaves", false, function(v) S.ChatJoins = v end, nil, "chatjoins")
	Button(Ctl, "Copy Chat Log", "Copy", function()
		local lines = {}
		for _, e in ipairs(logs) do table.insert(lines, lineText(e)) end
		if #lines == 0 then notify("Chat log is empty", T.Warn) return end
		copyText(table.concat(lines, "\n"))
	end)
	if writefile then
		Button(Ctl, "Save Chat Log To File", "Save", function()
			local lines = {}
			for _, e in ipairs(logs) do table.insert(lines, lineText(e)) end
			local ok = pcall(writefile, Hub.path("chatlog.txt"), table.concat(lines, "\n"))
			notify(ok and "Saved chatlog.txt in the SolosHub folder" or "Couldn't save log", ok and T.Good or T.Bad)
		end)
	end
	Button(Ctl, "Clear Chat Log", "Clear", function() table.clear(logs) rebuild() end)

	local Alert = Section(ChatTab, "Alerts", "Get a notification when someone says something")
	Toggle(Alert, "Alert When My Name Is Mentioned", true, function(v) S.ChatMention = v end, nil, "chatmention")
	local kwRow = InfoRow(Alert, "Keywords: none")
	Input(Alert, "Add Keyword", "word to watch for", "Add", function(text)
		text = text:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if text == "" then return end
		table.insert(keywords, text)
		kwRow.Text = "Keywords: " .. table.concat(keywords, ", ")
	end)
	Button(Alert, "Clear Keywords", "Clear", function() table.clear(keywords) kwRow.Text = "Keywords: none" end)

	local Quick = Section(ChatTab, "Send Message", "Say something in the server chat")
	local msgBox
	msgBox = Input(Quick, "Message", "type a message", "Send", function(text) sendChat(text) msgBox.Text = "" end)
	for _, phrase in ipairs({ "gg", "brb", "back", "thanks!", "good luck" }) do
		Button(Quick, "Say: " .. phrase, "Send", function() sendChat(phrase) end)
	end
end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: MACROS  (v3.5)
-- ====================================================
do
	local MacroTab = AddTab("Macros", ICONS.Macros)
	local canFile = (writefile and readfile and isfile) and true or false
	local FILE = Hub.path("macros.json")
	local binds, autoexec, autoEnabled = {}, {}, false
	S.Binds = true

	if canFile and isfile(FILE) then
		local ok, d = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(FILE)) end)
		if ok and type(d) == "table" then
			for _, b in ipairs(d.binds or {}) do
				local ok2, kc = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[b.key] end)
				if ok2 and kc and type(b.cmd) == "string" then table.insert(binds, { key = kc, cmd = b.cmd }) end
			end
			for _, c in ipairs(d.autoexec or {}) do if type(c) == "string" then table.insert(autoexec, c) end end
			autoEnabled = d.auto == true
		end
	end
	local function save()
		if not canFile then return end
		local out = { binds = {}, autoexec = autoexec, auto = autoEnabled }
		for _, b in ipairs(binds) do table.insert(out.binds, { key = b.key.Name, cmd = b.cmd }) end
		pcall(function() writefile(FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(out)) end)
	end
	local function makeHolder(parent)
		local h = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, parent)
		new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, h)
		return h
	end
	local function listRows(holder, items, textOf, onDel)
		for _, ch in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("Frame") then ch:Destroy() end end
		for i, it in ipairs(items) do
			local row = Row(holder, 32)
			row.LayoutOrder = i
			Label(row, { Text = textOf(it), Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, 0), TextSize = 11, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
			local del = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0.5, -11), BackgroundColor3 = T.Bad, Text = "Del", TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11 }, row)
			round(del, 6)
			del.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Hub.play("click") table.remove(items, i) onDel() end)
		end
	end

	-- command key binds
	do
		local pendingKey = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
		local Sec = Section(MacroTab, "Command Key Binds", "Press a key to run any command from the command bar")
		Toggle(Sec, "Enable Key Binds", true, function(v) S.Binds = v end, nil, "binds")
		Keybind(Sec, "Key To Bind", pendingKey, function(k) pendingKey = k end, true)
		local holder
		local function rebuild()
			listRows(holder, binds, function(b) return b.key.Name .. "   ->   " .. b.cmd end, function() save() rebuild() end)
		end
		Input(Sec, "Command", "e.g. fly   or   speed 80", "Bind", function(text)
			text = text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
			if pendingKey == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then notify("Pick a key first", T.Warn) return end
			if text == "" then notify("Type a command first", T.Warn) return end
			table.insert(binds, { key = pendingKey, cmd = text })
			save()
			rebuild()
			notify("Bound " .. pendingKey.Name .. " -> " .. text, T.Good)
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Clear All Binds", "Clear", function() table.clear(binds) save() rebuild() end)
		holder = makeHolder(Sec)
		rebuild()
		bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
			if gp or BindingActive or not Unlocked or not S.Binds or i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
			for _, b in ipairs(binds) do
				if i.KeyCode == b.key and S.Run then pcall(S.Run, b.cmd) end
			end
		end)
	end

	-- autoexec
	do
		local Sec = Section(MacroTab, "Startup Commands", "Commands that run automatically after the hub loads")
		local holder
		local function rebuild()
			listRows(holder, autoexec, function(c) return c end, function() save() rebuild() end)
		end
		Toggle(Sec, "Run Startup Commands On Load", autoEnabled, function(v) autoEnabled = v save() end, nil, "autoexec")
		Input(Sec, "Add Command", "e.g. esp on", "Add", function(text)
			text = text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
			if text == "" then return end
			table.insert(autoexec, text)
			save()
			rebuild()
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Run Startup Commands Now", "Run", function()
			for _, c in ipairs(autoexec) do if S.Run then pcall(S.Run, c) end end
		end)
		holder = makeHolder(Sec)
		rebuild()
		task.spawn(function()
			while Running and not Unlocked do task.wait(0.5) end
			if not Running then return end
			task.wait(1.5)
			if autoEnabled and S.Run then
				for _, c in ipairs(autoexec) do
					if not Running then return end
					pcall(S.Run, c)
					task.wait(0.2)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	-- timed command
	do
		local rep = { cmd = "", sec = 10, on = false }
		local Sec = Section(MacroTab, "Repeat Command", "Run one command over and over on a timer")
		local cmdRow = InfoRow(Sec, "Command: none")
		Input(Sec, "Command To Repeat", "e.g. heal", "Set", function(text)
			rep.cmd = text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
			cmdRow.Text = "Command: " .. (rep.cmd == "" and "none" or rep.cmd)
		end)
		Slider(Sec, "Interval (sec)", 1, 300, 10, function(v) rep.sec = v end, "repsec")
		Toggle(Sec, "Repeat Command", false, function(v)
			if v and rep.cmd == "" then
				notify("Set a command first", T.Warn)
				C.repeatcmd.Set(false)
				return
			end
			rep.on = v
		end, nil, "repeatcmd")
		task.spawn(function()
			local acc = 0
			while Running do
				task.wait(0.25)
				if rep.on then
					acc = acc + 0.25
					if acc >= rep.sec then
						acc = 0
						pcall(S.Run, rep.cmd)
					end
				else
					acc = 0
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	-- path recorder
	do
		local path, recording, loopPath, playing = {}, false, false, false
		local Sec = Section(MacroTab, "Path Recorder", "Record where you walk, then replay it automatically")
		local lbl = InfoRow(Sec, "Recorded points: 0")
		local function refreshLbl() lbl.Text = "Recorded points: " .. #path .. (playing and "   (playing)" or "") end
		Toggle(Sec, "Record Path", false, function(v)
			recording = v
			if v then table.clear(path) end
			refreshLbl()
		end, nil, "rec")
		local acc = 0
		bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
			if not recording then acc = 0 return end
			acc = acc + dt
			if acc < 0.3 then return end
			acc = 0
			local r = getRoot()
			if not r then return end
			local last = path[#path]
			if not last or (r.Position - last).Magnitude > 3 then
				table.insert(path, r.Position)
				refreshLbl()
			end
		end)
		local function play()
			if #path == 0 then notify("Record a path first", T.Warn) return end
			if playing then return end
			playing = true
			refreshLbl()
			task.spawn(function()
				repeat
					for _, p in ipairs(path) do
						if not playing or not Running then break end
						local h = getHum()
						if not h then break end
						h:MoveTo(p)
						local t0 = os.clock()
						while playing and Running and os.clock() - t0 < 4 do
							local r = getRoot()
							if r and (r.Position - p).Magnitude < 4 then break end
							task.wait(0.05)
						end
					end
				until (not loopPath) or (not playing) or (not Running)
				playing = false
				if Running then refreshLbl() end
			end)
		end
		local function stop()
			playing = false
			local h, r = getHum(), getRoot()
			if h and r then h:MoveTo(r.Position) end
			refreshLbl()
		end
		S.PlayPath, S.StopPath = play, stop
		onUnload(function() playing = false end)
		Button(Sec, "Play Path", "Play", play)
		Button(Sec, "Stop Playback", "Stop", stop)
		Toggle(Sec, "Loop Playback", false, function(v) loopPath = v end, nil, "looppath")
		Button(Sec, "Clear Path", "Clear", function() table.clear(path) refreshLbl() end)
		Button(Sec, "Copy Path Points", "Copy", function()
			if #path == 0 then notify("Nothing recorded", T.Warn) return end
			local lines = {}
			for _, p in ipairs(path) do table.insert(lines, string.format("%.1f, %.1f, %.1f", p.X, p.Y, p.Z)) end
			copyText(table.concat(lines, "\n"))
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Save Path As Waypoints", "Save", function()
			if #path == 0 then notify("Nothing recorded", T.Warn) return end
			local step = math.max(1, math.ceil(#path / 20))
			local n = 0
			for i = 1, #path, step do
				n = n + 1
				table.insert(WP, { name = "Path " .. n, cf = CFrame.new(path[i]) })
			end
			rebuildWaypoints()
			notify("Saved " .. n .. " waypoints", T.Good)
		end)
	end
end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: EXPLORER  (v3.5)
-- ====================================================
do
	local ExpTab = AddTab("Explorer", ICONS.Explorer)
	local hoverOn, pinned, lastT = false, nil, nil
	local showResults
	local hl = new("Highlight", { FillTransparency = 0.7, OutlineTransparency = 0, FillColor = T.Accent, OutlineColor = T.Accent, DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop, Enabled = false }, EspGui)
	S.HoverHL, S.PinKey, S.CopyHoverKey = true, Enum.KeyCode.X, Enum.KeyCode.V

	local function posOf(inst)
		if inst:IsA("BasePart") then return inst.CFrame end
		if inst:IsA("Model") then return inst:GetPivot() end
		local p = inst:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BasePart")
		if p then return p.CFrame end
		local m = inst:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
		return m and m:GetPivot() or nil
	end

	-- hover inspector
	do
		local Sec = Section(ExpTab, "Hover Inspector", "Point at anything in the world to inspect it")
		Toggle(Sec, "Enable Hover Inspector", false, function(v)
			hoverOn = v
			if not v then hl.Enabled = false pinned = nil end
		end, nil, "hover")
		Toggle(Sec, "Highlight Hovered Part", true, function(v) S.HoverHL = v end, nil, "hoverhl")
		local rows = {
			Name = InfoRow(Sec, "Name: -"), Class = InfoRow(Sec, "Class: -"), Path = InfoRow(Sec, "Path: -"),
			Pos = InfoRow(Sec, "Position: -"), Size = InfoRow(Sec, "Size: -"), Dist = InfoRow(Sec, "Distance: -"), Info = InfoRow(Sec, "Details: -"),
		}
		rows.Path.TextTruncate, rows.Info.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		task.spawn(function()
			while Running do
				if hoverOn then
					pcall(function()
						local t = pinned or Mouse.Target
						if t and t.Parent then
							lastT = t
							rows.Name.Text = "Name: " .. t.Name .. (pinned and "   (pinned)" or "")
							rows.Class.Text = "Class: " .. t.ClassName
							rows.Path.Text = "Path: " .. t:GetFullName()
							if t:IsA("BasePart") then
								rows.Pos.Text = string.format("Position: %.1f, %.1f, %.1f", t.Position.X, t.Position.Y, t.Position.Z)
								rows.Size.Text = string.format("Size: %.1f x %.1f x %.1f", t.Size.X, t.Size.Y, t.Size.Z)
								rows.Info.Text = "Material: " .. t.Material.Name .. "  |  Anchored: " .. tostring(t.Anchored) .. "  |  CanCollide: " .. tostring(t.CanCollide)
								local r = getRoot()
								rows.Dist.Text = r and ("Distance: " .. math.floor((t.Position - r.Position).Magnitude) .. " studs") or "Distance: -"
							end
							if S.HoverHL and not t:IsA("Terrain") then hl.Adornee = t hl.Enabled = true else hl.Enabled = false end
						else
							hl.Enabled = false
						end
					end)
				end
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
		end)
		bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
			if gp or BindingActive or not Unlocked or not hoverOn or i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
			if i.KeyCode == S.PinKey then
				if pinned then
					pinned = nil
					notify("Unpinned")
				else
					pinned = Mouse.Target
					if pinned then notify("Pinned " .. pinned.Name) end
				end
			elseif i.KeyCode == S.CopyHoverKey then
				local t = pinned or Mouse.Target
				if t then copyText(t:GetFullName()) end
			end
		end)
		Keybind(Sec, "Pin / Unpin Hotkey", S.PinKey, function(k) S.PinKey = k end, true)
		Keybind(Sec, "Copy Path Hotkey", S.CopyHoverKey, function(k) S.CopyHoverKey = k end, true)
		Button(Sec, "Copy Inspected Path", "Copy", function() if lastT then copyText(lastT:GetFullName()) else notify("Nothing inspected yet", T.Warn) end end)
		Button(Sec, "Copy Inspected Position", "Copy", function()
			local cf = lastT and posOf(lastT)
			if cf then copyText(string.format("%.1f, %.1f, %.1f", cf.X, cf.Y, cf.Z)) else notify("Nothing inspected yet", T.Warn) end
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Teleport To Inspected Part", "Teleport", function()
			local cf = lastT and posOf(lastT)
			if cf then doTeleport(cf + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)) else notify("Nothing inspected yet", T.Warn) end
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Unpin", "Unpin", function() pinned = nil end)
		onUnload(function() hl.Enabled = false end)
	end

	-- finders
	local function collect(pred, labeler, limit)
		local items = {}
		local ch = getChar()
		for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if pred(d) and not (ch and d:IsDescendantOf(ch)) then
				local cf = posOf(d)
				if cf then table.insert(items, { label = labeler(d), cf = cf, path = d:GetFullName() }) end
				if #items >= (limit or 60) then break end
			end
		end
		return items
	end
	do
		local Sec = Section(ExpTab, "Object Finder", "Search the workspace and jump to what you find")
		Input(Sec, "Find By Name", "part or model name", "Search", function(text)
			text = text:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
			if text == "" then notify("Type a name first", T.Warn) return end
			showResults(collect(function(d) return (d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Model")) and d.Name:lower():find(text, 1, true) ~= nil end, function(d) return d.Name .. "  [" .. d.ClassName .. "]" end))
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Find Proximity Prompts", "Find", function()
			showResults(collect(function(d) return d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") end, function(d) return (d.ActionText ~= "" and d.ActionText or d.Name) .. "  [prompt]" end))
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Find Click Detectors", "Find", function()
			showResults(collect(function(d) return d:IsA("ClickDetector") end, function(d) return (d.Parent and d.Parent.Name or d.Name) .. "  [click]" end))
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Find Seats", "Find", function()
			showResults(collect(function(d) return d.ClassName == "Seat" or d.ClassName == "VehicleSeat" end, function(d) return d.Name .. "  [" .. d.ClassName .. "]" end))
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Find Spawn Points", "Find", function()
			showResults(collect(function(d) return d:IsA("SpawnLocation") end, function(d) return d.Name .. "  [spawn]" end))
		end)
	end
	do
		local Sec = Section(ExpTab, "Results", "Click Go to teleport, Copy for the full path")
		local list = new("ScrollingFrame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 210), BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new() }, Sec)
		round(list, 8)
		new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, list)
		new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4) }, list)
		function showResults(items)
			for _, ch in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("Frame") then ch:Destroy() end end
			if #items == 0 then notify("Nothing found", T.Warn) return end
			for i, it in ipairs(items) do
				local row = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = i }, list)
				round(row, 6)
				Label(row, { Text = it.label, Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 1, 0), TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Text, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
				local go = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -108, 0.5, -11), BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent, Text = "Go", TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11 }, row)
				round(go, 6)
				local cp = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -58, 0.5, -11), BackgroundColor3 = T.Btn, Text = "Copy", TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11 }, row)
				round(cp, 6)
				go.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Hub.play("click") doTeleport(it.cf + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)) end)
				cp.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Hub.play("click") copyText(it.path) end)
			end
			notify("Found " .. #items .. " result(s)", T.Good)
		end
	end

	-- interactables
	do
		local Sec = Section(ExpTab, "Interactables", "Proximity prompt helpers")
		local promptOrig = weak()
		local function setInstant(on)
			for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
					if on then
						if promptOrig[d] == nil then promptOrig[d] = d.HoldDuration end
						d.HoldDuration = 0
					elseif promptOrig[d] ~= nil then
						d.HoldDuration = promptOrig[d]
						promptOrig[d] = nil
					end
				end
			end
		end
		bind(Workspace.DescendantAdded, function(d)
			if S.InstantPrompts and d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
				if promptOrig[d] == nil then promptOrig[d] = d.HoldDuration end
				d.HoldDuration = 0
			end
		end)
		onUnload(function() setInstant(false) end)
		Toggle(Sec, "Instant Proximity Prompts (no hold)", false, function(v) S.InstantPrompts = v setInstant(v) end, nil, "instaprompt")
		Button(Sec, "Fire Nearest Prompt", "Fire", function()
			local root = getRoot()
			if not root then return end
			if not fireproximityprompt then notify("Not supported by this environment", T.Warn) return end
			local best, bd = nil, math.huge
			for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and d.Enabled then
					local cf = posOf(d)
					if cf then
						local dist = (cf.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
						if dist < bd then best, bd = d, dist end
					end
				end
			end
			if best then pcall(fireproximityprompt, best) notify("Fired prompt (" .. math.floor(bd) .. " studs away)") else notify("No prompts found", T.Warn) end
		end)
	end

	-- world scan
	do
		local Sec = Section(ExpTab, "World Scan", "Count what the map contains")
		local r = {}
		for _, k in ipairs({ "Parts", "Models", "Lights", "Sounds", "Prompts", "Seats", "Spawns", "Players" }) do r[k] = InfoRow(Sec, k .. ": -") end
		Button(Sec, "Scan World", "Scan", function()
			local c = { Parts = 0, Models = 0, Lights = 0, Sounds = 0, Prompts = 0, Seats = 0, Spawns = 0 }
			for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("Terrain") then
				elseif d:IsA("BasePart") then
					c.Parts = c.Parts + 1
					if d.ClassName == "Seat" or d.ClassName == "VehicleSeat" then c.Seats = c.Seats + 1 end
					if d:IsA("SpawnLocation") then c.Spawns = c.Spawns + 1 end
				elseif d:IsA("Model") then c.Models = c.Models + 1
				elseif d:IsA("Light") then c.Lights = c.Lights + 1
				elseif d:IsA("Sound") then c.Sounds = c.Sounds + 1
				elseif d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then c.Prompts = c.Prompts + 1 end
			end
			for k, v in pairs(c) do r[k].Text = k .. ": " .. v end
			r.Players.Text = "Players: " .. #Players:GetPlayers()
		end)
	end
end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: TOOLBOX  (v3.5)
-- ====================================================
do
	local BoxTab = AddTab("Toolbox", ICONS.Toolbox)
	local canFile = (writefile and readfile and isfile) and true or false
	local timers = { swOn = false, swStart = 0, swElapsed = 0, cdEnd = nil, breakOn = false, breakMin = 30, breakNext = 0 }
	local swLbl, cdLbl

	-- notepad
	do
		local NOTE_FILE = Hub.path("notes.txt")
		local Sec = Section(BoxTab, "Notepad", canFile and "Saved automatically when you click away" or "Notes last until you unload")
		local noteRow = Row(Sec, 150)
		local note = new("TextBox", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", PlaceholderText = "Write notes here...", PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 95, 100), TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, MultiLine = true, TextWrapped = true, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, ClearTextOnFocus = false }, noteRow)
		if canFile and isfile(NOTE_FILE) then
			local ok, txt = pcall(readfile, NOTE_FILE)
			if ok and type(txt) == "string" then note.Text = txt end
		end
		local function saveNote() if canFile then pcall(writefile, NOTE_FILE, note.Text) end end
		bind(note.FocusLost, saveNote)
		onUnload(saveNote)
		Button(Sec, "Copy Notes", "Copy", function() if note.Text ~= "" then copyText(note.Text) else notify("Notepad is empty", T.Warn) end end)
		Button(Sec, "Clear Notes", "Clear", function() note.Text = "" saveNote() end)
	end

	-- stopwatch + countdown
	do
		local Sec = Section(BoxTab, "Stopwatch & Timer", "Track time while you play")
		swLbl = InfoRow(Sec, "Stopwatch: 00:00.00")
		swLbl.Font, swLbl.TextSize, swLbl.TextColor3 = Enum.Font.GothamBold, 14, T.Text
		local lapRow = InfoRow(Sec, "Laps: none")
		local laps = {}
		local function elapsed() return timers.swElapsed + (timers.swOn and (os.clock() - timers.swStart) or 0) end
		Toggle(Sec, "Stopwatch Running", false, function(v)
			if v then timers.swStart = os.clock() else timers.swElapsed = elapsed() end
			timers.swOn = v
		end, nil, "stopwatch")
		Button(Sec, "Lap", "Lap", function()
			local e = elapsed()
			table.insert(laps, string.format("%02d:%05.2f", e // 60, e % 60))
			lapRow.Text = "Laps: " .. table.concat(laps, "  |  ", math.max(1, #laps - 3), #laps)
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Reset Stopwatch", "Reset", function()
			timers.swElapsed, timers.swStart = 0, os.clock()
			table.clear(laps)
			lapRow.Text = "Laps: none"
		end)
		cdLbl = InfoRow(Sec, "Countdown: not running")
		Input(Sec, "Countdown (seconds)", "e.g. 90", "Start", function(text)
			local n = tonumber(text)
			if not n or n <= 0 then notify("Enter a number of seconds", T.Warn) return end
			timers.cdEnd = os.clock() + n
			notify("Countdown started: " .. n .. "s")
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Cancel Countdown", "Cancel", function() timers.cdEnd = nil cdLbl.Text = "Countdown: not running" end)
		task.spawn(function()
			while Running do
				pcall(function()
					local e = elapsed()
					swLbl.Text = string.format("Stopwatch: %02d:%05.2f", e // 60, e % 60)
					if timers.cdEnd then
						local left = timers.cdEnd - os.clock()
						if left <= 0 then
							timers.cdEnd = nil
							cdLbl.Text = "Countdown: finished!"
							notify("Countdown finished!", T.Good)
						else
							cdLbl.Text = string.format("Countdown: %02d:%02d", left // 60, math.ceil(left % 60) % 60)
						end
					end
					if timers.breakOn and os.clock() >= timers.breakNext then
						timers.breakNext = os.clock() + timers.breakMin * 60
						notify("Time for a break! Stretch and drink some water.", T.Warn)
					end
				end)
				task.wait(0.05)
			end
		end)
	end

	-- break reminder
	do
		local Sec = Section(BoxTab, "Break Reminder", "A gentle nudge to take breaks")
		Toggle(Sec, "Remind Me To Take Breaks", false, function(v)
			timers.breakOn = v
			timers.breakNext = os.clock() + timers.breakMin * 60
		end, nil, "breakremind")
		Slider(Sec, "Every (minutes)", 5, 120, 30, function(v)
			timers.breakMin = v
			timers.breakNext = os.clock() + v * 60
		end, "breakmin")
	end

	-- calculator
	do
		local function evalExpr(s)
			s = s:gsub("%s+", "")
			if s == "" then error("empty expression") end
			local pos = 1
			local function peek() return s:sub(pos, pos) end
			local parseExpr
			local function parseAtom()
				local c = peek()
				if c == "(" then
					pos = pos + 1
					local v = parseExpr()
					if peek() ~= ")" then error("missing )") end
					pos = pos + 1
					return v
				elseif c == "-" then
					pos = pos + 1
					return -parseAtom()
				end
				local n = s:match("^%d*%.?%d+", pos)
				if not n then error("unexpected '" .. c .. "'") end
				pos = pos + #n
				return tonumber(n)
			end
			local function parsePow()
				local b = parseAtom()
				if peek() == "^" then pos = pos + 1 return b ^ parsePow() end
				return b
			end
			local function parseTerm()
				local v = parsePow()
				while peek() == "*" or peek() == "/" or peek() == "%" do
					local op = peek()
					pos = pos + 1
					local r = parsePow()
					if op == "*" then v = v * r elseif op == "/" then v = v / r else v = v % r end
				end
				return v
			end
			function parseExpr()
				local v = parseTerm()
				while peek() == "+" or peek() == "-" do
					local op = peek()
					pos = pos + 1
					local r = parseTerm()
					if op == "+" then v = v + r else v = v - r end
				end
				return v
			end
			local v = parseExpr()
			if pos <= #s then error("unexpected '" .. peek() .. "'") end
			return v
		end
		S.Calc = evalExpr
		local Sec = Section(BoxTab, "Calculator", "Supports + - * / % ^ and parentheses")
		local res = InfoRow(Sec, "Result: -")
		local lastResult
		Input(Sec, "Expression", "e.g. (12 + 8) * 3 / 4", "=", function(text)
			local ok, v = pcall(evalExpr, text)
			if ok then
				lastResult = v
				res.Text = "Result: " .. tostring(v)
			else
				res.Text = "Error: " .. tostring(v):gsub("^.-:%d+: ", "")
			end
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Copy Result", "Copy", function() if lastResult then copyText(tostring(lastResult)) else notify("Nothing calculated yet", T.Warn) end end)
	end

	-- randomizers
	do
		local Sec = Section(BoxTab, "Randomizers", "Dice, coin flips and picking players")
		local out = InfoRow(Sec, "Last result: -")
		local sides = 6
		Slider(Sec, "Dice Sides", 2, 100, 6, function(v) sides = v end, "dicesides")
		Button(Sec, "Roll Dice", "Roll", function()
			local n = math.random(sides)
			out.Text = "Last result: rolled " .. n .. " (d" .. sides .. ")"
			notify("Rolled " .. n .. " (d" .. sides .. ")")
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Flip A Coin", "Flip", function()
			local r = math.random(2) == 1 and "Heads" or "Tails"
			out.Text = "Last result: " .. r
			notify("Coin: " .. r)
		end)
		Button(Sec, "Pick Random Player (select them)", "Pick", function()
			local p = findPlayer("random")
			if p and p ~= LocalPlayer then
				S.Target = p
				refreshPlayerList()
				out.Text = "Last result: picked " .. p.DisplayName
				notify("Picked " .. p.DisplayName)
			else
				notify("No other players", T.Warn)
			end
		end)
	end
end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: THEME  (v3.5)
-- ====================================================
do
	local ThemeTab = AddTab("Theme", ICONS.Theme)
	local PRESETS = {
		{ "Solos Purple", 124, 77, 255 }, { "Blue", 0, 140, 255 }, { "Cyan", 0, 200, 210 }, { "Teal", 26, 188, 156 }, { "Green", 46, 204, 113 },
		{ "Lime", 163, 203, 56 }, { "Yellow", 241, 196, 15 }, { "Orange", 230, 126, 34 }, { "Red", 231, 76, 60 },
		{ "Pink", 255, 105, 180 }, { "Purple", 155, 89, 182 }, { "Indigo", 88, 101, 242 },
	}
	local FONT_NAMES = { "Gotham", "Ubuntu", "SourceSans", "Code" }
	local FONTS = { Enum.Font.Gotham, Enum.Font.Ubuntu, Enum.Font.SourceSans, Enum.Font.Code }
	local presetIdx, fontIdx = 1, 1

	local function near(a, b) return math.abs(a.R - b.R) < 0.002 and math.abs(a.G - b.G) < 0.002 and math.abs(a.B - b.B) < 0.002 end
	local function applyAccent(c)
		local old = T.Accent
		if old == c then return end
		for _, d in ipairs(Gui:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("GuiObject") then
				if near(d.BackgroundColor3, old) then d.BackgroundColor3 = c end
				if (d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox")) and near(d.TextColor3, old) then d.TextColor3 = c end
				if (d:IsA("ImageLabel") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and near(d.ImageColor3, old) then d.ImageColor3 = c end
			elseif d:IsA("UIStroke") and near(d.Color, old) then
				d.Color = c
			end
		end
		T.Accent = c
	end
	local function applyFont(idx)
		local oldF, newF = FONTS[fontIdx], FONTS[idx]
		for _, d in ipairs(Gui:GetDescendants()) do
			if (d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox")) and d.Font == oldF then d.Font = newF end
		end
		fontIdx = idx
	end
	local function setBright(p)
		local f = p / 100
		local function sc(r, g, b) return Color3.fromRGB(math.min(255, r * f), math.min(255, g * f), math.min(255, b * f)) end
		Main.BackgroundColor3 = sc(15, 16, 18)
		Side.BackgroundColor3 = sc(11, 12, 13)
		local filler = Side:FindFirstChildOfClass("Frame")
		if filler then filler.BackgroundColor3 = sc(11, 12, 13) end
	end
	local function setRadius(v)
		local a, b = Main:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner"), Side:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
		if a then a.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, v) end
		if b then b.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, v) end
		for _, bc in ipairs(S.BgCorners or {}) do bc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, v) end
	end
	local function fromSliders() applyAccent(Color3.fromRGB(C.accr.Get(), C.accg.Get(), C.accb.Get())) end

	local Acc = Section(ThemeTab, "Accent Color", "Recolors sliders, tabs, buttons and highlights live")
	local presetBtn
	presetBtn = Button(Acc, "Accent Preset", "Blue", function()
		presetIdx = presetIdx % #PRESETS + 1
		local p = PRESETS[presetIdx]
		C.accr.SetSilent(p[2]) C.accg.SetSilent(p[3]) C.accb.SetSilent(p[4])
		applyAccent(Color3.fromRGB(p[2], p[3], p[4]))
		presetBtn.Text = p[1]
	end)
	Slider(Acc, "Red", 20, 254, 0, function() fromSliders() end, "accr")
	Slider(Acc, "Green", 20, 254, 140, function() fromSliders() end, "accg")
	Slider(Acc, "Blue", 20, 254, 254, function() fromSliders() end, "accb")

	local Win = Section(ThemeTab, "Window Style", "Shape, brightness and font")
	local fontBtn
	fontBtn = Button(Win, "Interface Font (existing text)", "Gotham", function()
		local nextIdx = fontIdx % #FONTS + 1
		applyFont(nextIdx)
		fontBtn.Text = FONT_NAMES[nextIdx]
	end)
	Slider(Win, "Corner Radius", 0, 24, 14, setRadius, "radius")
	Slider(Win, "Background Brightness %", 50, 200, 100, setBright, "bright")

	local Lay = Section(ThemeTab, "Layout", "Keep long tabs tidy")
	Button(Lay, "Collapse All Sections", "Collapse", function() for _, f in ipairs(S.Sections) do f(true) end end)
	Button(Lay, "Expand All Sections", "Expand", function() for _, f in ipairs(S.Sections) do f(false) end end)
	Button(Lay, "Reset Theme To Default", "Reset", function()
		presetIdx = 1
		C.accr.SetSilent(0) C.accg.SetSilent(140) C.accb.SetSilent(254)
		applyAccent(Color3.fromRGB(0, 140, 255))
		presetBtn.Text = "Blue"
		applyFont(1)
		fontBtn.Text = "Gotham"
		C.radius.SetSilent(14) setRadius(14)
		C.bright.SetSilent(100) setBright(100)
		notify("Theme reset")
	end)

	-- NEW (v3.8): background image from workspace/SolosHub/themes ------------------------
	do
		local gca = getcustomasset or getsynasset
		local canList = (Hub.ok and listfiles and gca) and true or false
		local THEME_FILE = Hub.path("theme.json")
		local state = { image = "", opacity = 100, panel = 25, dark = 30, fit = 1, auto = true }
		local FITS = { { "Crop", Enum.ScaleType.Crop }, { "Fit", Enum.ScaleType.Fit }, { "Stretch", Enum.ScaleType.Stretch } }
		local EXT = { png = true, jpg = true, jpeg = true, webp = true }

		if Hub.ok and isfile(THEME_FILE) then
			local ok, d = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(THEME_FILE)) end)
			if ok and type(d) == "table" then
				for k, v in pairs(state) do if type(d[k]) == type(v) then state[k] = d[k] end end
			end
		end
		state.fit = math.clamp(math.floor(state.fit), 1, #FITS)

		local bg = new("ImageLabel", { Name = "ThemeBackground", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = "", ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop, ZIndex = 0, Visible = false, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, Main)
		local shade = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.7, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, bg)
		S.BgCorners = { new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14) }, bg), round(shade, 14) }
		onUnload(function() bg:Destroy() end)

		local function applyPanels()
			local t = (bg.Visible and state.panel or 0) / 100
			for _, d in ipairs(Gui:GetDescendants()) do
				if (d:IsA("Frame") or d:IsA("ScrollingFrame")) and near(d.BackgroundColor3, T.Row) then d.BackgroundTransparency = t end
			end
			Side.BackgroundTransparency = t
			local filler = Side:FindFirstChildOfClass("Frame")
			if filler then filler.BackgroundTransparency = (S.SideImgOn and 1 or t) end
		end
		local function applyLook()
			bg.ImageTransparency = 1 - state.opacity / 100
			bg.ScaleType = FITS[state.fit][2]
			shade.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - state.dark / 100
			applyPanels()
		end
		local function saveTheme()
			if Hub.ok then pcall(function() writefile(THEME_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(state)) end) end
		end
		local function setImage(name)
			if not name or name == "" then
				state.image = ""
				bg.Visible = false
				bg.Image = ""
				applyPanels()
				return true
			end
			local ok, asset = pcall(gca, Hub.themes .. "/" .. name)
			if not ok or not asset then notify("Couldn't load " .. name, T.Bad) return false end
			state.image = name
			bg.Image = asset
			bg.Visible = true
			applyLook()
			return true
		end

		local Sec = Section(ThemeTab, "Background Image", "Drop images into the themes folder, refresh, then press Apply")
		InfoRow(Sec, "Folder: workspace/" .. Hub.themes)
		Button(Sec, "Copy Themes Folder Path", "Copy", function() copyText(Hub.themes) end)
		local listFrame = new("ScrollingFrame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 170), BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new() }, Sec)
		round(listFrame, 8)
		new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, listFrame)
		new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4) }, listFrame)

		local refreshList
		local function note(text)
			Label(new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, listFrame), { Text = text, Position = UDim2.new(), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Sub })
		end
		function refreshList()
			for _, ch in ipairs(listFrame:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("Frame") then ch:Destroy() end end
			if not canList then note("This executor can't list files / load custom images") return end
			local ok, files = pcall(listfiles, Hub.themes)
			local names = {}
			if ok and type(files) == "table" then
				for _, p in ipairs(files) do
					local name = tostring(p):match("[^/\\]+$")
					local ext = name and name:lower():match("%.(%w+)$")
					if ext and EXT[ext] then table.insert(names, name) end
				end
			end
			table.sort(names, function(a, b) return a:lower() < b:lower() end)
			if #names == 0 then note("No images yet - add a .png or .jpg to the folder, then Refresh") return end
			for i, name in ipairs(names) do
				local active = (state.image == name)
				local row = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = i }, listFrame)
				round(row, 6)
				Label(row, { Text = (active and "✓  " or "") .. name, Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 1, 0), TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = active and T.Good or T.Text, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
				local b = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0.5, -11), BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent, Text = active and "Active" or "Apply", TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11 }, row)
				round(b, 6)
				b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					Hub.play("click")
					if setImage(name) then
						saveTheme()
						refreshList()
						notify("Background: " .. name, T.Good)
					end
				end)
			end
		end
		Button(Sec, "Refresh Image List", "Refresh", function() refreshList() end)
		Button(Sec, "Remove Background", "Remove", function()
			setImage("")
			saveTheme()
			refreshList()
			notify("Background removed")
		end)
		Slider(Sec, "Image Opacity %", 10, 100, state.opacity, function(v) state.opacity = v applyLook() saveTheme() end, "bgopacity")
		Slider(Sec, "Image Darkness %", 0, 90, state.dark, function(v) state.dark = v applyLook() saveTheme() end, "bgdark")
		Slider(Sec, "Panel Transparency %", 0, 80, state.panel, function(v) state.panel = v applyPanels() saveTheme() end, "bgpanel")
		local fitBtn
		fitBtn = Button(Sec, "Image Fit", FITS[state.fit][1], function()
			state.fit = state.fit % #FITS + 1
			fitBtn.Text = FITS[state.fit][1]
			applyLook()
			saveTheme()
		end)
		Toggle(Sec, "Restore Background On Start", state.auto, function(v) state.auto = v saveTheme() end, nil, "bgauto")

		refreshList()
		if canList and state.auto and state.image ~= "" then
			local ok, exists = pcall(isfile, Hub.themes .. "/" .. state.image)
			if ok and exists then setImage(state.image) refreshList() end
		end
		-- rows built after this point (dashboard favorites, etc.) pick up the transparency too
		task.spawn(function()
			while Running do
				task.wait(3)
				if bg.Visible then pcall(applyPanels) end
			end
		end)
	end


	-- NEW (v3.9): sidebar (tab bar) image, tab icon style, hover fix ---------------------------
	do
		local gca = getcustomasset or getsynasset
		local canList = (Hub.ok and listfiles and gca) and true or false
		local UI_FILE = Hub.path("ui.json")
		local st = { side = "", sideOpacity = 100, sideDark = 25, sideFit = 1, icons = 1 }
		local FITS = { { "Crop", Enum.ScaleType.Crop }, { "Fit", Enum.ScaleType.Fit }, { "Stretch", Enum.ScaleType.Stretch } }
		local ICON_MODES = { "Emoji", "Images", "None" }
		local EXT = { png = true, jpg = true, jpeg = true, webp = true }
		if Hub.ok and isfile(UI_FILE) then
			local ok, d = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(UI_FILE)) end)
			if ok and type(d) == "table" then
				for k, v in pairs(st) do if type(d[k]) == type(v) then st[k] = d[k] end end
			end
		end
		st.sideFit = math.clamp(math.floor(st.sideFit), 1, #FITS)
		st.icons = math.clamp(math.floor(st.icons), 1, #ICON_MODES)
		local function save()
			if Hub.ok then pcall(function() writefile(UI_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(st)) end) end
		end

		-- the image lives INSIDE the sidebar, behind the tab buttons but above the window background
		Side.ClipsDescendants = true
		local sbg = new("ImageLabel", { Name = "SidebarBackground", Size = UDim2.new(1, 14, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = "", ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop, ZIndex = 0, Visible = false, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, Side)
		local sShade = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.75, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, sbg)
		local c1, c2 = new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14) }, sbg), round(sShade, 14)
		if S.BgCorners then table.insert(S.BgCorners, c1) table.insert(S.BgCorners, c2) end
		onUnload(function() sbg:Destroy() end)

		local function fillerFrame() return Side:FindFirstChildOfClass("Frame") end
		local function applyLook()
			sbg.ImageTransparency = 1 - st.sideOpacity / 100
			sbg.ScaleType = FITS[st.sideFit][2]
			sShade.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - st.sideDark / 100
		end
		local function setSide(name)
			if not name or name == "" then
				st.side = ""
				sbg.Visible, sbg.Image = false, ""
				S.SideImgOn = false
				local f = fillerFrame()
				if f then f.BackgroundTransparency = Side.BackgroundTransparency end
				return true
			end
			local ok, asset = pcall(gca, Hub.themes .. "/" .. name)
			if not ok or not asset then notify("Couldn't load " .. name, T.Bad) return false end
			st.side = name
			sbg.Image = asset
			sbg.Visible = true
			S.SideImgOn = true
			local f = fillerFrame()
			if f then f.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end
			applyLook()
			return true
		end

		local Sec = Section(ThemeTab, "Sidebar Image", "Background for the tab bar - sits under the tab buttons, over the window background")
		InfoRow(Sec, "Uses the same folder: workspace/" .. Hub.themes)
		local listFrame = new("ScrollingFrame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 140), BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new() }, Sec)
		round(listFrame, 8)
		new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, listFrame)
		new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4) }, listFrame)
		local refreshList
		local function note(text)
			Label(new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, listFrame), { Text = text, Position = UDim2.new(), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Sub })
		end
		function refreshList()
			for _, ch in ipairs(listFrame:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("Frame") then ch:Destroy() end end
			if not canList then note("This executor can't list files / load custom images") return end
			local ok, files = pcall(listfiles, Hub.themes)
			local names = {}
			if ok and type(files) == "table" then
				for _, p in ipairs(files) do
					local name = tostring(p):match("[^/\\]+$")
					local ext = name and name:lower():match("%.(%w+)$")
					if ext and EXT[ext] then table.insert(names, name) end
				end
			end
			table.sort(names, function(a, b) return a:lower() < b:lower() end)
			if #names == 0 then note("No images yet - add a .png or .jpg to the folder, then Refresh") return end
			for i, name in ipairs(names) do
				local active = (st.side == name)
				local row = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = i }, listFrame)
				round(row, 6)
				Label(row, { Text = (active and "✓  " or "") .. name, Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 1, 0), TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = active and T.Good or T.Text, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
				local b = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0.5, -11), BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent, Text = active and "Active" or "Apply", TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11 }, row)
				round(b, 6)
				b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					Hub.play("click")
					if setSide(name) then
						save()
						refreshList()
						notify("Sidebar image: " .. name, T.Good)
					end
				end)
			end
		end
		Button(Sec, "Refresh Image List", "Refresh", function() refreshList() end)
		Button(Sec, "Remove Sidebar Image", "Remove", function() setSide("") save() refreshList() notify("Sidebar image removed") end)
		Slider(Sec, "Sidebar Opacity %", 10, 100, st.sideOpacity, function(v) st.sideOpacity = v applyLook() save() end, "sideop")
		Slider(Sec, "Sidebar Darkness %", 0, 90, st.sideDark, function(v) st.sideDark = v applyLook() save() end, "sidedark")
		local fitBtn
		fitBtn = Button(Sec, "Sidebar Image Fit", FITS[st.sideFit][1], function()
			st.sideFit = st.sideFit % #FITS + 1
			fitBtn.Text = FITS[st.sideFit][1]
			applyLook()
			save()
		end)

		-- startup restore
		applyLook()
		refreshList()
		if canList and st.side ~= "" then
			local ok, exists = pcall(isfile, Hub.themes .. "/" .. st.side)
			if ok and exists then setSide(st.side) refreshList() end
		end

		-- tab buttons: never stay half-highlighted after the mouse leaves
		task.spawn(function()
			while Running do
				pcall(function()
					for name, b in pairs(TabButtons) do
						if name ~= S.CurTab then
							b.Btn.BackgroundTransparency = (b.Btn.GuiState == Enum.GuiState.Hover) and 0.6 or 1
						end
					end
				end)
				task.wait(0.15)
			end
		end)
	end

end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: HUB CHAT  (v3.11) - private chat between people running Solos Hub in the same server
-- Roblox clients can't message each other directly, so this uses an opt-in relay (ntfy.sh by default)
-- through your executor's request function. Room = this server's JobId (+ optional passphrase).
-- ====================================================
do
	local HcTab = AddTab("Hub Chat", ICONS.Chat)
	local httpReq = (syn and syn.request) or http_request or request or (http and http.request) or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
	local HC = { on = false, pass = "", relay = "https://ntfy.sh", poll = 4, last = nil, fastUntil = 0, busy = false, backoff = 0, warned = false }
	local seenIds, roster, logs, myId = {}, {}, {}, HttpService:GenerateGUID(false):sub(1, 8)
	local nextN, lastSend = 0, 0

	local function hash(s, seed)
		local h = seed
		for i = 1, #s do h = (h * 33 + s:byte(i)) % 4294967296 end
		return string.format("%08x", h)
	end
	local function topic()
		local key = tostring(game.JobId ~= "" and game.JobId or game.PlaceId) .. "|" .. HC.pass
		return "solos" .. hash(key, 5381) .. hash(key:reverse(), 7919)
	end
	local function relayUrl() return (HC.relay:gsub("/+$", "")) end

	-- UI ------------------------------------------------
	local Status = Section(HcTab, "Hub Chat", "Chat with other Solos Hub users in this server")
	local statusRow = InfoRow(Status, "Status: off")
	InfoRow(Status, "Messages go through a 3rd-party relay in plain text. Not encrypted.")
	if not httpReq then
		statusRow.Text = "Status: unavailable (your executor has no request function)"
	end

	local Feed = Section(HcTab, "Messages", "Only people in this room see these")
	local feedFrame = new("ScrollingFrame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 200), BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new() }, Feed)
	round(feedFrame, 8)
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, feedFrame)
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8) }, feedFrame)

	local items = {}
	local function trim()
