		if #items > 150 then
			local o = table.remove(items, 1)
			if o.Parent then o:Destroy() end
		end
		task.defer(function() if feedFrame.Parent then feedFrame.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 1e6) end end)
	end
	local function addLine(text, color) -- small system line
		nextN = nextN + 1
		table.insert(items, new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text, TextColor3 = color or T.Sub, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextWrapped = true, LayoutOrder = nextN }, feedFrame))
		trim()
	end
	-- chat card: pfp, big display name, small @username underneath, then the message
	local function addMsg(userId, display, username, text, mine, tag)
		nextN = nextN + 1
		table.insert(logs, display .. " (@" .. username .. "): " .. text)
		if #logs > 200 then table.remove(logs, 1) end
		local card = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundColor3 = mine and T.Hover or T.Side, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = nextN }, feedFrame)
		round(card, 10)
		local pfp = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 38), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), BackgroundColor3 = T.Btn, BorderSizePixel = 0, Image = "" }, card)
		round(pfp, 19)
		Thumb.get(userId, function(img) if pfp.Parent then pfp.Image = img end end)
		local col = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(54, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, card)
		new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 0), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, col)
		new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 7), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 9) }, col)
		new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = display .. (tag and ("  " .. tag) or ""), TextColor3 = mine and T.Accent or T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 15, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, LayoutOrder = 1 }, col)
		new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 13), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "@" .. username, TextColor3 = T.Sub, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 10, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, LayoutOrder = 2 }, col)
		new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 228, 232), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 13, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, TextWrapped = true, LayoutOrder = 3 }, col)
		table.insert(items, card)
		trim()
	end

	local Online = Section(HcTab, "Online", "Hub users seen in the last minute")
	local rosterFrame = new("ScrollingFrame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 80), BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new() }, Online)
	round(rosterFrame, 8)
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 1), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, rosterFrame)
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8) }, rosterFrame)
	local function drawRoster()
		for _, ch in ipairs(rosterFrame:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then ch:Destroy() end end
		local names = {}
		for uid, r in pairs(roster) do
			if os.clock() - r.t > 75 or not Players:GetPlayerByUserId(uid) then roster[uid] = nil else table.insert(names, r.name) end
		end
		table.sort(names)
		for i, n in ipairs(names) do
			new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "• " .. n, TextColor3 = T.Good, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, LayoutOrder = i }, rosterFrame)
		end
	end

	-- relay ---------------------------------------------
	local function sendRaw(payload)
		if not httpReq then return false end
		payload.id, payload.u, payload.ts = payload.id or HttpService:GenerateGUID(false):sub(1, 8), LocalPlayer.UserId, os.time()
		local ok, res = pcall(httpReq, { Url = relayUrl() .. "/" .. topic(), Method = "POST", Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "text/plain" }, Body = HttpService:JSONEncode(payload) })
		if ok and res and res.StatusCode == 429 then HC.backoff = 30 end
		return ok and res and (res.StatusCode or 200) < 300, payload.id
	end

	local function handle(p)
		if type(p) ~= "table" or type(p.id) ~= "string" or seenIds[p.id] then return end
		if math.abs(os.time() - (tonumber(p.ts) or 0)) > 120 then return end
		seenIds[p.id] = true
		local uid = tonumber(p.u)
		local plr = uid and Players:GetPlayerByUserId(uid)
		if not plr or plr == LocalPlayer then return end
		if p.k == "m" and type(p.m) == "string" then
			local to = tonumber(p.to)
			if to and to ~= LocalPlayer.UserId then return end
			local text = p.m:gsub("%c", " "):sub(1, 200)
			roster[uid] = { name = plr.DisplayName, t = os.clock() }
			addMsg(plr.UserId, plr.DisplayName, plr.Name, text, false, to and "(whisper)" or nil)
			HC.fastUntil = os.clock() + 30
			if S.Notify then notify(plr.DisplayName .. ": " .. text:sub(1, 40), T.Accent) end
		elseif p.k == "p" then
			if not roster[uid] then addLine("* " .. plr.DisplayName .. " joined Hub Chat", T.Sub) end
			roster[uid] = { name = plr.DisplayName, t = os.clock() }
		elseif p.k == "bye" then
			roster[uid] = nil
		end
	end

	local function poll()
		if HC.busy or not HC.on or not httpReq then return end
		HC.busy = true
		local since = HC.last or "2m"
		local ok, res = pcall(httpReq, { Url = relayUrl() .. "/" .. topic() .. "/json?poll=1&since=" .. since, Method = "GET" })
		HC.busy = false
		if not (ok and res) then statusRow.Text = "Status: relay unreachable" return end
		if res.StatusCode == 429 then HC.backoff = 30 statusRow.Text = "Status: rate limited, slowing down" return end
		if (res.StatusCode or 200) >= 300 then statusRow.Text = "Status: relay error " .. tostring(res.StatusCode) .. " " .. tostring(res.Body):sub(1, 60):gsub("%c", " ") .. " @ " .. relayUrl() return end
		statusRow.Text = "Status: connected - room " .. topic():sub(6, 11)
		for line in tostring(res.Body):gmatch("[^\n]+") do
			local okd, ev = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, line)
			if okd and type(ev) == "table" and ev.event == "message" then
				HC.last = ev.id or HC.last
				local okp, p = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, tostring(ev.message))
				if okp then handle(p) end
			end
		end
	end

	local function announce() if HC.on then task.spawn(sendRaw, { k = "p" }) end end

	local function setOn(v)
		if v and not httpReq then notify("Your executor has no request function", T.Bad) return end
		HC.on = v
		if v then
			HC.last = nil
			addLine("* Connected. Others using the hub in this server will appear under Online.", T.Sub)
			statusRow.Text = "Status: connecting..."
			announce()
			task.spawn(poll)
		else
			statusRow.Text = "Status: off"
			table.clear(roster)
			drawRoster()
		end
	end
	Toggle(Status, "Enable Hub Chat (uses a relay server)", false, function(v) setOn(v) end)

	local function send(text)
		text = (text or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if text == "" then notify("Type a message first", T.Warn) return end
		if not HC.on then notify("Turn Hub Chat on first", T.Warn) return end
		if os.clock() - lastSend < 1 then notify("Slow down", T.Warn) return end
		lastSend = os.clock()
		text = text:sub(1, 200)
		local to, shown, to_name
		local who, rest = text:match("^/w%s+(%S+)%s+(.+)$")
		if who then
			local target = findPlayer(who)
			if not target or not roster[target.UserId] then notify("That player isn't in Hub Chat", T.Warn) return end
			to, text, shown, to_name = target.UserId, rest, true, target.DisplayName
		end
		local id = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false):sub(1, 8)
		seenIds[id] = true
		addMsg(LocalPlayer.UserId, LocalPlayer.DisplayName, LocalPlayer.Name, text, true, shown and ("(whisper to " .. to_name .. ")") or nil)
		HC.fastUntil = os.clock() + 30
		task.spawn(function()
			if not sendRaw({ k = "m", m = text, to = to, id = id }) then notify("Message may not have sent", T.Warn) end
			HC.fastUntil = os.clock() + 30
		end)
	end
	local msgBox
	msgBox = Input(Feed, "Message", "text, or /w name text", "Send", function(text) send(text) msgBox.Text = "" end)

	local Opt = Section(HcTab, "Room Settings", "Everyone in the same server joins the same room automatically")
	local passBox
	passBox = Input(Opt, "Passphrase", "optional, must match", "Set", function(text)
		HC.pass = text
		HC.last = nil
		table.clear(roster)
		drawRoster()
		notify(text == "" and "Public room (this server)" or "Passphrase room set", T.Good)
		announce()
	end)
	local relayBox
	relayBox = Input(Opt, "Relay URL", "https://ntfy.sh", "Set", function(text)
		text = text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if text:match("^https?://") then HC.relay = text HC.last = nil notify("Relay set", T.Good) else notify("Must start with http(s)://", T.Warn) end
	end)
	Slider(Opt, "Idle Poll Seconds", 2, 15, 4, function(v) HC.poll = v end)
	Button(Opt, "Copy Chat Log", "Copy", function()
		if #logs == 0 then notify("Nothing to copy", T.Warn) return end
		copyText(table.concat(logs, "\n"))
	end)
	Button(Opt, "Clear Messages", "Clear", function()
		table.clear(logs)
		for _, ch in ipairs(feedFrame:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("GuiObject") then ch:Destroy() end end
		table.clear(items)
	end)

	-- loop + cleanup
	task.spawn(function()
		local pollAcc, hbAcc = 0, 0
		while Running do
			task.wait(0.5)
			if HC.on then
				if HC.backoff > 0 then
					HC.backoff = HC.backoff - 0.5
				else
					pollAcc, hbAcc = pollAcc + 0.5, hbAcc + 0.5
					if pollAcc >= ((os.clock() < (HC.fastUntil or 0)) and 1.5 or HC.poll) then pollAcc = 0 task.spawn(poll) end
					if hbAcc >= 30 then hbAcc = 0 announce() end
				end
				if math.floor(os.clock()) % 2 == 0 then drawRoster() end
			end
		end
	end)
	onUnload(function()
		if HC.on then
			HC.on = false
			task.spawn(sendRaw, { k = "bye" })
		end
	end)
end

-- ====================================================
-- TAB: COMBAT  (v3.13) - hitbox, fling, tool and survival features
-- ====================================================
do
	local CombatTab = AddTab("Combat", ICONS.Esp)
	for _, id in ipairs({ "touchfling", "camlock", "facetarget", "hitbox", "headhitbox", "reach" }) do CONFIG_SKIP[id] = true end

	local X = {
		Hitbox = false, HeadBox = false, TeamCheck = false, Size = 10, Trans = 0.7,
		Fling = false, FlingPower = 10000, FlingTime = 3,
		Swing = false, CPS = 10, Reach = false, ReachSize = 15,
		CamLock = false, Smooth = 30, FaceTarget = false,
		NoRag = false, NoSit = false, AntiKB = false,
		DashKey = Enum.KeyCode.F, DashPower = 80, DashCool = 0, DashUntil = 0,
	}

	-- ---------------------------------------------------- hitbox expander
	local hb = weak()
	local function hbApply(part)
		if not hb[part] then hb[part] = { part.Size, part.Transparency, part.CanCollide, part.Massless } end
		local size = Vector3.new(X.Size, X.Size, X.Size)
		if part.Size ~= size then part.Size = size end
		part.Transparency = X.Trans
		part.CanCollide = false
		part.Massless = true
	end
	local function hbRestorePart(part)
		local o = hb[part]
		if not o then return end
		hb[part] = nil
		if part.Parent then
			part.Size, part.Transparency, part.CanCollide, part.Massless = o[1], o[2], o[3], o[4]
		end
	end
	local function hbRestoreAll()
		for part in pairs(hb) do hbRestorePart(part) end
	end
	bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
		if not X.Hitbox and not X.HeadBox and next(hb) == nil then return end
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= LocalPlayer then
				local ch = p.Character
				local skip = X.TeamCheck and S.SameTeam(p)
				local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local head = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("Head")
				if root then
					if X.Hitbox and not skip then hbApply(root) else hbRestorePart(root) end
				end
				if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then
					if X.HeadBox and not skip then hbApply(head) else hbRestorePart(head) end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	onUnload(hbRestoreAll)

	-- ---------------------------------------------------- fling
	task.spawn(function() -- touch fling: spike velocity for one physics step, then restore it
		while Running do
			if X.Fling then
				local r = getRoot()
				if r then
					local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity
					r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = v * X.FlingPower + Vector3.new(0, X.FlingPower, 0)
					RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
					r = getRoot()
					if r then r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = v end
				end
			end
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
	end)

	local flinging = false
	local function flingTarget(p)
		if flinging then notify("Already flinging", T.Warn) return end
		local r = getRoot()
		local tr = p and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not r or not tr then notify("Need a character and a target", T.Warn) return end
		flinging = true
		local home = r.CFrame
		notify("Flinging " .. p.DisplayName .. "...", T.Warn)
		task.spawn(function()
			local t0 = os.clock()
			while Running and os.clock() - t0 < X.FlingTime do
				local r2 = getRoot()
				local tr2 = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not r2 or not tr2 then break end
				r2.CFrame = tr2.CFrame
				r2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(X.FlingPower, X.FlingPower, X.FlingPower)
				r2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, X.FlingPower, 0)
				RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			end
			local r3 = getRoot()
			if r3 then
				r3.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				r3.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				r3.CFrame = home
			end
			flinging = false
		end)
	end

	-- ---------------------------------------------------- auto swing, tool reach
	task.spawn(function()
		while Running do
			if X.Swing and not UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then
				local ch = getChar()
				local tool = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
				if tool then pcall(function() tool:Activate() end) end
				task.wait(1 / math.max(X.CPS, 1))
			else
				task.wait(0.2)
			end
		end
	end)

	local reachOrig = weak()
	local function restoreReach(handle)
		local o = reachOrig[handle]
		if o then
			reachOrig[handle] = nil
			if handle.Parent then handle.Size, handle.Massless = o[1], o[2] end
		end
	end
	bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
		local ch = getChar()
		for handle in pairs(reachOrig) do
			if not X.Reach or not handle.Parent or handle.Parent.Parent ~= ch then restoreReach(handle) end
		end
		if X.Reach and ch then
			local tool = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
			local handle = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
			if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then
				if not reachOrig[handle] then reachOrig[handle] = { handle.Size, handle.Massless } end
				handle.Massless = true
				handle.Size = Vector3.new(X.ReachSize, X.ReachSize, X.ReachSize)
			end
		end
	end)
	onUnload(function() for handle in pairs(reachOrig) do restoreReach(handle) end end)

	-- ---------------------------------------------------- camera lock + face target
	bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
		if S.Freecam then return end
		local t = S.Target
		local ch = t and t.Parent and t.Character
		if X.CamLock and ch then
			local head = ch:FindFirstChild("Head") or ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if head then
				local c = cam()
				local goal = CFrame.lookAt(c.CFrame.Position, head.Position)
				c.CFrame = c.CFrame:Lerp(goal, math.clamp(X.Smooth / 100, 0.01, 1))
			end
		end
		if X.FaceTarget and ch then
			local tr, r = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), getRoot()
			if tr and r then
				r.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(r.Position, Vector3.new(tr.Position.X, r.Position.Y, tr.Position.Z))
			end
		end
	end)

	-- ---------------------------------------------------- survival: no ragdoll, anti sit, anti knockback
	local ragApplied, sitApplied = false, false
	bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
		local h, r = getHum(), getRoot()
		if not h or h.Health <= 0 then return end
		if X.NoRag then
			ragApplied = true
			h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
			h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
			local st = h:GetState()
			if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
				h:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
			end
			if h.PlatformStand and not S.Fly then h.PlatformStand = false end
		elseif ragApplied then
			ragApplied = false
			h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true)
			h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
		end
		if X.NoSit then
			sitApplied = true
			h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
			if h.Sit then h.Sit = false end
		elseif sitApplied then
			sitApplied = false
			h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, true)
		end
		if X.AntiKB and r and not S.Fly and not X.Fling and os.clock() > X.DashUntil then
			local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			if Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z).Magnitude > h.WalkSpeed + 60 then
				r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, v.Y, 0)
			end
		end
	end)
	onUnload(function()
		local h = getHum()
		if h then
			h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true)
			h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
			h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, true)
		end
	end)

	-- ---------------------------------------------------- dash
	bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
		if gp or BindingActive or not Unlocked or i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
		if X.DashKey ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and i.KeyCode == X.DashKey and os.clock() > X.DashCool then
			local r = getRoot()
			if not r then return end
			X.DashCool = os.clock() + 0.5
			X.DashUntil = os.clock() + 0.35
			local look = r.CFrame.LookVector
			local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
			if flat.Magnitude > 0.01 then
				r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = flat.Unit * X.DashPower + Vector3.new(0, r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
			end
		end
	end)

	-- ---------------------------------------------------- UI
	local HbSec = Section(CombatTab, "Hitbox Expander", "Makes other players' hitboxes bigger on your client (only helps in games that trust client-side hits)")
	Toggle(HbSec, "Expand Body Hitbox", false, function(v) X.Hitbox = v end, nil, "hitbox")
	Toggle(HbSec, "Expand Head Hitbox", false, function(v) X.HeadBox = v end, nil, "headhitbox")
	Slider(HbSec, "Hitbox Size", 2, 50, 10, function(v) X.Size = v end, "hbsize")
	Slider(HbSec, "Hitbox Transparency %", 0, 100, 70, function(v) X.Trans = v / 100 end, "hbtrans")
	Toggle(HbSec, "Skip Teammates", false, function(v) X.TeamCheck = v end, nil, "hbteam")

	local FlSec = Section(CombatTab, "Fling", "Touch fling and targeted fling (you may get flung too - use Anti-Void)")
	Toggle(FlSec, "Touch Fling (walk into people)", false, function(v) X.Fling = v end, "Premium User", "touchfling")
	Slider(FlSec, "Fling Power", 1000, 50000, 10000, function(v) X.FlingPower = v end, "flingpower")
	Slider(FlSec, "Fling Duration (sec)", 1, 10, 3, function(v) X.FlingTime = v end, "flingtime")
	Button(FlSec, "Fling Selected Player", "Fling", function()
		if S.Target and S.Target.Parent then flingTarget(S.Target) else notify("Select a player first", T.Warn) end
	end, "Premium User")
	Button(FlSec, "Fling Nearest Player", "Fling", function()
		local p = nearestPlayer()
		if p then flingTarget(p) else notify("No players nearby", T.Warn) end
	end, "Premium User")

	local ToolSec = Section(CombatTab, "Tool Helpers", "Auto swing, longer reach, tool cycling and camera lock")
	Toggle(ToolSec, "Auto Swing / Activate Held Tool", false, function(v) X.Swing = v end, nil, "autoswing")
	Slider(ToolSec, "Clicks Per Second", 1, 30, 10, function(v) X.CPS = v end, "swingcps")
	Toggle(ToolSec, "Tool Reach (bigger handle)", false, function(v) X.Reach = v end, nil, "reach")
	Slider(ToolSec, "Reach Size", 4, 50, 15, function(v) X.ReachSize = v end, "reachsize")
	local toolIdx = 0
	Button(ToolSec, "Cycle Held Tool", "Next", function()
		local h, bp = getHum(), LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
		if not h or not bp then return end
		local list = {}
		for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do if t:IsA("Tool") then table.insert(list, t) end end
		if #list == 0 then notify("No tools in your backpack", T.Warn) return end
		toolIdx = toolIdx % #list + 1
		h:EquipTool(list[toolIdx])
	end)
	Button(ToolSec, "Unequip Tool", "Unequip", function() local h = getHum() if h then h:UnequipTools() end end)
	Toggle(ToolSec, "Camera Lock On Selected Player", false, function(v) X.CamLock = v end, "Premium User", "camlock")
	Slider(ToolSec, "Camera Lock Smoothness %", 5, 100, 30, function(v) X.Smooth = v end, "camsmooth")
	Toggle(ToolSec, "Always Face Selected Player", false, function(v) X.FaceTarget = v end, nil, "facetarget")

	local SurvSec = Section(CombatTab, "Survival & Movement", "Stay on your feet, resist knockback, dash")
	Toggle(SurvSec, "No Ragdoll / Stun Recovery", false, function(v) X.NoRag = v end, nil, "noragdoll")
	Toggle(SurvSec, "Anti Sit", false, function(v) X.NoSit = v end, nil, "antisit")
	Toggle(SurvSec, "Anti Knockback", false, function(v) X.AntiKB = v end, nil, "antikb")
	Keybind(SurvSec, "Dash Hotkey  (Backspace = none)", X.DashKey, function(k) X.DashKey = k end, true)
	Slider(SurvSec, "Dash Power", 20, 250, 80, function(v) X.DashPower = v end, "dashpower")
end

-- ====================================================
-- GUI ORGANIZATION  (v3.5): favorites, top bar info, grouped sidebar
-- ====================================================
do
	-- dashboard favorites (mirrors of the most-used toggles, kept in sync)
	local page = Tabs.Dashboard
	local Fav = Section(page, "Favorites", "Quick toggles for your most-used features (synced with their tabs)")
	local mirrors = {}
	for _, f in ipairs({ { "Fly", "fly" }, { "Noclip", "noclip" }, { "Walk Speed", "speed" }, { "Infinite Jump", "infjump" }, { "ESP", "esp" }, { "Fullbright", "fullbright" }, { "Crosshair", "crosshair" }, { "Anti-AFK", "antiafk" } }) do
		local id = f[2]
		mirrors[id] = Toggle(Fav, f[1], false, function(v)
			local o = C[id]
			if o and o.Get() ~= v then o.Fire(v) end
		end)
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		while Running do
			pcall(function()
				for id, m in pairs(mirrors) do
					local o = C[id]
					if o and m.Get() ~= o.Get() then m.Set(o.Get()) end
				end
			end)
			task.wait(0.4)
		end
	end)
	local n = 0
	for _, ch in ipairs(page:GetChildren()) do
		if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch ~= Fav then n = n + 10 ch.LayoutOrder = n end
	end
	Fav.LayoutOrder = 15

	-- live fps / ping in the top bar
	local tfps = 60
	bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function(dt) tfps = tfps * 0.9 + (1 / math.max(dt, 0.001)) * 0.1 end)
	local topInfo = Label(TopBar, { Text = "", Size = UDim2.new(0, 170, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -250, 0, 0), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Sub, ZIndex = 11 })
	task.spawn(function()
		while Running do
			pcall(function()
				local ok, p = pcall(function() return Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue() end)
				p = (ok and p) and math.floor(p) or math.floor(LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() * 2000)
				topInfo.Text = math.floor(tfps + 0.5) .. " FPS  •  " .. p .. " ms"
			end)
			task.wait(1)
		end
	end)

	-- ================= universal categories (reference layout): sidebar category -> sub-nav -> feature cards =================
	do
		local function adopt(P, name, tab, icon, sub)
			if Tabs[tab] then P.Adopt(name, Tabs[tab], icon, sub, tab) end
		end
		local function take(P, name, tab, titles, icon, sub)
			if Tabs[tab] then P.Take(name, Tabs[tab], titles, icon, sub, tab) end
		end
		local P = Hub.MergePanel("Combat", "attack", "Dominate with powerful combat features.")
		adopt(P, "Hitbox", "Combat", "aimbot", "Expand hitboxes and tune your aim helpers.")
		take(P, "Fling", "Combat", { "Fling" }, "attack", "Fling players off their feet.")
		take(P, "Helpers", "Combat", { "Tool Helpers" }, "magnet", "Handy helpers for tools and swings.")
		take(P, "Survival", "Combat", { "Survival & Movement" }, "godmode", "Stay alive and stay mobile.")

		P = Hub.MergePanel("Movement", "walkspeed", "Move faster, higher and further.")
		take(P, "Movement", "Player", { "Movement", "Movement Tools", "Void Hold" }, "walkspeed", "Speed, jump and ground movement.")
		take(P, "Flight", "Player", { "Flight" }, "fly", "Fly around the map.")

		P = Hub.MergePanel("Visuals", "esp", "See more with ESP, camera and world options.")
		adopt(P, "ESP", "ESP", "esp", "Players, boxes, names and more.")
		adopt(P, "View", "Visuals", "show", "Camera and crosshair options.")
		adopt(P, "World", "World", "night", "Lighting and physics.")

		P = Hub.MergePanel("Player", "player", "Your character and the players around you.")
		adopt(P, "Character", "Player", "player", "Character tweaks and utilities.")
		adopt(P, "Players", "Players", "players", "Select, inspect and interact with players.")

		P = Hub.MergePanel("Teleports", "teleports", "Instantly go to different locations.")
		adopt(P, "Teleports", "Teleports", "teleports", "Jump to players, spots and coordinates.")
		take(P, "Waypoints", "Teleports", { "Waypoints", "Quick Slots", "Waypoint Tour & Hotkeys" }, "pin", "Save places and jump between them.")

		P = Hub.MergePanel("Misc", "espbox", "Tools, servers, commands and utilities.")
		adopt(P, "Tools", "Tools", "magnet", "Performance and quality-of-life tools.")
		adopt(P, "Server", "Servers", "espnames", "Server info, hopping and the server browser.")
		adopt(P, "Commands", "Commands", "logs", "Run commands and read the output.")
		adopt(P, "Macros", "Macros", "active", "Key binds, startup and repeat commands.")
		adopt(P, "Explorer", "Explorer", "search", "Inspect and find objects in the game.")
		adopt(P, "Toolbox", "Toolbox", "loot", "Notepad, timers and calculators.")
		adopt(P, "Chat", "Chat", "notifications", "Chat log, alerts and messages.")

		P = Hub.MergePanel("Settings", "settings", "Customize your experience.")
		adopt(P, "General", "Settings", "settings", "Interface, controls and sounds.")
		take(P, "Account", "Settings", { "Account", "Config", "Saved Data Folder", "Danger Zone" }, "player", "Account, saved config and data.")
		adopt(P, "Theme", "Theme", "color", "Accent color, window style and backgrounds.")
		adopt(P, "Hub Chat", "Hub Chat", "discord", "Chat with other Solos Hub users.")
	end
	bind(Hub.FootBtn.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("tab") selectTab("Settings") end)

	-- sidebar order (flat list like the reference)
	local groups = {
		{ "OVERVIEW", { "Dashboard" } },
		{ "FEATURES", { "Combat", "Movement", "Visuals", "Player", "Teleports" } },
		{ "MORE", { "Misc", "Settings" } },
	}
	-- everything below this divider is global (works in every game); the game's own tab sits above it
	local uni = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = 0 }, NavScroll)
	new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 3), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, uni)
	Label(uni, { Text = "UNIVERSAL", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 7), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = T.Accent })
	local order = 0
	for _, g in ipairs(groups) do
		order = order + 1
		for _, name in ipairs(g[2]) do
			order = order + 1
			local b = TabButtons[name]
			if b then b.Btn.LayoutOrder = order end
		end
	end
end

-- ====================================================
-- GLOBAL SEARCH  (v3.6): find tabs + features, jump to them, highlight the feature
-- ====================================================
do
	local sbRow = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 28), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 42), BackgroundColor3 = T.Row, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, Side)
	round(sbRow, 7)
	local sbStroke = new("UIStroke", { Color = T.Hover, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, sbRow)
	local sbox = new("TextBox", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", PlaceholderText = "Search tabs & features...", PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 95, 100), TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left }, sbRow)
	local clr = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -25, 0.5, -11), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "✕", TextColor3 = T.Sub, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, Visible = false }, sbRow)
	Hub.Icons.button(clr, "close_l", "X", 12, Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 120))

	local drop = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(350, 272), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 74), BackgroundColor3 = T.Side, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 40 }, Main)
	round(drop, 10)
	new("UIStroke", { Color = T.Hover, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, drop)
	local hdr = Label(drop, { Text = "", Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4), TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = T.Sub })
	local list = new("ScrollingFrame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -30), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 28), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new() }, drop)
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, list)
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 2), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 2) }, list)

	-- index every feature row (toggle / slider / button / input / keybind) and section title
	local index = {}
	local function buildIndex()
		table.clear(index)
		local n = 0
		-- pages to index: plain tabs (Dashboard) + every visible sub-page of every panel
		local jobs = {}
		for tabName, page in pairs(Tabs) do
			if not (S.TabOwner and S.TabOwner[tabName]) then
				local tb = TabButtons[tabName]
				if not tb or tb.Btn.Visible then table.insert(jobs, { page = page, tab = tabName, order = tb and tb.Btn.LayoutOrder or 99 }) end
			end
		end
		for title, pn in pairs(S.Panels or {}) do
			local tb = TabButtons[title]
			if tb and tb.Btn.Visible then
				for sname, f in pairs(pn.Subs) do
					local it = pn.Items[sname]
					if it and it.Btn.Visible then table.insert(jobs, { page = f, tab = title, sub = sname, order = tb.Btn.LayoutOrder }) end
				end
			end
		end
		for _, job in ipairs(jobs) do
			local page, tabName, tabOrder = job.page, job.tab, job.order
			for _, d in ipairs(page:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("TextLabel") and not d:GetAttribute("SolosDesc") and d.Text ~= "" and d.Text ~= "▼" and d.Text ~= "▶" and not d.Text:match("^%-?[%d%.]+%%?$") then
					local par = d.Parent
					local sec = par and par.Parent
					local grp
					if sec and sec:GetAttribute("SolosGroup") then grp = sec sec = sec.Parent end
					if sec and sec.Parent == page and sec:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
						and ((par:IsA("Frame") and (par.BackgroundTransparency < 1 or par:GetAttribute("SolosRow")) and par.Size.Y.Offset ~= 30) or par:IsA("TextButton")) then
						local head = sec:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextButton")
						local headLbl = head and head:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
						local secTitle = headLbl and headLbl.Text or ""
						if secTitle ~= "Favorites" then
							n = n + 1
							table.insert(index, {
								kind = par:IsA("TextButton") and "section" or "feature",
								text = d.Text:gsub("%s+%[%a+%]$", ""),
								lower = d.Text:lower(), tab = tabName, tabOrder = tabOrder, sec = sec,
								secTitle = secTitle, target = par, seq = n, sub = job.sub, group = grp, page = page,
							})
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local activeHl
	local function highlight(t)
		if activeHl then activeHl:Destroy() end
		local st = new("UIStroke", { Color = T.Warn, Thickness = 2, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, t)
		activeHl = st
		task.spawn(function()
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				if not st.Parent then return end
				TweenService:Create(st, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { Thickness = 4 }):Play()
				task.wait(0.35)
				TweenService:Create(st, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { Thickness = 1.5 }):Play()
				task.wait(0.35)
			end
			if not st.Parent then return end
			TweenService:Create(st, TweenInfo.new(0.4), { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
			task.wait(0.4)
			if st.Parent then st:Destroy() end
		end)
	end

	local firstHit
	local function jump(e)
		sbox:ReleaseFocus()
		sbox.Text = ""
		drop.Visible = false
		selectTab(e.tab)
		if e.sub and S.Panels and S.Panels[e.tab] then S.Panels[e.tab].Select(e.sub) end
		if e.kind == "tab" then return end
		task.spawn(function()
			local fn = S.SecMap[e.sec]
			if fn then fn(false) end
			if e.group and S.GroupOpen and S.GroupOpen[e.group] then S.GroupOpen[e.group](true) end
			task.wait(0.12)
			local page = e.page or Tabs[e.tab]
			if not (page and e.target.Parent) then return end
			local sc = 1
			local us = Main:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
			if us then sc = us.Scale end
			local rel = (e.target.AbsolutePosition.Y - page.AbsolutePosition.Y) / sc + page.CanvasPosition.Y
			page.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, rel - 50))
			highlight(e.target)
		end)
	end

	local function addResult(i, e)
		local b = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38), BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, LayoutOrder = i }, list)
		round(b, 6)
		local sub
		if e.kind == "tab" then sub = "Open this tab"
		elseif e.kind == "section" then sub = e.tab .. "   ›   section"
		else sub = e.tab .. (e.sub and ("   ›   " .. e.sub) or "") .. "   ›   " .. e.secTitle end
		Label(b, { Text = (e.kind == "tab" and "Tab: " or "") .. e.text, Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.Text, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
		Label(b, { Text = sub, Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 21), TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = e.kind == "tab" and T.Accent or T.Sub })
		bind(b.MouseEnter, function() b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(46, 50, 60) end)
		bind(b.MouseLeave, function() b.BackgroundColor3 = T.Hover end)
		bind(b.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("click") jump(e) end)
	end

	local function runSearch(q)
		for _, c in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("GuiObject") then c:Destroy() end end
		firstHit = nil
		q = q:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		clr.Visible = (q ~= "")
		if q == "" then drop.Visible = false return end
		local words = {}
		for w in q:gmatch("%S+") do table.insert(words, w) end
		local function all(s)
			for _, w in ipairs(words) do if not s:find(w, 1, true) then return false end end
			return true
		end
		local tabHits, featHits = {}, {}
		for name, b in pairs(TabButtons) do
			if b.Btn.Visible and all(name:lower()) then table.insert(tabHits, { kind = "tab", text = name, tab = name, order = b.Btn.LayoutOrder }) end
		end
		for pname, pn in pairs(S.Panels or {}) do
			local pb = TabButtons[pname]
			if pb and pb.Btn.Visible then
				for _, sn in ipairs(pn.Names) do
					local it = pn.Items[sn]
					if it and it.Btn.Visible and all(sn:lower()) then table.insert(tabHits, { kind = "tab", text = sn, tab = pname, sub = sn, order = pb.Btn.LayoutOrder + 0.1 }) end
				end
			end
		end
		table.sort(tabHits, function(a, b) return a.order < b.order end)
		for _, e in ipairs(index) do
			if all(e.lower) then
				e.score = (e.lower:sub(1, #q) == q) and 0 or 1
				table.insert(featHits, e)
			end
		end
		table.sort(featHits, function(a, b)
			if a.score ~= b.score then return a.score < b.score end
			if a.tabOrder ~= b.tabOrder then return a.tabOrder < b.tabOrder end
			return a.seq < b.seq
		end)
		local shown = {}
		for _, e in ipairs(tabHits) do table.insert(shown, e) end
		for i, e in ipairs(featHits) do
			if i > 40 then break end
			table.insert(shown, e)
		end
		drop.Visible = true
		if #shown == 0 then
			hdr.Text = "No tabs or features match \"" .. q .. "\""
			return
		end
		hdr.Text = #tabHits + #featHits .. " result(s)  -  Enter opens the first"
		firstHit = shown[1]
		for i, e in ipairs(shown) do addResult(i, e) end
	end

	bind(sbox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function() runSearch(sbox.Text) end)
	bind(sbox.Focused, function()
		buildIndex()
		sbStroke.Color = T.Accent
		runSearch(sbox.Text)
	end)
	bind(sbox.FocusLost, function(enter)
		sbStroke.Color = T.Hover
		if enter and firstHit then jump(firstHit) return end
		task.delay(0.25, function() if not sbox:IsFocused() then drop.Visible = false end end)
	end)
	bind(clr.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("click") sbox.Text = "" end)
	bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
		if i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard or not Unlocked or not Main.Visible then return end
		if i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F and UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) and not BindingActive then
			sbox:CaptureFocus()
			task.wait()
			sbox.Text = ""
		elseif i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape and sbox:IsFocused() then
			sbox.Text = ""
			sbox:ReleaseFocus()
		end
	end)
end

-- ====================================================
-- HOTKEYS
-- ====================================================
bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(input, gp)
	if gp or BindingActive or not Unlocked or input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
	local k = input.KeyCode
	if k == S.MenuKey then
		Main.Visible = not Main.Visible
	elseif k == S.FlyKey then
		C.fly.Fire(not C.fly.Get())
	elseif k == S.NoclipKey and k ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
		C.noclip.Fire(not C.noclip.Get())
	end
end)

-- Discord: tries to open the invite in the Discord desktop app (local RPC), always copies the link too
Hub.DISCORD = "https://discord.gg/w7X5R7uSxp"
function Hub.copyDiscord()
	local clip = setclipboard or toclipboard or set_clipboard or (Clipboard and Clipboard.set)
	if clip and pcall(clip, Hub.DISCORD) then notify("Discord link copied", T.Good) else notify(Hub.DISCORD, T.Accent) end
end
function Hub.joinDiscord()
	local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request)
	local joined = false
	if req then
		local ok, res = pcall(req, {
			Url = "http://127.0.0.1:6463/rpc?v=1", Method = "POST",
			Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json", ["Origin"] = "https://discord.com" },
			Body = HttpService:JSONEncode({ cmd = "INVITE_BROWSER", args = { code = Hub.DISCORD:match("discord%.gg/(%w+)") }, nonce = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false) }),
		})
		joined = ok and res and (res.StatusCode or 0) >= 200 and (res.StatusCode or 0) < 300
	end
	if joined then notify("Opening Discord invite...", T.Good) else Hub.copyDiscord() end
end

-- logo image (executor file functions needed: writefile + getcustomasset). Returns an asset id or nil.
Hub.LOGO_URL = "https://i.postimg.cc/gzVsPRqt/Solos-Hubb.png"
function Hub.logoAsset()
	if Hub._logo ~= nil then return Hub._logo or nil end
	Hub._logo = false
	local getAsset = getcustomasset or getsynasset
	if not (getAsset and writefile and readfile and isfile) then return nil end
	local path = Hub.path("logo.png")
	local function valid(d) return type(d) == "string" and #d > 100 and d:sub(2, 4) == "PNG" end
	if not (isfile(path) and valid(readfile(path))) then
		local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request)
		local ok, body = pcall(function()
			if req then return req({ Url = Hub.LOGO_URL, Method = "GET" }).Body end
			return game:HttpGet(Hub.LOGO_URL)
		end)
		if not (ok and valid(body)) then return nil end
		if not pcall(writefile, path, body) then return nil end
	end
	local ok2, asset = pcall(getAsset, path)
	if ok2 and asset then Hub._logo = asset return asset end
	return nil
end

do
	local VERIFY_URL = "https://solos-keys.discordsilkz.workers.dev/verify" -- <-- your Worker address + /verify

	local KEY_FILE = Hub.path("key.txt")
	local canFile = (writefile and readfile and isfile) and true or false
	local getClip = getclipboard or fromclipboard
	local WHITE = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local keyExpiresAt, currentKey = nil, nil

	local function tierColor(role)
		return (role == "Owner" and Color3.fromRGB(245, 100, 100)) or (role == "Premium User" and Color3.fromRGB(0, 180, 255)) or T.Sub
	end
	-- colours + small UI helpers for the key / loading / picker screens (one table = one local slot)
	local UI = {
		BRAND = Color3.fromRGB(124, 77, 255), BRAND_LIGHT = Color3.fromRGB(170, 120, 255),
		FIELD = Color3.fromRGB(17, 17, 33), FIELD_STROKE = Color3.fromRGB(52, 48, 92),
		MUTED = Color3.fromRGB(132, 128, 175), OFFC = Color3.fromRGB(42, 40, 72), RED = Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 120),
	}
	local function makeCard(w, h)
		local card = new("CanvasGroup", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -w / 2, 0.5, -h / 2), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(11, 11, 22), BorderSizePixel = 0, GroupTransparency = 1 }, Gui)
		round(card, 16)
		new("UIStroke", { Color = UI.BRAND, Transparency = 0.3, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, card)
		-- soft purple wash from the top (a child frame: a gradient on the CanvasGroup itself would tint everything inside)
		local wash = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, card)
		new("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(26, 20, 56), Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 20)), Rotation = 90 }, wash)
		return card
	end
	-- icon from the sprite sheet (stays hidden until the sheet is on disk)
	function UI.setIcon(img, name, after)
		Hub.Icons.get(name, function(a, o, s)
			if img.Parent then
				img.Image = a img.ImageRectOffset = o img.ImageRectSize = s img.Visible = true
				if after then after() end
			end
		end)
	end
	function UI.iconImg(parent, name, size, pos)
		local img = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), Position = pos or UDim2.new(), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false }, parent)
		UI.setIcon(img, name)
		return img
	end
	-- "Solos Hub" with a purple Hub, like the reference
	function UI.brandTitle(parent, props, size)
		props.Text = 'Solos <font color="#8a5cff">Hub</font>'
		props.RichText = true
		props.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		props.TextSize = size
		props.TextColor3 = WHITE
		return Label(parent, props)
	end
	-- row of [icon][text] centered inside a button/frame; returns the text label and the icon
	function UI.iconText(parent, iconName, text, textSize, color, font)
		local inner = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, parent)
		new("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center, Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, inner)
		local ic
		if iconName then
			ic = UI.iconImg(inner, iconName, textSize + 7)
			ic.LayoutOrder = 1
		end
		local lbl = new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text, TextColor3 = color or WHITE, Font = font or Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = textSize, LayoutOrder = 2 }, inner)
		return lbl, ic, inner
	end
	local function logo(parent, pos, size)
		size = size or 46
		local b = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), Position = pos, BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, parent)
		round(b, size / 2)
		new("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(T.Accent, Color3.fromRGB(155, 89, 182)), Rotation = 45 }, b)
		local letter = Label(b, { Text = "S", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Position = UDim2.new(), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = math.floor(size / 2), TextColor3 = WHITE })
		-- custom logo: downloaded once, cached in the SolosHub folder, falls back to the "S" if anything fails
		task.spawn(function()
			local asset = Hub.logoAsset()
			if asset and b.Parent then
				local img = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = asset, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop, ZIndex = 2 }, b)
				round(img, size / 2)
				letter.Visible = false
				b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			end
		end)
		return b
	end
	local function appear(card)
		local target = card.Position
		card.Position = target + UDim2.fromOffset(0, 16)
		TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { GroupTransparency = 0, Position = target }):Play()
	end
	local function vanish(card)
		TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.25), { GroupTransparency = 1 }):Play()
		task.wait(0.27)
	end

	-- ------------------------------------------------------------ ask the server if a key is valid
	-- returns: tier, errorText, expiresAtUnix, wasNetworkProblem
	local function verifyKey(key)
		if key == "FREE" then return "Free User" end
		local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request)
		local url = VERIFY_URL .. "?key=" .. HttpService:UrlEncode(key) .. "&uid=" .. LocalPlayer.UserId
		local ok, body = pcall(function()
			if req then return req({ Url = url, Method = "GET" }).Body end
			return game:HttpGet(url)
		end)
		if not ok or type(body) ~= "string" then return nil, "Couldn't reach the key server", nil, true end
		local ok2, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(body) end)
		if not ok2 or type(data) ~= "table" then return nil, "Bad response from the key server", nil, true end
		if data.ok and RANKS[data.tier] then return data.tier, nil, data.expires end
		return nil, data.error or "Invalid key"
	end

	-- ------------------------------------------------------------ hide tabs that don't belong to the chosen game
	local function filterTabs(keep)
		local set = {}
		for _, n in ipairs(keep) do set[n] = true end
		for name, b in pairs(TabButtons) do
			local pn = S.Panels and S.Panels[name]
			if S.TabOwner and S.TabOwner[name] and not pn then
				b.Btn.Visible = false -- an old tab that now lives inside a panel
			elseif pn and pn.Universal then
				-- a Universal category shows the sub-pages whose old tab is in the keep list
				local any = false
				for sname, it in pairs(pn.Items) do
					local src = pn.SubSrc[sname]
					it.Btn.Visible = (src ~= nil and set[src] == true)
					if it.Btn.Visible then any = true end
				end
				b.Btn.Visible = any
				pn.Refresh()
			else
				b.Btn.Visible = set[name] == true
			end
		end
		local kids = {}
		for _, c in ipairs(NavScroll:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("GuiObject") then table.insert(kids, c) end end
		table.sort(kids, function(a, b) return a.LayoutOrder < b.LayoutOrder end)
		local header, any
		for _, c in ipairs(kids) do
			if c:IsA("TextLabel") then
				if header then header.Visible = any end
				header, any = c, false
			elseif c:IsA("TextButton") and c.Visible then
				any = true
			end
		end
		if header then header.Visible = any end
	end

	-- ------------------------------------------------------------ GAME MODULES (one script per game, loaded on demand)
	-- Universal stays in this file. Every other game lives in its own script. Put the RAW URL of each script here;
	-- when that game is picked the hub downloads it and runs it with loadstring(src)(Hub, S, T, C).
	-- Leave a URL empty to load SolosHub/mod_<id>.lua from the executor workspace instead (handy for testing).
	Hub.ModuleURLs = {
		mm2 = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/RealSoloo/idkkkk/refs/heads/main/Mm2.txt",       -- mm2.lua        Murder Mystery 2
		arsenal = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/RealSoloo/idkkkk/refs/heads/main/Arsenal.txt",   -- arsenal.lua    Arsenal
		pf = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/RealSoloo/idkkkk/refs/heads/main/Pf.txt",        -- pf.lua         Phantom Forces
		lt2 = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/RealSoloo/idkkkk/refs/heads/main/Lt2.txt",       -- lt2.lua        Lumber Tycoon 2
		doomspire = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/RealSoloo/idkkkk/refs/heads/main/Doomspire.txt", -- doomspire.lua  No Cooldown Doomspire Brickbattle
		bab = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/RealSoloo/idkkkk/refs/heads/main/Bab.txt",       -- bab.lua        Build A Boat
	}

	-- Generated bundled modules: rebuild with scripts/build_standalone.py.
	Hub.BundledModules = {
		mm2 = [==[
-- Solos Hub game module: mm2.lua
-- Loaded by the main hub script (picker -> loadstring). Do not run this file on its own.
--
-- Roles are detected from tools: whoever owns a "Knife" is the Murderer, a "Gun" is the Sheriff.

local Hub, S, T, C = ...
local API = Hub.API
local Live = API.Live -- live values: Live.Running, Live.Unlocked, Live.BindingActive
local Dropdown, Collapse, PlayerList = API.Dropdown, API.Collapse, API.PlayerList

local AddTab = API.AddTab
local Button = API.Button
local Esp = API.Esp
local EspGui = API.EspGui
local InfoRow = API.InfoRow
local LocalPlayer = API.LocalPlayer
local PlayerGui = API.PlayerGui
local Players = API.Players
local RunService = API.RunService
local Section = API.Section
local Slider = API.Slider
local TabButtons = API.TabButtons
local Tabs = API.Tabs
local Toggle = API.Toggle

local bind = API.bind
local cam = API.cam
local filterTabs = API.filterTabs
local getChar = API.getChar
local getHum = API.getHum
local getRoot = API.getRoot

local new = API.new
local notify = API.notify

local spectate = API.spectate

if game.PlaceId ~= 142823291 then notify("You're not in Murder Mystery 2 - this module needs that game", T.Warn) end
local Panel = Hub.GamePanel("Murder Mystery 2", "attack", "Round info, loot and attack tools.")
local RoundTab = Panel.Sub("Round", "round", "Live round info, spectating and role ESP.")
local LootTab = Panel.Sub("Loot", "loot", "Gun, coins and sheriff tools.")
local AttackTab = Panel.Sub("Attack", "attack", "Pick a target, fling and kill.")
Hub.gameHeader("MURDER MYSTERY 2")
TabButtons["Murder Mystery 2"].Btn.LayoutOrder = -19
filterTabs({ "Murder Mystery 2", "Dashboard", "Player", "ESP", "Players", "Teleports", "Commands", "Hub Chat", "Theme", "Settings" })

local M = {
	esp = false, showInno = true, names = true, fill = 0.6, gunEsp = true, alert = false, alertR = 40, announce = true,
	autoGun = false, coinFarm = false, coinDelay = 0.12, walkFling = false, antiFling = false, aura = false, auraR = 12,
	busy = false, token = 0, vel = Vector3.zero,
}
local COLORS = { Murderer = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60), Sheriff = Color3.fromRGB(70, 140, 255), Innocent = Color3.fromRGB(70, 220, 120) }

-- ---------------------------------------------------- helpers
local function rootOf(p) return p and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end
local function alive(p)
	local h = p and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	return h ~= nil and h.Health > 0
end
local function hasTool(p, name)
	local ch, bp = p.Character, p:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
	return (ch and ch:FindFirstChild(name)) or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name))
end
local function roleOf(p)
	if hasTool(p, "Knife") then return "Murderer" end
	if hasTool(p, "Gun") then return "Sheriff" end
	return "Innocent"
end
local function findRole(role)
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if alive(p) and roleOf(p) == role and hasTool(p, role == "Murderer" and "Knife" or "Gun") then return p end
	end
	return nil
end
local function gunDrop()
	local g = Workspace:FindFirstChild("GunDrop", true)
	return g and g:IsA("BasePart") and g or nil
end
local function toolNamed(n)
	local ch, bp = LocalPlayer.Character, LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
	local t = (ch and ch:FindFirstChild(n)) or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(n))
	return t and t:IsA("Tool") and t or nil
end
local function equip(tool)
	if tool.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
		local h = getHum()
		if h then h:EquipTool(tool) else tool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character end
	end
end
local lastWarn = 0
local function warn2(text) if os.clock() - lastWarn > 2 then lastWarn = os.clock() notify(text, T.Warn) end end
local function stopAll() M.token = M.token + 1 M.busy = false end
local function runSeq(fn)
	if M.busy then notify("Already running - press Stop first", T.Warn) return end
	M.token = M.token + 1
	local my = M.token
	M.busy = true
	task.spawn(function()
		local good, err = pcall(fn, function() return Live.Running and M.token == my end)
		if not good then warn2("Error: " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 60)) end
		if M.token == my then M.busy = false end
	end)
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- role ESP (highlight + name tag)
local objs = {}
local function clearEsp()
	for p, o in pairs(objs) do
		if o.hl then o.hl:Destroy() end
		if o.bb then o.bb:Destroy() end
		objs[p] = nil
	end
end
local gunHl, gunBb
local function clearGun()
	if gunHl then gunHl:Destroy() gunHl = nil end
	if gunBb then gunBb:Destroy() gunBb = nil end
end
local function updateEsp()
	local myRoot = getRoot()
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		local o = objs[p]
		local show = M.esp and p ~= LocalPlayer and alive(p) and p.Character
		local role = show and roleOf(p)
		if show and role == "Innocent" and not M.showInno then show = false end
		if not show then
			if o then o.hl.Enabled = false o.bb.Enabled = false end
		else
			if not o then
				o = {}
				o.hl = new("Highlight", { FillTransparency = M.fill, OutlineTransparency = 0, DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop }, EspGui)
				o.bb = new("BillboardGui", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 36), StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0), AlwaysOnTop = true }, EspGui)
				o.lbl = new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 13, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4 }, o.bb)
				objs[p] = o
			end
			local col = COLORS[role]
			o.hl.Adornee = p.Character
			o.hl.FillColor, o.hl.OutlineColor, o.hl.FillTransparency, o.hl.Enabled = col, col, M.fill, true
			local head = p.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or rootOf(p)
			o.bb.Adornee = head
			o.bb.Enabled = M.names
			local dist = (myRoot and rootOf(p)) and math.floor((rootOf(p).Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude) or 0
			o.lbl.Text = (role == "Innocent" and p.DisplayName or (role .. " - " .. p.DisplayName)) .. "  [" .. dist .. "]"
			o.lbl.TextColor3 = col
		end
	end
	for p, o in pairs(objs) do
		if not p.Parent then o.hl:Destroy() o.bb:Destroy() objs[p] = nil end
	end
	-- dropped gun
	local g = M.gunEsp and gunDrop()
	if g then
		if not gunHl then
			gunHl = new("Highlight", { FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60), OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60), FillTransparency = 0.3, DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop }, EspGui)
			gunBb = new("BillboardGui", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(120, 24), AlwaysOnTop = true, StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2, 0) }, EspGui)
			new("TextLabel", { Name = "T", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60), TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4 }, gunBb)
		end
		gunHl.Adornee, gunBb.Adornee = g, g
		local d = myRoot and math.floor((g.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude) or 0
		gunBb.T.Text = "DROPPED GUN [" .. d .. "]"
	else
		clearGun()
	end
end
onUnload(function() clearEsp() clearGun() end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- state / info + alerts
local infoMurd, infoSher, infoMe, infoGun
local lastMurd, lastSher, lastAlert = nil, nil, 0
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		local murd, sher = findRole("Murderer"), findRole("Sheriff")
		if infoMurd then
			infoMurd.Set(murd and (murd.DisplayName .. " (@" .. murd.Name .. ")") or "-", murd and COLORS.Murderer or T.Sub)
			infoSher.Set(sher and (sher.DisplayName .. " (@" .. sher.Name .. ")") or "-", sher and COLORS.Sheriff or T.Sub)
			local myRole = roleOf(LocalPlayer)
			infoMe.Set(myRole, COLORS[myRole] or T.Text)
			local g, r = gunDrop(), getRoot()
			infoGun.Set(g and ((r and math.floor((g.Position - r.Position).Magnitude) or "?") .. " studs away") or "None", g and T.Warn or T.Sub)
		end
		if M.announce then
			if murd and murd ~= lastMurd then notify("Murderer: " .. murd.DisplayName, COLORS.Murderer) end
			if sher and sher ~= lastSher then notify("Sheriff: " .. sher.DisplayName, COLORS.Sheriff) end
		end
		lastMurd, lastSher = murd, sher
		if M.alert and murd and murd ~= LocalPlayer and os.clock() - lastAlert > 3 then
			local r, mr = getRoot(), rootOf(murd)
			if r and mr and (mr.Position - r.Position).Magnitude <= M.alertR then
				lastAlert = os.clock()
				notify("Murderer is " .. math.floor((mr.Position - r.Position).Magnitude) .. " studs away!", COLORS.Murderer)
			end
		end
		task.wait(0.5)
	end
end)
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		pcall(updateEsp)
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- gun grab
local function grabGun(ok)
	local g, r = gunDrop(), getRoot()
	if not g or not r then notify("No dropped gun right now", T.Warn) return false end
	local home = r.CFrame
	r.CFrame = g.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
	r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
	local t0 = os.clock()
	while (not ok or ok()) and gunDrop() and os.clock() - t0 < 1 do task.wait(0.05) end
	local root = getRoot()
	if root then root.CFrame = home root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end
	return true
end
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if M.autoGun and not M.busy and gunDrop() and roleOf(LocalPlayer) ~= "Sheriff" and alive(LocalPlayer) then
			grabGun()
			task.wait(2)
		end
		task.wait(0.3)
	end
end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- coins
local function coins()
	local list, r = {}, getRoot()
	for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name == "Coin_Server" and d.Parent then table.insert(list, d) end
	end
	if r then table.sort(list, function(a, b) return (a.Position - r.Position).Magnitude < (b.Position - r.Position).Magnitude end) end
	return list
end
local function collectCoins(ok)
	local list = coins()
	if #list == 0 then notify("No coins found", T.Warn) return end
	local r = getRoot()
	local home = r and r.CFrame
	local n = 0
	for _, c in ipairs(list) do
		if ok and not ok() then break end
		local root = getRoot()
		if not root then break end
		if c.Parent then
			root.CFrame = c.CFrame
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			n = n + 1
			task.wait(M.coinDelay)
		end
	end
	local root = getRoot()
	if root and home then root.CFrame = home end
	notify("Visited " .. n .. " coins", T.Good)
end
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if M.coinFarm and not M.busy and alive(LocalPlayer) then
			local list = coins()
			local r = getRoot()
			if #list > 0 and r then
				local c = list[1]
				r.CFrame = c.CFrame
				r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
		end
		task.wait(math.max(M.coinDelay, 0.05))
	end
end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- fling / anti-fling
bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
	if not M.walkFling then return end
	local r = getRoot()
	if not r then return end
	M.vel = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity
	r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = M.vel * 10000 + Vector3.new(0, 10000, 0)
end)
bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
	if not M.walkFling then return end
	local r = getRoot()
	if r then r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = M.vel end
end)
local noCol = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local function restoreCol()
	for p in pairs(noCol) do if p.Parent then p.CanCollide = true end end
	table.clear(noCol)
end
bind(RunService.Stepped, function()
	if not M.antiFling then return end
	for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if pl ~= LocalPlayer and pl.Character then
			for _, d in ipairs(pl.Character:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.CanCollide then d.CanCollide = false noCol[d] = true end
			end
		end
	end
	local r = getRoot()
	if r and (r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 250 or r.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude > 100) then
		r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		r.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end
end)
-- ---------------------------------------------------- target tracker + aim solver (auto shoot AND fling use this)
-- Other players are drawn a little in the past (network + interpolation), but the server judges shots, and the physics
-- contact that flings someone, against where they REALLY are. So every target gets a short history of the positions we
-- see, and solve() predicts where they will be when the shot / contact lands:
--   * horizontal: a fast fit (last ~0.14 s) blended with a slow fit (last ~0.45 s). A runner is extrapolated; someone
--     spamming A/D is aimed at the middle of their wobble instead of a point they are about to leave.
--   * vertical: a real jump arc (gravity) that stops at the floor, so a jumping target is aimed at where they will be
--     in the air, not where they were.
--   * confidence 0..1: low for erratic strafing, for a jump spammer standing on the ground (they can take off at any
--     moment) and for a target that will land before the shot does. The shooter waits for a good moment.
local TRK = {}
local rpFloor = RaycastParams.new()
rpFloor.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
local StatsSvc = game:GetService("Stats")
local function pingTime() -- round trip in seconds
	local rtt
	pcall(function() rtt = StatsSvc.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue() / 1000 end)
	if not rtt or rtt <= 0 then
		local one = 0.06
		pcall(function() one = LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() end)
		rtt = one * 2
	end
	return math.clamp(rtt, 0.02, 1)
end
-- how far behind the server the positions we see are: our ping (x tuning) + the interpolation delay
local function lagTime()
	return math.clamp(pingTime() * M.lagMul + M.interp, 0, 0.9)
end
local function trackerOf(p)
	local k = TRK[p]
	if not k then
		k = { s = {}, jumps = {}, lastVy = 0, floorY = nil, floorT = 0 }
		TRK[p] = k
	end
	k.want = os.clock()
	return k
end
bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
	local now = os.clock()
	for p, k in pairs(TRK) do
		if not p.Parent or now - k.want > 2 then
			TRK[p] = nil
		else
			local r = rootOf(p)
			if r then
				local s = k.s
				local last = s[#s]
				if last and (r.Position - last.pos).Magnitude > 40 then table.clear(s) end -- respawn / teleport: start over
				table.insert(s, { t = now, pos = r.Position })
				while #s > 0 and now - s[1].t > 0.6 do table.remove(s, 1) end
			end
		end
	end
end)
-- least-squares line through the newest `w` seconds of samples: velocity + fitted position at the newest sample
local function lsq(s, w)
	local n = #s
	if n < 3 then return nil end
	local tn = s[n].t
	local i0 = n
	while i0 > 1 and tn - s[i0 - 1].t <= w do i0 = i0 - 1 end
	local cnt = n - i0 + 1
	if cnt < 3 then return nil end
	local st, stt, sp, stp = 0, 0, Vector3.zero, Vector3.zero
	for i = i0, n do
		local d = s[i]
		local tt = d.t - tn
		st = st + tt
		stt = stt + tt * tt
		sp = sp + d.pos
		stp = stp + d.pos * tt
	end
	local den = cnt * stt - st * st
	if math.abs(den) < 1e-9 then return nil end
	local v = (stp * cnt - sp * st) / den
	return v, (sp - v * st) / cnt
end
local function floorOf(k, r)
	local now = os.clock()
	if now - k.floorT > 0.12 then
		k.floorT = now
		local ex = {}
		for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if pl.Character then table.insert(ex, pl.Character) end end
		rpFloor.FilterDescendantsInstances = ex
		local res = Workspace:Raycast(r.Position, Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), rpFloor)
		k.floorY = res and res.Position.Y or nil
	end
	return k.floorY
end
-- where will `p` be `horizon` seconds from now? returns position, confidence (0..1), short description
local function solve(p, horizon)
	local r = rootOf(p)
	if not r then return nil end
	local k = trackerOf(p)
	local s = k.s
	local vS, aS = lsq(s, 0.14)
	local vL = lsq(s, 0.45)
	if not (vS and vL) then
		local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity -- no history yet: replicated velocity, low confidence
		return r.Position + Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z) * horizon, 0.25, "warming up"
	end
	-- horizontal: steady runner = trust the fast fit, wobbling strafer = trust the slow (averaged) fit
	local hS, hL = Vector3.new(vS.X, 0, vS.Z), Vector3.new(vL.X, 0, vL.Z)
	local steadyH = math.clamp(1 - (hS - hL).Magnitude / (hL.Magnitude + 10), 0, 1)
	local hv = hL:Lerp(hS, steadyH)
	if hv.Magnitude > 60 then hv = hv.Unit * 60 end
	local x, z = aS.X + hv.X * horizon, aS.Z + hv.Z * horizon
	-- vertical: jump arc with the floor as a stop
	local g = Workspace.Gravity
	local fy = floorOf(k, r)
	local dist = fy and (aS.Y - fy) or nil
	if dist and math.abs(vS.Y) < 3 and dist < 8 then k.stand = math.min(k.stand or dist, dist) end -- learn their standing height
	local stand = k.stand or 3.1
	local airborne
	if dist then airborne = dist > stand + 0.9 else airborne = math.abs(vS.Y) > 8 end
	local now = os.clock()
	if vS.Y > 28 and k.lastVy <= 28 then table.insert(k.jumps, now) end -- a takeoff
	k.lastVy = vS.Y
	while #k.jumps > 0 and now - k.jumps[1] > 2 do table.remove(k.jumps, 1) end
	local y, conf, state = aS.Y, steadyH, "grounded"
	if M.ballistic and (airborne or vS.Y > 6) then
		y = aS.Y + vS.Y * horizon - 0.5 * g * horizon * horizon
		local tLand = math.huge
		if fy then
			local floorCenter = fy + stand
			local disc = vS.Y * vS.Y + 2 * g * (aS.Y - floorCenter)
			if disc > 0 then tLand = (vS.Y + math.sqrt(disc)) / g end
			if y < floorCenter then y = floorCenter end
		end
		if tLand - horizon < 0.06 then conf, state = conf * 0.3, "landing" else conf, state = conf * 0.95, "airborne" end
	elseif #k.jumps >= 2 then
		conf, state = conf * 0.3, "jump spam" -- on the ground but about to take off again: wait for them to be in the air
	elseif steadyH < 0.5 then
		state = "strafing"
	end
	return Vector3.new(x, y, z), conf, state
end

-- one fling attempt. Sits where the target will REALLY be and sweeps a volume around that point; the sweep grows when
-- the target is hard to predict (jump spam / strafing), so they still get touched.
local function flingOnce(p, ok)
	local r, tr = getRoot(), rootOf(p)
	if not r or not tr then return false end
	local home, t0, p0, phase, flung = r.CFrame, os.clock(), tr.Position, 0, false
	trackerOf(p)
	local conn
	conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		local root, troot = getRoot(), rootOf(p)
		if not ok() or not root or not troot or os.clock() - t0 > (M.flingTime or 4) then conn:Disconnect() return end
		-- target flew away = success
		if (troot.Position - p0).Magnitude > 120 or troot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 300 then flung = true conn:Disconnect() return end
		local pos, conf = solve(p, lagTime() + M.flingLead)
		if not pos then conn:Disconnect() return end
		phase = phase + dt
		local loose, sc = 1 - conf, M.flingSweep
		local off = Vector3.new(
			math.sin(phase * 37) * (1.2 + 2.2 * loose) * sc,
			math.sin(phase * 23 + 1.3) * (2.2 + 3.5 * loose) * sc + loose * 2,
			math.cos(phase * 31 + 2.1) * (1.2 + 2.2 * loose) * sc)
		root.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos + off)
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(9e5, 9e5, 9e5)
		root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(9e5, 9e5, 9e5)
	end)
	while conn.Connected do task.wait(0.05) end
	local root = getRoot()
	if root then
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity, root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero, Vector3.zero
		root.CFrame = home
	end
	return flung
end
local function flingPlayer(p)
	local r, tr = getRoot(), rootOf(p)
	if not r or not tr then notify("Target has no character", T.Warn) return end
	runSeq(function(ok)
		local flung = false
		for attempt = 1, (M.flingRetries or 1) + 1 do
			if not ok() then break end
			flung = flingOnce(p, ok)
			if flung then break end
			task.wait(0.15)
		end
		if not M.loopFling then notify(flung and "Flung them" or "Fling timed out", flung and T.Good or T.Warn) end
	end)
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- murderer tools
local function stab(p, ok, maxTime)
	local t0 = os.clock()
	while ok() and alive(p) and os.clock() - t0 < (maxTime or 2) do
		local r, tr = getRoot(), rootOf(p)
		local knife = toolNamed("Knife")
		if not r or not tr or not knife then return false end
		equip(knife)
		local back = tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 2.5)
		r.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(back.Position, tr.Position)
		r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		pcall(function() knife:Activate() end)
		task.wait(0.04)
	end
	return not alive(p)
end
local function killAll()
	if not toolNamed("Knife") then notify("You need the Knife (be the Murderer)", T.Warn) return end
	runSeq(function(ok)
		local r = getRoot()
		if not r then return end
		local home, n = r.CFrame, 0
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if not ok() then break end
			if p ~= LocalPlayer and alive(p) and rootOf(p) and stab(p, ok) then n = n + 1 end
		end
		local root = getRoot()
		if root then root.CFrame = home end
		notify("Eliminated " .. n, n > 0 and T.Good or T.Warn)
	end)
end
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if M.aura and not M.busy then
			local knife, r = toolNamed("Knife"), getRoot()
			if knife and r then
				for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					local pr = p ~= LocalPlayer and alive(p) and rootOf(p)
					if pr and (pr.Position - r.Position).Magnitude <= M.auraR then
						equip(knife)
						pcall(function() knife:Activate() end)
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
		task.wait(0.05)
	end
end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- escape platform
local platform, platHome
local function platformOff()
	if platform then platform:Destroy() platform = nil end
	local r = getRoot()
	if r and platHome then r.CFrame = platHome end
	platHome = nil
end
onUnload(function() M.walkFling, M.antiFling, M.aura, M.autoGun, M.coinFarm = false, false, false, false, false stopAll() restoreCol() platformOff() end)

	if i == 1 then stopSpectate() return end
	local p = findRole(name)
	if p then
		spectate(p)
	else
		notify("No " .. name:lower() .. " yet", T.Warn)
		specDd.Set(1, true)
		stopSpectate()
	end
end)

end)
