Button(Coin, "Collect All Coins", "Collect", function() runSeq(function(ok) collectCoins(ok) end) end)
Toggle(Coin, "Auto Coin Farm", false, function(v) M.coinFarm = v end, nil, "mmcoinfarm")
local CoinOpt = Collapse(Coin, "Farm Options")
Slider(CoinOpt, "Delay Between Coins (x0.01s)", 5, 100, 12, function(v) M.coinDelay = v / 100 end, "mmcoindelay")

local Tg = Section(AttackTab, "Target", "Pick who Fling and Kill hit - no need to open the Players tab", "players")
local tgInfo = InfoRow(Tg, "Selected: none")
PlayerList(Tg, 150)
local function tgUpdate()
	local p = S.Target
	tgInfo.Text = (p and p.Parent) and ("Selected: " .. p.DisplayName .. "  (@" .. p.Name .. ")") or "Selected: none"
end
do
	local prev = S.OnTargetChanged
	S.OnTargetChanged = function() if prev then pcall(prev) end tgUpdate() end
end
tgUpdate()

local Fl = Section(AttackTab, "Fling", "Physics based - works best when you touch the player", "attack")
Toggle(Fl, "Walk Fling", false, function(v) M.walkFling = v end, nil, "mmwalkfling", "Fling players you walk into")
Toggle(Fl, "Anti-Fling", false, function(v) M.antiFling = v if not v then restoreCol() end end, nil, "mmantifling", "Others can't push you")
local flingDd = Dropdown(Fl, "Fling Target", { "Selected Player", "Murderer", "Sheriff" }, 1)
Button(Fl, "Fling", "Fling", function()
	local i, p = flingDd.Get(), nil
	if i == 1 then
		p = S.Target
		if not (p and p.Parent) then notify("Pick a player in the Target list first", T.Warn) return end
	else
		local role = (i == 2) and "Murderer" or "Sheriff"
		p = findRole(role)
		if not p then notify("No " .. role:lower() .. " yet", T.Warn) return end
	end
	flingPlayer(p)
end)
Button(Fl, "Stop", "Stop", function() stopAll() end)
local FlOpt = Collapse(Fl, "Fling Options")

local Sf = Section(RoundTab, "Safety", "Stay away from the murderer", "warning")
Toggle(Sf, "Sky Platform (hide up high)", false, function(v)
	if v then
		local r = getRoot()
		if not r then return end
		platHome = r.CFrame
		platform = new("Part", { Anchored = true, Size = Vector3.new(40, 1, 40), Transparency = 0.6, Material = Enum.Material.Neon, Color = T.Accent, CFrame = r.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 300, 0) }, Workspace)
		r.CFrame = platform.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
	else
		platformOff()
	end
end, nil, "mmplatform")
Button(Sf, "Teleport Away From Murderer", "Go", function()
	local m, r = findRole("Murderer"), getRoot()
	local mr = rootOf(m)
	if not (mr and r) then notify("No murderer found", T.Warn) return end
	local dir = (r.Position - mr.Position)
	dir = dir.Magnitude > 1 and dir.Unit or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
	r.CFrame = CFrame.new(r.Position + dir * 80 + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0))
end)

-- ====================================================== extra features (v3.17)
M.tracers, M.coinEsp, M.autoEscape, M.escapeR, M.gunAlert = false, false, false, 25, false
M.autoShoot, M.loopKill, M.loopFling, M.safeFarm, M.tweenSpd = false, false, false, false, 60
M.leadT, M.autoRage, M.rageDist, M.rageGap = 0.05, false, 3, 0.35
M.shootRange, M.shotCd, M.retreat, M.lastShot = 40, 2.5, true, 0
M.flingTime = 4
-- aim solver / fling tuning (see solve())
M.lagMul, M.interp, M.ballistic, M.waitGood, M.minConf, M.patience, M.keepGun = 1, 0.1, true, true, 0.6, 3, true
M.flingRetries, M.flingSweep, M.flingLead = 1, 1, 0
local sg = new("ScreenGui", { Name = "SolosMM2", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true, DisplayOrder = 3 }, PlayerGui)
local lines, coinHl = {}, {}
local rpGround = RaycastParams.new()
rpGround.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

-- tracers to the murderer / sheriff
bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
	local c = cam()
	if not c then return end
	local vp = c.ViewportSize
	local from = Vector2.new(vp.X / 2, vp.Y)
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p ~= LocalPlayer then
			local l = lines[p]
			local role = M.tracers and alive(p) and roleOf(p)
			local r = role and role ~= "Innocent" and rootOf(p)
			if r then
				local sp, on = c:WorldToViewportPoint(r.Position)
				if on then
					if not l then
						l = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, sg)
						lines[p] = l
					end
					local to = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y)
					local d = to - from
					l.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d.Magnitude, 2)
					l.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((from.X + to.X) / 2, (from.Y + to.Y) / 2)
					l.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X))
					l.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS[role]
					l.Visible = true
				elseif l then
					l.Visible = false
				end
			elseif l then
				l.Visible = false
			end
		end
	end
	for p, l in pairs(lines) do
		if not p.Parent then l:Destroy() lines[p] = nil end
	end
end)

-- coin ESP + dropped gun alert
local gunWas = false
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if M.coinEsp then
			local n = 0
			for _, c in ipairs(coins()) do
				n = n + 1
				if n > 60 then break end
				if not coinHl[c] then
					coinHl[c] = new("Highlight", { Adornee = c, FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0), OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0), FillTransparency = 0.4, DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop }, EspGui)
				end
			end
			for c, h in pairs(coinHl) do
				if not c.Parent then h:Destroy() coinHl[c] = nil end
			end
		elseif next(coinHl) then
			for c, h in pairs(coinHl) do h:Destroy() coinHl[c] = nil end
		end
		local g = gunDrop() ~= nil
		if g and not gunWas and M.gunAlert then notify("The sheriff died - gun dropped!", COLORS.Sheriff) end
		gunWas = g
		task.wait(0.75)
	end
end)

-- escape helpers
local function groundAt(pos)
	rpGround.FilterDescendantsInstances = { getChar() or Workspace }
	local res = Workspace:Raycast(pos + Vector3.new(0, 150, 0), Vector3.new(0, -400, 0), rpGround)
	return res and (res.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)) or (pos + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0))
end
local function awayFromMurderer(dist)
	local m, r = findRole("Murderer"), getRoot()
	local mr = rootOf(m)
	if not (mr and r) then return false end
	local dir = r.Position - mr.Position
	dir = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
	dir = dir.Magnitude > 1 and dir.Unit or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
	r.CFrame = CFrame.new(groundAt(r.Position + dir * dist))
	r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
	return true
end
local lastEscape = 0
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if M.autoEscape and alive(LocalPlayer) and roleOf(LocalPlayer) ~= "Murderer" and os.clock() - lastEscape > 1.5 then
			local m, r = findRole("Murderer"), getRoot()
			local mr = rootOf(m)
			if mr and r and m ~= LocalPlayer and (mr.Position - r.Position).Magnitude <= M.escapeR then
				lastEscape = os.clock()
				awayFromMurderer(70)
			end
		end
		task.wait(0.15)
	end
end)

-- smooth (tweened) coin farm
local visited, coinCache, coinCacheT = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }), {}, 0
local function nextCoin(from)
	if os.clock() - coinCacheT > 1 then coinCache, coinCacheT = coins(), os.clock() end
	local best, bd
	for _, c in ipairs(coinCache) do
		local seen = visited[c]
		if c.Parent and not (seen and os.clock() - seen < 3) then
			local d = (c.Position - from).Magnitude
			if not bd or d < bd then best, bd = c, d end
		end
	end
	return best, bd
end
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		local moved = false
		if M.safeFarm and not M.coinFarm and not M.busy and alive(LocalPlayer) then
			local r = getRoot()
			local c, d
			if r then c, d = nextCoin(r.Position) end
			if c then
				local t = math.max(d / math.max(M.tweenSpd, 10), 0.03)
				local tw = TweenService:Create(r, TweenInfo.new(t, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { CFrame = c.CFrame })
				tw:Play()
				task.wait(t)
				visited[c] = os.clock() -- move on to the next coin right away
				moved = true
			elseif r then
				coinCacheT = 0
			end
		end
		if not moved then task.wait(0.2) end
	end
end)

-- sheriff: shoot the murderer (remote path is a guess - falls back to aiming + clicking)
-- keep the murderer tracked while auto shoot / rage is on, so the first shot already has a history to aim from
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if M.autoShoot or M.autoRage then
			local m = findRole("Murderer")
			if m and m ~= LocalPlayer then trackerOf(m) end
		end
		task.wait(0.5)
	end
end)
local rpLos = RaycastParams.new()
rpLos.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
local function lineOfSight(mr)
	local r = getRoot()
	if not r then return false end
	rpLos.FilterDescendantsInstances = { getChar() or Workspace }
	local res = Workspace:Raycast(r.Position, mr.Position - r.Position, rpLos)
	return res == nil or res.Instance:IsDescendantOf(mr.Parent)
end
local function fireAt(gun, pos, conf, state)
	local ok = pcall(function() gun.KnifeLocal.CreateBeam.RemoteFunction:InvokeServer(1, pos, "AH2") end)
	if not ok then
		local c = cam()
		if c then c.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(c.CFrame.Position, pos) end
		pcall(function() gun:Activate() end)
	end
	if M.shotRow then M.shotRow.Set(math.floor(conf * 100 + 0.5) .. "% - " .. state, conf >= 0.6 and T.Good or T.Warn) end
end
-- equip and give the tool a moment to be ready: a shot fired the instant it is equipped is often ignored
local function equipGun(gun)
	if gun.Parent == LocalPlayer.Character then return true end
	equip(gun)
	local t0 = os.clock()
	while os.clock() - t0 < 0.6 and gun.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character do task.wait(0.03) end
	task.wait(0.12)
	return gun.Parent == LocalPlayer.Character
end
-- take ONE shot at a good moment: waits for enough history, then for the solver to be confident (or for the patience
-- timeout). strict = automatic mode: give up if they leave range / cover while we wait.
local function takeShot(m, strict)
	local gun = toolNamed("Gun")
	if not (gun and m and rootOf(m)) then return false end
	if not equipGun(gun) then return false end
	local t0 = os.clock()
	while Live.Running do
		local mr, root = rootOf(m), getRoot()
		if not (mr and root and alive(m) and alive(LocalPlayer) and toolNamed("Gun")) then return false end
		if strict and ((mr.Position - root.Position).Magnitude > M.shootRange + 8 or not lineOfSight(mr)) then return false end
		local waited = os.clock() - t0
		local pos, conf, state = solve(m, lagTime() + M.leadT)
		local warm = #trackerOf(m).s >= 6
		if warm and pos and (not M.waitGood or conf >= M.minConf or waited >= M.patience) then
			M.lastShot = os.clock()
			fireAt(gun, pos, conf, state)
			return true
		end
		if waited > M.patience + 1.5 then return false end
		task.wait(0.02)
	end
	return false
end
local function shootMurderer()
	if not toolNamed("Gun") then notify("You need the Gun (be the Sheriff)", T.Warn) return end
	local m = findRole("Murderer")
	if not (m and rootOf(m)) then notify("No murderer to shoot", T.Warn) return end
	task.spawn(function()
		if not takeShot(m, false) then notify("Couldn't take the shot", T.Warn) end
	end)
end
-- rage: stick directly behind the murderer, keep the camera locked on them and keep firing until they die or the gun is lost
local function rageShoot()
	runSeq(function(ok)
		local r = getRoot()
		if not r then return end
		local gun0 = toolNamed("Gun")
		if gun0 then equipGun(gun0) end
		local home, last, shots = r.CFrame, 0, 0
		local start = os.clock()
		-- after teleporting behind them the server still has us at the old spot for a moment: do not fire until it has caught up
		local settle = math.clamp(pingTime() * 0.6 + 0.08, 0.1, 0.5)
		local waitSince = start + settle
		while ok() do
			local gun, root = toolNamed("Gun"), getRoot()
			local m = findRole("Murderer")
			local mr = rootOf(m)
			if not gun or not root or not mr or m == LocalPlayer or not alive(LocalPlayer) then break end
			trackerOf(m)
			local back = mr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, M.rageDist)
			root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(back.Position, mr.Position)
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			if gun.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character then equip(gun) end
			local c = cam()
			if c then c.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(c.CFrame.Position, mr.Position) end
			local now = os.clock()
			if gun.Parent == LocalPlayer.Character and now - start >= settle and now - last >= M.rageGap then
				local pos, conf, state = solve(m, lagTime())
				if pos and #trackerOf(m).s >= 6 and (not M.waitGood or conf >= M.minConf or now - waitSince >= M.patience) then
					last, waitSince, shots = now, now, shots + 1
					fireAt(gun, pos, conf, state)
				end
			end
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
		local root = getRoot()
		if root and alive(LocalPlayer) then root.CFrame = home root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end
		if shots > 0 then notify("Rage shoot finished (" .. shots .. " shots)", T.Sub) end
	end)
end
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if M.autoRage and not M.busy and toolNamed("Gun") and alive(LocalPlayer) and findRole("Murderer") then rageShoot() task.wait(0.5)
		elseif M.autoShoot and not M.autoRage and toolNamed("Gun") and alive(LocalPlayer) then
			-- only take shots that are likely to land: close enough, a clear line of sight and (see takeShot) a predictable target
			local m, r = findRole("Murderer"), getRoot()
			local mr = rootOf(m)
			if mr and r and m ~= LocalPlayer and (mr.Position - r.Position).Magnitude <= M.shootRange and lineOfSight(mr) then
				local gun = toolNamed("Gun")
				if M.keepGun and gun and gun.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character then equip(gun) end -- already in hand when the moment comes
				if os.clock() - M.lastShot >= M.shotCd and takeShot(m, true) and M.retreat then
					task.wait(0.4)
					local m2 = findRole("Murderer")
					local mr2, r2 = rootOf(m2), getRoot()
					if mr2 and r2 and alive(m2) and (mr2.Position - r2.Position).Magnitude < 45 then awayFromMurderer(70) end
				end
			end
		end
		task.wait(0.3)
	end
end)

-- knife kill helpers
local function killList(list, quiet)
	if not toolNamed("Knife") then if not quiet then notify("You need the Knife (be the Murderer)", T.Warn) end return end
	runSeq(function(ok)
		local r = getRoot()
		if not r then return end
		local home, n = r.CFrame, 0
		for _, p in ipairs(list) do
			if not ok() then break end
			if p ~= LocalPlayer and alive(p) and rootOf(p) and stab(p, ok) then n = n + 1 end
		end
		local root = getRoot()
		if root then root.CFrame = home end
		if not quiet then notify("Eliminated " .. n, n > 0 and T.Good or T.Warn) end
	end)
end
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if M.loopKill and not M.busy and toolNamed("Knife") and alive(LocalPlayer) then
			local list = {}
			for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= LocalPlayer and alive(p) and rootOf(p) then table.insert(list, p) end end
			if #list > 0 then killList(list, true) end
		end
		if M.loopFling and not M.busy then
			local p = findRole("Murderer")
			if p and p ~= LocalPlayer then flingPlayer(p) end
		end
		task.wait(0.5)
	end
end)
onUnload(function()
	M.tracers, M.coinEsp, M.autoEscape, M.autoShoot, M.autoRage, M.loopKill, M.loopFling, M.safeFarm = false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false
	for c, h in pairs(coinHl) do h:Destroy() end
	table.clear(coinHl)
	sg:Destroy()
end)

-- alive counter row (Round tab)
local infoAlive = Hub.Stat(Info, "Players alive", "-")
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		local n, total = 0, 0
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			total = total + 1
			if alive(p) then n = n + 1 end
		end
		infoAlive.Set(n .. " / " .. total)
		task.wait(1)
	end
end)

-- ---- Attack tab
local Mu = Section(AttackTab, "Murderer Tools", "Only works while you hold the Knife", "attack")
Button(Mu, "Kill Everyone (knife)", "Start", killAll)
local killDd = Dropdown(Mu, "Kill Target", { "Selected Player", "Sheriff", "Nearest Player" }, 1)

local Tpm = Section(Tabs["Teleports"], "Murder Mystery 2 Teleports", "Jump to round targets", "teleports")
local tpDd = Dropdown(Tpm, "Teleport To", { "Murderer", "Sheriff", "Nearest Player", "Random Player" }, 1)
Button(Tpm, "Teleport", "Go", function()
	local i, target = tpDd.Get(), nil
	if i == 1 then
		target = findRole("Murderer")
	elseif i == 2 then
		target = findRole("Sheriff")
	elseif i == 3 then
		target = nearestPlayer()
	else
		local pool = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= LocalPlayer and alive(p) and rootOf(p) then table.insert(pool, p) end end
		target = pool[math.random(math.max(#pool, 1))]
	end
	local tr, r = rootOf(target), getRoot()
	if tr and r then r.CFrame = tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 4) else notify("No " .. tpDd.Name():lower() .. " found", T.Warn) end
end)]==],
		arsenal = [==[
-- Solos Hub game module: arsenal.lua
-- Loaded by the main hub script (picker -> loadstring). Do not run this file on its own.
--
-- Aimbot / triggerbot / hitbox, ESP (chams, boxes, tracers) and weapon tweaks.
-- Weapon + ammo tweaks read Arsenal's own value objects (names are best guesses) and are wrapped in pcall.

local Hub, S, T, C = ...
local API = Hub.API
local Live = API.Live -- live values: Live.Running, Live.Unlocked, Live.BindingActive
local Dropdown, Collapse, PlayerList = API.Dropdown, API.Collapse, API.PlayerList

local AddTab = API.AddTab
local Button = API.Button
local EspGui = API.EspGui
local Keybind = API.Keybind
local LocalPlayer = API.LocalPlayer
local Players = API.Players
local RunService = API.RunService
local Section = API.Section
local Slider = API.Slider
local TabButtons = API.TabButtons
local Tabs = API.Tabs
local Toggle = API.Toggle
local UIS = API.UIS

local env = API.env

local getHum = API.getHum

local new = API.new

local stopSpectate = API.stopSpectate

if game.PlaceId ~= 286090429 then notify("You're not in Arsenal - this module needs that game", T.Warn) end
local Panel = Hub.GamePanel("Arsenal", "aimbot", "Aimbot, ESP and weapon tweaks.")
local AimTab = Panel.Sub("Aimbot", "aimbot", "Aim assist, triggerbot and rage options.")
local EspTab2 = Panel.Sub("ESP", "esp", "See enemies through walls.")
local WepTab = Panel.Sub("Weapons", "attack", "Weapon mods and movement.")
Hub.gameHeader("ARSENAL")
TabButtons["Arsenal"].Btn.LayoutOrder = -19
filterTabs({ "Arsenal", "Dashboard", "Player", "Visuals", "Players", "Teleports", "Commands", "Hub Chat", "Theme", "Settings" })

local A = {
	aim = false, mode = 1, key = Enum.KeyCode.E, part = 1, fov = 150, showFov = true, smooth = 0.3, teamCheck = true,
	wall = true, maxDist = 1500, sticky = false, predict = false, predAmt = 0.1, noFF = true, target = nil,
	trig = false, trigDelay = 0.05, hit = false, hitPart = 1, hitSize = 8, hitTrans = 0.7,
	rage = false, rageDist = 4,
	esp = false, box = true, names = true, health = true, dist = true, held = false, chams = true, tracers = false,
	showTeam = false, teamColors = false, espMax = 2000, fillT = 0.6, tracerFrom = 1,
	ammo = false, ammoVal = 999, noRecoil = false, rapid = false, fastReload = false, autoFire = false,
	customFov = false, fovVal = 90, bhop = false,
}
local AIM_PARTS = { "Head", "UpperTorso", "HumanoidRootPart", "Closest" }
local AIM_MODES = { "Hold Right Mouse", "Hold Key", "Always On" }
local HB_PARTS = { "Head", "HumanoidRootPart" }
local mouse = LocalPlayer:GetMouse()

-- ---------------------------------------------------- helpers
local function rootOf(p) return p and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end
local function hpOf(p)
	local h = p and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	return h and h.Health or 0, h and h.MaxHealth or 100
end
local function alive(p)
	local h = p and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	return h ~= nil and h.Health > 0
end
local function isEnemy(p)
	if p == LocalPlayer then return false end
	if A.teamCheck and S.SameTeam(p) then return false end
	return true
end
local function shielded(p) return A.noFF and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField") ~= nil end
local function enemyList()
	local out = {}
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if isEnemy(p) and alive(p) and rootOf(p) and not shielded(p) then table.insert(out, p) end
	end
	return out
end
local function click()
	if type(env.mouse1click) == "function" then pcall(env.mouse1click) return end
	local ch = getChar()
	local tool = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
	if tool then pcall(function() tool:Activate() end) end
end

local rp = RaycastParams.new()
rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
local function visible(part, ch)
	local c = cam()
	rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = { getChar() or c, c }
	local origin = c.CFrame.Position
	local res = Workspace:Raycast(origin, part.Position - origin, rp)
	return res == nil or res.Instance:IsDescendantOf(ch)
end
local function aimPartOf(ch, mousePos)
	local m = A.part
	if m ~= 4 then
		return ch:FindFirstChild(AIM_PARTS[m]) or ch:FindFirstChild("Torso") or ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	end
	local c, best, bestD = cam(), nil, math.huge
	for _, n in ipairs({ "Head", "UpperTorso", "HumanoidRootPart" }) do
		local pt = ch:FindFirstChild(n)
		if pt then
			local sp = c:WorldToViewportPoint(pt.Position)
			local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - mousePos).Magnitude
			if d < bestD then best, bestD = pt, d end
		end
	end
	return best
end
local function bestTarget()
	local c, mp, myr = cam(), UIS:GetMouseLocation(), getRoot()
	local best, bestPart, bestD = nil, nil, A.fov
	for _, p in ipairs(enemyList()) do
		local part = aimPartOf(p.Character, mp)
		if part then
			local sp, on = c:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
			if on then
				local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - mp).Magnitude
				local far = myr and (part.Position - myr.Position).Magnitude > A.maxDist
				if d < bestD and not far and (not A.wall or visible(part, p.Character)) then best, bestPart, bestD = p, part, d end
			end
		end
	end
	return best, bestPart
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- FOV circle + aim loop
local fovGui = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false }, EspGui)
round(fovGui, 999)
local fovStroke = new("UIStroke", { Color = T.Accent, Thickness = 1.5, Transparency = 0.2 }, fovGui)
local function aimActive()
	if UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then return false end
	if A.mode == 3 then return true end
	if A.mode == 1 then return UIS:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) end
	return A.key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and UIS:IsKeyDown(A.key)
end
bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
	local mp = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
	fovGui.Visible = A.showFov and A.aim
	fovGui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(A.fov * 2, A.fov * 2)
	fovGui.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(mp.X, mp.Y)
	fovStroke.Color = A.target and T.Good or T.Accent
	if not A.aim or not aimActive() then A.target = nil return end
	local c = cam()
	if not c then return end
	local part
	if A.sticky and A.target and alive(A.target) and A.target.Character then
		part = aimPartOf(A.target.Character, mp)
		if not part then A.target = nil end
	end
	if not part then A.target, part = bestTarget() end
	if not (A.target and part) then return end
	local pos = part.Position
	if A.predict then pos = pos + part.AssemblyLinearVelocity * A.predAmt end
	c.CFrame = c.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.lookAt(c.CFrame.Position, pos), math.clamp(A.smooth, 0.01, 1))
end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- triggerbot + rage
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if A.trig and not UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then
			local t = mouse.Target
			local m = t and t:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			local p = m and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m)
			if p and isEnemy(p) and alive(p) and not shielded(p) then
				click()
				task.wait(math.max(A.trigDelay, 0.01))
			end
		end
		task.wait()
	end
end)
task.spawn(function() -- rage: teleport behind the nearest enemy, face them and shoot
	while Live.Running do
		if A.rage and alive(LocalPlayer) then
			local r = getRoot()
			local best, bestD
			for _, p in ipairs(enemyList()) do
				local d = r and (rootOf(p).Position - r.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
				if not bestD or d < bestD then best, bestD = p, d end
			end
			local tr = rootOf(best)
			if r and tr then
				local back = tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, A.rageDist)
				r.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(back.Position, tr.Position)
				r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				local c = cam()
				local head = best.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or tr
				if c then c.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(c.CFrame.Position, head.Position) end
				click()
			end
		end
		task.wait(0.05)
	end
end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- hitbox expander (client side)
local hbOrig = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local function hbRestore()
	for part, o in pairs(hbOrig) do
		if part.Parent then part.Size, part.Transparency, part.CanCollide, part.Massless = o[1], o[2], o[3], o[4] end
	end
	table.clear(hbOrig)
end
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if A.hit then
			for _, p in ipairs(enemyList()) do
				local part = p.Character:FindFirstChild(HB_PARTS[A.hitPart])
				if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
					if not hbOrig[part] then hbOrig[part] = { part.Size, part.Transparency, part.CanCollide, part.Massless } end
					part.Size = Vector3.new(A.hitSize, A.hitSize, A.hitSize)
					part.Transparency, part.CanCollide, part.Massless = A.hitTrans, false, true
				end
			end
		elseif next(hbOrig) then
			hbRestore()
		end
		task.wait(0.25)
	end
end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- ESP (chams, box, name/health/distance, tracers)
local objs = {}
local function killObj(o)
	for _, k in ipairs({ "hl", "boxGui", "tagGui", "line" }) do if o[k] then o[k]:Destroy() end end
end
local function makeObj()
	local o = {}
	o.hl = new("Highlight", { DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop, Enabled = false }, EspGui)
	o.boxGui = new("BillboardGui", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(4, 5.6), AlwaysOnTop = true, LightInfluence = 0, Enabled = false }, EspGui)
	o.boxFrame = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, o.boxGui)
	o.stroke = new("UIStroke", { Thickness = 1.5, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, o.boxFrame)
	o.hpBack = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, -6, 0, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, o.boxFrame)
	o.hpFill = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = T.Good, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, o.hpBack)
	o.tagGui = new("BillboardGui", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 36), StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.9, 0), AlwaysOnTop = true, LightInfluence = 0, Enabled = false }, EspGui)
	o.tag = new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 12, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4 }, o.tagGui)
	o.line = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false }, EspGui)
	return o
end
local function hideObj(o)
	o.hl.Enabled, o.boxGui.Enabled, o.tagGui.Enabled, o.line.Visible = false, false, false, false
end
local function espColor(p)
	if A.teamColors then
		local tc = S.TeamColorOf(p)
		if tc then return tc end
	end
	if S.SameTeam(p) then return T.Good end
	return Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
end
local function updateEsp()
	local c, myr = cam(), getRoot()
	local vp = c and c.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(0, 0)
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p ~= LocalPlayer then
			local o = objs[p]
			local ch = p.Character
			local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local show = A.esp and root and alive(p) and (A.showTeam or not S.SameTeam(p))
			local dist = (show and myr) and (root.Position - myr.Position).Magnitude or 0
			if show and dist > A.espMax then show = false end
			if not show then
				if o then hideObj(o) end
			else
				if not o then o = makeObj() objs[p] = o end
				local col = espColor(p)
				o.hl.Adornee, o.hl.FillColor, o.hl.OutlineColor, o.hl.FillTransparency = ch, col, col, A.fillT
				o.hl.Enabled = A.chams
				o.boxGui.Adornee, o.stroke.Color = root, col
				o.boxGui.Enabled = A.box
				o.hpBack.Visible = A.health
				local hp, mhp = hpOf(p)
				local frac = math.clamp(hp / math.max(mhp, 1), 0, 1)
				o.hpFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, frac)
				o.hpFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255 * (1 - frac), 255 * frac, 60)
				o.tagGui.Adornee = root
				local lines = {}
				if A.names then table.insert(lines, p.DisplayName) end
				local info = {}
				if A.health then table.insert(info, math.floor(hp) .. "hp") end
				if A.dist then table.insert(info, math.floor(dist) .. "m") end
				if #info > 0 then table.insert(lines, table.concat(info, "  ")) end
				if A.held then
					local tool = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
					if tool then table.insert(lines, tool.Name) end
				end
				o.tag.Text, o.tag.TextColor3 = table.concat(lines, "\n"), col
				o.tagGui.Enabled = #lines > 0
				if A.tracers and c then
					local sp, on = c:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)
					if on then
						local from = A.tracerFrom == 1 and Vector2.new(vp.X / 2, vp.Y) or Vector2.new(vp.X / 2, vp.Y / 2)
						local to = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y)
						local d = to - from
						o.line.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d.Magnitude, 1.5)
						o.line.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((from.X + to.X) / 2, (from.Y + to.Y) / 2)
						o.line.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X))
						o.line.BackgroundColor3 = col
						o.line.Visible = true
					else
						o.line.Visible = false
					end
				else
					o.line.Visible = false
				end
			end
		end
	end
	for p, o in pairs(objs) do
		if not p.Parent then killObj(o) objs[p] = nil end
	end
end
bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function() pcall(updateEsp) end)
local function clearEsp()
	for p, o in pairs(objs) do killObj(o) objs[p] = nil end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- weapon tweaks (guessed value names, restored afterwards)
local wOrig = {}
local function weaponFolder()
	local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	return rs:FindFirstChild("Weapons")
end
local function setWeaponValue(name, value)
	local f = weaponFolder()
	if not f then return end
	for _, w in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
		local v = w:FindFirstChild(name)
		if v and v:IsA("ValueBase") then
			if wOrig[v] == nil then wOrig[v] = v.Value end
			v.Value = value
		end
	end
end
local function restoreWeaponValue(name)
	for v, o in pairs(wOrig) do
		if v.Parent and v.Name == name then v.Value = o wOrig[v] = nil end
	end
end
local function restoreAllWeapons()
	for v, o in pairs(wOrig) do if v.Parent then v.Value = o end end
	table.clear(wOrig)
end
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		pcall(function()
			if A.noRecoil then setWeaponValue("RecoilControl", 0) setWeaponValue("Spread", 0) end
			if A.rapid then setWeaponValue("FireRate", 0.02) end
			if A.fastReload then setWeaponValue("ReloadTime", 0.01) setWeaponValue("EquipTime", 0.01) end
			if A.autoFire then setWeaponValue("Auto", true) end
			if A.ammo then
				local gui = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("GUI")
				local vars = gui and gui:FindFirstChild("Client") and gui.Client:FindFirstChild("Variables")
				if vars then
					for _, n in ipairs({ "ammocount", "ammocount2" }) do
						local v = vars:FindFirstChild(n)
						if v and v:IsA("ValueBase") then v.Value = A.ammoVal end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		task.wait(0.5)
	end
end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- field of view + bunny hop
bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
	if A.customFov then local c = cam() if c then c.FieldOfView = A.fovVal end end
end)
bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
	if not A.bhop or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() or not UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then return end
	local h = getHum()
	if h and h.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then h:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
end)
bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
	if gp or Live.BindingActive or not Live.Unlocked or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
	if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and A.toggleKey and A.toggleKey ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and i.KeyCode == A.toggleKey then
		A.aim = not A.aim
		if A.aimToggle then A.aimToggle.Set(A.aim) end
		notify("Aimbot " .. (A.aim and "on" or "off"), A.aim and T.Good or T.Sub)
	end
end)
onUnload(function()
	A.aim, A.trig, A.hit, A.rage, A.esp, A.ammo, A.noRecoil, A.rapid, A.fastReload, A.autoFire, A.customFov, A.bhop = false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false
	hbRestore() restoreAllWeapons() clearEsp()
	if fovGui then fovGui:Destroy() end
end)

local Mv = Section(Tabs["Player"], "Arsenal Movement", "Arsenal only", "player")
Toggle(Mv, "Bunny Hop", false, function(v) A.bhop = v end, nil, "arsbhop", "Hold Space")
local Tp = Section(Tabs["Teleports"], "Arsenal Teleports", "Jump to enemies", "teleports")
local function nearestEnemy()
	local r, best, bestD = getRoot(), nil, nil
	for _, p in ipairs(enemyList()) do
		local d = r and (rootOf(p).Position - r.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
		if not bestD or d < bestD then best, bestD = p, d end
	end
	return best
end
local tpDd = Dropdown(Tp, "Action", { "Teleport Behind Nearest Enemy", "Teleport To Random Enemy", "Spectate Nearest Enemy" }, 1)
Button(Tp, "Run", "Go", function()
	local i, r = tpDd.Get(), getRoot()
	if i == 1 then
		local best = nearestEnemy()
		local tr = rootOf(best)
		if r and tr then r.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt((tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 5)).Position, tr.Position) else notify("No enemy found", T.Warn) end
	elseif i == 2 then
		local list = enemyList()
		if #list == 0 or not r then notify("No enemy found", T.Warn) return end
		r.CFrame = rootOf(list[math.random(#list)]).CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 5)
	else
		local best = nearestEnemy()
		if best then spectate(best) else notify("No enemy found", T.Warn) end
	end
end)
Button(Tp, "Stop Spectating", "Stop", function() stopSpectate() end)]==],
		pf = [==[
-- Solos Hub game module: pf.lua
-- ESP revision: pf-esp-10 (shared team resolution + per-character rendering recovery)
-- Loaded by the main hub script (picker -> loadstring). Do not run this file on its own.
--
-- Built to be low-profile: no teleporting, no speed, no hitboxes, no remote/metatable hooks, no getgc.
-- Aim assist supports camera tracking or mouse movement; ESP is drawn locally.
-- Nothing here can be guaranteed undetectable - the game has its own anti-cheat, so use an alt account.

local Hub, S, T, C = ...
local API = Hub.API
local Live = API.Live -- live values: Live.Running, Live.Unlocked, Live.BindingActive
local Dropdown, Collapse, PlayerList = API.Dropdown, API.Collapse, API.PlayerList

local AddTab = API.AddTab
local Button = API.Button
local EspGui = API.EspGui
local InfoRow = API.InfoRow
local Keybind = API.Keybind
local LocalPlayer = API.LocalPlayer
local Players = API.Players
local PlayerGui = API.PlayerGui
local RunService = API.RunService
local Section = API.Section
local Slider = API.Slider
local TabButtons = API.TabButtons
local Toggle = API.Toggle
local UIS = API.UIS
local Workspace = API.Workspace
local bind = API.bind
local cam = API.cam
local copyText = API.copyText
local env = API.env
local filterTabs = API.filterTabs
local new = API.new
local notify = API.notify
local onUnload = API.onUnload
local round = API.round

if game.PlaceId ~= 292439477 then notify("You're not in Phantom Forces - this module needs that game", T.Warn) end
local Panel = Hub.GamePanel("Phantom Forces", "aimbot", "Aim assist, ESP and radar.")
local AimTab = Panel.Sub("Aim Assist", "aimbot", "Low-profile aim assist and triggerbot.")
local EspTab3 = Panel.Sub("ESP", "esp", "ESP, radar and visuals.")
Hub.gameHeader("PHANTOM FORCES")
TabButtons["Phantom Forces"].Btn.LayoutOrder = -19
-- movement / teleport tabs are deliberately hidden here: they are the easiest things for the anti-cheat to catch
filterTabs({ "Phantom Forces", "Dashboard", "Hub Chat", "Theme", "Settings" })

local P = {
	aim = false, mode = 1, aimMethod = 2, teamCheck = true, key = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, part = 1, fov = 110, showFov = true, strength = 0.16, maxStep = 16,
	deadzone = 2, scale = 1, wall = true, maxDist = 700, sticky = true, humanize = true, delay = 0.12, jitter = 3,
	trig = false, trigAds = true, trigMin = 0.05, trigMax = 0.12,
	esp = false, box = true, names = true, dist = true, chams = false, tracers = false, showTeam = false, visColor = true, espMax = 1200,
	radar = false, radarRange = 250, alert = false, alertDist = 60, teamMode = 1,
	target = nil, targetSince = 0, offset = Vector3.zero,
}
local AIM_PARTS = { "Head", "Torso", "Closest" }
local AIM_MODES = { "Hold Right Mouse (ADS)", "Hold Key", "Always On" }
local tgs = {}
local function tog(parent, text, default, cb, desc)
	local o = Toggle(parent, text, default, cb, nil, nil, desc) -- no id on purpose: these never auto-enable from a saved config
	table.insert(tgs, o)
	return o
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- helpers
local function playersRoot() return Workspace:FindFirstChild("Players") end
local function findPart(m, names)
	for _, n in ipairs(names) do
		local p = m:FindFirstChild(n)
		if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then return p end
	end
	-- Part casing differs between character versions. Do not recursively match
	-- weapons/accessories or turn a team container into a character.
	for _, p in ipairs(m:GetChildren()) do
		if p:IsA("BasePart") then
			for _, n in ipairs(names) do
				if p.Name:lower() == n:lower() then return p end
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end
local function firstPart(m)
	if m.PrimaryPart then return m.PrimaryPart end
	for _, ch in ipairs(m:GetChildren()) do
		if ch:IsA("BasePart") then return ch end
	end
	return nil
end
local HEAD_NAMES, TORSO_NAMES = { "Head", "head" }, { "Torso", "UpperTorso", "HumanoidRootPart", "torso", "Chest" }
local function headOf(m)
	local named = findPart(m, HEAD_NAMES)
	if named then return named end
	-- Randomized PF models still keep their direct body parts. The highest
	-- body-part center is a usable head fallback without matching weapon parts.
	local best
	for _, part in ipairs(m:GetChildren()) do
		if part:IsA("BasePart") and (not best or part.Position.Y > best.Position.Y) then best = part end
	end
	return best or firstPart(m)
end
local function torsoOf(m)
	local named = findPart(m, TORSO_NAMES)
	if named then return named end
	local best, volume = nil, -1
	for _, part in ipairs(m:GetChildren()) do
		if part:IsA("BasePart") then
			local size = part.Size
			local v = size.X * size.Y * size.Z
			if v > volume then best, volume = part, v end
		end
	end
	return best or firstPart(m)
end
local function teamKey(value)
	return tostring(value or ""):lower():gsub("[%s_%-]", "")
end
-- PF uses faction names in some places and BrickColor folder names in others.
-- Compare normalized aliases, not just the literal Player.Team.Name.
local TEAM_ALIASES = {
	phantoms = { "Phantoms", "Phantom", "Bright blue" },
	ghosts = { "Ghosts", "Ghost", "Bright orange" },
}
local function addTeamName(set, value)
	if not value then return end
	local key = teamKey(value)
	if key == "" then return end
	set[key] = true
	for _, names in pairs(TEAM_ALIASES) do
		local matches = false
		for _, name in ipairs(names) do
			if key == teamKey(name) then matches = true break end
		end
		if matches then
			for _, name in ipairs(names) do set[teamKey(name)] = true end
		end
	end
end
local function factionOf(value)
	local key = teamKey(value)
	for faction, names in pairs(TEAM_ALIASES) do
		for _, name in ipairs(names) do
			if key == teamKey(name) then return faction end
		end
	end
	return nil
end
-- Read the game's own leaderboard; never use this hub's labels as evidence.
local roster, rosterAt, rosterChecked = {}, -math.huge, -math.huge
local owners = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local replication, replicationChecked = nil, -math.huge
local function plainText(value)
	return tostring(value or ""):gsub("<[^>]*>", ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$"):lower()
end
local function gameGui(node)
	local cur = node
	while cur and cur ~= PlayerGui do
		if cur.Name:sub(1, 5) == "Solos" then return false end
		if cur:IsA("GuiObject") and not cur.Visible then return false end
		if cur:IsA("ScreenGui") and not cur.Enabled then return false end
		cur = cur.Parent
	end
	return cur == PlayerGui
end
local function commonParent(a, b)
	local cur = a.Parent
	while cur and cur ~= PlayerGui do
		if b:IsDescendantOf(cur) then return cur end
		cur = cur.Parent
	end
	return nil
end
local function headerBranch(header, root)
	local cur = header
	while cur.Parent and cur.Parent ~= root do cur = cur.Parent end
	return cur
end
local function readScoreboard()
	if not PlayerGui then return end
	local now = os.clock()
	if now - rosterChecked < 0.75 then return end
	rosterChecked = now
	local phantoms, ghosts, labels = {}, {}, {}
	local playersByText, ambiguous = {}, {}
	for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		playersByText[plainText(pl.Name)] = pl
	end
	for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		local key = plainText(pl.DisplayName)
		if playersByText[key] and playersByText[key] ~= pl then ambiguous[key] = true
		elseif not ambiguous[key] then playersByText[key] = pl end
	end
	for key in pairs(ambiguous) do playersByText[key] = nil end
	for _, node in ipairs(PlayerGui:GetDescendants()) do
		if (node:IsA("TextLabel") or node:IsA("TextButton")) and gameGui(node) then
			local text = plainText(node.Text)
			if text == "phantoms" then table.insert(phantoms, node)
			elseif text == "ghosts" then table.insert(ghosts, node)
			elseif playersByText[text] then table.insert(labels, { node = node, player = playersByText[text] }) end
		end
	end
	for _, ph in ipairs(phantoms) do
		for _, gh in ipairs(ghosts) do
			local root = commonParent(ph, gh)
			if root then
				local left, right = headerBranch(ph, root), headerBranch(gh, root)
				local found, conflicts = {}, {}
				for _, row in ipairs(labels) do
					local node, faction = row.node, nil
					if node:IsDescendantOf(root) then
						if left ~= right and node:IsDescendantOf(left) then faction = "phantoms"
						elseif left ~= right and node:IsDescendantOf(right) then faction = "ghosts"
						else
							-- Some scoreboards keep the header and row containers separate.
							local a, b, n = ph.AbsolutePosition, gh.AbsolutePosition, node.AbsolutePosition
							if math.abs(a.Y - b.Y) <= 48 and math.abs(a.X - b.X) > 100 and
								n.Y > math.max(a.Y, b.Y) and n.X >= math.min(a.X, b.X) then
								local split = (a.X + b.X) / 2
								faction = (n.X < split) == (a.X < b.X) and "phantoms" or "ghosts"
							end
						end
					end
					if faction then
						if found[row.player] and found[row.player] ~= faction then conflicts[row.player] = true end
						found[row.player] = faction
					end
				end
				for pl in pairs(conflicts) do found[pl] = nil end
				if next(found) then
					roster, rosterAt = found, now
					local n = 0 for _ in pairs(found) do n = n + 1 end
					P.rosterStatus = "leaderboard: " .. n .. " players"
					return
				end
			end
		end
	end
	P.rosterStatus = now - rosterAt <= 20 and "leaderboard cached (20s maximum)" or "open the game's leaderboard to read teams"
end
local function rosterFaction(pl)
	if os.clock() - rosterAt <= 20 then return roster[pl] end
	return nil
end
local function getReplication()
	if replication then return replication end
	if os.clock() - replicationChecked < 5 then return nil end
	replicationChecked = os.clock()
	local sources = {}
	local function add(value) if type(value) == "table" then table.insert(sources, value) end end
	add(shared)
	add(env.shared)
	local getRuntime = env.getrenv or getrenv
	if type(getRuntime) == "function" then
		local ok, runtime = pcall(getRuntime)
		if ok and type(runtime) == "table" then add(runtime.shared) end
	end
	for _, source in ipairs(sources) do
		local loader = rawget(source, "require")
		if type(loader) == "function" then
			local ok, module = pcall(loader, "ReplicationInterface")
			if ok and type(module) == "table" and type(module.getEntry) == "function" then
				replication = module
				return module
			end
		end
	end
	P.ownerStatus = "replication interface unavailable; randomized bodies cannot be linked to usernames"
	return nil
end
local function replicationModel(entry)
	if type(entry) ~= "table" then return nil end
	local third = entry._thirdPersonObject
	if type(entry.getThirdPersonObject) == "function" then third = entry:getThirdPersonObject() end
	if type(third) ~= "table" then return nil end
	local model = third._character or third._characterModel
	if type(third.getCharacterModel) == "function" then model = third:getCharacterModel() end
	if typeof(model) == "Instance" and model:IsA("Model") then return model end
	return nil
end
local function refreshOwnership()
	local fresh = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local module = getReplication()
	local count = 0
	if module then
		for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local ok, model = pcall(function() return replicationModel(module.getEntry(pl)) end)
			if ok and model and model.Parent then fresh[model] = pl count = count + 1 end
		end
		P.ownerStatus = "replication interface: " .. count .. " model owners"
	end
	owners = fresh
end
local function playerFaction(pl)
	local fromBoard = rosterFaction(pl)
	if fromBoard then return fromBoard end
	if pl.Team then
		local faction = factionOf(pl.Team.Name)
		if faction then return faction end
		if pl.Team.TeamColor then
			faction = factionOf(pl.Team.TeamColor.Name)
			if faction then return faction end
		end
	end
	-- PF can leave Neutral true and Team nil while still publishing its faction color.
	if pl.TeamColor then return factionOf(pl.TeamColor.Name) end
	return nil
end
local function folderFaction(m, root)
	local cur = m.Parent
	while cur and cur ~= root and cur ~= Workspace do
		local faction = factionOf(cur.Name)
		if faction then return faction, cur.Name end
		cur = cur.Parent
	end
	return nil
end
local function myFaction()
	if P.teamMode == 2 then return "phantoms", "manual" end
	if P.teamMode == 3 then return "ghosts", "manual" end
	local faction = playerFaction(LocalPlayer)
	if faction then return faction, "player team" end
	local character, root = LocalPlayer.Character, playersRoot()
	if character and root and character:IsDescendantOf(root) then
		faction = folderFaction(character, root)
		if faction then return faction, "local character folder" end
	end
	return nil, "unresolved - select Your team on the ESP tab"
end
local function myTeamNames()
	local set = {}
	local faction = myFaction()
	if faction then
		addTeamName(set, faction)
	else
		if LocalPlayer.Team then addTeamName(set, LocalPlayer.Team.Name) end
		if not LocalPlayer.Neutral and LocalPlayer.TeamColor then
			addTeamName(set, LocalPlayer.TeamColor.Name)
		end
	end
	return set
end
local function sameKnownTeam(pl)
	local mine, theirs = myFaction(), playerFaction(pl)
	if not mine or not theirs then return nil end
	return mine == theirs
end
local function modelPlayer(m)
	local pl = owners[m] or Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m) or Players:FindFirstChild(m.Name)
	return pl and pl:IsA("Player") and pl or nil
end
local function looksLikeChar(m, loose)
	if not m:IsA("Model") or m == LocalPlayer.Character or m:IsDescendantOf(cam()) then return false end
	if loose then return firstPart(m) ~= nil end
	return findPart(m, HEAD_NAMES) ~= nil and findPart(m, TORSO_NAMES) ~= nil
end
-- scan result is cached (refreshed by the loop below) so the per-frame code stays cheap.
-- Team is taken from the Workspace.Players team folder (own team folder = teammates); falls back to the player's team.
local cache, cacheSet = {}, {}
local function groupOf(m)
	local root, cur = playersRoot(), m
	if not root then return nil end
	while cur.Parent and cur.Parent ~= root do cur = cur.Parent end
	if cur.Parent == root and cur ~= m and cur:IsA("Folder") then return cur end
	return nil
end
local function calibratedEnemy(group)
	local root = playersRoot()
	if P.myGroup and P.myGroup.Parent ~= root then P.myGroup = nil end
	if P.enemyGroup and P.enemyGroup.Parent ~= root then P.enemyGroup = nil end
	if not group then return nil end
	if group == P.myGroup then return false end
	if group == P.enemyGroup then return true end
	-- A complementary team is certain only when there are exactly two groups.
	local groups = {}
	if root then
		for _, child in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Folder") then table.insert(groups, child) end
		end
	end
	if #groups == 2 and (group == groups[1] or group == groups[2]) then
		if P.myGroup then return true end
		if P.enemyGroup then return false end
	end
	return nil
end
local function scan(loose)
	P.scanErr = nil
	local ok, err = pcall(readScoreboard)
	if not ok then P.rosterStatus = "leaderboard read failed: " .. tostring(err) end
	ok, err = pcall(refreshOwnership)
	if not ok then P.ownerStatus = "owner lookup failed: " .. tostring(err) end
	local root, mine = playersRoot(), myTeamNames()
	local faction, source = myFaction()
	P.teamFaction, P.teamSource = faction, source
	if loose and not root then return end
	local bases = {}
	if root then table.insert(bases, root) end
	local roots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Roots")
	if roots and not loose then table.insert(bases, roots) end
	if #bases == 0 then table.insert(bases, Workspace) end
	local found, set, hasMine = {}, {}, false
	if root then
		for _, f in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
			if mine[teamKey(f.Name)] then hasMine = true end
		end
	end
	for _, base in ipairs(bases) do
	for _, d in ipairs(base:GetDescendants()) do
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			if set[d] or d.Name == LocalPlayer.Name or not looksLikeChar(d, loose) then return end
			if d.Parent and d.Parent:IsA("Model") and looksLikeChar(d.Parent, loose) then return end
			local top = d
			while top.Parent and top.Parent ~= base do top = top.Parent end
			local folderName = (top ~= d) and top.Name or (d.Parent and d.Parent.Name) or ""
			local enemy = nil
			local pl = modelPlayer(d)
			if pl == LocalPlayer then return end
			local modelFaction, factionFolder = folderFaction(d, root)
			if factionFolder then folderName = factionFolder end
			-- A manual faction selection works even when Roblox Player.Team is
			-- unset, and does not need our faction's folder to contain a player.
			local same = nil
			if pl then same = sameKnownTeam(pl) end
			local group = groupOf(d)
			local calibrated = calibratedEnemy(group)
			if calibrated ~= nil then
				enemy = calibrated
			elseif same ~= nil then
				enemy = not same
			elseif faction and modelFaction then
				enemy = faction ~= modelFaction
			elseif hasMine then
				enemy = not mine[teamKey(folderName)]
			end
			local e = { m = d, enemy = enemy, player = pl, folder = folderName, group = group }
			table.insert(found, e)
			set[d] = e
		end)
		if not ok then P.scanErr = tostring(err) end
	end
	end
	local groupFactions, groupConflict = {}, {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(found) do
		local f = entry.player and playerFaction(entry.player)
		if entry.group and f then
			if groupFactions[entry.group] and groupFactions[entry.group] ~= f then groupConflict[entry.group] = true end
			groupFactions[entry.group] = f
		end
	end
	for _, entry in ipairs(found) do
		local f = entry.group and not groupConflict[entry.group] and groupFactions[entry.group]
		if entry.enemy == nil and faction and f then entry.enemy = faction ~= f end
	end
	cache, cacheSet = found, set
end
local function collect(includeTeam, includeUnknown)
	local out = {}
	for _, e in ipairs(cache) do
		if e.m.Parent and (e.enemy or includeTeam or P.allEnemy or (includeUnknown and e.enemy == nil)) then table.insert(out, e) end
	end
	return out
end
local function collectEsp()
	local out = {}
	for _, e in ipairs(cache) do
		if e.m.Parent and (not P.teamCheck or e.enemy == true) then
			table.insert(out, e)
		end
	end
	return out
end
-- Calibration must not depend on CanQuery: replicated PF body parts can be
-- visible but absent from Workspace:Raycast results. Intersect the center ray
-- with their oriented boxes directly, then allow a small screen-space margin.
local function bodyRayDistance(part, origin, direction, limit)
	local o = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(origin)
	local d = part.CFrame:VectorToObjectSpace(direction)
	local near, far = 0, limit
	for _, axis in ipairs({ "X", "Y", "Z" }) do
		local half = part.Size[axis] / 2
		if math.abs(d[axis]) < 1e-8 then
			if o[axis] < -half or o[axis] > half then return nil end
		else
			local a, b = (-half - o[axis]) / d[axis], (half - o[axis]) / d[axis]
			if a > b then a, b = b, a end
			near, far = math.max(near, a), math.min(far, b)
			if near > far then return nil end
		end
	end
	return near
end
local function calibrationCandidate(c)
	if not c.ViewportSize then return nil end
	local center = Vector2.new(c.ViewportSize.X / 2, c.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
	local ray = c:ViewportPointToRay(center.X, center.Y)
	local best, bestDistance, close, closePixels, closeDepth = nil, P.espMax, nil, 16, math.huge
	for _, entry in ipairs(cache) do
		if entry.group and entry.m.Parent then
			-- Ignore nested weapon/accessory geometry. Only the direct body parts
			-- count; their names and query/collision flags do not matter.
			for _, part in ipairs(entry.m:GetChildren()) do
				if part:IsA("BasePart") then
					local ok, distance = pcall(bodyRayDistance, part, ray.Origin, ray.Direction, P.espMax)
					if ok and distance and distance < bestDistance then best, bestDistance = entry, distance end
					local sp, on = c:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
					if on and sp.Z > 0 and sp.Z <= P.espMax then
						local pixels = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Magnitude
						if pixels < closePixels or (pixels == closePixels and sp.Z < closeDepth) then
							close, closePixels, closeDepth = entry, pixels, sp.Z
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return best or close
end
local function applyTeamCalibration(entry, isEnemy, source)
	if not entry or not entry.group or entry.group.Parent ~= playersRoot() then return false end
	P.myGroup, P.enemyGroup = nil, nil
	if isEnemy then P.enemyGroup = entry.group else P.myGroup = entry.group end
	P.calibrationSource = source
	P.target = nil
	scan(false)
	if #cache == 0 then scan(true) end
	return true
end
local function calibrateTeam(isEnemy)
	local c = cam()
	if not c then return notify("Camera is not ready", T.Warn) end
	scan(false)
	if #cache == 0 then scan(true) end
	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	local ignore = { c }
	local ignored = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Ignore")
	if ignored then table.insert(ignore, ignored) end
	if LocalPlayer.Character then table.insert(ignore, LocalPlayer.Character) end
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
	local hit = Workspace:Raycast(c.CFrame.Position, c.CFrame.LookVector * P.espMax, params)
	local cur = hit and hit.Instance
	local entry
	while cur and cur ~= Workspace do
		if cacheSet[cur] then entry = cacheSet[cur] break end
		cur = cur.Parent
	end
	if not entry or not entry.group then entry = calibrationCandidate(c) end
	if not entry or not entry.group then
		return notify(#cache == 0 and "No character models found yet - deploy and try again" or
			"Put a player's body at the center of the screen, then press F6 or F7", T.Warn)
	end
	applyTeamCalibration(entry, isEnemy, "manual")
	notify(isEnemy and "Enemy group marked - team check updated" or "Teammate group marked - team check updated", T.Good)
end
-- Use the game's own crosshair relation text, never our ESP labels, menu
-- controls, scoreboard roster, model IDs, or an assumed first target.
local hintLabels, hintsChecked = {}, -math.huge
local pendingGroup, pendingModel, pendingRelation, pendingAt
local function resetAutoCandidate()
	pendingGroup, pendingModel, pendingRelation, pendingAt = nil, nil, nil, nil
end
local function nativeCrosshairRelation(c)
	if not PlayerGui then return nil, "PlayerGui unavailable" end
	if os.clock() - hintsChecked >= 1 then
		hintsChecked = os.clock()
		hintLabels = {}
		for _, node in ipairs(PlayerGui:GetDescendants()) do
			if node:IsA("TextLabel") or node:IsA("TextButton") then table.insert(hintLabels, node) end
		end
	end
	local center = Vector2.new(c.ViewportSize.X / 2, c.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
	local relation, found = nil, false
	for _, node in ipairs(hintLabels) do
		if node.Parent and gameGui(node) and node.TextTransparency < 0.95 then
			local text = plainText(node.Text)
			if text == "friendly" or text == "enemy" then
				local pos, size = node.AbsolutePosition, node.AbsoluteSize
				local x, y = pos.X + size.X / 2, pos.Y + size.Y / 2
				-- Use the actual text alignment when a HUD label spans the screen.
				local bounds = node.TextBounds
				if bounds then
					if node.TextXAlignment == Enum.TextXAlignment.Left then x = pos.X + bounds.X / 2
					elseif node.TextXAlignment == Enum.TextXAlignment.Right then x = pos.X + size.X - bounds.X / 2 end
					if node.TextYAlignment == Enum.TextYAlignment.Top then y = pos.Y + bounds.Y / 2
					elseif node.TextYAlignment == Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom then y = pos.Y + size.Y - bounds.Y / 2 end
				end
				-- Includes Roblox's top GUI inset while excluding corner HUD/menus.
				if math.abs(x - center.X) <= 120 and math.abs(y - center.Y) <= 100 then
					local isEnemy = text == "enemy"
					if found and relation ~= isEnemy then return nil, "Conflicting game HUD indicators" end
					relation, found = isEnemy, true
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if not found then return nil, "Team detection: briefly look at a teammate" end
	if relation then return nil, "Team detection: waiting for a teammate" end
	return false
end
local function updateAutoTeam()
	if Live.BindingActive or not Live.Unlocked or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then resetAutoCandidate() return end
	local c = cam()
	if not c then resetAutoCandidate() return end
	local relation, reason = nativeCrosshairRelation(c)
	if relation == nil then
		resetAutoCandidate()
		calibratedEnemy(nil) -- discard references to groups removed by the game
		P.autoTeamStatus = (P.myGroup or P.enemyGroup) and "Team check ready" or reason
		return
	end
	local entry = calibrationCandidate(c)
	if not entry or not entry.group then
		resetAutoCandidate() P.autoTeamStatus = "HUD indicator found; waiting for a body under crosshair" return
	end
	if calibratedEnemy(entry.group) == relation then
		resetAutoCandidate() P.autoTeamStatus = "Team groups confirmed by game HUD" return
	end
	if pendingGroup ~= entry.group or pendingModel ~= entry.m or pendingRelation ~= relation then
		pendingGroup, pendingModel, pendingRelation, pendingAt = entry.group, entry.m, relation, os.clock()
		P.autoTeamStatus = "Reading game " .. (relation and "Enemy" or "Friendly") .. " indicator..."
		return
	end
	if os.clock() - pendingAt < 0.2 then return end
	if applyTeamCalibration(entry, relation, "game crosshair " .. (relation and "Enemy" or "Friendly")) then
		P.autoTeamStatus = "Teams detected automatically from game HUD"
	end
	resetAutoCandidate()
end
local function enemyModelOf(inst)
	local cur = inst
	while cur and cur ~= Workspace do
		local e = cacheSet[cur]
		if e then return (e.enemy or P.allEnemy) and cur or nil end
		cur = cur.Parent
	end
	return nil
end
local rp = RaycastParams.new()
rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
local function refreshFilter()
	local list = { cam() }
	local ig = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Ignore")
	if ig then table.insert(list, ig) end
	for _, e in ipairs(cache) do
		if e.enemy == false and e.m.Parent then table.insert(list, e.m) end
	end
	if LocalPlayer.Character then table.insert(list, LocalPlayer.Character) end
	rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = list
end
local function visibleModel(m, part)
	local c = cam()
	local origin = c.CFrame.Position
	local res = Workspace:Raycast(origin, part.Position - origin, rp)
	if res == nil or res.Instance:IsDescendantOf(m) then return true end
	local hit = res.Instance
	return hit.Transparency >= 0.9 or not hit.CanCollide
end
local function pickPart(m, center)
	if P.part == 1 then return headOf(m) or torsoOf(m) end
	if P.part == 2 then return torsoOf(m) or headOf(m) end
	local c, best, bestD = cam(), nil, math.huge
	for _, pt in ipairs({ headOf(m), torsoOf(m) }) do
		if pt then
			local sp = c:WorldToViewportPoint(pt.Position)
			local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Magnitude
			if d < bestD then best, bestD = pt, d end
		end
	end
	return best
end
local function bestTarget(center)
	local c = cam()
	local best, bestPart, bestD = nil, nil, P.fov
	for _, e in ipairs(collect(false)) do
		local part = pickPart(e.m, center)
		if part then
			local sp, on = c:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
			if on then
				local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Magnitude
				local dist = (part.Position - c.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
				if d < bestD and dist <= P.maxDist and (not P.wall or visibleModel(e.m, part)) then best, bestPart, bestD = e.m, part, d end
			end
		end
	end
	return best, bestPart
end
local function aimActive()
	if UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then return false end
	if P.mode == 3 then return true end
	if P.mode == 1 then return UIS:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) end
	return P.key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and UIS:IsKeyDown(P.key)
end
local function press(down)
	if down and type(env.mouse1press) == "function" then pcall(env.mouse1press) return true end
	if not down and type(env.mouse1release) == "function" then pcall(env.mouse1release) return true end
	return false
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- FOV circle + aim assist
local fovGui = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false }, EspGui)
round(fovGui, 999)
local fovStroke = new("UIStroke", { Color = T.Accent, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.5 }, fovGui)
local function updateAim(dt)
	P.aimErr = nil
	local c = cam()
	if not c then P.aimState = "waiting for camera" return end
	local vp = c.ViewportSize
	local center = Vector2.new(vp.X / 2, vp.Y / 2)
	fovGui.Visible = P.showFov and P.aim
	fovGui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(P.fov * 2, P.fov * 2)
	fovGui.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(center.X, center.Y)
	fovStroke.Color = P.target and T.Good or T.Accent
	if not P.aim then P.target = nil P.aimState = "off" return end
	if not aimActive() then P.target = nil P.aimState = "waiting for ADS / key" return end
	refreshFilter()
	local m, part
	if P.sticky and P.target and P.target.Parent then
		local entry = cacheSet[P.target]
		if entry and (entry.enemy == true or P.allEnemy) then
			m = P.target
			part = pickPart(m, center)
		end
		local keep = false
		if part then
			local sp, on = c:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
			local distance = (part.Position - c.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
			keep = on and distance <= P.maxDist and
				(Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Magnitude <= P.fov * 1.3 and
				(not P.wall or visibleModel(m, part))
		end
		if not keep then P.target, m, part = nil, nil, nil end
	end
	if not m then
		m, part = bestTarget(center)
		if m then
			P.target, P.targetSince = m, os.clock()
			local j = P.jitter / 10
			P.offset = Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * j, (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * j, (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * j)
		else
			P.target = nil
		end
	end
	if not (m and part) then
		local faction = myFaction()
		if #cache == 0 then
				P.aimState = "no character models found - copy diagnostic report"
			else
				local unresolved = false
				for _, entry in ipairs(cache) do
					if entry.enemy == nil then unresolved = true break end
				end
				P.aimState = unresolved and "team detection: briefly look at a teammate" or
					(not faction and "select Your team on ESP tab" or "no confirmed visible enemy in FOV / range")
			end
		return
	end
	if P.humanize and os.clock() - P.targetSince < P.delay then P.aimState = "reaction delay" return end
	local aimPos = part.Position
	if P.humanize then aimPos = aimPos + P.offset end
	local sp, on = c:WorldToViewportPoint(aimPos)
	if not on then P.aimState = "target off screen" return end
	local delta = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center
	if delta.Magnitude < P.deadzone then P.aimState = "on target" return end
	if P.aimMethod == 1 then
		-- Apply after the game's normal camera update so it cannot immediately
		-- overwrite the CFrame in the same frame. No mouse API is required.
		local alpha = 1 - (1 - math.clamp(P.strength, 0.01, 1)) ^ (math.min(dt or 1/60, 0.1) * 60)
		c.CFrame = c.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.lookAt(c.CFrame.Position, aimPos), alpha)
		P.aimState = "tracking (camera)"
	else
		local move = env.mousemoverel or mousemoverel
		if type(move) ~= "function" then
			P.aimErr = "Mouse movement unavailable. Select Camera tracking."
			P.aimState = "mouse API unavailable"
			return
		end
		local step = delta * P.strength
		if step.Magnitude > P.maxStep then step = step.Unit * P.maxStep end
		local ok, err = pcall(move, step.X * P.scale, step.Y * P.scale)
		if not ok then
			P.aimErr = "Mouse movement failed: " .. tostring(err)
			P.aimState = "mouse API failed"
		else
			P.aimState = "tracking (mouse)"
		end
	end
end
local aimBinding = "SolosHubPFAim_" .. tostring({})
RunService:BindToRenderStep(aimBinding, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function(dt)
	local ok, err = pcall(updateAim, dt)
	if not ok then P.aimErr = tostring(err) P.aimState = "error" end
end)
onUnload(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(aimBinding) end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- triggerbot (human-like reaction + hold time)
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if P.trig and (not P.trigAds or UIS:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2)) and not UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then
			local c = cam()
			if c then
				refreshFilter()
				local res = Workspace:Raycast(c.CFrame.Position, c.CFrame.LookVector * P.maxDist, rp)
				if res and enemyModelOf(res.Instance) then
					task.wait(P.trigMin + math.random() * math.max(P.trigMax - P.trigMin, 0))
					local again = Workspace:Raycast(c.CFrame.Position, c.CFrame.LookVector * P.maxDist, rp)
					if P.trig and again and enemyModelOf(again.Instance) then
						if press(true) then
							task.wait(0.03 + math.random() * 0.04)
							press(false)
						elseif type(env.mouse1click) == "function" then
							pcall(env.mouse1click)
						end
						task.wait(0.08 + math.random() * 0.12)
					end
				end
			end
		end
		task.wait()
	end
end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- ESP (all drawn locally with GUI objects)
local objs, visCache = {}, setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local function killObj(o)
	for _, k in ipairs({ "box", "name", "dist", "line", "hl" }) do if o[k] then o[k]:Destroy() end end
end
local function makeObj(o)
	o = o or {}
	o.box = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false }, EspGui)
	o.stroke = new("UIStroke", { Thickness = 1.2, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, o.box)
	o.name = new("TextLabel", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 14), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 11, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5, Visible = false }, EspGui)
	o.dist = new("TextLabel", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(120, 12), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 10, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5, Visible = false }, EspGui)
	o.line = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false }, EspGui)
	o.hl = new("Highlight", { FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 0.2, DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop, Enabled = false }, EspGui)
	return o
end
local function hideObj(o)
	o.box.Visible, o.name.Visible, o.dist.Visible, o.line.Visible, o.hl.Enabled = false, false, false, false, false
end
local function clearEsp()
	for m, o in pairs(objs) do killObj(o) objs[m] = nil end
end
local ENEMY_VIS, ENEMY_HID = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70), Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 60)
-- Clip bounding-box edges to the near plane before projecting. Rejecting an
-- entire box because just one corner is behind the camera hides nearby players.
local function projectBounds(c, cf, size)
	local corners, points = {}, {}
	local near = 0.05
	for i = 0, 7 do
		local sx = (i % 2 == 0) and -1 or 1
		local sy = (math.floor(i / 2) % 2 == 0) and -1 or 1
		local sz = (i < 4) and -1 or 1
		local p = c.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(cf:PointToWorldSpace(
			Vector3.new(sx * size.X / 2, sy * size.Y / 2, sz * size.Z / 2)))
		corners[i + 1] = p
		if p.Z <= -near then table.insert(points, p) end
	end
	for i = 0, 7 do
		for _, step in ipairs({1, 2, 4}) do
			if math.floor(i / step) % 2 == 0 then
				local a, b = corners[i + 1], corners[i + step + 1]
				if (a.Z <= -near) ~= (b.Z <= -near) then
					local t = (-near - a.Z) / (b.Z - a.Z)
					table.insert(points, a + (b - a) * t)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local minX, minY, maxX, maxY = math.huge, math.huge, -math.huge, -math.huge
	for _, p in ipairs(points) do
		local sp = c:WorldToViewportPoint(c.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(p))
		minX, minY = math.min(minX, sp.X), math.min(minY, sp.Y)
		maxX, maxY = math.max(maxX, sp.X), math.max(maxY, sp.Y)
	end
	local vp = c.ViewportSize
	if #points == 0 or maxX <= 0 or maxY <= 0 or minX >= vp.X or minY >= vp.Y then return nil end
	minX, minY = math.max(0, minX), math.max(0, minY)
	maxX, maxY = math.min(vp.X, maxX), math.min(vp.Y, maxY)
	if maxX <= minX or maxY <= minY then return nil end
	return minX, minY, maxX, maxY
end
local function drawModel(c, e)
	local m = e.m
	local cf, size = m:GetBoundingBox()
	local dist = (cf.Position - c.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
	local o = objs[m]
	if dist > P.espMax then
		if o then hideObj(o) end
		return false
	end
	local minX, minY, maxX, maxY = projectBounds(c, cf, size)
	if not minX then
		if o then hideObj(o) end
		return false
	end
	if not o then
		o = {} -- Register first so a partially created overlay can be cleaned up.
		objs[m] = o
		makeObj(o)
	end
	local col = e.enemy == nil and T.Sub or (e.enemy and ENEMY_HID or T.Good)
	if e.enemy and (not P.visColor or visCache[m]) then col = ENEMY_VIS end
	o.box.Position, o.box.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(minX, minY), UDim2.fromOffset(maxX - minX, maxY - minY)
	o.stroke.Color, o.box.Visible = col, P.box
	local cx = (minX + maxX) / 2
	local pl = e.player or modelPlayer(m)
	o.name.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, math.max(14, minY - 1))
	local label = pl and pl.DisplayName
	if not label then
		if e.enemy == true then label = "Enemy"
		elseif e.enemy == false then label = "Teammate"
		else label = "Unknown player" end
	end
	o.name.Text, o.name.TextColor3, o.name.Visible = label, col, P.names
	o.dist.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, math.min(c.ViewportSize.Y - 12, maxY + 1))
	o.dist.Text, o.dist.TextColor3, o.dist.Visible = math.floor(dist * 0.28) .. "m", col, P.dist
	o.hl.Adornee, o.hl.OutlineColor, o.hl.Enabled = m, col, P.chams
	o.line.Visible = P.tracers
	if P.tracers then
		local from, to = Vector2.new(c.ViewportSize.X / 2, c.ViewportSize.Y), Vector2.new(cx, maxY)
		local d = to - from
		o.line.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d.Magnitude, 1)
		o.line.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((from.X + to.X) / 2, (from.Y + to.Y) / 2)
		o.line.Rotation, o.line.BackgroundColor3 = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X)), col
	end
	return true
end
local function updateEsp()
	local c = cam()
	P.drawn, P.espErr, P.renderFailed = 0, nil, 0
	if not c then
		for _, o in pairs(objs) do hideObj(o) end
		return
	end
	local seen = {}
	if P.esp then
		for _, e in ipairs(collectEsp()) do
			seen[e.m] = true
			local ok, drawn = pcall(drawModel, c, e)
			if ok then
				if drawn then P.drawn = P.drawn + 1 end
			else
				P.renderFailed = P.renderFailed + 1
				P.espErr = tostring(drawn)
				-- A stale/partially replicated model must not stop every other row.
				local o = objs[e.m]
				if o then killObj(o) objs[e.m] = nil end
			end
		end
	end
	for m, o in pairs(objs) do
		if not seen[m] or not m.Parent then killObj(o) objs[m] = nil end
	end
end
bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
	local ok, err = pcall(updateEsp)
	if not ok then P.espErr = tostring(err) end
end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- radar (top-down, rotates with your view)
local RADAR = 70
local radar = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, -20), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(RADAR * 2, RADAR * 2), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 14), BackgroundTransparency = 0.45, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false }, EspGui)
round(radar, 8)
new("UIStroke", { Color = T.Accent, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.6 }, radar)
new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, radar)
local dots = {}
bind(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
	radar.Visible = P.radar
	if not P.radar then return end
	local c = cam()
	if not c then return end
	local list = collect(false)
	for i, e in ipairs(list) do
		local d = dots[i]
		if not d then
			d = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5), BackgroundColor3 = ENEMY_VIS, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, radar)
			dots[i] = d
		end
		local head = headOf(e.m)
		if head then
			local rel = c.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(head.Position)
			local v = Vector2.new(rel.X, rel.Z) / P.radarRange * RADAR
			if v.Magnitude > RADAR - 4 then v = v.Unit * (RADAR - 4) end
			d.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, v.X, 0.5, v.Y)
			d.Visible = true
		else
			d.Visible = false
		end
	end
	for i = #list + 1, #dots do dots[i].Visible = false end
end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- visibility cache, enemy info + proximity alert
local infoRow
local lastAlert = 0
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		local c = cam()
		if c and (P.esp or P.alert or infoRow) then
			refreshFilter()
			local n, nearest = 0, math.huge
			for _, e in ipairs(collect(false)) do
				n = n + 1
				local head = headOf(e.m)
				if head then
					visCache[e.m] = visibleModel(e.m, head)
					local d = (head.Position - c.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
					if d < nearest then nearest = d end
				end
			end
			if infoRow then
				infoRow.Text = "Enemies: " .. n .. (n > 0 and ("   Nearest: " .. math.floor(nearest * 0.28) .. "m") or "")
			end
			if P.alert and nearest <= P.alertDist and os.clock() - lastAlert > 4 then
				lastAlert = os.clock()
				notify("Enemy nearby - " .. math.floor(nearest * 0.28) .. "m", Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 60))
			end
		end
		task.wait(0.1)
	end
end)

task.spawn(function() -- keep the character list fresh
	while Live.Running do
		local ok, err = pcall(scan, false)
		if not ok then P.scanErr = tostring(err) end
		if #cache == 0 then -- strict detection found nothing: accept any model inside Workspace.Players
			local ok2, err2 = pcall(scan, true)
			if not ok2 then P.scanErr = tostring(err2) end
		end
		task.wait(playersRoot() and 0.4 or 1.5)
	end
end)
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		local ok, err = pcall(updateAutoTeam)
		if not ok then resetAutoCandidate() P.autoTeamStatus = "HUD detection error: " .. tostring(err) end
		task.wait(0.1)
	end
end)
local statusRow, espRow, teamRow, rosterRow, ownerRow, autoTeamRow
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if autoTeamRow then autoTeamRow.Text = P.autoTeamStatus or "Team detection is automatic - briefly look at a teammate" end
		if rosterRow then rosterRow.Text = P.rosterStatus or "Open the game's leaderboard to read teams" end
		if ownerRow then ownerRow.Text = P.ownerStatus or "Checking model ownership..." end
		if teamRow then
			local faction, source = myFaction()
			teamRow.Text = (P.myGroup or P.enemyGroup) and "Team groups calibrated: " .. (P.calibrationSource or "manual") or
				("Your team: " .. (faction or "unknown") .. " | " .. source)
		end
		if espRow then
			local unknown = 0
			for _, e in ipairs(cache) do if e.enemy == nil then unknown = unknown + 1 end end
			local err = P.espErr or P.scanErr
			espRow.Text = "pf-esp-10 | Found " .. #cache .. " models (" .. #collect(false) .. " enemy, " .. unknown .. " unknown) | drawn " .. tostring(P.drawn or 0) .. " | failed " .. tostring(P.renderFailed or 0) .. (err and (" | ERROR: " .. err) or "")
		end
		if statusRow then
			statusRow.Text = "Enemies: " .. #collect(false) .. " | " .. (P.aimState or "off") ..
				(P.aimErr and (" | ERROR: " .. P.aimErr) or "")
		end
		task.wait(0.3)
	end
end)
local function report()
	local out = {}
	local function add(s) table.insert(out, s) end
	add("ESP revision: pf-esp-10")
	add("built-in team detection status=" .. tostring(P.autoTeamStatus) .. " source=" .. tostring(P.calibrationSource))
	add(tostring(P.rosterStatus))
	add(tostring(P.ownerStatus))
	local faction, source = myFaction()
	add("calibrated teammate group=" .. tostring(P.myGroup and P.myGroup.Name) .. " enemy group=" .. tostring(P.enemyGroup and P.enemyGroup.Name))
	add("selected team mode=" .. tostring(P.teamMode) .. " faction=" .. tostring(faction) .. " source=" .. source)
	add("scan error=" .. tostring(P.scanErr) .. " render error=" .. tostring(P.espErr))
	add("aim method=" .. tostring(P.aimMethod) .. " state=" .. tostring(P.aimState) .. " error=" .. tostring(P.aimErr) .. " ESP team check=" .. tostring(P.teamCheck))
	add("enabled=" .. tostring(P.esp) .. " showTeam=" .. tostring(P.showTeam) .. " maxDistance=" .. tostring(P.espMax) .. " drawn=" .. tostring(P.drawn or 0))
	add("PlaceId " .. game.PlaceId .. " | team " .. tostring(LocalPlayer.Team and LocalPlayer.Team.Name) .. " | teamcolor " .. LocalPlayer.TeamColor.Name)
	add("mousemoverel=" .. tostring(type(env.mousemoverel)) .. " mouse1press=" .. tostring(type(env.mouse1press)) .. " mouse1click=" .. tostring(type(env.mouse1click)))
	add("detected total=" .. #cache .. " enemies=" .. #collect(false))
	for i, e in ipairs(cache) do
		if i > 12 then break end
		add("model=" .. e.m.Name .. " folder=" .. tostring(e.folder) .. " enemy=" .. tostring(e.enemy) .. " owner=" .. tostring(e.player and e.player.Name))
	end
	local names = {}
	for i, ch in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
		if i > 60 then break end
		table.insert(names, ch.Name .. "(" .. ch.ClassName .. ")")
	end
	add("Workspace children: " .. table.concat(names, ", "))
	local function dumpRoot(root, label)
	if root then
		add(label .. " exists, class " .. root.ClassName .. " descendants=" .. #root:GetDescendants())
		for i, f in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
			if i > 6 then break end
			add("  " .. f.Name .. " (" .. f.ClassName .. ") children=" .. #f:GetChildren())
			for j, m in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
				if j > 2 then break end
				local kids = {}
				for k, p in ipairs(m:GetChildren()) do
					if k > 25 then break end
					table.insert(kids, p.Name .. "(" .. p.ClassName .. ")")
				end
				add("    " .. m.Name .. " (" .. m.ClassName .. "): " .. table.concat(kids, ", "))
			end
		end
	else
		add(label .. " does NOT exist")
	end
	end
	dumpRoot(playersRoot(), "Workspace.Players")
	dumpRoot(Workspace:FindFirstChild("Roots"), "Workspace.Roots")
	local text = table.concat(out, "\n")
	copyText(text)
	notify("Diagnostic copied - paste it to me", T.Good)
end
local function panic()
	P.aim, P.trig, P.esp, P.radar, P.alert, P.chams = false, false, false, false, false, false
	for _, o in ipairs(tgs) do pcall(function() o.Set(false) end) end
	clearEsp()
end
onUnload(function()
	P.aim, P.trig, P.esp, P.radar, P.alert = false, false, false, false, false
	clearEsp()
	fovGui:Destroy()
	radar:Destroy()
	pcall(press, false)
end)
