		lt2 = [==[
-- Solos Hub game module: lt2.lua
-- Loaded by the main hub script (picker -> loadstring). Do not run this file on its own.
--
-- Mechanics ported from the user's previous script: cut = Interaction.RemoteProxy:FireServer(CutEvent, args),
-- logs live in Workspace.LogModels, trees in Workspace.*TreeRegion* folders, ownership of a log is claimed by
-- spamming Interaction.ClientIsDragging before moving its parts. UNTESTED in game.
--
-- v3: additional features adapted from the user's supplied Dynxe reference.
-- Existing pages and shared GUI are preserved. Adds lasso and pyramid selection,
-- wood/length matching, owned-plank bulk selection, blueprint filling and purchases,
-- and the Rukiryaxe material/collection flow. See LT2_FEATURES.md for scope and tests.
-- These game interactions require live LT2 verification; source-specific remote
-- and world paths are checked at runtime rather than assumed to be available.

local Hub, S, T, C = ...
local API = Hub.API
local Live = API.Live -- live values: Live.Running, Live.Unlocked, Live.BindingActive
local Dropdown, Collapse, PlayerList = API.Dropdown, API.Collapse, API.PlayerList
local Input = API.Input

local AddTab = API.AddTab
local Button = API.Button
local EspGui = API.EspGui

if game.PlaceId ~= 13822889 then notify("You're not in Lumber Tycoon 2 - this module needs that game", T.Warn) end
local Panel = Hub.GamePanel("Lumber Tycoon 2", "autofarm", "Trees, logs and base tools.")
local TreeTab = Panel.Sub("Wood", "autofarm", "Find, chop and collect trees.")
local LogTab = Panel.Sub("Logs", "loot", "Move and sell logs.")
local PlotTab = Panel.Sub("Plot", "lock", "Land, save slots and cleanup.")
local ShopTab = Panel.Sub("Shop", "premium", "Buy from the stores.")
local ToolsTab = Panel.Sub("Tools", "magnet", "Select, move and sort objects.")
local VehicleTab = Panel.Sub("Vehicle", "speed", "Vehicle tuning and pad spawner.")
local WorldTab = Panel.Sub("World", "night", "Map scenery toggles.")
local ProtTab = Panel.Sub("Protection", "godmode", "Axe recovery.")

TabButtons["Lumber Tycoon 2"].Btn.LayoutOrder = -19
filterTabs({ "Lumber Tycoon 2", "Dashboard", "Player", "ESP", "Players", "Teleports", "Commands", "Hub Chat", "Theme", "Settings" })

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Interaction = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Interaction")
local RemoteProxy = Interaction and Interaction:FindFirstChild("RemoteProxy")
local DragRemote = Interaction and Interaction:FindFirstChild("ClientIsDragging")

local LT = {
	class = nil, classes = {}, pick = 1, busy = false, token = 0, lock = nil,
	fires = 60, fireDelay = 1, sweep = 1, back = true, bring = true,
	sellPos = Vector3.new(315, 0, 88), sellWrap = 8,
	esp = false, espAll = false, espMax = 1500, stopKey = Enum.KeyCode.X,
}
local LTExtra = { pickers = {}, stopHooks = {} }
local PICK_MODES = { "Biggest Tree", "Nearest Tree" }
local statusRow, axeRow, countRow, logRow
local showTreePreview

-- ---------------------------------------------------- axe data (from the old script's LT2Axes module)
-- { baseDamage, [TreeClass] = damage against that class }
local AXES = {
	["The Many Axe"] = { 10.2 }, ["Amber Axe"] = { 3.39 }, ["Bluesteel Axe"] = { 2.8, BlueSpruce = 12.1 },
	["Johiro"] = { 1.8 }, ["Candy Corn Axe"] = { 1.75 }, ["Rukiryaxe"] = { 1.68 },
	["Bird Axe"] = { 1.65, CaveCrawler = 3.9, Volcano = 2.5 }, ["Silver Axe"] = { 1.6 },
	["End Times Axe"] = { 1.58, LoneCave = 1e7 }, ["Alpha Axe of Testing"] = { 1.5 }, ["Pig Axe"] = { 1.5 },
	["Hardened Axe"] = { 1.45 }, ["Beta Axe of Bosses"] = { 1.45 }, ["Beesaxe"] = { 1.4 },
	["Gingerbread Axe"] = { 1.2, Koa = 11, Walnut = 8.5 }, ["Pie Axe"] = { 0.95, Cherry = 1.9 },
	["Steel Axe"] = { 0.93 }, ["CHICKEN AXE"] = { 0.9 }, ["OverGrown Axe"] = { 0.8, GreenSwampy = 7, GoldSwampy = 5.3 },
	["Spearmint Axe"] = { 0.8 }, ["Fire Axe"] = { 0.6, Volcano = 6.35 }, ["Plain Axe"] = { 0.55 },
	["Rusty Axe"] = { 0.55 }, ["Cave Axe"] = { 0.4, CaveCrawler = 7.2 }, ["Frost Axe"] = { 0.36, Frost = 6 },
	["Basic Hatchet"] = { 0.2 }, ["Candy Cane Axe"] = { 0 }, ["Refined Axe"] = { 0 }, ["Inverse Axe"] = { -1 },
}
local function axeDamage(name, class)
	local a = AXES[name]
	if not a then return 1 end
	local v = class and a[class]
	if v == nil then v = a[1] end
	return v
end
local function axeNameOf(tool)
	local v = tool:FindFirstChild("ToolTip")
	if v and v:IsA("StringValue") then return v.Value end
	local ok, tip = pcall(function() return tool.ToolTip end)
	if ok and type(tip) == "string" then return tip end
	return nil
end
local function bestAxe(class)
	local best, bestName, bestScore
	local function try(tool)
		if not tool:IsA("Tool") or tool.Name == "BlueprintTool" then return end
		local nm = axeNameOf(tool)
		if not nm or nm == "" then return end
		local low = nm:lower()
		if not AXES[nm] and not low:find("axe", 1, true) and not low:find("hatchet", 1, true) then return end
		local score = axeDamage(nm, class)
		if not bestScore or score > bestScore then best, bestName, bestScore = tool, nm, score end
	end
	local ch = getChar()
	if ch then for _, c in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do try(c) end end
	local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
	if bp then for _, c in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do try(c) end end
	return best, bestName, bestScore
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- tree / log helpers
local function regions()
	local out = {}
	for _, f in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
		if f.Name:lower():find("treeregion", 1, true) then table.insert(out, f) end
	end
	return out
end
local function classOf(tree)
	local tc = tree:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")
	return tc and tc:IsA("StringValue") and tc.Value or nil
end
local function sectionsOf(tree)
	local s = {}
	for _, p in ipairs(tree:GetChildren()) do
		if p.Name == "WoodSection" and p:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(s, p) end
	end
	table.sort(s, function(a, b) return a.Position.Y < b.Position.Y end)
	return s
end
local function baseSection(tree)
	local list = sectionsOf(tree)
	local base = list[1]
	for _, s in ipairs(list) do
		local id = s:FindFirstChild("ID")
		if id and id.Value == 1 then base = s break end
	end
	return base
end
local function scanClasses()
	local seen, list = {}, {}
	for _, f in ipairs(regions()) do
		for _, m in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
			if m:IsA("Model") then
				local c = classOf(m)
				if c and not seen[c] and #sectionsOf(m) > 1 then seen[c] = true table.insert(list, c) end
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(list)
	LT.classes = list
	return list
end
-- class == nil means any class. mode 1 = most sections, 2 = closest to you.
local function pickTree(class, mode)
	local r = getRoot()
	local best, bestV
	for _, f in ipairs(regions()) do
		for _, m in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
			if m:IsA("Model") and (class == nil or classOf(m) == class) and classOf(m) then
				local n = #sectionsOf(m)
				local skip = n < 2 or (mode == 1 and classOf(m) == "Generic" and n < 12)
				if not skip then
					local v
					if mode == 1 and class ~= nil then
						v = -n
					else
						local ok, pv = pcall(function() return m:GetPivot().Position end)
						v = (ok and r) and (pv - r.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
					end
					if not bestV or v < bestV then best, bestV = m, v end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return best
end
local function ownedByMe(model)
	local o = model:FindFirstChild("Owner")
	if o then
		if o:IsA("ObjectValue") and o.Value == LocalPlayer then return true end
		if o:IsA("StringValue") and o.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then return true end
		local os2 = o:FindFirstChild("OwnerString")
		if os2 and os2:IsA("StringValue") and os2.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then return true end
	end
	local s = model:FindFirstChild("OwnerString")
	return s ~= nil and s:IsA("StringValue") and s.Value == LocalPlayer.Name
end
local function myLogs()
	local out = {}
	local lm = Workspace:FindFirstChild("LogModels")
	if lm then for _, m in ipairs(lm:GetChildren()) do if m:IsA("Model") and ownedByMe(m) then table.insert(out, m) end end end
	return out
end
local function snapshotLogs()
	local snap, lm = {}, Workspace:FindFirstChild("LogModels")
	if lm then for _, m in ipairs(lm:GetChildren()) do snap[m] = true end end
	return snap
end
-- new log models of this class that are mine (or whose owner label hasn't loaded yet)
local function newLogs(class, snap)
	local out, lm = {}, Workspace:FindFirstChild("LogModels")
	if not lm then return out end
	for _, m in ipairs(lm:GetChildren()) do
		if not snap[m] and m:IsA("Model") then
			local c = m:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")
			if c and c.Value == class and (m:FindFirstChild("Owner") == nil or ownedByMe(m)) then table.insert(out, m) end
		end
	end
	return out
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- preview widget (rotating 3D model or an image, with a caption)
local function makePreview(parent, height, caption)
	local f = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, parent)
	local box = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, -26), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 6), BackgroundColor3 = T.Bg, BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true }, f)
	round(box, 8)
	local vp = new("ViewportFrame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 190), LightColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Visible = false }, box)
	local cam = new("Camera", { FieldOfView = 50 }, vp)
	vp.CurrentCamera = cam
	local img = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit, Visible = false }, box)
	local cap = new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, -19), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = caption, TextColor3 = T.Sub, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd }, f)
	local obj, shown = {}, nil
	local center, dist, rad, ang = Vector3.zero, 10, 5, 0
	local function clear()
		if shown then shown:Destroy() shown = nil end
		vp.Visible = false
		img.Visible = false
	end
	function obj.Clear(text) clear() cap.Text = text or caption end
	function obj.Caption(text) cap.Text = text end
	function obj.Image(asset, text)
		clear()
		img.Image = asset
		img.Visible = true
		cap.Text = text or ""
	end
	function obj.Model(model, text)
		clear()
		cap.Text = text or ""
		if not model then return end
		if LT.previews == false then cap.Text = (text or "") .. "  (preview off)" return end
		local ok, c = pcall(function() return model:Clone() end)
		if not (ok and c) then cap.Text = (text or "") .. "  (no preview)" return end
		for _, d in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BaseScript") or d:IsA("Sound") then d:Destroy() end
		end
		local cf, size
		if c:IsA("BasePart") then
			cf, size = c.CFrame, c.Size
		else
			local okb, a, b = pcall(c.GetBoundingBox, c)
			if okb then cf, size = a, b end
		end
		if not cf then c:Destroy() cap.Text = (text or "") .. "  (no preview)" return end
		c.Parent = vp
		shown = c
		center = cf.Position
		rad = math.max(size.Magnitude / 2, 1)
		dist = rad / math.tan(math.rad(cam.FieldOfView / 2)) * 1.1
		vp.Visible = true
	end
	local conn
	conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		if not f.Parent then conn:Disconnect() return end
		if not shown or not vp.Visible then return end
		ang = ang + dt * 0.7
		cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(center + Vector3.new(math.cos(ang) * dist, rad * 0.4, math.sin(ang) * dist), center)
	end)
	return obj
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- status / job runner
local function setStatus(text) if statusRow then statusRow.Text = "Status: " .. text end end
local function dropLock() if LT.lock then LT.lock:Disconnect() LT.lock = nil end end
local function stopAll()
	LT.token = LT.token + 1 LT.busy = false dropLock() setStatus("Idle")
	for _, stop in ipairs(LTExtra.stopHooks) do pcall(stop) end
end
local function runJob(label, fn)
	if LT.busy then notify("Already running - press Stop first", T.Warn) return end
	if not RemoteProxy or not DragRemote then
		Interaction = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Interaction")
		RemoteProxy = Interaction and Interaction:FindFirstChild("RemoteProxy")
		DragRemote = Interaction and Interaction:FindFirstChild("ClientIsDragging")
	end
	if not RemoteProxy or not DragRemote then notify("LT2 remotes not found - are you in Lumber Tycoon 2?", T.Bad) return end
	LT.token = LT.token + 1
	local my = LT.token
	LT.busy = true
	setStatus(label)
	task.spawn(function()
		local alive = function() return Live.Running and LT.token == my end
		local good, err = pcall(fn, alive)
		if not good then notify("LT2 error: " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 70), T.Bad) end
		if LT.token == my then LT.busy = false dropLock() setStatus("Idle") end
	end)
end
local function waitRespawn(alive)
	local deadline = os.clock() + 30
	repeat task.wait(0.2) until not alive() or os.clock() > deadline or (getHum() and getHum().Health > 0 and getRoot())
	task.wait(1)
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- cutting
local function cutSection(section, tool, axeName, class, stop)
	if not section or not section.Parent then return end
	local idObj = section:FindFirstChild("ID")
	if not idObj then return end
	local cutEvent = section:FindFirstChild("CutEvent") or section.Parent:FindFirstChild("CutEvent") or (section.Parent.Parent and section.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("CutEvent"))
	if not cutEvent then return end
	local sizeY = section.Size.Y
	local args = {
		sectionId = idObj.Value, faceVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1),
		height = sizeY * math.clamp(0.1 + (8 - sizeY) / 60, 0.1, 0.2),
		hitPoints = axeDamage(axeName, class), cooldown = 0, cuttingClass = "Axe", tool = tool,
	}
	for _ = 1, LT.fires do
		if not section.Parent or stop() then break end
		RemoteProxy:FireServer(cutEvent, args)
		task.wait(math.max(LT.fireDelay / 100, 0.005))
	end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- moving logs (claim ownership, then set CFrame)
local function claimDrag(model, timeout, alive)
	local owner = model:FindFirstChild("Owner")
	local li = (owner and owner:FindFirstChild("LastInteraction")) or model:FindFirstChild("LastInteraction")
	local changed, conn = false, nil
	if li then conn = li:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function() changed = true end) end
	local deadline = os.clock() + timeout
	while Live.Running and (not alive or alive()) and os.clock() < deadline and not changed do
		pcall(DragRemote.FireServer, DragRemote, model)
		task.wait()
	end
	if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
	return changed
end
local function moveLog(part, goalCF)
	if not part or not part.Parent then return false end
	local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or part.Parent
	local r = getRoot()
	if r then
		local flat = (r.Position - part.Position) * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
		if flat.Magnitude > 10 then r.CFrame = CFrame.new(part.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) task.wait(0.05) end
	end
	claimDrag(model, 1)
	if part.Parent then part.CFrame = goalCF end
	task.wait(0.1)
	return true
end
local function bringParts(parts, alive, center)
	local r = getRoot()
	if not r then return end
	local home = r.CFrame
	center = center or home
	for i, p in ipairs(parts) do
		if not alive() then break end
		local col, row = (i - 1) % 5, math.floor((i - 1) / 5)
		moveLog(p, center * CFrame.new(col * 5, 0, -6 - row * 6))
	end
	local rr = getRoot()
	if rr and LT.back then rr.CFrame = home end
end
local function innerWoods(models)
	local out = {}
	for _, m in ipairs(models) do
		local iw = m:FindFirstChild("InnerWood")
		if iw and iw:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(out, iw) end
	end
	return out
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- chop one tree (teleport onto the base, cut until it falls)
local function chopTree(tree, alive)
	local class = classOf(tree)
	if not class then return false end
	local tool, axeName = bestAxe(class)
	if not tool then notify("No axe found in your backpack", T.Warn) return false end
	if showTreePreview then showTreePreview(tree, "Chopping") end
	local r = getRoot()
	if not r or #sectionsOf(tree) == 0 then return false end
	local snap = snapshotLogs()
	local origin = r.CFrame
	local base = baseSection(tree)
	r.CFrame = base.CFrame
	r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	dropLock()
	LT.lock = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		local rr = getRoot()
		if rr and base and base.Parent then rr.CFrame = base.CFrame rr.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end
	end)
	task.wait(0.1)
	local lastCheck, fellNow = 0, false
	local function fell()
		if fellNow then return true end
		if os.clock() - lastCheck > 0.25 then
			lastCheck = os.clock()
			fellNow = tree.Parent == nil or #newLogs(class, snap) > 0
		end
		return fellNow
	end
	while alive() and not fell() do
		local h = getHum()
		if not h or h.Health <= 0 then
			waitRespawn(alive)
			if not alive() then break end
			tool, axeName = bestAxe(class)
			if not tool then notify("No axe after respawn - stopped", T.Warn) break end
		end
		base = baseSection(tree)
		if not base then break end
		cutSection(base, tool, axeName, class, function() return not alive() or fell() end)
		task.wait(LT.sweep / 10)
	end
	dropLock()
	if not alive() then return false end
	task.wait(0.3)
	local rr = getRoot()
	if rr and LT.back then rr.CFrame = origin end
	if LT.bring then
		local stumps = innerWoods(newLogs(class, snap))
		if #stumps > 0 then setStatus("Bringing logs") bringParts(stumps, alive) end
	end
	return true
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- selling (sections one by one onto the sell spot)
local function sellLogs(alive)
	local models = myLogs()
	if #models == 0 then notify("You have no logs to sell", T.Warn) return end
	local r = getRoot()
	local home = r and r.CFrame
	local idx = 0
	for _, m in ipairs(models) do
		if not alive() then break end
		local secs = {}
		for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name == "WoodSection" then table.insert(secs, d) end
		end
		table.sort(secs, function(a, b) return a.Position.Y < b.Position.Y end)
		for _, s in ipairs(secs) do
			if not alive() then break end
			if s.Parent then
				for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
					if (d:IsA("Weld") or d:IsA("WeldConstraint") or d:IsA("ManualWeld")) and (d.Part0 == s or d.Part1 == s) then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
				end
				task.wait(0.1)
				idx = idx + 1
				local slot = (idx - 1) % LT.sellWrap
				moveLog(s, CFrame.new(LT.sellPos.X + slot * 3, LT.sellPos.Y, LT.sellPos.Z))
			end
		end
	end
	local rr = getRoot()
	if rr and home and LT.back then rr.CFrame = home end
end

-- ====================================================== UI : Trees tab
local Tf = Section(TreeTab, "Tree Finder", "Pick what to chop - the best axe in your backpack is chosen automatically", "autofarm")
statusRow = InfoRow(Tf, "Status: Idle")
axeRow = InfoRow(Tf, "Best axe: -")
countRow = InfoRow(Tf, "Trees of this type: -")
local typeDd
local treePrev = makePreview(Tf, 170, "Next tree preview")
local typeSearch = ""
local function shownClasses()
	local all = LT.classes or {}
	if typeSearch == "" then return all end
	local out = {}
	for _, c in ipairs(all) do
		if c:lower():find(typeSearch, 1, true) then table.insert(out, c) end
	end
	return out
end
showTreePreview = function(tree, prefix)
	if not tree or not tree.Parent then treePrev.Clear("No tree found") LT.prevTree = nil return end
	local class = classOf(tree) or "?"
	local ok, pv = pcall(function() return tree:GetPivot().Position end)
	local r = getRoot()
	local text = (prefix and (prefix .. ": ") or "") .. class .. "  -  " .. #sectionsOf(tree) .. " sections"
	if ok and r then text = text .. "  -  " .. math.floor((pv - r.Position).Magnitude) .. "m away" end
	if LT.prevTree == tree then treePrev.Caption(text) return end
	LT.prevTree = tree
	treePrev.Model(tree, text)
end
local function refreshInfo()
	local c = LT.class
	if not c then
		axeRow.Text = "Best axe: -"
		countRow.Text = "Trees of this type: -"
		treePrev.Clear("Pick a tree type")
		LT.prevTree = nil
		return
	end
	local _, nm, dmg = bestAxe(c)
	axeRow.Text = nm and ("Best axe: " .. nm .. "  (damage " .. tostring(dmg) .. ")") or "Best axe: none found in your backpack"
	local n = 0
	for _, f in ipairs(regions()) do for _, m in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do if m:IsA("Model") and classOf(m) == c and #sectionsOf(m) > 1 then n = n + 1 end end end
	countRow.Text = "Trees of this type: " .. n
	if not LT.busy then showTreePreview(pickTree(c, LT.pick)) end
end
local function syncType()
	if not typeDd then return end
	local shown = shownClasses()
	local opts = shown
	if #opts == 0 then opts = { (LT.classes and #LT.classes > 0) and "No match" or "None" } end
	typeDd.SetOptions(opts, true)
	local at = 1
	for i, c in ipairs(opts) do if c == LT.class then at = i end end
	typeDd.Set(at, true)
end
local function setClass(c)
	LT.class = c
	syncType()
	refreshInfo()
end
local function refreshTypes()
	local list = scanClasses()
	local keep = false
	for _, c in ipairs(list) do if c == LT.class then keep = true end end
	if not keep then setClass(list[1]) else refreshInfo() end
	notify("Found " .. #list .. " tree type(s)", T.Good)
end
local function applyTypeSearch(text)
	typeSearch = (text or ""):lower()
	local shown = shownClasses()
	local has = false
	for _, c in ipairs(shown) do if c == LT.class then has = true end end
	if not has and shown[1] then LT.class = shown[1] LT.prevTree = nil end
	syncType()
	refreshInfo()
end
local typeBox = Input(Tf, "Search Tree Types", "type to filter", "Search", function(text) applyTypeSearch(text) end)
bind(typeBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function() applyTypeSearch(typeBox.Text) end)
typeDd = Dropdown(Tf, "Tree Type", { "None" }, 1, function(i)
	local c = shownClasses()[i]
	if c then LT.class = c LT.prevTree = nil refreshInfo() end
end, nil, "Press Scan to list the tree types in this server")
Button(Tf, "Refresh Tree Types", "Scan", refreshTypes)
Dropdown(Tf, "Which Tree First", PICK_MODES, LT.pick, function(i) LT.pick = i LT.prevTree = nil refreshInfo() end)
Button(Tf, "Teleport To Selected Tree", "Go", function()
	local tree, r = pickTree(LT.class, LT.pick), getRoot()
	local b = tree and baseSection(tree)
	if b and r then r.CFrame = b.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0) else notify("No tree of that type found", T.Warn) end
end)

-- ====================================================== extra sections in Teleports
-- vehicle-aware teleport: when you sit in a vehicle it is moved with you (loose cargo on known pickups / trucks too)
local VEHICLE_PRESETS = {
	Pickup1_Vehicle = { Size = Vector3.new(10, 6, 18), Offset = CFrame.new(0, 3, 11.5) },
	UtilityTruck_Vehicle = { Size = Vector3.new(7, 3, 10), Offset = CFrame.new(0, 1.75, 5.75) },
	UtilityTruck2_Vehicle = { Size = Vector3.new(8, 3, 11), Offset = CFrame.new(0, 1.75, 6) },
}
local function vehicleTp(veh, pos)
	local target = CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
	local anch = {}
	for _, d in ipairs(veh:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") then anch[d] = d.Anchored d.Anchored = true end
	end
	local nameV = veh:FindFirstChild("ItemName")
	local preset = nameV and nameV:IsA("StringValue") and VEHICLE_PRESETS[nameV.Value]
	if preset then
		local pivot = veh:GetPivot()
		local move = target * pivot:Inverse()
		local op = OverlapParams.new()
		op.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		op.FilterDescendantsInstances = { veh, getChar() }
		op.RespectCanCollide = false
		local done = {}
		for _, part in ipairs(Workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(pivot * preset.Offset, preset.Size, op)) do
			if not part.Anchored then
				local m = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
				if m and m ~= Workspace then
					if not done[m] then done[m] = true m:PivotTo(move * m:GetPivot()) end
				elseif not done[part] then
					done[part] = true
					part.CFrame = move * part.CFrame
				end
			end
		end
	end
	veh:PivotTo(target)
	task.wait(0.05)
	for p, a in pairs(anch) do if p.Parent then p.Anchored = a end end
end
local function tpTo(v)
	local r, h = getRoot(), getHum()
	if not r then return end
	local seat = h and h.SeatPart
	local veh = seat and seat:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
	if veh and LT.carry ~= false and veh ~= getChar() and (veh:FindFirstChild("Configuration", true) or veh:FindFirstChild("ItemName")) then
		vehicleTp(veh, v)
		return
	end
	if h and h.SeatPart then h.Sit = false task.wait(0.05) end
	r.CFrame = CFrame.new(v + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
end
local function tpSection(title, sub, list)
	local sec = Section(Tabs["Teleports"], title, sub, "teleports")
	local names = {}
	for i, e in ipairs(list) do names[i] = e[1] end
	local dd = Dropdown(sec, "Destination", names, 1)
	Button(sec, "Teleport", "Go", function() local e = list[dd.Get()] if e then tpTo(e[2]) end end)
end

	local props = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Properties")
	local pp = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("PropertyPurchasing")
	local claim = pp and pp:FindFirstChild("ClientPurchasedProperty")
	if not props or not claim then notify("Property system not found", T.Bad) return end
	for _, plot in ipairs(props:GetChildren()) do
		local owner, origin = plot:FindFirstChild("Owner"), plot:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare")
		if owner and origin and owner.Value == nil then
			claim:FireServer(plot, origin.OriginCFrame.Value.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
			notify("Claim sent", T.Good)
			task.wait(0.5)
			local ch = getChar()
			if ch then ch:MoveTo(origin.Position) end
			return
		end
	end
	notify("No free plot found", T.Warn)
end)

	for _, plot in ipairs(props and props:GetChildren() or {}) do
		local owner, origin = plot:FindFirstChild("Owner"), plot:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare")
		if owner and origin and owner.Value == LocalPlayer then tpTo(origin.Position) return end
	end
	notify("You don't own a plot yet", T.Warn)
end)

-- ====================================================== v2: new tabs ported from the Dynxe script (UNTESTED in game)
-- Everything below finds its remotes / folders defensively. If the game changed, you get a toast instead of an error.
local function commas(n)
	local s = tostring(math.floor(n))
	local out = s:reverse():gsub("(%d%d%d)", "%1,"):reverse()
	return (out:gsub("^,", ""))
end
local cleanups = {}
local function addCleanup(f) table.insert(cleanups, f) end
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local mouse = LocalPlayer:GetMouse()

local function mainPartOf(m)
	if not m then return nil end
	if m:IsA("BasePart") then return m end
	return m:FindFirstChild("Main") or m.PrimaryPart or m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end
local function topModel(inst, pm, lmf)
	local cur = inst
	while cur and cur ~= Workspace do
		local p = cur.Parent
		if p and ((pm and p == pm) or (lmf and p == lmf)) then return cur end
		cur = p
	end
	return nil
end
local function myPlot()
	local props = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Properties")
	if not props then return nil end
	for _, plot in ipairs(props:GetChildren()) do
		local o = plot:FindFirstChild("Owner")
		if o and o.Value == LocalPlayer then return plot end
	end
	return nil
end
local function async(fn)
	task.spawn(function()
		local ok, err = pcall(fn)
		if not ok then notify("LT2 error: " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 70), T.Bad) end
	end)
end

-- Shared helpers for the added LT2 tools. No external loader is used.
function LTExtra.activatePicker(name)
	for id, stop in pairs(LTExtra.pickers) do if id ~= name then stop() end end
end
function LTExtra.withReturn(fn)
	local root, token = getRoot(), LT.token
	local home = root and root.CFrame
	local ok, err = pcall(fn)
	-- Do not move the player out of a newer job or during unload.
	if Live.Running and LT.token <= token + 1 and home then
		local current = getRoot()
		if current then current.CFrame = home current.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end
	end
	if not ok then error(err) end
end
function LTExtra.moveOwned(model, goal, alive, timeout)
	if not alive() or not model.Parent or not ownedByMe(model) then return false end
	local part, root = mainPartOf(model), getRoot()
	if not (part and part:IsA("BasePart") and not part.Anchored and root) then return false end
	if (root.Position - part.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
		root.CFrame = CFrame.new(part.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
		task.wait(0.05)
	end
	if not alive() then return false end
	claimDrag(model, timeout or 2, alive)
	if not alive() or not part.Parent or not ownedByMe(model) then return false end
	local pivot = goal * part.CFrame:Inverse() * model:GetPivot()
	model:PivotTo(pivot)
	part.AssemblyLinearVelocity, part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero, Vector3.zero
	task.wait(0.1)
	return true
end
function LTExtra.isBlueprint(model)
	local kind = model and model:FindFirstChild("Type")
	return model ~= nil and model:IsA("Model") and ownedByMe(model) and kind ~= nil and kind.Value == "Blueprint"
end
function LTExtra.scanPlanks(class, low, high)
	local result, folder = {}, Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
	if not folder or low > high then return result end
	for _, model in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if model:IsA("Model") and model.Name == "Plank" and ownedByMe(model) then
			local part, treeClass = mainPartOf(model), classOf(model)
			if part and part:IsA("BasePart") and not part.Anchored and treeClass and
				(not class or class == treeClass) and part.Size.Y >= low and part.Size.Y <= high then
				table.insert(result, { model = model, part = part, class = treeClass, sizeY = part.Size.Y })
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(result, function(a, b) return a.sizeY < b.sizeY end)
	return result
end
function LTExtra.sameSelectionKind(a, b, matchLength)
	if a.Name ~= b.Name then return false end
	if a.Name == "Plank" then
		if classOf(a) ~= classOf(b) then return false end
		if matchLength then
			local pa, pb = mainPartOf(a), mainPartOf(b)
			if not pa or not pb or math.abs(pa.Size.Y - pb.Size.Y) > 0.05 then return false end
		end
	end
	return true
end
function LTExtra.layout(count, columns, layers, rows, pyramid)
	local raw = {}
	if count <= 0 then return raw end
	for layer = 0, layers - 1 do
		local width = pyramid and (columns - layer) or columns
		local depth = pyramid and (rows - layer) or rows
		if width <= 0 or depth <= 0 then break end
		local inset = pyramid and layer * 0.5 or 0
		for row = 0, depth - 1 do
			for col = 0, width - 1 do
				table.insert(raw, Vector3.new(col + inset, layer, row + inset))
				if #raw == count then break end
			end
			if #raw == count then break end
		end
		if #raw == count then break end
	end
	if #raw < count then return nil, "Selection exceeds layout capacity; increase X / Y / Z" end
	local x, z = 0, 0
	for _, point in ipairs(raw) do x = x + point.X z = z + point.Z end
	for i, point in ipairs(raw) do raw[i] = Vector3.new(point.X - x / count, point.Y, point.Z - z / count) end
	return raw
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- WOOD: get trees (quantity) + chop logs into sections
local function cutAtHeight(section, tool, axeName, class, height, stop)
	local idObj = section:FindFirstChild("ID")
	if not idObj then return end
	local cutEvent = section:FindFirstChild("CutEvent") or section.Parent:FindFirstChild("CutEvent") or (section.Parent.Parent and section.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("CutEvent"))
	if not cutEvent then return end
	local args = {
		sectionId = idObj.Value, faceVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1), height = height,
		hitPoints = axeDamage(axeName, class), cooldown = 0, cuttingClass = "Axe", tool = tool,
	}
	for _ = 1, LT.fires do
		if not section.Parent or stop() then break end
		RemoteProxy:FireServer(cutEvent, args)
		task.wait(math.max(LT.fireDelay / 100, 0.005))
	end
end
local function woodCount(model)
	local n = 0
	for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name == "WoodSection" then n = n + 1 end
	end
	return n
end
local function chopLogsIntoSections(alive)
	local lm = Workspace:FindFirstChild("LogModels")
	if not lm then notify("No LogModels folder", T.Warn) return end
	local queue = myLogs()
	if #queue == 0 then notify("None of your logs found", T.Warn) return end
	local cuts = 0
	while #queue > 0 and alive() do
		local model = table.remove(queue, 1)
		if model and model.Parent then
			local tc = model:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")
			local class = tc and tc.Value or "Generic"
			local tool, axeName = bestAxe(class)
			if not tool then notify("No axe in your backpack", T.Warn) return end
			local iw = model:FindFirstChild("InnerWood", true)
			local stump
			for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("Weld") or d:IsA("ManualWeld") then
					local p0, p1 = d.Part0, d.Part1
					if iw and p0 == iw and p1 and p1.Name == "WoodSection" then stump = p1 end
					if iw and p1 == iw and p0 and p0.Name == "WoodSection" then stump = p0 end
				end
			end
			local guard = 0
			while model.Parent and woodCount(model) > 1 and alive() and guard < 200 do
				guard = guard + 1
				local conn = {}
				for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
					if (d:IsA("Weld") or d:IsA("ManualWeld")) and d.Name == "Tree Weld" then
						local p0, p1 = d.Part0, d.Part1
						if p0 and p1 and p0.Name == "WoodSection" and p1.Name == "WoodSection" then
							conn[p0] = (conn[p0] or 0) + 1
							conn[p1] = (conn[p1] or 0) + 1
						end
					end
				end
				local tip
				for sec, n in pairs(conn) do
					if n == 1 and sec ~= stump and sec.Parent then tip = sec break end
				end
				if not tip then break end
				local weld, partner
				for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
					if (d:IsA("Weld") or d:IsA("ManualWeld")) and d.Name == "Tree Weld" then
						local p0, p1 = d.Part0, d.Part1
						if p0 == tip and p1 and p1.Name == "WoodSection" then weld, partner = d, p1 break end
						if p1 == tip and p0 and p0.Name == "WoodSection" then weld, partner = d, p0 break end
					end
				end
				if not weld then break end
				local cutPart = partner:FindFirstChild("ID") and partner or tip
				local jointPos = (weld.Part0.CFrame * weld.C0).Position
				local localY = (cutPart.CFrame:Inverse() * CFrame.new(jointPos)).Position.Y
				local height = math.clamp(localY + cutPart.Size.Y / 2, 0.05, cutPart.Size.Y - 0.05)
				local r = getRoot()
				if r then
					r.CFrame = CFrame.new(jointPos + cutPart.CFrame.RightVector * 4)
					r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
					task.wait(0.1)
				end
				local before = {}
				for _, m in ipairs(lm:GetChildren()) do before[m] = true end
				cutAtHeight(cutPart, tool, axeName, class, height, function() return not alive() end)
				cuts = cuts + 1
				task.wait(0.1)
				for _, m in ipairs(lm:GetChildren()) do
					if not before[m] and m:IsA("Model") and ownedByMe(m) and woodCount(m) > 1 then table.insert(queue, m) end
				end
				setStatus("Chopping logs - " .. cuts .. " cut(s), " .. #queue .. " queued")
			end
		end
	end
end

local Qt = Section(TreeTab, "Get Trees", "Chop several trees of the selected type in a row", "autofarm")
LT.qty = 1
Slider(Qt, "Quantity", 1, 25, 1, function(v) LT.qty = v end)
Button(Qt, "Get Trees  (selected type x quantity)", "Start", function()
	local c = LT.class
	if not c then notify("Pick a tree type first (Refresh Tree Types)", T.Warn) return end
	runJob("Getting " .. LT.qty .. " x " .. c, function(alive)
		for i = 1, LT.qty do
			if not alive() then break end
			setStatus("Tree " .. i .. " / " .. LT.qty)
			local tree = pickTree(c, LT.pick)
			if not tree then notify("No more " .. c .. " trees", T.Warn) break end
			chopTree(tree, alive)
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)
end, nil, "Chops, brings the logs, repeats")
Button(Qt, "Chop All My Logs Into Sections", "Chop", function()
	runJob("Chopping logs into sections", chopLogsIntoSections)
end, nil, "Cuts every log of yours into single sections, joint by joint")
Button(Qt, "Stop", "Stop", function() stopAll() notify("Stopped", T.Sub) end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- chop audio + preview switch
do
	local Au = Section(TreeTab, "Audio And Previews", "Quiet the chopping spam and switch the previews off if they cause trouble", "settings")
	local muteOn, muted = true, {}
	local function muteS(s)
		if muted[s] == nil then muted[s] = s.Volume end
		s.Volume = 0
	end
	Toggle(Au, "Mute Sounds While A Job Runs", true, function(v) muteOn = v end, nil, "lt2mute", "Silences sounds from the axe, your character and new world sounds while chopping / buying")
	local addConn = game.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
		if muteOn and LT.busy and d:IsA("Sound") then
			d.Volume = 0
			pcall(function() d:Stop() end)
		end
	end)
	addCleanup(function() addConn:Disconnect() end)
	task.spawn(function()
		local was = false
		while Live.Running do
			task.wait(0.25)
			local on = muteOn and LT.busy
			if on then
				for _, root in ipairs({ getChar(), LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack") }) do
					if root then
						for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
							if d:IsA("Sound") then muteS(d) end
						end
					end
				end
			elseif was then
				for s, v in pairs(muted) do
					if s.Parent then s.Volume = v end
					muted[s] = nil
				end
			end
			was = on
		end
	end)
	addCleanup(function() for s, v in pairs(muted) do if s.Parent then s.Volume = v end end end)
	Toggle(Au, "Show 3D / Item Previews", true, function(v)
		LT.previews = v
		LT.prevTree = nil
		if not v then treePrev.Clear("Previews off") else refreshInfo() end
	end, nil, "lt2previews", "Turn off if anything lags or sounds odd")
end

end, nil, nil, "Only works on things you own")

local plotCool = false

local function loadSaveRemote(name)
	local f = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("LoadSaveRequests")
	return f and f:FindFirstChild(name)
end
local Pm = Section(PlotTab, "Save Slots", "Load or save your base", "lock")
local slotDd = Dropdown(Pm, "Slot", { "1", "2", "3", "4", "5", "6" }, 1)
Button(Pm, "Load Selected Slot", "Load", function()
	local r = loadSaveRemote("RequestLoad")
	if not r then notify("RequestLoad remote not found", T.Bad) return end
	local slot = slotDd.Get()
	async(function() r:InvokeServer(slot) end)
	notify("Loading slot " .. slot, T.Good)
end)
Button(Pm, "Save Slot", "Save", function()
	if plotCool then notify("Save is on cooldown", T.Warn) return end
	local cur = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("CurrentSaveSlot")
	if not cur or cur.Value <= 0 then notify("No active slot - load a slot first", T.Warn) return end
	local r = loadSaveRemote("RequestSave")
	if not r then notify("RequestSave remote not found", T.Bad) return end
	local slot = cur.Value
	async(function()
		local ok, res = pcall(function() return r:InvokeServer(slot) end)
		if not ok then notify("Save failed: " .. tostring(res):sub(1, 60), T.Bad) return end
		notify("Save request sent for slot " .. slot, T.Good)
		plotCool = true
		for s = 60, 1, -1 do
			if not Live.Running then return end
			if plotStatus then plotStatus.Text = "Save cooldown: " .. s .. "s" end
			task.wait(1)
		end
		plotCool = false
		if plotStatus then plotStatus.Text = "Save ready" end
	end)
end, nil, "Forces a save of the slot you have loaded (60s cooldown)")
plotStatus = InfoRow(Pm, "Save ready")

	local props = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Properties")
	local pp = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("PropertyPurchasing")
	local claim = pp and pp:FindFirstChild("ClientPurchasedProperty")
	if not props or not claim then notify("Property system not found", T.Bad) return end
	for _, plot in ipairs(props:GetChildren()) do
		local owner, origin = plot:FindFirstChild("Owner"), plot:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare")
		if owner and origin and owner.Value == nil then
			claim:FireServer(plot, origin.OriginCFrame.Value.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))

	end

end)

	local pp = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("PropertyPurchasing")
	local expand = pp and pp:FindFirstChild("ClientExpandedProperty")
	local plot = myPlot()
	local origin = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare")
	if not (expand and origin) then notify("You need a plot first", T.Warn) return end
	local sp = origin.Position
	local offsets = {
		{ 0, 40 }, { 0, -40 }, { 40, 0 }, { -40, 0 }, { 40, 40 }, { 40, -40 }, { -40, 40 }, { -40, -40 },
		{ 80, 0 }, { -80, 0 }, { 0, 80 }, { 0, -80 }, { 80, 80 }, { 80, -80 }, { -80, 80 }, { -80, -80 },
		{ 40, 80 }, { -40, 80 }, { 80, 40 }, { 80, -40 }, { -80, 40 }, { -80, -40 }, { 40, -80 }, { -40, -80 },
	}
	async(function()
		for _, o in ipairs(offsets) do
			expand:FireServer(plot, CFrame.new(sp.X + o[1], sp.Y, sp.Z + o[2]))
			task.wait(0.05)
		end
		notify("Expand requests sent", T.Good)
	end)
end, nil, "Buys every neighbouring square the game allows")
Button(Pl, "Delete 'Sold To' Sign", "Delete", function()
	local interaction = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Interaction")
	local destroy = interaction and interaction:FindFirstChild("DestroyStructure")
	local pm = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
	if not (destroy and pm) then notify("DestroyStructure remote not found", T.Bad) return end
	for _, m in ipairs(pm:GetChildren()) do
		if m:IsA("Model") and ownedByMe(m) then
			local st = m:FindFirstChild("Settings")
			local flag = st and st:FindFirstChild("PropertySoldSign")
			if flag and flag.Value == true then
				destroy:FireServer(m)
				notify("Sign deleted", T.Good)
				return
			end
		end
	end
	notify("No sold sign found", T.Warn)
end)
local wipeArmed = 0
Button(Pl, "Wipe Plot  (press twice)", "Wipe", function()
	if os.clock() - wipeArmed > 5 then
		wipeArmed = os.clock()
		notify("Press Wipe again within 5s to delete EVERYTHING you own on the plot", T.Warn)
		return
	end
	wipeArmed = 0
	local interaction = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Interaction")
	local destroy = interaction and interaction:FindFirstChild("DestroyStructure")
	local pm = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
	if not (destroy and pm) then notify("Wipe system not available", T.Bad) return end
	async(function()
		local todo = {}
		for _, m in ipairs(pm:GetChildren()) do
			if m:IsA("Model") and ownedByMe(m) then table.insert(todo, m) end
		end
		if #todo == 0 then notify("Nothing to wipe", T.Warn) return end
		notify("Clearing " .. #todo .. " object(s)...", T.Warn)
		local gone = 0
		for _, m in ipairs(todo) do
			if not Live.Running then return end
			local t = 0
			while m.Parent ~= nil and t < 5 do
				pcall(destroy.FireServer, destroy, m)
				task.wait(0.05)
				t = t + 0.05
			end
			if m.Parent == nil then gone = gone + 1 end
		end
		notify("Wiped " .. gone .. " object(s)", T.Good)
	end)
end, nil, "Deletes everything of yours in PlayerModels (items, structures, furniture)")

local Pc = Section(PlotTab, "Plot Color", "Client-side only - recolours every plot square", "esp")

local function applyPlotColor()
	local props = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Properties")
	if not props then return end
	local col = Color3.fromRGB(pc.r, pc.g, pc.b)
	for _, plot in ipairs(props:GetChildren()) do
		for _, ch in ipairs(plot:GetChildren()) do
			if ch:IsA("BasePart") then ch.Color = col end
		end
	end
end

Slider(Pc, "Green", 0, 255, 104, function(v) pc.g = v end)
Slider(Pc, "Blue", 0, 255, 63, function(v) pc.b = v end)
Button(Pc, "Apply Plot Color", "Apply", applyPlotColor)
Toggle(Pc, "Keep Color On New Squares", false, function(v)
	pc.keep = v
	if pc.conn then pc.conn:Disconnect() pc.conn = nil end
	local props = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Properties")
	if v and props then
		pc.conn = props.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
			if d:IsA("BasePart") then task.defer(function() d.Color = Color3.fromRGB(pc.r, pc.g, pc.b) end) end
		end)
		applyPlotColor()
	end
end)
addCleanup(function() if pc.conn then pc.conn:Disconnect() end end)

local SH = { items = {}, all = {}, qty = 1, npcIds = {}, search = "" }
