local storeDd, itemDd, itemPrev

local function storeFolder(e)
	local s = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
	return s and s:FindFirstChild(e[2])
end
local function npcOf(e)
	local f = storeFolder(e)
	return f and f:FindFirstChild(e[3])
end
local function counterPart(e)
	local f = storeFolder(e)
	local c = f and f:FindFirstChild("Counter")
	if not c then return nil end
	if c:IsA("BasePart") then return c end
	return c.PrimaryPart or c:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end
local function dialogRemotes()
	local d = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("NPCDialog")
	return d and d:FindFirstChild("PromptChat"), d and d:FindFirstChild("PlayerChatted")
end
local function fetchFunds()
	local r = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GetFunds", true)
	if not (r and r:IsA("RemoteFunction")) then return nil end
	local ok, v = pcall(function() return r:InvokeServer() end)
	if ok and type(v) == "number" then return v end
	return nil
end
local function fetchNpcId(name, npc)
	local prompt = dialogRemotes()
	if not prompt then return nil end
	if not npc:FindFirstChild("Dialog") then Instance.new("Dialog", npc) end
	local data
	local c = prompt.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(active, chat)
		if active and chat and chat.Character == npc then data = chat end
	end)
	pcall(function() prompt:FireServer(true, npc, npc.Dialog) end)
	local t = os.clock()
	repeat task.wait(0.05) until data or os.clock() - t > 5
	c:Disconnect()
	pcall(function() prompt:FireServer(false, npc, npc.Dialog) end)
	task.wait(0.2)
	if data and data.ID then SH.npcIds[name] = data.ID return data.ID end
	return nil
end
local function safeInvoke(chatted, arg, action)
	local done, success = false, false
	task.spawn(function()
		success = pcall(function() chatted:InvokeServer(arg, action) end)
		done = true
	end)
	local t = os.clock()
	while not done and os.clock() - t < 4 do task.wait(0.03) end
	return done and success
end
local function inMine(inst)
	local pm = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
	if pm and inst:IsDescendantOf(pm) then
		local model = topModel(inst, pm)
		return model ~= nil and ownedByMe(model)
	end
	local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
	if bp and inst:IsDescendantOf(bp) then return true end
	local ch = getChar()
	return ch ~= nil and inst:IsDescendantOf(ch)
end
local function itemState(main)
	if not main.Parent then
		task.wait(0.12)
		if not main.Parent then return "gone" end
	end
	return inMine(main) and "success" or "pending"
end
-- boxes ("Box" models with BoxItemName + Main) live in Workspace.Stores.ShopItems folders; match a folder to a store by distance to its counter
local function shopItemsFor(e)
	local stores = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
	local cp = counterPart(e)
	if not (stores and cp) then return nil end
	local best, bestD
	for _, ch in ipairs(stores:GetChildren()) do
		if ch.Name == "ShopItems" then
			local sample = ch:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
			if sample then
				local d = (sample.Position - cp.Position).Magnitude
				if not bestD or d < bestD then best, bestD = ch, d end
			end
		end
	end
	return best
end
local function scanAllItems()
	SH.all = {}
	local info = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("ClientItemInfo")
	for si, e in ipairs(STORES) do
		local cont = shopItemsFor(e)
		local seen = {}
		if cont then
			for _, box in ipairs(cont:GetChildren()) do
				local nv = box.Name == "Box" and box:FindFirstChild("BoxItemName")
				if nv and nv:IsA("StringValue") and not seen[nv.Value] then
					seen[nv.Value] = true
					local f = info and info:FindFirstChild(nv.Value)
					local n = f and f:FindFirstChild("ItemName")
					local p = f and f:FindFirstChild("Price")
					local im = f and f:FindFirstChild("ItemImage")
					table.insert(SH.all, {
						key = nv.Value, name = n and tostring(n.Value) or nv.Value,
						price = p and tonumber((tostring(p.Value):gsub("[^%d]", ""))) or nil,
						image = im and im.Value or nil, store = si,
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(SH.all, function(a, b) return a.name < b.name end)
end
local function showItemPreview(i)
	local it = SH.items[i]
	if not it then itemPrev.Clear("No item selected") return end
	local cap = it.name .. (it.price and ("  -  $" .. commas(it.price)) or "") .. "  -  " .. STORES[it.store][1]
	local img = it.image and tostring(it.image) or ""
	if img ~= "" then
		if tonumber(img) then img = "rbxassetid://" .. img end
		itemPrev.Image(img, cap)
		return
	end
	local cont = shopItemsFor(STORES[it.store])
	if cont then
		for _, b in ipairs(cont:GetChildren()) do
			local nv = b.Name == "Box" and b:FindFirstChild("BoxItemName")
			if nv and nv.Value == it.key then itemPrev.Model(b, cap) return end
		end
	end
	itemPrev.Clear(cap)
end
local function refreshItems(rescan)
	if rescan or #SH.all == 0 then scanAllItems() end
	local only = storeDd.Get() - 1 -- 0 = all stores
	local q = SH.search
	SH.items = {}
	local names, total = {}, 0
	for _, it in ipairs(SH.all) do
		if (only == 0 or it.store == only) and (q == "" or it.name:lower():find(q, 1, true)) then
			total = total + 1
			if #SH.items < 40 then
				it.label = it.name .. (it.price and (" ($" .. commas(it.price) .. ")") or "") .. (only == 0 and ("  [" .. STORES[it.store][1] .. "]") or "")
				table.insert(SH.items, it)
				names[#names + 1] = it.label
			end
		end
	end
	if #names == 0 then names = { "No items found" } end
	itemDd.SetOptions(names)
	if shopStatus then
		shopStatus.Text = total .. " match(es)" .. (total > #SH.items and (" - showing the first " .. #SH.items .. ", refine your search") or "")
	end
	showItemPreview(1)
end
-- flow (same order the Dynxe script uses): item onto the counter first, THEN stand at the cashier and get the ID, then confirm,
-- then bring the item back to dropCF. The cashier ID can only be fetched while you are standing near the cashier.
local function buyBox(e, box, alive, dropCF)
	if not alive() then return false end
	local main = box:FindFirstChild("Main")
	local npc = npcOf(e)
	local _, chatted = dialogRemotes()
	local cp = counterPart(e)
	if not (main and npc and chatted and cp) then notify("Store parts not found", T.Bad) return false end
	-- 1. go to the item, take ownership, put it on the counter
	local r = getRoot()
	if r then r.CFrame = CFrame.new(main.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)) task.wait(0.15) end
	claimDrag(box, 2, alive)
	if not alive() then return false end
	if main.Parent then
		local up = cp.CFrame.UpVector
		main.CFrame = CFrame.new(cp.Position + up * (cp.Size.Y / 2 + main.Size.Y / 2 + 0.05))
		main.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		main.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	end
	task.wait(0.1)
	-- 2. stand at the cashier, then get the dialog ID
	if not alive() then return false end
	r = getRoot()
	if r then r.CFrame = e[4] task.wait(0.4) end
	if not npc:FindFirstChild("Dialog") then Instance.new("Dialog", npc) end
	local id = SH.npcIds[e[3]] or fetchNpcId(e[3], npc)
	if not alive() then return false end
	if not id then notify("Could not get the cashier's ID - try again", T.Bad) return false end
	local arg = { ID = id, Character = npc, Name = e[3], Dialog = npc.Dialog }
	-- 3. confirm until the item belongs to you
	local deadline = os.clock() + 30
	local bought = false
	while alive() and os.clock() < deadline do
		local st = itemState(main)
		if st == "success" then bought = true break end
		if st == "gone" then break end
		if not safeInvoke(chatted, arg, "Initiate") then
			-- cashier did not answer: clear the dialog and fetch a fresh ID
			task.wait(0.6)
			for _ = 1, 3 do safeInvoke(chatted, arg, "EndChat") task.wait(0.3) end
			SH.npcIds[e[3]] = nil
			local nid = fetchNpcId(e[3], npc)
			if nid then arg.ID = nid end
		else
			local inner = os.clock() + 3
			while alive() and os.clock() < inner do
				st = itemState(main)
				if st ~= "pending" then break end
				if not safeInvoke(chatted, arg, "ConfirmPurchase") then break end
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
			st = itemState(main)
			if st == "success" then bought = true end
			safeInvoke(chatted, arg, "EndChat")
			if bought or st == "gone" then break end
			task.wait(0.2)
		end
	end
	safeInvoke(chatted, arg, "EndChat")
	-- 4. bring the item back to where you started
	if alive() and bought and dropCF and main.Parent then
		claimDrag(main:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or box, 1)
		if main.Parent then
			main.CFrame = dropCF
			main.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		end
	end
	return bought
end

local Sb = Section(ShopTab, "Store Shopping", "Search every store, preview the item, then buy it", "premium")
local storeNames = { "All Stores" }
for i, e in ipairs(STORES) do storeNames[i + 1] = e[1] end
local shopBox = Input(Sb, "Search Items", "type a name", "Search", function(text) SH.search = text:lower() refreshItems(false) end)
bind(shopBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function() SH.search = shopBox.Text:lower() refreshItems(false) end)
storeDd = Dropdown(Sb, "Store", storeNames, 1, function() refreshItems(false) end)
itemDd = Dropdown(Sb, "Item", { "Press Rescan Stores" }, 1, function(i) showItemPreview(i) end)
itemPrev = makePreview(Sb, 160, "Item preview")
Button(Sb, "Rescan Stores", "Scan", function() refreshItems(true) end, nil, "Reads every store's shelves again")
Slider(Sb, "Quantity", 1, 10, 1, function(v) SH.qty = v end)
Button(Sb, "Purchase", "Buy", function()
	local it = SH.items[itemDd.Get()]
	if not it then notify("Search or rescan first", T.Warn) return end
	local e = STORES[it.store]
	runJob("Buying " .. it.label, function(alive)
		local r0 = getRoot()
		local home = r0 and r0.CFrame
		local bought = 0
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			for i = 1, SH.qty do
				if not alive() then break end
				local cont = shopItemsFor(e)
				local box
				if cont then
					for _, b in ipairs(cont:GetChildren()) do
						local nv = b.Name == "Box" and b:FindFirstChild("BoxItemName")
						local m = b:FindFirstChild("Main")
						if nv and nv.Value == it.key and m and not m.Anchored then box = b break end
					end
				end
				if not box then notify("Ran out of that item", T.Warn) break end
				setStatus("Buying " .. i .. " / " .. SH.qty)
				local drop = home and home * CFrame.new(((i - 1) % 5) * 4, -1, -4 - math.floor((i - 1) / 5) * 4)
				if buyBox(e, box, alive, drop) then bought = bought + 1 end
			end
		end)
		if not ok then notify("Shop error: " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 70), T.Bad) end
		-- always go back to where you started, even if something failed or you pressed Stop
		task.wait(0.2)
		local rr = getRoot()
		if rr and home then rr.CFrame = home rr.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end
		notify("Bought " .. bought .. " / " .. SH.qty, bought > 0 and T.Good or T.Warn)
		local f = fetchFunds()
		if f and fundsRow then fundsRow.Text = "Funds: $" .. commas(f) end
	end)
end, nil, "Teleports to the store, buys, then returns you")
Button(Sb, "Stop", "Stop", function() stopAll() notify("Stopped", T.Sub) end)
shopStatus = InfoRow(Sb, "Scanning stores...")
fundsRow = InfoRow(Sb, "Funds: -")
Button(Sb, "Check Funds", "Check", function()
	async(function()
		local f = fetchFunds()
		fundsRow.Text = f and ("Funds: $" .. commas(f)) or "Funds: could not read"
	end)
end)

task.delay(2, function() if Live.Running then pcall(refreshItems, true) end end)

local Sp = Section(ShopTab, "Special", "One-off purchases", "premium")
Button(Sp, "Power of Ease ($10,009,000)", "Buy", function()
	runJob("Buying Power of Ease", function(alive)
		local funds = fetchFunds()
		if not funds then notify("Could not read your funds", T.Bad) return end
		if funds < 10009000 then notify("Not enough money", T.Warn) return end
		local r = getRoot()
		if not r then return end
		local home = r.CFrame
		for _, e in ipairs(STORES) do
			local n = npcOf(e)
			if n and not SH.npcIds[e[3]] then fetchNpcId(e[3], n) task.wait(0.15) end
		end
		r.CFrame = CFrame.new(1059.4, 17.2, 1130.3)
		task.wait(0.15)
		local best, bestD
		for _, e in ipairs(STORES) do
			local cp = counterPart(e)
			if cp and npcOf(e) then
				local d = (cp.Position - r.Position).Magnitude
				if not bestD or d < bestD then best, bestD = e, d end
			end
		end
		local _, chatted = dialogRemotes()
		local id = best and SH.npcIds[best[3]]
		if not (best and chatted and id) then notify("No cashier found", T.Bad) r.CFrame = home return end
		local npc = npcOf(best)
		local arg = { ID = id, Character = npc, Name = best[3], Dialog = npc:FindFirstChild("Dialog") }
		local deadline = os.clock() + 60
		while alive() and os.clock() < deadline do
			safeInvoke(chatted, arg, "Initiate")
			safeInvoke(chatted, arg, "ConfirmPurchase")
			safeInvoke(chatted, arg, "EndChat")
			local f = fetchFunds()
			if f and f < funds then notify("Power of Ease purchased", T.Good) break end
			task.wait(0.2)
		end
		local rr = getRoot()
		if rr then rr.CFrame = home end
	end)
end, nil, "Teleports to Strange Man's spot and spams the purchase until your money drops")
-- Additional purchases use the same cashier flow and current job token.
local function blueprintFolder()
	local folder = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerBlueprints")
	return folder and folder:FindFirstChild("Blueprints")
end
local function blueprintOwned(key)
	local folder = blueprintFolder()
	return folder and folder:FindFirstChild(key) ~= nil or false
end
local function itemDefinition(key)
	for _, item in ipairs(SH.all) do if item.key == key then return item end end
end
local function stockBox(item)
	local folder = shopItemsFor(STORES[item.store])
	if folder then
		for _, box in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
			local key, main = box:FindFirstChild("BoxItemName"), box:FindFirstChild("Main")
			if box.Name == "Box" and key and key.Value == item.key and main and main:IsA("BasePart") and not main.Anchored then return box end
		end
	end
end
local function purchasedBox(key)
	local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
	if folder then
		for _, box in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
			local name = box:FindFirstChild("PurchasedBoxItemName") or box:FindFirstChild("BoxItemName")
			if name and name.Value == key and ownedByMe(box) then return box end
		end
	end
end
local function openPurchasedBox(key, alive)
	local event = Interaction and Interaction:FindFirstChild("ClientInteracted")
	if not event then return false, "ClientInteracted remote not found" end
	local deadline = os.clock() + 8
	while alive() and os.clock() < deadline do
		local box, root = purchasedBox(key), getRoot()
		if box and root then
			event:FireServer(box, "Open box", root.CFrame)
			return true
		end
		task.wait(0.1)
	end
	return false, alive() and "Owned purchase box did not appear" or "Stopped"
end
local function buyOneKey(key, alive)
	if not alive() then return false, "Stopped" end
	local item = itemDefinition(key)
	if not item then return false, key .. " is not stocked in the scanned stores" end
	local funds = fetchFunds()
	if not funds or not item.price then return false, "Could not verify price / funds for " .. key end
	if funds < item.price then return false, "Not enough funds for " .. item.name end
	local box = stockBox(item)
	if not box then return false, item.name .. " is out of stock" end
	if not buyBox(STORES[item.store], box, alive) then return false, "Purchase not confirmed: " .. item.name end
	if not alive() then return false, "Stopped" end
	return openPurchasedBox(key, alive)
end
local blueprintSection = Section(ShopTab, "Blueprint Purchases", "Buy and open missing blueprint boxes using the existing stores", "premium")
local blueprintList, blueprintDropdown, blueprintRow = {}, nil, nil
local function refreshBlueprintPurchases()
	blueprintList = {}
	for key in pairs(BLUEPRINT_KEYS) do table.insert(blueprintList, key) end
	table.sort(blueprintList)
	local names, owned = {}, 0
	for _, key in ipairs(blueprintList) do
		if blueprintOwned(key) then owned = owned + 1 end
		local item = itemDefinition(key)
		table.insert(names, (blueprintOwned(key) and "[Owned] " or "") .. key .. (item and item.price and (" ($" .. commas(item.price) .. ")") or ""))
	end
	blueprintDropdown.SetOptions(names)
	blueprintRow.Text = "Blueprints unlocked: " .. owned .. " / " .. #blueprintList
end
blueprintDropdown = Dropdown(blueprintSection, "Blueprint", { "Press Scan" }, 1)
blueprintRow = InfoRow(blueprintSection, "Blueprints: press Scan")
Button(blueprintSection, "Refresh Blueprint Catalog", "Scan", function() scanAllItems() refreshBlueprintPurchases() end)
local function purchaseBlueprints(keys)
	if not blueprintFolder() then notify("Load your base first; blueprint inventory is unavailable", T.Warn) return end
	runJob("Purchasing blueprints", function(alive)
		LTExtra.withReturn(function()
			scanAllItems()
			local unlocked, skipped, failed = 0, 0, 0
			for i, key in ipairs(keys) do
				if not alive() then break end
				if blueprintOwned(key) then skipped = skipped + 1
				else
					setStatus("Blueprint purchase " .. i .. " / " .. #keys)
					local ok, reason = buyOneKey(key, alive)
					if ok then
						local deadline = os.clock() + 5
						while alive() and not blueprintOwned(key) and os.clock() < deadline do task.wait(0.1) end
					end
					if blueprintOwned(key) then unlocked = unlocked + 1
					else failed = failed + 1 if alive() and failed <= 3 then notify(reason or ("Unlock not confirmed: " .. key), T.Warn) end end
				end
			end
			if alive() then notify("Blueprints unlocked: " .. unlocked .. " | owned: " .. skipped .. " | unavailable: " .. failed, unlocked > 0 and T.Good or T.Sub) end
		end)
		refreshBlueprintPurchases()
	end)
end
Button(blueprintSection, "Purchase Selected Blueprint", "Buy", function()
	local key = blueprintList[blueprintDropdown.Get()]
	if not key then notify("Scan the blueprint catalog first", T.Warn) return end
	purchaseBlueprints({ key })
end)
Button(blueprintSection, "Purchase All Missing Blueprints", "Buy", function()
	local keys = {} for key in pairs(BLUEPRINT_KEYS) do table.insert(keys, key) end table.sort(keys)
	purchaseBlueprints(keys)
end)
Button(blueprintSection, "Stop Blueprint Purchases", "Stop", stopAll)

local RUKIRY_PLAN = {
	{ key = "CanOfWorms", goal = CFrame.new(317.3, 46.0, 1918.1) },
	{ key = "BagOfSand", goal = CFrame.new(319.5, 46.0, 1914.9) },
	{ key = "LightBulb", goal = CFrame.new(322.4, 43.6, 1916.4) },
}
local function ownedQuestItem(key)
	local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
	if folder then
		for _, model in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
			local item = model:FindFirstChild("ItemName")
			if item and item.Value == key and ownedByMe(model) and mainPartOf(model) then return model end
		end
	end
end
local function rukiryCount()
	local count = 0
	local function check(container)
		if container then
			for _, tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
				if tool:IsA("Tool") then
					local name = axeNameOf(tool) or tool.Name
					if name == "Rukiryaxe" or name == "Rukiry Axe" then count = count + 1 end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	check(getChar()) check(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
	return count
end
local questSection = Section(ShopTab, "Rukiryaxe Quest", "Buy the three materials, place them at the shrine and collect the reward", "loot")
Button(questSection, "Complete Rukiryaxe Quest", "Start", function()
	runJob("Rukiryaxe quest", function(alive)
		LTExtra.withReturn(function()
			scanAllItems()
			local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
			local interact = Interaction and Interaction:FindFirstChild("ClientInteracted")
			if not folder or not interact then notify("Quest folders / interaction remote unavailable", T.Warn) return end
			local old, before = {}, rukiryCount()
			for _, model in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do old[model] = true end
			for _, entry in ipairs(RUKIRY_PLAN) do
				if not alive() then return end
				setStatus("Quest material: " .. entry.key)
				local model = ownedQuestItem(entry.key)
				if not model then
					local ok, reason = buyOneKey(entry.key, alive)
					if not ok then if alive() then notify(reason, T.Warn) end return end
					local deadline = os.clock() + 6
					while alive() and os.clock() < deadline and not model do model = ownedQuestItem(entry.key) task.wait(0.1) end
				end
				if not model or not LTExtra.moveOwned(model, entry.goal, alive) then
					if alive() then notify("Could not deliver " .. entry.key .. "; quest stopped", T.Warn) end return
				end
			end
			local root = getRoot()
			if not alive() or not root then return end
			root.CFrame = CFrame.new(320.6, 45.8, 1919.2)
			local reward, deadline = nil, os.clock() + 20
			while alive() and os.clock() < deadline and not reward do
				for _, model in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
					local name, part = model:FindFirstChild("ToolName"), mainPartOf(model)
					if not old[model] and name and name.Value == "Rukiryaxe" and part and (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude < 50 then
						local owner = model:FindFirstChild("Owner")
						local ownerString = owner and owner:FindFirstChild("OwnerString")
						if ownedByMe(model) or (ownerString and ownerString.Value == "") then reward = model break end
					end
				end
				if not reward then task.wait(0.2) end
			end
			if not alive() then return end
			if not reward then notify("Materials delivered; no new quest reward appeared within 20s", T.Warn) return end
			deadline = os.clock() + 8
			while alive() and os.clock() < deadline and rukiryCount() <= before do
				local handle = reward:FindFirstChild("Handle") or mainPartOf(reward)
				root = getRoot()
				if not root or not handle then break end
				root.CFrame = handle.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
				claimDrag(reward, 0.3, alive)
				if alive() and handle.Parent then interact:FireServer(reward, "Pick up tool", handle.CFrame) end
				task.wait(0.2)
			end
			if alive() then notify(rukiryCount() > before and "Rukiryaxe pickup confirmed" or "Reward found; pickup was not confirmed", rukiryCount() > before and T.Good or T.Warn) end
		end)
	end)
end)
Button(questSection, "Stop Rukiryaxe Quest", "Stop", stopAll)
task.delay(1, function() if Live.Running then refreshBlueprintPurchases() end end)

end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- TOOLS: object selection + sorting
do
local SEL = { list = {}, hl = {}, click = nil, same = false, keep = true, back = false, timeout = 3, X = 4, Y = 1, Z = 4, pad = 2, pyramid = false, matchLength = true }
local function setSel(model, on)
	if on and not SEL.list[model] then
		SEL.list[model] = true
		SEL.hl[model] = new("Highlight", { Adornee = model, FillColor = T.Accent, FillTransparency = 0.5, OutlineColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }, EspGui)
	elseif not on and SEL.list[model] then
		SEL.list[model] = nil
		if SEL.hl[model] then SEL.hl[model]:Destroy() SEL.hl[model] = nil end
	end
end
local function clearSel()
	for m in pairs(SEL.list) do setSel(m, false) end
end
local function selList()
	local out = {}
	for m in pairs(SEL.list) do
		if m.Parent then table.insert(out, m) else setSel(m, false) end
	end
	return out
end
local selRow
local function updSel() if selRow then selRow.Text = "Selected: " .. #selList() .. " object(s)" end end
local function moveModel(model, goal, alive)
	local part = mainPartOf(model)
	if not (part and part.Parent) then return false end
	local r = getRoot()
	if r then
		local flat = (r.Position - part.Position) * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
		if flat.Magnitude > 10 then r.CFrame = CFrame.new(part.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) task.wait(0.05) end
	end
	claimDrag(model, SEL.timeout, alive)
	if alive() and part.Parent then part.CFrame = goal end
	task.wait(0.05)
	return true
end
local function sendSelection(centerCF, grid, alive)
	local list = selList()
	if #list == 0 then notify("Nothing selected", T.Warn) return end
	local r = getRoot()
	local home = r and r.CFrame
	local cell, cellY = 4, 2
	if grid then
		for _, m in ipairs(list) do
			local ok, sz = pcall(function() return m:GetExtentsSize() end)
			if ok then cell = math.max(cell, sz.X, sz.Z) cellY = math.max(cellY, sz.Y) end
		end
		cell, cellY = cell + SEL.pad, cellY + SEL.pad * 0.5
	end
	local positions
	if grid then
		local err
		positions, err = LTExtra.layout(#list, SEL.X, SEL.Y, SEL.Z, SEL.pyramid)
		if not positions then notify(err, T.Warn) return end
	end
	for i, m in ipairs(list) do
		if not alive() then break end
		local k = i - 1
		local col, row, layer
		if grid then
			col, row, layer = positions[i].X, positions[i].Z, positions[i].Y
		else
			col, row, layer = k % 5, math.floor(k / 5), 0
		end
		local step = grid and cell or 5
		local goal = centerCF * CFrame.new(col * step, 1 + layer * (grid and cellY or 3), -row * step)
		moveModel(m, goal, alive)
	end
	if SEL.back and home then local rr = getRoot() if rr then rr.CFrame = home end end
	if not SEL.keep then clearSel() end
	updSel()
end
local function plotCenter()
	local plot = myPlot()
	local o = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare")
	return o and CFrame.new(o.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) or nil
end

local Sl = Section(ToolsTab, "Selection Tools", "Click things of yours (or anyone's loose items) to select them, then move or sort them", "magnet")
local clickToggle
clickToggle = Toggle(Sl, "Click Selection", false, function(v)
	if SEL.click then SEL.click:Disconnect() SEL.click = nil end
	if not v then return end
	LTExtra.activatePicker("selection")
	SEL.click = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i, gp)
		if gp or i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or not mouse.Target then return end
		local pm, lmf = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels"), Workspace:FindFirstChild("LogModels")
		local model = topModel(mouse.Target, pm, lmf)
		if not (model and model:IsA("Model")) then return end
		local on = not SEL.list[model]
		if SEL.same then
			for _, m in ipairs(model.Parent:GetChildren()) do
				if m:IsA("Model") and LTExtra.sameSelectionKind(model, m, SEL.matchLength) and ownedByMe(m) then setSel(m, on) end
			end
		else
			setSel(model, on)
		end
		updSel()
	end)
end, nil, nil, "Left click an object to add / remove it")
LTExtra.pickers.selection = function()
	if SEL.click then SEL.click:Disconnect() SEL.click = nil end
	clickToggle.Set(false)
end
table.insert(LTExtra.stopHooks, LTExtra.pickers.selection)
-- Rectangle selection uses the same selection list, highlights and move jobs.
local lasso = { on = false, start = nil }
local lassoFrame = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.86,
	BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 20 }, EspGui)
new("UIStroke", { Color = T.Accent, Thickness = 1 }, lassoFrame)
local lassoToggle
local function stopLasso()
	lasso.on, lasso.start = false, nil
	lassoFrame.Visible = false
	if lassoToggle then lassoToggle.Set(false) end
end
LTExtra.pickers.lasso = stopLasso
table.insert(LTExtra.stopHooks, stopLasso)
lassoToggle = Toggle(Sl, "Lasso Selection", false, function(v)
	if v then LTExtra.activatePicker("lasso") end
	lasso.on = v
	if not v then stopLasso() end
end, nil, nil, "Drag a rectangle around your objects to add them to the selection")
bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(input, processed)
	if not lasso.on or processed or Live.BindingActive or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then lasso.start = UIS:GetMouseLocation() end
end)
bind(UIS.InputChanged, function(input)
	if not lasso.start or input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then return end
	local current = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
	lassoFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.min(current.X, lasso.start.X), math.min(current.Y, lasso.start.Y))
	lassoFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.abs(current.X - lasso.start.X), math.abs(current.Y - lasso.start.Y))
	lassoFrame.Visible = true
end)
bind(UIS.InputEnded, function(input)
	if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or not lasso.start then return end
	local start, finish = lasso.start, UIS:GetMouseLocation()
	lasso.start, lassoFrame.Visible = nil, false
	if math.abs(start.X - finish.X) < 6 or math.abs(start.Y - finish.Y) < 6 then return end
	local camera = API.cam()
	if not camera then return end
	for _, folderName in ipairs({ "PlayerModels", "LogModels" }) do
		local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(folderName)
		if folder then
			for _, model in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
				local part = model:IsA("Model") and mainPartOf(model)
				if part and ownedByMe(model) then
					local p, on = camera:WorldToScreenPoint(part.Position)
					if on and p.Z > 0 and p.X >= math.min(start.X, finish.X) and p.X <= math.max(start.X, finish.X) and
						p.Y >= math.min(start.Y, finish.Y) and p.Y <= math.max(start.Y, finish.Y) then setSel(model, true) end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	updSel()
end)
Button(Sl, "Select All My Planks", "Select", function()
	for _, entry in ipairs(LTExtra.scanPlanks(nil, 0, math.huge)) do setSel(entry.model, true) end
	updSel()
end)
Button(Sl, "Select All My Loose Items", "Select", function()
	local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
	if folder then
		for _, model in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
			local part = model:IsA("Model") and mainPartOf(model)
			if part and not part.Anchored and ownedByMe(model) and not LTExtra.isBlueprint(model) then setSel(model, true) end
		end
	end
	updSel()
end)
addCleanup(function() stopLasso() lassoFrame:Destroy() end)

Toggle(Sl, "Group Selection (same name, yours)", false, function(v) SEL.same = v end, nil, nil, "Clicking selects every object of that name you own")
Button(Sl, "Select All My Logs", "Select", function()
	for _, m in ipairs(myLogs()) do setSel(m, true) end
	updSel()
end)
Button(Sl, "Clear Selection", "Clear", function() clearSel() updSel() end)
selRow = InfoRow(Sl, "Selected: 0 object(s)")
Button(Sl, "Teleport Selection To Me", "Bring", function()
	local r = getRoot()
	if not r then return end
	local cf = r.CFrame
	runJob("Moving selection", function(alive) sendSelection(CFrame.new(cf.Position) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -6), false, alive) end)
end)
Button(Sl, "Teleport Selection To My Plot", "Send", function()
	local c = plotCenter()
	if not c then notify("You don't own a plot yet", T.Warn) return end
	runJob("Moving selection to plot", function(alive) sendSelection(c, false, alive) end)
end)
Button(Sl, "Sort Selection Into A Grid (in front of me)", "Sort", function()
	local r = getRoot()
	if not r then return end
	local look = r.CFrame.LookVector * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
	local base = CFrame.lookAt(r.Position + look.Unit * 8, r.Position + look.Unit * 20)
	runJob("Sorting selection", function(alive) sendSelection(base * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0), true, alive) end)
end, nil, "Uses the grid sliders below")
Button(Sl, "Stop", "Stop", function() stopAll() notify("Stopped", T.Sub) end)
local SlOpt = Collapse(Sl, "Selection Settings")
Toggle(SlOpt, "Pyramid Sorter", false, function(v) SEL.pyramid = v end, nil, nil, "Each layer shrinks and stays centered over the layer below")
Toggle(SlOpt, "Match Plank Length In Groups", true, function(v) SEL.matchLength = v end, nil, nil, "Group selection matches wood type and plank length")
Slider(SlOpt, "Grid Columns (X)", 1, 20, 4, function(v) SEL.X = v end)
Slider(SlOpt, "Grid Layers (Y)", 1, 10, 1, function(v) SEL.Y = v end)
Slider(SlOpt, "Grid Rows (Z)", 1, 20, 4, function(v) SEL.Z = v end)
Slider(SlOpt, "Padding (studs)", 0, 10, 2, function(v) SEL.pad = v end)
Slider(SlOpt, "Ownership Timeout (s)", 1, 10, 3, function(v) SEL.timeout = v end)
Toggle(SlOpt, "Keep Selection After Moving", true, function(v) SEL.keep = v end)
Toggle(SlOpt, "Return To Origin After Moving", false, function(v) SEL.back = v end)
addCleanup(function() if SEL.click then SEL.click:Disconnect() end clearSel() end)
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- PLOT: plank selection and blueprint filling
-- Ported from the reference Build module into the existing Plot tab.
do
local Build = { class = nil, minY = 0, maxY = 100, click = false, pick = false }
local classNames, classDropdown, fillToggle, buildRow = {}, nil, nil, nil
local function refreshBuildStatus()
	local count = #LTExtra.scanPlanks(Build.class, Build.minY, Build.maxY)
	if buildRow then buildRow.Text = "Planks: " .. count .. " | type: " .. tostring(Build.class or "all") end
end
local function refreshPlankTypes(wanted)
	local seen = {}
	classNames = { "All my wood types" }
	for _, entry in ipairs(LTExtra.scanPlanks(nil, 0, math.huge)) do seen[entry.class] = true end
	local types = {} for class in pairs(seen) do table.insert(types, class) end table.sort(types)
	for _, class in ipairs(types) do table.insert(classNames, class) end
	classDropdown.SetOptions(classNames)
	local selected = 1
	for i, name in ipairs(classNames) do if name == (wanted or Build.class) then selected = i end end
	classDropdown.Set(selected, true)
	Build.class = selected > 1 and classNames[selected] or nil
	refreshBuildStatus()
end
local function blueprints()
	local result, folder = {}, Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
	if folder then
		for _, model in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
			if LTExtra.isBlueprint(model) and mainPartOf(model) then table.insert(result, model) end
		end
	end
	local root = getRoot()
	if root then table.sort(result, function(a, b) return (mainPartOf(a).Position - root.Position).Magnitude < (mainPartOf(b).Position - root.Position).Magnitude end) end
	return result
end
local function feedBlueprint(blueprint, alive)
	if not alive() or not blueprint.Parent or not LTExtra.isBlueprint(blueprint) then return false, "Blueprint unavailable" end
	local list = LTExtra.scanPlanks(Build.class, Build.minY, Build.maxY)
	if #list == 0 then return false, "No matching owned planks" end
	local part = mainPartOf(blueprint)
	if not part then return false, "Blueprint has no placement part" end
	local plank = list[1]
	local before = plank.part.Size.Y
	if not LTExtra.moveOwned(plank.model, CFrame.new(part.Position), alive) then return false, "Plank could not be moved" end
	-- A move is only a delivery attempt. Confirm server consumption or progress.
	local deadline = os.clock() + 4
	while alive() and os.clock() < deadline do
		if not blueprint.Parent or not LTExtra.isBlueprint(blueprint) then return true, "Blueprint completed" end
		if not plank.model.Parent or not plank.part.Parent or plank.part.Size.Y < before - 0.01 then return true, "Plank consumed" end
		task.wait(0.1)
	end
	return false, alive() and "No fill progress confirmed; check plank size and blueprint requirements" or "Stopped"
end
local fillSection = Section(PlotTab, "Blueprint Filling", "Deliver your selected planks to your own blueprints", "magnet")
classDropdown = Dropdown(fillSection, "Plank Wood Type", classNames, 1, function(i)
	Build.class = i > 1 and classNames[i] or nil
	refreshBuildStatus()
end)
Button(fillSection, "Refresh Owned Planks", "Scan", function() refreshPlankTypes() end)
Button(fillSection, "Pick Plank Type By Clicking", "Pick", function()
	LTExtra.activatePicker("planktype")
	Build.pick = true
	notify("Click one of your planks to select its wood type", T.Sub)
end)
Slider(fillSection, "Min Plank Length (x0.1 stud)", 0, 1000, 0, function(v) Build.minY = v / 10 refreshBuildStatus() end)
Slider(fillSection, "Max Plank Length (x0.1 stud)", 1, 1000, 1000, function(v) Build.maxY = v / 10 refreshBuildStatus() end)
buildRow = InfoRow(fillSection, "Planks: press Scan")
local function stopFillClick()
	Build.click = false
	if fillToggle then fillToggle.Set(false) end
end
LTExtra.pickers.blueprint = stopFillClick
LTExtra.pickers.planktype = function() Build.pick = false end
table.insert(LTExtra.stopHooks, stopFillClick)
table.insert(LTExtra.stopHooks, LTExtra.pickers.planktype)
fillToggle = Toggle(fillSection, "Click To Fill Blueprint", false, function(v)
	if v then LTExtra.activatePicker("blueprint") end
	Build.click = v
end, nil, nil, "Click an owned blueprint to deliver one matching plank")
bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(input, processed)
	if processed or Live.BindingActive or not Live.Unlocked or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() or
		input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
	local model = topModel(mouse.Target, Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels"))
	if Build.pick then
		if model and model.Name == "Plank" and ownedByMe(model) and classOf(model) then
			Build.pick = false refreshPlankTypes(classOf(model))
		end
	elseif Build.click and not LT.busy and LTExtra.isBlueprint(model) then
		runJob("Filling blueprint", function(alive)
			LTExtra.withReturn(function()
				local ok, reason = feedBlueprint(model, alive)
				if alive() then notify(reason, ok and T.Good or T.Warn) end
			end)
			refreshBuildStatus()
		end)
	end
end)
Button(fillSection, "Fill All My Blueprints", "Fill", function()
	if Build.minY > Build.maxY then notify("Minimum plank length is above maximum", T.Warn) return end
	runJob("Filling blueprints", function(alive)
		LTExtra.withReturn(function()
			local list = blueprints()
			if #list == 0 then notify("No owned blueprints found", T.Warn) return end
			local delivered, failed = 0, 0
			for index, blueprint in ipairs(list) do
				if not alive() then break end
				-- Multiple planks may be needed. Each successful iteration must consume
				-- material; failures stop that blueprint instead of looping forever.
				for attempt = 1, 100 do
					if not alive() or not blueprint.Parent or not LTExtra.isBlueprint(blueprint) then break end
					setStatus("Blueprint " .. index .. " / " .. #list .. " | delivery " .. attempt)
					local ok, reason = feedBlueprint(blueprint, alive)
					if not ok then failed = failed + 1 if alive() then notify(reason, T.Warn) end break end
					delivered = delivered + 1
				end
			end
			if alive() then notify("Confirmed deliveries: " .. delivered .. " | stalled blueprints: " .. failed, delivered > 0 and T.Good or T.Warn) end
		end)
		refreshBuildStatus()
	end)
end)
Button(fillSection, "Stop Blueprint Job", "Stop", stopAll)
addCleanup(function() Build.pick = false stopFillClick() end)
task.delay(1, function() if Live.Running then refreshPlankTypes() end end)
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- VEHICLE
do
local VEH = { cfg = nil, veh = nil, defaults = nil, orig = {}, custom = {}, resetOnExit = true, pad = nil, roll = false, color = 1032 }
local VCOLORS = {
	{ "Orange-Brown", 345 }, { "Black", 1003 }, { "Muted Red", 154 }, { "Cool Gray", 131 }, { "Chocolate Brown", 25 },
	{ "Slate Gray", 148 }, { "Dusty Rose", 153 }, { "Earthy Gray", 27 }, { "Pale Mint", 151 }, { "Olive", 200 },
	{ "Sage Green", 210 }, { "Beige", 5 }, { "Olive-Brown", 108 }, { "Hot Pink", 1032 },
}
local vehRow
local function findVehConfig()
	local h = getHum()
	local seat = h and h.SeatPart
	if not seat then return nil, nil end
	local v = seat
	while v.Parent and v.Parent ~= Workspace and v.Parent.Name ~= "PlayerModels" do v = v.Parent end
	if v and v:IsA("Model") then
		local c = v:FindFirstChild("Configuration", true)
		if c and c:FindFirstChild("MaxSpeed") then return c, v end
	end
	return nil, nil
end
local function readVeh(cfg)
	local out = {}
	for _, n in ipairs({ "MaxSpeed", "SteerAngle", "SteerVelocity" }) do
		local s = cfg:FindFirstChild(n)
		out[n] = s and s:IsA("ValueBase") and s.Value or nil
	end
	return out
end
local function writeVeh(cfg, vals)
	if not (cfg and vals) then return end
	for n, v in pairs(vals) do
		local s = cfg:FindFirstChild(n)
		if s and s:IsA("ValueBase") then s.Value = v end
	end
end
local function applyVeh(name, value)
	VEH.custom[name] = value
	if VEH.cfg then
		local s = VEH.cfg:FindFirstChild(name)
		if s and s:IsA("ValueBase") then s.Value = value end
	else
		notify("Sit in a vehicle first", T.Warn)
	end
end
local function padFire()
	local i = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Interaction")
	local rp = i and i:FindFirstChild("RemoteProxy")
	if VEH.pad and rp then rp:FireServer(VEH.pad) end
end

local Vh = Section(VehicleTab, "Vehicle Modifications", "Works while you are sitting in a vehicle", "speed")
local vSpeed = Slider(Vh, "Max Speed (x0.1)", 1, 100, 1, function(v) applyVeh("MaxSpeed", v / 10) end)
local vAngle = Slider(Vh, "Steer Angle (x0.1)", 1, 20, 1, function(v) applyVeh("SteerAngle", v / 10) end)
local vVel = Slider(Vh, "Steer Velocity (x0.01)", 1, 5, 1, function(v) applyVeh("SteerVelocity", v / 100) end)
Button(Vh, "Flip Vehicle", "Flip", function()
	local h = getHum()
	local seat = h and h.SeatPart
	if not seat then notify("Sit in a vehicle first", T.Warn) return end
	local veh = seat.Parent
	local p = veh:IsA("Model") and (veh.PrimaryPart or seat) or seat
	p.CFrame = p.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi) + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
end)
Button(Vh, "Reset Vehicle Modifications", "Reset", function()
	if VEH.cfg and VEH.defaults then
		writeVeh(VEH.cfg, VEH.defaults)
		VEH.custom = {}
		if VEH.defaults.MaxSpeed then vSpeed.SetSilent(math.clamp(VEH.defaults.MaxSpeed * 10, 1, 100)) end
		if VEH.defaults.SteerAngle then vAngle.SetSilent(math.clamp(VEH.defaults.SteerAngle * 10, 1, 20)) end
		if VEH.defaults.SteerVelocity then vVel.SetSilent(math.clamp(VEH.defaults.SteerVelocity * 100, 1, 5)) end
	else
		notify("Sit in a vehicle first", T.Warn)
	end
end)
Toggle(Vh, "Reset Modifications On Exit", true, function(v) VEH.resetOnExit = v end)
vehRow = InfoRow(Vh, "Vehicle: none")

local Vp = Section(VehicleTab, "Vehicle Pad Spawner", "Re-spawn a vehicle from a pad until it rolls the colour you want", "speed")
local vcNames = {}
for i, c in ipairs(VCOLORS) do vcNames[i] = c[1] end
Dropdown(Vp, "Target Color", vcNames, 14, function(i) VEH.color = VCOLORS[i][2] end)
local padRow = InfoRow(Vp, "Pad: none selected")
Button(Vp, "Select Vehicle Pad", "Select", function()
	padRow.Text = "Click the pad..."
	local conn
	conn = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
		if i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
		conn:Disconnect()
		local cur = mouse.Target
		for _ = 1, 10 do
			if not cur then break end
			local ev = cur:FindFirstChild("ButtonRemote_SpawnButton")
			if ev then
				VEH.pad = ev
				padRow.Text = "Pad: " .. cur.Name
				return
			end
			cur = cur.Parent
		end
		padRow.Text = "Pad not found - try again"
	end)
end)
Button(Vp, "Respawn Vehicle", "Respawn", function()
	if not VEH.pad then notify("Select a pad first", T.Warn) return end
	padFire()
end)
local function spawnAndGetColor()
	local watch = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels") or Workspace
	local existing = {}
	for _, m in ipairs(watch:GetChildren()) do existing[m] = true end
	local done, result = false, nil
	local conn = watch.ChildAdded:Connect(function(model)
		if done or existing[model] or not model:IsA("Model") then return end
		local st = model:WaitForChild("Settings", 0.5)
		local cv = st and st:WaitForChild("Color", 0.5)
		if not cv then return end
		local dl = os.clock() + 0.4
		while cv.Value == 0 and os.clock() < dl do task.wait(0.02) end
		if cv.Value ~= 0 and not done then done = true result = cv.Value end
	end)
	padFire()
	local dl = os.clock() + 0.5
	while not done and os.clock() < dl and VEH.roll do task.wait(0.02) end
	conn:Disconnect()
	return result
end
local rollToggle
rollToggle = Toggle(Vp, "Auto-Roll Color", false, function(v)
	if v and not VEH.pad then notify("Select a pad first", T.Warn) rollToggle.Set(false) return end
	VEH.roll = v
	if not v then return end
	async(function()
		while VEH.roll and Live.Running do
			local col = spawnAndGetColor()
			if col == VEH.color then
				VEH.roll = false
				rollToggle.Set(false)
				notify("Found the target colour!", T.Good)
			end
		end
	end)
end, nil, nil, "Keeps respawning until the colour matches")
addCleanup(function() VEH.roll = false end)

task.spawn(function()
	local lastSeat
	while Live.Running do
		task.wait(0.5)
		local h = getHum()
		local seat = h and h.SeatPart
		if seat ~= lastSeat then
			lastSeat = seat
			if seat then
				VEH.cfg, VEH.veh = nil, nil
				for _ = 1, 6 do
					local c, v = findVehConfig()
					if c then VEH.cfg, VEH.veh = c, v break end
					task.wait(0.5)
				end
				if VEH.cfg then
					if not VEH.orig[VEH.veh] then VEH.orig[VEH.veh] = readVeh(VEH.cfg) end
					VEH.defaults = readVeh(VEH.cfg)
					vehRow.Text = "Vehicle: " .. VEH.veh.Name
					if VEH.defaults.MaxSpeed then vSpeed.SetSilent(math.clamp(VEH.defaults.MaxSpeed * 10, 1, 100)) end
					if VEH.defaults.SteerAngle then vAngle.SetSilent(math.clamp(VEH.defaults.SteerAngle * 10, 1, 20)) end
					if VEH.defaults.SteerVelocity then vVel.SetSilent(math.clamp(VEH.defaults.SteerVelocity * 100, 1, 5)) end
					for n, v in pairs(VEH.custom) do applyVeh(n, v) end
				else
					vehRow.Text = "Vehicle: seated (no config found)"
				end
			else
				if VEH.resetOnExit and VEH.cfg and VEH.veh and VEH.orig[VEH.veh] then writeVeh(VEH.cfg, VEH.orig[VEH.veh]) end
				VEH.cfg, VEH.veh, VEH.defaults, VEH.custom = nil, nil, nil, {}
				vehRow.Text = "Vehicle: none"
			end
		end
	end
end)
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- EXTRAS: hard dragger, BTools, water walk, hover info, auto farm, axe tools, logs ESP, hotkeys
do
	-- ===== Tools tab: dragging
	local HD = { P = 120000, D = 1000, conn = nil }
	local Dg = Section(ToolsTab, "Dragging", "Make the game's own drag tool stronger", "magnet")
	local hdToggle = Toggle(Dg, "Hard Dragger", false, function(v)
		if HD.conn then HD.conn:Disconnect() HD.conn = nil end
		if not v then return end
		HD.conn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
			local d = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Dragger")
			if not d then return end
			local bp = d:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition")
			local bg = d:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro")
			if bp then
				bp.P = HD.P
				bp.D = HD.D
				bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * math.huge
			end
			if bg then
				bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * math.huge
				bg.P = 1200
				bg.D = 140
			end
		end)
	end, nil, nil, "Dragged objects follow your mouse tightly and ignore their weight")
	Slider(Dg, "Drag Strength (x1000)", 10, 500, 120, function(v) HD.P = v * 1000 end)
	Slider(Dg, "Drag Damping", 100, 3000, 1000, function(v) HD.D = v end)
	addCleanup(function() if HD.conn then HD.conn:Disconnect() end end)

	-- ===== Tools tab: BTools (client side only - deleted things come back when you rejoin)
	local BT = { stack = {}, tools = nil, conns = {}, restore = true }
	local Bt = Section(ToolsTab, "BTools", "Delete tool + Undo tool. Local only - only hides things on your screen", "magnet")
	local btRow = InfoRow(Bt, "Deleted: 0")
	local function wholeObject(part)
		local node = part
		while node.Parent and node.Parent ~= Workspace and node.Parent:IsA("Model") do node = node.Parent end
		return node
	end
	local function restoreAll()
		for i = #BT.stack, 1, -1 do
			local e = BT.stack[i]
			if e.inst then
				e.inst.Parent = e.parent
				if e.cf and e.inst:IsA("BasePart") then e.inst.CFrame = e.cf end
			end
			BT.stack[i] = nil
		end
		btRow.Text = "Deleted: 0"
	end
	local function btoolsOff()
		for _, c in ipairs(BT.conns) do c:Disconnect() end
		BT.conns = {}
		if BT.tools then
			for _, t in ipairs(BT.tools) do t:Destroy() end
			BT.tools = nil
		end
	end
	local btToggle = Toggle(Bt, "BTools", false, function(v)
		btoolsOff()
		if not v then
			if BT.restore then restoreAll() end
			return
		end
		local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
		if not bp then notify("No backpack found", T.Warn) return end
		local del = Instance.new("Tool")
		del.Name, del.ToolTip, del.RequiresHandle, del.CanBeDropped = "Delete", "Click an object to hide it (hold Left Alt for the whole object)", false, false
		del.Parent = bp
		local undo = Instance.new("Tool")
		undo.Name, undo.ToolTip, undo.RequiresHandle, undo.CanBeDropped = "Undo", "Click to bring back the last deleted object", false, false
		undo.Parent = bp
		BT.tools = { del, undo }
		table.insert(BT.conns, del.Activated:Connect(function()
			local t = mouse.Target
			if not t or t == Workspace.Terrain or not t:IsA("BasePart") then return end
			local ch = getChar()
			if ch and t:IsDescendantOf(ch) then return end
			if t.Parent == Workspace then return end
			local victim = UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftAlt) and wholeObject(t) or t
			table.insert(BT.stack, { inst = victim, parent = victim.Parent, cf = victim:IsA("BasePart") and victim.CFrame or nil })
			victim.Parent = nil
			btRow.Text = "Deleted: " .. #BT.stack
		end))
		table.insert(BT.conns, undo.Activated:Connect(function()
			local e = table.remove(BT.stack)
			if not e then notify("Nothing to undo", T.Warn) return end
			e.inst.Parent = e.parent
			if e.cf and e.inst:IsA("BasePart") then e.inst.CFrame = e.cf end
			btRow.Text = "Deleted: " .. #BT.stack
		end))
	end, nil, nil, "Adds Delete and Undo tools to your backpack")
	Toggle(Bt, "Restore Everything When Turned Off", true, function(v) BT.restore = v end)
	Button(Bt, "Restore All Deleted", "Restore", restoreAll)
	addCleanup(function() btoolsOff() restoreAll() end)

	-- ===== Tools tab: water walk + hover info
	local WW = false
	local Mv2 = Section(ToolsTab, "Movement Extras", "Walk on the lake surface", "player")
	local wwToggle = Toggle(Mv2, "Water Walk", false, function(v) WW = v end, nil, nil, "Pushes you back up when you sink to the water surface")
	bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
		if not WW then return end
		local r = getRoot()
		if r and r.Position.Y <= 1 and r.Position.Y >= -5 then
			local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
			r.CFrame = r.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)
		end
	end)

	local hover = { on = false, hl = nil, last = nil, acc = 0 }
	local Hv = Section(ToolsTab, "Hover Info", "Point at an object to see its name and owner", "info")
	local hoverRow = InfoRow(Hv, "Hovering: -")
	Toggle(Hv, "Hover Info", false, function(v)
		hover.on = v
		if not v then
			hoverRow.Text = "Hovering: -"
			if hover.hl then hover.hl:Destroy() hover.hl = nil end
			hover.last = nil
		end
	end)
	bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
		if not hover.on then return end
		hover.acc = hover.acc + dt
		if hover.acc < 0.1 then return end
		hover.acc = 0
		local t = mouse.Target
		local m = t and topModel(t, Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels"), Workspace:FindFirstChild("LogModels"))
		if m == hover.last then return end
		hover.last = m
		if hover.hl then hover.hl:Destroy() hover.hl = nil end
		if not m then hoverRow.Text = "Hovering: -" return end
		hover.hl = new("Highlight", { Adornee = m, FillColor = T.Accent, FillTransparency = 0.6, OutlineColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }, EspGui)
		local of = m:FindFirstChild("Owner")
		local os2 = of and of:FindFirstChild("OwnerString")
		local owner = os2 and os2.Value
		if not owner and of and of:IsA("ObjectValue") and of.Value then owner = of.Value.Name end
		local tc = m:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")
		hoverRow.Text = "Hovering: " .. m.Name .. (tc and (" (" .. tostring(tc.Value) .. ")") or "") .. "  -  owner: " .. tostring(owner or "unknown")
	end)
	addCleanup(function() if hover.hl then hover.hl:Destroy() end end)

	-- ===== Wood tab: full auto farm + axe tools
	local AF = { every = 1 }
	local Af = Section(TreeTab, "Full Auto Farm", "Chop the selected type, collect the logs and sell them, on repeat", "autofarm")
	local afRow = InfoRow(Af, "Auto farm: idle")
	Button(Af, "Start Full Auto Farm", "Start", function()
		local c = LT.class
		if not c then notify("Pick a tree type first (Refresh Tree Types)", T.Warn) return end
		runJob("Full auto farm: " .. c, function(alive)
			local n = 0
			while alive() do
				local h = getHum()
				if not h or h.Health <= 0 then waitRespawn(alive) end
				local tree = pickTree(c, LT.pick)
				if not tree then notify("No more " .. c .. " trees - stopped", T.Warn) break end
				chopTree(tree, alive)
				n = n + 1
				afRow.Text = "Auto farm: " .. n .. " tree(s) chopped"
				if alive() and n % AF.every == 0 then
					setStatus("Selling logs")
					sellLogs(alive)
				end
				task.wait(0.5)
			end
			afRow.Text = "Auto farm: idle"
		end)
	end, nil, "Chop -> collect -> sell, repeating until you press Stop")
	Button(Af, "Stop", "Stop", function() stopAll() afRow.Text = "Auto farm: idle" notify("Stopped", T.Sub) end)
	Slider(Af, "Sell Every N Trees", 1, 10, 1, function(v) AF.every = v end)

	local Ax = Section(TreeTab, "Axe Tools", "Quick axe helpers", "autofarm")
	Button(Ax, "Equip Best Axe (selected type)", "Equip", function()
		local tool, name = bestAxe(LT.class or "Generic")
		local h = getHum()
		if tool and h then
			h:EquipTool(tool)
			notify("Equipped " .. tostring(name), T.Good)
		else
			notify("No axe found in your backpack", T.Warn)
		end
	end)
	Button(Ax, "Unequip Tools", "Unequip", function()
		local h = getHum()
		if h then h:UnequipTools() end
	end)

	-- ===== Logs tab: my logs ESP
	local LE = { on = false, hl = {} }
	local Le = Section(LogTab, "Log Highlights", "Outline every log you own so you can find them", "esp")
	Toggle(Le, "My Logs ESP", false, function(v)
		LE.on = v
		if not v then
			for m, h in pairs(LE.hl) do h:Destroy() LE.hl[m] = nil end
		end
	end)
	task.spawn(function()
		while Live.Running do
			task.wait(1)
			if LE.on then
				pcall(function()
					local mine = {}
					for _, m in ipairs(myLogs()) do
						mine[m] = true
						if not LE.hl[m] then
							LE.hl[m] = new("Highlight", { Adornee = m, FillColor = T.Good, FillTransparency = 0.6, OutlineColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }, EspGui)
						end
					end
					for m, h in pairs(LE.hl) do
						if not mine[m] or not m.Parent then h:Destroy() LE.hl[m] = nil end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
	addCleanup(function() for _, h in pairs(LE.hl) do h:Destroy() end end)

	-- ===== Tools tab: hotkeys
	local HK = { drag = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, bt = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, ww = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown }
	local Hk = Section(ToolsTab, "Hotkeys", "Press the key to flip the feature (Backspace clears a key)", "key")
	Keybind(Hk, "Hard Dragger", Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, function(k) HK.drag = k end, true)
	Keybind(Hk, "BTools", Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, function(k) HK.bt = k end, true)
	Keybind(Hk, "Water Walk", Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, function(k) HK.ww = k end, true)
	bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
		if gp or Live.BindingActive or i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
		local k = i.KeyCode
		if k == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return end
		if k == HK.drag then hdToggle.Fire(not hdToggle.Get()) end
		if k == HK.bt then btToggle.Fire(not btToggle.Get()) end
		if k == HK.ww then wwToggle.Fire(not wwToggle.Get()) end
	end)
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- WORLD (LT2-specific scenery toggles, local only)
local WD = { water = {}, boulders = {}, bridge = nil, volcano = true, scanned = false, enhanced = nil, tundraConn = nil }
local function scanWorld()
	if WD.scanned then return end
	WD.scanned = true
	local wr = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Water")
	if wr then
		for i, o in ipairs(wr:GetDescendants()) do
			if i % 500 == 0 then task.wait() end
			if o:IsA("BasePart") and o.Name == "Water" then table.insert(WD.water, { p = o, t = o.Transparency }) end
		end
	end
	local snow = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Region_Snow")
	local sp = snow and snow:FindFirstChild("PartSpawner")
	if sp then
		for i, o in ipairs(sp:GetDescendants()) do
			if i % 500 == 0 then task.wait() end
			if o:IsA("BasePart") and (o.Name == "Boulder" or o.Name == "SmallBoulder") and not (o:FindFirstChild("LavaLight") or o:FindFirstChild("Fire")) then
				table.insert(WD.boulders, { p = o, t = o.Transparency })
			end
		end
	end
end
local function sweepVolcano()
	local r = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Region_Volcano")
	local sp = r and r:FindFirstChild("PartSpawner")
	if not sp then return end
	for _, o in ipairs(sp:GetDescendants()) do
		if o.Name == "VolcanoBoulder" then pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
	end
end
local Wd = Section(WorldTab, "Scenery", "Local-only changes to the LT2 map", "night")
Toggle(Wd, "Water Visible", true, function(v)
	async(function()
		scanWorld()
		for _, d in ipairs(WD.water) do
			if d.p.Parent then d.p.Transparency = v and d.t or 1 d.p.CanCollide = false d.p.CanTouch = v end
		end
	end)
end, nil, nil, "Off = invisible and no swimming")
Toggle(Wd, "Lower Bridge", false, function(v)
	local bridge = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Bridge")
	if v then
		if bridge and not WD.bridge then WD.bridge = bridge:Clone() end
		local vlb = bridge and bridge:FindFirstChild("VerticalLiftBridge")
		local lift = vlb and vlb:FindFirstChild("Lift")
		if lift then
			for _, c in ipairs(lift:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("BasePart") and c.Name == "Base" then c.CFrame = CFrame.new(c.Position.X, 6.5, c.Position.Z) * c.CFrame.Rotation end
			end
		end
		if vlb then
			local kill = { BRope = true, Structure = true, Weight = true, WRope = true }
			for _, c in ipairs(vlb:GetChildren()) do if kill[c.Name] then c:Destroy() end end
		end
	elseif WD.bridge then
		if bridge then bridge:Destroy() end
		WD.bridge:Clone().Parent = Workspace
	end
end, nil, nil, "Lowers the lift bridge on your screen so you can walk across")
Toggle(Wd, "Tundra Boulders", true, function(v)
	async(function()
		scanWorld()
		for _, d in ipairs(WD.boulders) do
			if d.p.Parent then d.p.Transparency = v and d.t or 1 d.p.CanCollide = v end
		end
	end)
end)
Toggle(Wd, "Volcano Boulders", true, function(v) WD.volcano = v if not v then sweepVolcano() end end, nil, nil, "Off = keeps deleting them locally")
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if not WD.volcano then pcall(sweepVolcano) end
		task.wait(0.3)
	end
end)
Toggle(Wd, "Spook Event", false, function(v)
	local s = Lighting:FindFirstChild("Spook")
	if s then s.Value = v else notify("Spook value not found in Lighting", T.Warn) end
end)
local origLight
Toggle(Wd, "Enhanced Visuals", false, function(v)
	if v then
		origLight = origLight or { Brightness = Lighting.Brightness, Exposure = Lighting.ExposureCompensation }
		Lighting.Brightness = 3
		Lighting.ExposureCompensation = 0.5
		local b = Lighting:FindFirstChild("EnhancedBloom") or new("BloomEffect", { Name = "EnhancedBloom" }, Lighting)
		b.Intensity, b.Size, b.Threshold, b.Enabled = 1, 24, 2, true
		local c = Lighting:FindFirstChild("EnhancedCC") or new("ColorCorrectionEffect", { Name = "EnhancedCC" }, Lighting)
		c.Contrast, c.Saturation, c.TintColor, c.Enabled = 0.1, 0.15, Color3.fromRGB(255, 253, 245), true
		local r = Lighting:FindFirstChild("EnhancedRays") or new("SunRaysEffect", { Name = "EnhancedRays" }, Lighting)
		r.Intensity, r.Spread, r.Enabled = 0.1, 1, true
	else
		if origLight then Lighting.Brightness = origLight.Brightness Lighting.ExposureCompensation = origLight.Exposure end
		for _, n in ipairs({ "EnhancedBloom", "EnhancedCC", "EnhancedRays" }) do
			local e = Lighting:FindFirstChild(n)
			if e then e.Enabled = false end
		end
	end
end, nil, nil, "Bloom, colour correction and sun rays")
addCleanup(function()
	if origLight then Lighting.Brightness = origLight.Brightness Lighting.ExposureCompensation = origLight.Exposure end
	for _, n in ipairs({ "EnhancedBloom", "EnhancedCC", "EnhancedRays" }) do
		local e = Lighting:FindFirstChild(n)
		if e then e:Destroy() end
	end
end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- PROTECTION: axe recovery
local AR = { on = false, conn = nil, diedConn = nil, deathPos = nil, radius = 50, maxAxes = 9, timeout = 3 }
local function heldToolCount()
	local n = 0
	for _, c in ipairs({ LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"), getChar(), Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name) }) do
		if c then for _, v in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do if v.Name == "Tool" and v:IsA("Tool") then n = n + 1 end end end
	end
	return n
end
local function recoverAxes()
	task.wait(0.1)
	if not AR.on then return end
	local interaction = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Interaction")
	local ci = interaction and interaction:FindFirstChild("ClientInteracted")
	local pm = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
	if not (ci and pm) then return end
	local axes = {}
	for _, o in ipairs(pm:GetDescendants()) do
		if o.Name == "Model" and o:IsA("Model") then
			local of = o:FindFirstChild("Owner")
			local os2 = of and of:FindFirstChild("OwnerString")
			if os2 and os2.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
				local handle = o:FindFirstChild("Handle") or o.PrimaryPart
				if handle and (not AR.deathPos or (handle.Position - AR.deathPos).Magnitude <= AR.radius) then table.insert(axes, o) end
			end
		end
		if #axes >= AR.maxAxes then break end
	end
	local got = 0
	for _, axe in ipairs(axes) do
		local handle = axe:FindFirstChild("Handle") or axe.PrimaryPart
		if handle then
			local before = heldToolCount()
			local dl = os.clock() + AR.timeout
			while os.clock() < dl and axe.Parent do
				ci:FireServer(axe, "Pick up tool", handle.CFrame)
				task.wait(0.15)
				if heldToolCount() > before then got = got + 1 break end
			end
		end
	end
	if got > 0 then notify("Recovered " .. got .. " axe(s)", T.Good) end
end
local function hookDeath(char)
	if AR.diedConn then AR.diedConn:Disconnect() end
	local h = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
	if not h then return end
	AR.diedConn = h.Died:Connect(function()
		local r = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		AR.deathPos = r and r.Position or nil
	end)
end
local function setAxeRecovery(v)
	AR.on = v
	if AR.conn then AR.conn:Disconnect() AR.conn = nil end
	if AR.diedConn then AR.diedConn:Disconnect() AR.diedConn = nil end
	if not v then return end
	if LocalPlayer.Character then task.spawn(hookDeath, LocalPlayer.Character) end
	AR.conn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
		task.spawn(hookDeath, char)
		task.spawn(recoverAxes)
	end)
end
local Pr = Section(ProtTab, "Axe Recovery", "After you die, picks your axes back up from where you died", "godmode")
Toggle(Pr, "Axe Recovery", true, setAxeRecovery, nil, "lt2axerecover")
setAxeRecovery(true)
local PrOpt = Collapse(Pr, "Recovery Settings")
Slider(PrOpt, "Search Radius (studs)", 10, 200, 50, function(v) AR.radius = v end)
Slider(PrOpt, "Max Axes Per Respawn", 1, 20, 9, function(v) AR.maxAxes = v end)
Slider(PrOpt, "Pickup Timeout (s)", 1, 10, 3, function(v) AR.timeout = v end)
addCleanup(function() setAxeRecovery(false) end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- TELEPORTS: players, their plots, waypoints
local PS = {}
local function playerNames()
	local n = {}
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= LocalPlayer then table.insert(n, p.Name) end end
	table.sort(n)
	if #n == 0 then n = { "No other players" } end
	return n
end
local Tpl = Section(Tabs["Teleports"], "LT2 Players", "Jump to a player or their plot", "teleports")
local plDd = Dropdown(Tpl, "Player", playerNames(), 1)
Button(Tpl, "Teleport To Player", "Go", function()
	local p = Players:FindFirstChild(plDd.Name())
	local r = p and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not r then notify("Player not found", T.Warn) return end
	tpTo(r.Position)
end)
Button(Tpl, "Teleport To Their Plot", "Go", function()
	local p = Players:FindFirstChild(plDd.Name())
	local props = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Properties")
	if not (p and props) then notify("Player not found", T.Warn) return end
	for _, plot in ipairs(props:GetChildren()) do
		local o = plot:FindFirstChild("Owner")
		if o and o.Value == p then
			local origin = plot:FindFirstChild("OriginSquare") or plot:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
			if origin then tpTo(origin.Position) return end
		end
	end
	notify("They don't own a plot", T.Warn)
end)
Button(Tpl, "Refresh Player List", "Refresh", function() plDd.SetOptions(playerNames()) end)
bind(Players.PlayerAdded, function() plDd.SetOptions(playerNames(), true) end)
bind(Players.PlayerRemoving, function() task.defer(function() plDd.SetOptions(playerNames(), true) end) end)

local Wp = Section(Tabs["Teleports"], "LT2 Waypoints", "Save where you are and come back later", "teleports")
local wps, wpNames = {}, {}
local wpDd = Dropdown(Wp, "Waypoint", { "None saved" }, 1)
Toggle(Wp, "Teleport With Vehicle", true, function(v) LT.carry = v end, nil, "lt2carry", "Takes the vehicle (and loose cargo on pickups / trucks) with you")
Button(Wp, "Save Waypoint Here", "Save", function()
	local h = getHum()
	local seat = h and h.SeatPart
	local veh = seat and seat:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
	local pos = veh and veh:GetPivot().Position or (getRoot() and getRoot().Position)
	if not pos then return end
	local name = "Waypoint " .. (#wpNames + 1)
	wps[name] = pos
	table.insert(wpNames, name)
	wpDd.SetOptions(wpNames)
	wpDd.Set(#wpNames, true)
	notify("Saved " .. name, T.Good)
end)
Button(Wp, "Teleport To Waypoint", "Go", function()
	local p = wps[wpDd.Name()]
	if p then tpTo(p) else notify("Save a waypoint first", T.Warn) end
end)

-- ====================================================== loops
-- Tree ESP: billboards on the closest trees only, rebuilt every 1.5s
local espGuis = {}
local function clearEsp() for m, g in pairs(espGuis) do g:Destroy() espGuis[m] = nil end end
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		task.wait(1.5)
		if not LT.esp then
			if next(espGuis) then clearEsp() end
		else
			local r = getRoot()
			local list = {}
			if r then
				for _, f in ipairs(regions()) do
					for _, m in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
						local c = m:IsA("Model") and classOf(m)
						if c and (LT.espAll or c == LT.class) then
							local b = baseSection(m)
							if b then
								local d = (b.Position - r.Position).Magnitude
								if d <= LT.espMax then table.insert(list, { m = m, b = b, d = d, c = c }) end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
			table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.d < b.d end)
			local want = {}
			for i = 1, math.min(#list, 60) do
				local e = list[i]
				want[e.m] = true
				local g = espGuis[e.m]
				if not g then
					g = new("BillboardGui", { Adornee = e.b, AlwaysOnTop = true, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(130, 18), StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0) }, EspGui)
					new("TextLabel", { Name = "L", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = T.Good, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 12 }, g)
					espGuis[e.m] = g
				end
				g.L.Text = e.c .. " (" .. #sectionsOf(e.m) .. ")  " .. math.floor(e.d) .. "m"
			end
			for m, g in pairs(espGuis) do if not want[m] then g:Destroy() espGuis[m] = nil end end
		end
	end
end)
-- info rows
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		local models = myLogs()
		local secs = 0
		for _, m in ipairs(models) do for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do if d.Name == "WoodSection" and d:IsA("BasePart") then secs = secs + 1 end end end
		if logRow then logRow.Text = "Logs: " .. #models .. " log model(s)  /  " .. secs .. " section(s)" end
		if LT.class then refreshInfo() end
		task.wait(5)
	end
end)
bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
	if gp or Live.BindingActive or not Live.Unlocked or i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
	if LT.stopKey ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and i.KeyCode == LT.stopKey then stopAll() notify("Stopped", T.Sub) end
end)
onUnload(function()
	LT.token = LT.token + 1
	LT.busy = false
	dropLock()
	clearEsp()
	for _, f in ipairs(cleanups) do pcall(f) end
end)
task.defer(refreshTypes)]==],
		doomspire = [==[
-- Solos Hub game module: doomspire.lua
-- Loaded by the main hub script (picker -> loadstring). Do not run this file on its own.
--

local Hub, S, T, C = ...
local API = Hub.API
local Live = API.Live -- live values: Live.Running, Live.Unlocked, Live.BindingActive
local Dropdown, Collapse, PlayerList = API.Dropdown, API.Collapse, API.PlayerList

local AddTab = API.AddTab
local Button = API.Button
local EspGui = API.EspGui
local InfoRow = API.InfoRow
local Input = API.Input
local Keybind = API.Keybind
local LocalPlayer = API.LocalPlayer
local Players = API.Players

local new = API.new

if game.PlaceId ~= 79602128065352 then notify("You're not in Doomspire - this module needs that game", T.Warn) end
local Panel = Hub.GamePanel("Doomspire", "attack", "Bombs, raids and combat.")
local BombTab = Panel.Sub("Bombs", "attack", "Bomb patterns and drop tools.")
local RaidTab = Panel.Sub("Raids", "aimbot", "Raid patterns.")

TabButtons["Doomspire"].Btn.LayoutOrder = -19
-- keep only the tabs that matter for this game (flying / noclip live in Player)
filterTabs({ "Doomspire", "Dashboard", "Player", "ESP", "Players", "Teleports", "Commands", "Hub Chat", "Theme", "Settings" })

local DS = {
	bombSlot = 4, weapSlot = 5, weapMode = 1, weapName = "sword", cps = 40, timeout = 3, dist = 3,
	delay = 0.25, height = 0, mode = 1, equip = true, loop = false, key = Enum.KeyCode.B,
	killKey = Enum.KeyCode.K, stopKey = Enum.KeyCode.X,
	skipTeam = true, home = true, bombCount = 10, gap = 0.05, ringR = 25, ringN = 12, carpet = 2, spacing = 12,
	aura = false, auraR = 20, autoClick = false, loopAll = false, loopSel = false,
	reach = false, reachSize = 14, busy = false, token = 0, rot = 0,
}
local MODES = { "Mouse", "Me", "Selected Player", "Nearest Player" }
local WEAPONS = { "Hotbar Slot", "Held Tool", "Tool Name", "All Tools" }

-- ---------------------------------------------------- tools / hotbar slots
local slots = {}
local slotRow
local function snapshot()
	table.clear(slots)
	local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
	if bp then for _, c in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("Tool") then table.insert(slots, c.Name) end end end
	local parts = {}
	for i, n in ipairs(slots) do table.insert(parts, i .. ":" .. n) end
	if slotRow then slotRow.Text = "Slots  " .. (#parts > 0 and table.concat(parts, "   ") or "(unequip your tool, then press Refresh)") end
end
local function toolNamed(n)
	local ch, bp = getChar(), LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
	local t = (ch and ch:FindFirstChild(n)) or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(n))
	return t and t:IsA("Tool") and t or nil
end
local function slotTool(n) local nm = slots[n] return nm and toolNamed(nm) or nil end
local function allTools()
	local list, ch, bp = {}, getChar(), LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
	for _, src in ipairs({ ch, bp }) do
		for _, c in ipairs(src and src:GetChildren() or {}) do if c:IsA("Tool") then table.insert(list, c) end end
	end
	return list
end
local function equip(tool)
	if tool.Parent ~= getChar() then
		local h = getHum()
		if h then h:EquipTool(tool) else tool.Parent = getChar() end
	end
end
local function getBomb() return toolNamed("Timebomb") or slotTool(DS.bombSlot) end
local function getWeapon()
	local m = DS.weapMode
	if m == 1 then
		local t = slotTool(DS.weapSlot)
		if t then return t end
		for _, c in ipairs(allTools()) do if c.Name:lower():find("sword", 1, true) then return c end end
		return nil
	elseif m == 2 then
		local ch = getChar()
		return ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
	elseif m == 3 then
		for _, c in ipairs(allTools()) do if c.Name:lower():find(DS.weapName:lower(), 1, true) then return c end end
		return nil
	else
		local list = allTools()
		if #list == 0 then return nil end
		DS.rot = DS.rot % #list + 1
		return list[DS.rot]
	end
end
local function swing()
	local tool = getWeapon()
	if not tool then return false end
	equip(tool)
	pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
	return true
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- helpers
local lastWarn = 0
local function warn2(text)
	if os.clock() - lastWarn > 2 then lastWarn = os.clock() notify(text, T.Warn) end
end
local function alive(p)
	local h = p and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	return h ~= nil and h.Health > 0
end
local function rootOf(p) return p and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end
local function hpOf(p)
	local h = p and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	return h and h.Health or 0
end
local function stopAll() DS.token = DS.token + 1 DS.busy = false end
local function runSeq(fn)
	if DS.busy then notify("Already running - press Stop first", T.Warn) return end
	DS.token = DS.token + 1
	local my = DS.token
	DS.busy = true
	task.spawn(function()
		local ok = function() return Live.Running and DS.token == my end
		local good, err = pcall(fn, ok)
		if not good then warn2("Error: " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 60)) end
		if DS.token == my then DS.busy = false end
	end)
end
local function enemies()
	local list = {}
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p ~= LocalPlayer and alive(p) and rootOf(p) and not (DS.skipTeam and S.SameTeam(p)) then table.insert(list, p) end
	end
	local r = getRoot()
	if DS.prio == 2 then
		table.sort(list, function(a, b) return hpOf(a) < hpOf(b) end)
	elseif DS.prio == 3 then
		table.sort(list, function(a, b) return hpOf(a) > hpOf(b) end)
	elseif r then
		table.sort(list, function(a, b) return (rootOf(a).Position - r.Position).Magnitude < (rootOf(b).Position - r.Position).Magnitude end)
	end
	return list
end
local function goHome(home)
	local r = getRoot()
	if r and DS.home and home then r.CFrame = home r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- bombs
local function prepBomb()
	local tool = getBomb()
	local ev = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Fire")
	if not ev then warn2("Timebomb not found (check the slot numbers)") return nil end
	if DS.equip then equip(tool) end
	return ev
end
local function fireAt(ev, pos)
	pcall(function() ev:FireServer(CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, DS.height, 0))) end)
end
local function bombSpam(ev, pos, count, ok)
	for _ = 1, count do
		if not ok() then return end
		fireAt(ev, pos)
		task.wait(math.max(DS.gap, 0.01))
	end
end
local function targetPos(mode)
	if mode == 1 then
		local hit = LocalPlayer:GetMouse().Hit
		return hit and hit.Position
	elseif mode == 2 then
		local r = getRoot()
		return r and r.Position
	elseif mode == 3 then
		local tr = rootOf(S.Target)
		if not tr then return nil end
		if DS.lead then return tr.Position + tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity * DS.leadT end
		return tr.Position
	else
		local p = nearestPlayer()
		local tr = rootOf(p)
		if not tr then return nil end
		if DS.lead then return tr.Position + tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity * DS.leadT end
		return tr.Position
	end
end
local function drop(mode)
	local pos = targetPos(mode or DS.mode)
	if not pos then warn2("No drop position (" .. MODES[mode or DS.mode] .. ")") return end
	local ev = prepBomb()
	if ev then fireAt(ev, pos) end
end
S.DoomDrop = drop

-- enemy team spawns
local function enemySpawns()
	local out, anyTeam = {}, false
	local color = LocalPlayer.TeamColor
	local hasTeam = LocalPlayer.Team ~= nil
	for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("SpawnLocation") and not d.Neutral then
			anyTeam = true
			if not hasTeam or d.TeamColor ~= color then table.insert(out, d) end
		end
	end
	if not anyTeam then
		-- fallback: parts called "...spawn..." that aren't the colour of your team
		for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name:lower():find("spawn", 1, true) and not d:IsDescendantOf(getChar() or Workspace) and d.BrickColor ~= color then
				table.insert(out, d)
			end
		end
	end
	return out
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- weapon attack
local function attack(p, ok, maxTime)
	local t0, gap = os.clock(), 1 / math.max(DS.cps, 1)
	while ok() and alive(p) and os.clock() - t0 < (maxTime or DS.timeout) do
		local r, tr = getRoot(), rootOf(p)
		if not r or not tr then break end
		local back = tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, DS.dist)
		r.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(back.Position, tr.Position)
		r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		if not swing() then warn2("No weapon found (check weapon slot / mode)") return false end
		task.wait(gap)
	end
	return not alive(p)
end
local function killList(list)
	runSeq(function(ok)
		local r = getRoot()
		if not r then return end
		local home, n = r.CFrame, 0
		for _, p in ipairs(list) do
			if not ok() then break end
			if alive(p) and attack(p, ok) then n = n + 1 end
		end
		goHome(home)
		notify(("Done - %d of %d eliminated"):format(n, #list), n > 0 and T.Good or T.Warn)
	end)
end
local function killAll() killList(enemies()) end
local function killSelected()
	if S.Target and S.Target.Parent then killList({ S.Target }) else notify("Select a player in the Players tab first", T.Warn) end
end
local function killNearest()
	local p = enemies()[1]
	if p then killList({ p }) else notify("No enemies found", T.Warn) end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- loops
task.spawn(function() -- auto bomb loop
	while Live.Running do
		if DS.loop then drop() task.wait(math.max(DS.delay, 0.03)) else task.wait(0.1) end
	end
end)
task.spawn(function() -- kill aura / auto click / loop kills
	while Live.Running do
		local gap = 1 / math.max(DS.cps, 1)
		if DS.aura and not DS.busy then
			local r = getRoot()
			if r then
				for _, p in ipairs(enemies()) do
					if (rootOf(p).Position - r.Position).Magnitude <= DS.auraR then swing() break end
				end
			end
		end
		if DS.autoClick and not DS.busy then
			local ch = getChar()
			local t = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
			if t then pcall(function() t:Activate() end) end
		end
		if DS.loopAll and not DS.busy then killAll() end
		if DS.loopSel and not DS.busy and S.Target and alive(S.Target) then killList({ S.Target }) end
		task.wait(math.max(gap, 0.03))
	end
end)
-- sword reach (client side: bigger handle = bigger hit area)
local reachOrig = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local function restoreReach()
	for h, o in pairs(reachOrig) do if h.Parent then h.Size, h.Massless = o.size, o.massless end end
	table.clear(reachOrig)
end
bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
	if not DS.reach then return end
	local ch = getChar()
	local tool = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
	local h = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
	if h and h:IsA("BasePart") then
		if not reachOrig[h] then reachOrig[h] = { size = h.Size, massless = h.Massless } end
		h.Size = Vector3.new(DS.reachSize, DS.reachSize, DS.reachSize)
		h.Massless = true
	end
end)
bind(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
	if gp or Live.BindingActive or not Live.Unlocked or i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
	if DS.key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and i.KeyCode == DS.key then drop(1)
	elseif DS.killKey ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and i.KeyCode == DS.killKey then killAll()
	elseif DS.stopKey ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and i.KeyCode == DS.stopKey then stopAll() notify("Stopped", T.Sub) end
end)
bind(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function() task.wait(1.5) if Live.Running then snapshot() end end)
onUnload(function() stopAll() DS.loop, DS.aura, DS.autoClick, DS.loopAll, DS.loopSel, DS.reach = false, false, false, false, false, false restoreReach() end)
