local Raid = Section(RaidTab, "Bomb Raids", "Mass bombing sequences (press Stop to cancel)", "attack")
Toggle(Raid, "Return To Start Afterwards", true, function(v) DS.home = v end, nil, "dshome")
Button(Raid, "Raid Every Enemy Spawn", "Start", function()
	runSeq(function(ok)
		local r = getRoot()
		if not r then return end
		local spawns = enemySpawns()
		if #spawns == 0 then notify("No enemy spawns found", T.Warn) return end
		local ev = prepBomb()
		if not ev then return end
		local home = r.CFrame
		for i, sp in ipairs(spawns) do
			if not ok() then break end
			local root = getRoot()
			if not root then break end
			root.CFrame = sp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			task.wait(0.15)
			bombSpam(ev, sp.Position, DS.bombCount, ok)
		end
		goHome(home)
		notify("Spawn raid finished (" .. #spawns .. " spawns)", T.Good)
	end)
end)
Button(Raid, "Bomb Every Enemy Player", "Start", function()
	runSeq(function(ok)
		local ev = prepBomb()
		if not ev then return end
		local list = enemies()
		for _, p in ipairs(list) do
			if not ok() then break end
			local tr = rootOf(p)
			if tr then bombSpam(ev, tr.Position, DS.bombCount, ok) end
		end
		notify("Bombed " .. #list .. " players", T.Good)
	end)
end)
Button(Raid, "Bomb Selected Player", "Start", function()
	runSeq(function(ok)
		local tr = rootOf(S.Target)
		if not tr then notify("Select a player first", T.Warn) return end
		local ev = prepBomb()
		if ev then bombSpam(ev, tr.Position, DS.bombCount, ok) end
	end)
end)
Button(Raid, "Bomb Ring Around Me", "Start", function()
	runSeq(function(ok)
		local r = getRoot()
		if not r then return end
		local ev = prepBomb()
		if not ev then return end
		for i = 1, DS.ringN do
			if not ok() then break end
			local a = (i / DS.ringN) * math.pi * 2
			fireAt(ev, r.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * DS.ringR, 0, math.sin(a) * DS.ringR))
			task.wait(math.max(DS.gap, 0.01))
		end
	end)
end)
Button(Raid, "Carpet Bomb At Mouse", "Start", function()
	runSeq(function(ok)
		local hit = LocalPlayer:GetMouse().Hit
		if not hit then return end
		local ev = prepBomb()
		if not ev then return end
		for x = -DS.carpet, DS.carpet do
			for z = -DS.carpet, DS.carpet do
				if not ok() then return end
				fireAt(ev, hit.Position + Vector3.new(x * DS.spacing, 0, z * DS.spacing))
				task.wait(math.max(DS.gap, 0.01))
			end
		end
	end)
end)
Button(Raid, "Stop Everything", "Stop", function() stopAll() notify("Stopped", T.Sub) end)

Slider(RaidOpt, "Gap Between Bombs (x0.01s)", 1, 50, 5, function(v) DS.gap = v / 100 end, "dsgap")
Slider(RaidOpt, "Ring Radius", 5, 120, 25, function(v) DS.ringR = v end, "dsringr")
Slider(RaidOpt, "Ring Bombs", 4, 60, 12, function(v) DS.ringN = v end, "dsringn")
Slider(RaidOpt, "Carpet Size (grid radius)", 1, 6, 2, function(v) DS.carpet = v end, "dscarpet")

local spInfo = InfoRow(Sp, "Enemy spawns: press Scan")
local cache, idx = {}, 0
Button(Sp, "Scan Enemy Spawns", "Scan", function()
	cache = enemySpawns()
	spInfo.Text = "Enemy spawns found: " .. #cache
end)
Button(Sp, "Teleport To Next Enemy Spawn", "Go", function()
	if #cache == 0 then cache = enemySpawns() spInfo.Text = "Enemy spawns found: " .. #cache end
	if #cache == 0 then notify("No enemy spawns found", T.Warn) return end
	idx = idx % #cache + 1
	local r = getRoot()
	if r then r.CFrame = cache[idx].CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0) notify("Spawn " .. idx .. " of " .. #cache) end
end)
Button(Sp, "Teleport To My Spawn", "Go", function()
	local r = getRoot()
	local rl = LocalPlayer.RespawnLocation
	if not rl then
		for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("SpawnLocation") and not d.Neutral and d.TeamColor == LocalPlayer.TeamColor then rl = d break end
		end
	end
	if r and rl then r.CFrame = rl.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0) else notify("Couldn't find your spawn", T.Warn) end
end)

-- ====================================================== extra features (v3.17)
DS.rainR, DS.orbit, DS.orbitA, DS.loopBombAll, DS.spawnCamp, DS.noTp = 25, false, 0, false, false, false
DS.stick, DS.dodge, DS.dodgeR, DS.bombEsp, DS.lead, DS.leadT, DS.lineStep = false, false, 20, false, false, 0.25, 8
local liveBombs, bombHl, lastDodge = {}, {}, 0
local function isLiveBomb(d)
	if not (d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Model")) then return false end
	if not d.Name:lower():find("bomb", 1, true) then return false end
	local me, bp = getChar(), LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
	if (me and d:IsDescendantOf(me)) or (bp and d:IsDescendantOf(bp)) then return false end
	return d:IsDescendantOf(Workspace)
end
for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do if isLiveBomb(d) then liveBombs[d] = true end end
bind(Workspace.DescendantAdded, function(d)
	if d.Name:lower():find("bomb", 1, true) then
		task.defer(function() if isLiveBomb(d) then liveBombs[d] = true end end)
	end
end)
local function bombPos(b)
	if b:IsA("BasePart") then return b.Position end
	local ok, pv = pcall(function() return b:GetPivot().Position end)
	return ok and pv or nil
end
task.spawn(function() -- bomb ESP + auto dodge
	while Live.Running do
		for b in pairs(liveBombs) do
			if not b.Parent then
				liveBombs[b] = nil
				if bombHl[b] then bombHl[b]:Destroy() bombHl[b] = nil end
			elseif DS.bombEsp and not bombHl[b] then
				bombHl[b] = new("Highlight", { Adornee = b, FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70), OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), FillTransparency = 0.3, DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop }, EspGui)
			end
		end
		if not DS.bombEsp then
			for b, h in pairs(bombHl) do h:Destroy() bombHl[b] = nil end
		end
		local r = getRoot()
		if DS.dodge and r and os.clock() - lastDodge > 0.6 then
			for b in pairs(liveBombs) do
				local bp = bombPos(b)
				if bp and (bp - r.Position).Magnitude <= DS.dodgeR then
					local away = r.Position - bp
					away = Vector3.new(away.X, 0, away.Z)
					away = away.Magnitude > 0.5 and away.Unit or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
					r.CFrame = r.CFrame + away * (DS.dodgeR + 8)
					r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					lastDodge = os.clock()
					break
				end
			end
		end
		task.wait(0.1)
	end
end)

-- bomb patterns
local function randomIn(radius)
	local a, d = math.random() * math.pi * 2, math.sqrt(math.random()) * radius
	return Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * d, 0, math.sin(a) * d)
end
local function bombEveryone()
	runSeq(function(ok)
		local ev = prepBomb()
		if not ev then return end
		for _, p in ipairs(enemies()) do
			if not ok() then break end
			local tr = rootOf(p)
			if tr then bombSpam(ev, tr.Position + tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity * (DS.lead and DS.leadT or 0), DS.bombCount, ok) end
		end
	end)
end
local function raidSpawns()
	runSeq(function(ok)
		local r = getRoot()
		if not r then return end
		local spawns = enemySpawns()
		if #spawns == 0 then notify("No enemy spawns found", T.Warn) return end
		local ev = prepBomb()
		if not ev then return end
		local home = r.CFrame
		for _, sp in ipairs(spawns) do
			if not ok() then break end
			if not DS.noTp then
				local root = getRoot()
				if not root then break end
				root.CFrame = sp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)
				root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				task.wait(0.15)
			end
			bombSpam(ev, sp.Position, DS.bombCount, ok)
		end
		if not DS.noTp then goHome(home) end
	end)
end
task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		if DS.orbit and not DS.busy then
			local r, ev = getRoot(), getBomb() and getBomb():FindFirstChild("Fire")
			if r and ev then
				DS.orbitA = DS.orbitA + 0.7
				if DS.equip then equip(getBomb()) end
				fireAt(ev, r.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(DS.orbitA) * DS.ringR, 0, math.sin(DS.orbitA) * DS.ringR))
			end
		end
		if DS.loopBombAll and not DS.busy then bombEveryone() end
		if DS.spawnCamp and not DS.busy then raidSpawns() end
		if DS.stick and not DS.busy and S.Target and alive(S.Target) then
			local r, tr = getRoot(), rootOf(S.Target)
			if r and tr then
				local back = tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, DS.dist)
				r.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(back.Position, tr.Position)
				r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				swing()
			end
		end
		local w = 0.1
		if DS.orbit then w = DS.delay end
		task.wait(math.max(w, 0.05))
	end
end)
onUnload(function()
	DS.orbit, DS.loopBombAll, DS.spawnCamp, DS.stick, DS.dodge, DS.bombEsp = false, false, false, false, false, false
	for b, h in pairs(bombHl) do h:Destroy() end
	table.clear(bombHl)
end)

end)

local Rx = Section(RaidTab, "Spawn Raids+", "More ways to hit the enemy spawns", "teleports")

-- ---- Fighting tab
local PRIOS = { "Nearest", "Lowest Health", "Highest Health" }

	elseif i == 2 then

	else
		local spawns = enemySpawns()
		if #spawns == 0 or not r then notify("No enemy spawns found", T.Warn) return end

	end
end)]==],
		bab = [==[
-- Solos Hub game module: bab.lua  (Build A Boat For Treasure)  -- v3
-- Loaded by the main hub script (picker -> loadstring). Do not run this file on its own.
--
-- Written from memory of how the game is laid out, UNTESTED in game:
--   stages:  Workspace.BoatStages.NormalStages.CaveStage1 .. CaveStage10   (each has a DarknessPart checkpoint trigger)
--   finish:  Workspace.BoatStages.NormalStages.TheEnd.GoldenChest.Trigger   (touching it pays out gold, then you respawn)
-- Gold farm = move your own character through each stage trigger, then the chest, then repeat. The game trusts the client
-- for where your character is, which is why this style of farm works at all. Every name above is looked up defensively;
-- if the game changed, the Status row tells you which part it could not find instead of erroring.
--
-- v3: local build library, plot-relative placement preview, observed-tool auto-build.
--     Standard block support only; live placement must be learned and is not pre-verified here.
-- v2: farm movement types (Teleport / Tween / Step Hop), speed + easing, presets, noclip, wiggle + touch assist,
--     stage range, chest-only mode, lap timeout, run/time limits, lap stats, float platform, safe spot, anti-AFK.

local Hub, S, T, C = ...
local API = Hub.API
local Live = API.Live -- live values: Live.Running, Live.Unlocked, Live.BindingActive
local Dropdown, Collapse = API.Dropdown, API.Collapse

local Button = API.Button
local EspGui = API.EspGui
local Input = API.Input
local InfoRow = API.InfoRow
local copyText = API.copyText
local LocalPlayer = API.LocalPlayer
local RunService = API.RunService
local Section = API.Section
local Slider = API.Slider
local TabButtons = API.TabButtons
local Tabs = API.Tabs
local Toggle = API.Toggle
local Workspace = API.Workspace
local bind = API.bind
local filterTabs = API.filterTabs
local getChar = API.getChar
local getHum = API.getHum
local getRoot = API.getRoot
local new = API.new
local notify = API.notify
local onUnload = API.onUnload

if game.PlaceId ~= 537413528 then notify("You're not in Build A Boat - this module needs that game", T.Warn) end
local Panel = Hub.GamePanel("Build A Boat", "autofarm", "Gold farm, movement options and tools.")
local FarmTab = Panel.Sub("Gold Farm", "autofarm", "Start the farm, pick a preset, watch the numbers.")
local SetTab = Panel.Sub("Settings", "settings", "Movement type, speed, timing and limits.")
local ShopTab = Panel.Sub("Shop", "loot", "Buy and open chests, find the game remotes.")
local ToolsTab = Panel.Sub("Tools", "magnet", "Markers, platforms and quick actions.")
local HwTab = Panel.Sub("Halloween", "night", "Trick or treat houses - candy with danger escape.")
Hub.gameHeader("BUILD A BOAT")
TabButtons["Build A Boat"].Btn.LayoutOrder = -19
filterTabs({ "Build A Boat", "Dashboard", "Player", "ESP", "Players", "Teleports", "Commands", "Hub Chat", "Theme", "Settings" })

local METHODS = { "Teleport (instant)", "Tween (smooth)", "Step Hop (jumps)" }
local EASE_NAMES = { "Linear", "Smooth", "Ease Out" }
local EASE = {
	function(a) return a end,
	function(a) return 0.5 - math.cos(a * math.pi) / 2 end,
	function(a) return 1 - (1 - a) * (1 - a) end,
}

local B = {
	token = 0, farming = false,
	method = 1, speed = 400, ease = 2, lift = 0,
	stageWait = 2.3, jitter = 0.3, chestWait = 20, settle = 1.5, lapTimeout = 120,
	startStage = 1, endStage = 10, chestOnly = false,
	maxRuns = 0, maxMinutes = 0,
	hold = true, wiggle = false, touchAssist = false, resetIfStuck = true,
	noclip = true, noclipAlways = false, antiAfk = true,
	runs = 0, active = 0, mark = nil, startGold = nil,
	lastLap = nil, bestLap = nil, lapTotal = 0,
}
local statusRow, runsRow, lastRow, bestRow, avgRow, goldRow, gainRow, rateRow, perHrRow, timeRow
local farmToggle
local methodDd, speedSl, waitSl, jitterSl

-- ---------------------------------------------------- finding things in the map
local function stageFolder()
	local bs = Workspace:FindFirstChild("BoatStages")
	return bs and bs:FindFirstChild("NormalStages")
end
local function partOf(inst)
	if not inst then return nil end
	if inst:IsA("BasePart") then return inst end
	return inst:FindFirstChild("DarknessPart") or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end
local function stagePart(i)
	local f = stageFolder()
	return f and partOf(f:FindFirstChild("CaveStage" .. i))
end
local function chestPart()
	local f = stageFolder()
	local e = f and f:FindFirstChild("TheEnd")
	local chest = e and e:FindFirstChild("GoldenChest")
	if not chest then return nil end
	return chest:FindFirstChild("Trigger") or chest:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end
local function teamZone()
	local team = LocalPlayer.Team
	if not team then return nil end
	local want = team.Name:lower()
	for _, ch in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
		local n = ch.Name:lower()
		if n == want .. "zone" or n == want .. "base" then return partOf(ch) end
	end
	for _, ch in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
		local n = ch.Name:lower()
		if n:find(want, 1, true) and n:find("zone", 1, true) then return partOf(ch) end
	end
	return nil
end

-- gold is read from a value object named "Gold" somewhere under the player (leaderstats / Data). Only used for the stats.
local goldVal
local function findGold()
	if goldVal and goldVal.Parent then return goldVal end
	local ok, v = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Gold", true) end)
	if ok and v and v:IsA("ValueBase") then goldVal = v return v end
	return nil
end
local function commas(n)
	local s = tostring(math.floor(n))
	local out = s:reverse():gsub("(%d%d%d)", "%1,"):reverse()
	return (out:gsub("^,", ""))
end
local function clock(sec)
	sec = math.floor(sec)
	return string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", math.floor(sec / 3600), math.floor(sec / 60) % 60, sec % 60)
end
local function setStatus(text, color)
	if statusRow then statusRow.Set(text, color) end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- character + movement
local function alive()
	local h = getHum()
	return h ~= nil and h.Health > 0 and getRoot() ~= nil
end
local function killChar()
	local h = getHum()
	if h then h.Health = 0 end
end

-- plain teleport used by the Teleports tab (keeps the part's orientation like before)
local function tpTo(part, lift)
	local r = getRoot()
	if not (r and part) then return false end
	r.CFrame = part.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, lift or 0, 0)
	r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
	return true
end

-- keep your own rotation, only change position (so tweening does not tilt the character)
local function pin(part, up)
	local r = getRoot()
	if not (r and part) then return false end
	r.CFrame = CFrame.new(part.Position + Vector3.new(0, B.lift + (up or 0), 0)) * (r.CFrame - r.CFrame.Position)
	r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
	return true
end

-- optional extra: also ask the executor to fire the touch event (does nothing if the executor has no firetouchinterest)
local function touch(part)
	local f = API.env.firetouchinterest
	local r = getRoot()
	if type(f) == "function" and r and part then
		pcall(f, r, part, 0)
		pcall(f, r, part, 1)
	end
end

local function mine(my) return Live.Running and B.token == my end

local function waitAlive(my)
	while mine(my) do
		if alive() then return true end
		setStatus("Waiting to respawn", T.Sub)
		task.wait(0.3)
	end
	return false
end

-- move to a part using the chosen method. how = 1 teleport, 2 tween, 3 step hop. Returns false if interrupted / died.
local function moveTo(part, my, how)
	how = how or B.method
	local r = getRoot()
	if not (r and part) then return false end
	local target = part.Position + Vector3.new(0, B.lift, 0)
	local startPos = r.Position
	local rot = r.CFrame - r.CFrame.Position
	local dist = (target - startPos).Magnitude
	if how == 1 or dist < 1 then
		r.CFrame = CFrame.new(target) * rot
		r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		return true
	end
	local speed = math.max(B.speed, 1)
	if how == 3 then
		local steps = math.max(1, math.ceil(dist / 40))
		for i = 1, steps do
			r = getRoot()
			if not (mine(my) and r and alive()) then return false end
			r.CFrame = CFrame.new(startPos:Lerp(target, i / steps)) * rot
			r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			task.wait(math.max(40 / speed, 0.03))
		end
		return true
	end
	local dur = math.max(dist / speed, 0.03)
	local ease = EASE[B.ease] or EASE[1]
	local t0 = os.clock()
	while mine(my) do
		r = getRoot()
		if not (r and alive()) then return false end
		local a = math.min((os.clock() - t0) / dur, 1)
		r.CFrame = CFrame.new(startPos:Lerp(target, ease(a))) * rot
		r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		r.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		if a >= 1 then return true end
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end
	return false
end

-- stay on the part for `seconds` (re-pinning each step so physics can't slide you off the trigger)
local function stay(part, seconds, my)
	local untilT = os.clock() + seconds
	local flip = false
	while mine(my) and os.clock() < untilT do
		if not alive() then return false end
		if B.hold then
			flip = not flip
			pin(part, (B.wiggle and flip) and 3 or 0)
		end
		if B.touchAssist then touch(part) end
		task.wait(B.wiggle and 0.25 or 0.1)
	end
	return mine(my) and alive()
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- the farm
-- one lap: every stage, then the chest, then wait for the respawn. Returns true when the lap finished.
local function oneRun(my)
	if not waitAlive(my) then return false end
	local f = stageFolder()
	if not f then setStatus("Stages not found (BoatStages)", T.Bad) return false end
	local lapStart = os.clock()
	local function late()
		if os.clock() - lapStart > B.lapTimeout then
			setStatus("Lap timed out - resetting", T.Warn)
			killChar()
			return true
		end
		return false
	end
	if not B.chestOnly then
		local first, last = math.min(B.startStage, B.endStage), math.max(B.startStage, B.endStage)
		local found = 0
		for i = first, last do
			if not mine(my) or late() then return false end
			local part = stagePart(i)
			if part then
				found = found + 1
				setStatus("Stage " .. i .. " / " .. last, T.Good)
				if not moveTo(part, my) then return false end
				if not stay(part, B.stageWait + math.random() * B.jitter, my) then return false end
			end
		end
		if found == 0 then setStatus("No stage triggers found", T.Bad) return false end
	end
	local chest = chestPart()
	if not chest then setStatus("Golden chest not found", T.Bad) return false end
	setStatus("Opening the chest", T.Warn)
	local startChar = getChar()
	if not moveTo(chest, my) then return false end
	local deadline = os.clock() + B.chestWait
	local flip = false
	while mine(my) and os.clock() < deadline and getChar() == startChar do
		flip = not flip
		pin(chest, (B.wiggle and flip) and 3 or 0)
		if B.touchAssist then touch(chest) end
		task.wait(B.wiggle and 0.25 or 0.2)
	end
	if mine(my) and getChar() == startChar and B.resetIfStuck then
		setStatus("Chest did not respawn you - resetting", T.Warn)
		killChar()
	end
	local tries = 0
	while mine(my) and tries < 60 and not (getChar() ~= startChar and getRoot() and getHum()) do
		task.wait(0.3)
		tries = tries + 1
	end
	task.wait(B.settle) -- let the new character settle at spawn
	return mine(my)
end

local function elapsedTime()
	return B.active + (B.mark and (os.clock() - B.mark) or 0)
end

local function refreshInfo()
	if not runsRow then return end
	runsRow.Set(tostring(B.runs))
	lastRow.Set(B.lastLap and string.format("%.1fs", B.lastLap) or "-")
	bestRow.Set(B.bestLap and string.format("%.1fs", B.bestLap) or "-")
	avgRow.Set(B.runs > 0 and string.format("%.1fs", B.lapTotal / B.runs) or "-")
	local g = findGold()
	goldRow.Set(g and commas(g.Value) or "not found", g and T.Text or T.Sub)
	local elapsed = elapsedTime()
	timeRow.Set(clock(elapsed))
	perHrRow.Set(elapsed > 60 and string.format("%.1f / hr", B.runs / (elapsed / 3600)) or "measuring...")
	if g and B.startGold then
		local gained = g.Value - B.startGold
		gainRow.Set((gained >= 0 and "+" or "") .. commas(gained), gained > 0 and T.Good or T.Sub)
		rateRow.Set(elapsed > 60 and (commas(gained / (elapsed / 3600)) .. " / hr") or "measuring...", T.Text)
	else
		gainRow.Set("-", T.Sub)
		rateRow.Set("-", T.Sub)
	end
end

local function markStop()
	if B.mark then
		B.active = B.active + (os.clock() - B.mark)
		B.mark = nil
	end
end

local function stopFarm()
	B.token = B.token + 1
	B.farming = false
	markStop()
	setStatus("Idle", T.Sub)
	if farmToggle then farmToggle.Set(false) end
end

local function startFarm(limit)
	B.token = B.token + 1
	local my = B.token
	B.farming = true
	if not B.mark then B.mark = os.clock() end
	local g = findGold()
	if g and not B.startGold then B.startGold = g.Value end
	local cap = limit or B.maxRuns
	local began = os.clock()
	local done = 0
	task.spawn(function()
		local why = nil
		while mine(my) do
			if B.maxMinutes > 0 and os.clock() - began >= B.maxMinutes * 60 then
				why = "time limit reached"
				break
			end
			local t0 = os.clock()
			local ok, res = pcall(oneRun, my)
			if not ok then
				warn("[SolosHub] Build A Boat farm error: " .. tostring(res))
				setStatus("Error - retrying", T.Bad)
				task.wait(2)
			elseif res then
				local lap = os.clock() - t0
				B.runs = B.runs + 1
				B.lapTotal = B.lapTotal + lap
				B.lastLap = lap
				if not B.bestLap or lap < B.bestLap then B.bestLap = lap end
				done = done + 1
				refreshInfo()
				if cap and cap > 0 and done >= cap then
					if not limit then why = "run limit reached" end
					break
				end
			else
				task.wait(2) -- missing parts / died: pause before trying again
			end
		end
		if B.token == my then
			B.farming = false
			markStop()
			setStatus(done > 0 and ("Finished " .. done .. " run(s)") or "Idle", T.Sub)
			if farmToggle then farmToggle.Set(false) end
			if why then notify("Build A Boat farm stopped: " .. why, T.Warn) end
		end
	end)
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- presets (they just set the controls below)
local PRESETS = {
	{ method = 1, speed = 400, wait = 25, jitter = 3 }, -- Safe
	{ method = 2, speed = 400, wait = 20, jitter = 3 }, -- Balanced
	{ method = 2, speed = 900, wait = 13, jitter = 2 }, -- Fast
}
local function applyPreset(i)
	local p = PRESETS[i]
	if not p then return end
	methodDd.Set(p.method)
	speedSl.Set(p.speed)
	waitSl.Set(p.wait)
	jitterSl.Set(p.jitter)
	notify("Preset applied", T.Good)
end

end

	new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 36), BackgroundTransparency = 0.25, Text = text, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 13 }, gui)
	table.insert(markers, gui)
end

	clearMarkers()

				new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 36), BackgroundTransparency = 0.25, Text = ch.Name, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 13 }, gui)
				table.insert(zoneMarkers, gui)
			end
		end
	end
	if #zoneMarkers == 0 then notify("No team zones found", T.Warn) end
end, nil, nil, "Labels every part in Workspace whose name ends in Zone")
