-- ---------------------------------------------------- UI: Shop
-- The shop / chest features call the game's own remotes. The names below are from memory and UNVERIFIED: if a call says
-- "remote not found", press "Scan Remotes", look at the copied list, and type the real name into the box.
local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local SH = { buyTok = 0, openTok = 0, amount = 1, interval = 1.0, keep = 0 }
local CHESTS = { "Common Chest", "Uncommon Chest", "Rare Chest", "Epic Chest", "Legendary Chest" }
local KEYS = { "shop", "buy", "bought", "chest", "open", "claim", "item", "gold", "purchase", "crate", "reward" }
local itemDd, itemBox, buyBox, openBox
local shopStatus, remotesRow, shopGoldRow

local function findRemote(name)
	if not name or name == "" then return nil end
	for _, root in ipairs({ Workspace, RS }) do
		local ok, r = pcall(function() return root:FindFirstChild(name, true) end)
		if ok and r and (r:IsA("RemoteEvent") or r:IsA("RemoteFunction")) then return r end
	end
	return nil
end
local function callRemote(r, ...)
	if r:IsA("RemoteFunction") then return pcall(r.InvokeServer, r, ...) end
	return pcall(r.FireServer, r, ...)
end
local function boxText(box, default)
	if box and box.Text ~= "" then return box.Text end
	return default
end
local function chosenItem()
	return boxText(itemBox, CHESTS[itemDd.Get()])
end

local function doBuy()
	local name = boxText(buyBox, "ItemBoughtFromShop")
	local r = findRemote(name)
	if not r then shopStatus.Set("Buy remote not found: " .. name, T.Bad) return false end
	local g = findGold()
	if g and g.Value < SH.keep * 100 then shopStatus.Set("Below the gold reserve", T.Warn) return false end
	local item = chosenItem()
	local ok = callRemote(r, item, SH.amount)
	shopStatus.Set(ok and ("Sent buy: " .. item .. " x" .. SH.amount) or "Buy call errored", ok and T.Good or T.Bad)
	return ok
end
local function doOpen()
	local name = boxText(openBox, "OpenItemRemote")
	local r = findRemote(name)
	if not r then shopStatus.Set("Open remote not found: " .. name, T.Bad) return false end
	local item = chosenItem()
	local ok = callRemote(r, item, SH.amount)
	shopStatus.Set(ok and ("Sent open: " .. item .. " x" .. SH.amount) or "Open call errored", ok and T.Good or T.Bad)
	return ok
end
local function runLoop(key, fn)
	SH[key] = SH[key] + 1
	local my = SH[key]
	task.spawn(function()
		while Live.Running and SH[key] == my do
			pcall(fn)
			task.wait(SH.interval)
		end
	end)
end

local Sp = Section(ShopTab, "Chests", "Buy chests with gold and open them", "loot")
itemDd = Dropdown(Sp, "Chest Type", CHESTS, 1, nil, nil, "Pick one, or type an exact item name below")
itemBox = Input(Sp, "Custom Item Name", "(optional) exact name", "Set", function() end)
Slider(Sp, "Amount Per Call", 1, 50, 1, function(v) SH.amount = v end, "babshopamount")
Button(Sp, "Buy Once", "Buy", doBuy, nil, "Sends one buy request for the chosen item")
Button(Sp, "Open Once", "Open", doOpen, nil, "Sends one open request for the chosen item")
Toggle(Sp, "Auto Buy", false, function(v)
	if v then runLoop("buyTok", doBuy) else SH.buyTok = SH.buyTok + 1 end
end, nil, nil, "Keeps buying on the interval below (spends your gold)")
Toggle(Sp, "Auto Open", false, function(v)
	if v then runLoop("openTok", doOpen) else SH.openTok = SH.openTok + 1 end
end, nil, nil, "Keeps opening on the interval below")
Slider(Sp, "Loop Interval (x0.1s)", 2, 100, 10, function(v) SH.interval = v / 10 end, "babshopinterval")
Slider(Sp, "Keep Gold Reserve (x100)", 0, 1000, 0, function(v) SH.keep = v end, "babshopkeep")
shopStatus = Hub.Stat(Sp, "Shop status", "Idle", T.Sub)
shopGoldRow = Hub.Stat(Sp, "Gold", "-")

local Rm = Section(ShopTab, "Remotes", "If a call says remote not found, set the real names here", "info")
buyBox = Input(Rm, "Buy Remote Name", "ItemBoughtFromShop", "Set", function() end)
openBox = Input(Rm, "Open Remote Name", "OpenItemRemote", "Set", function() end)
remotesRow = Hub.Stat(Rm, "Remotes found", "-")
Button(Rm, "Scan Remotes", "Scan", function()
	local out = {}
	for _, root in ipairs({ Workspace, RS }) do
		for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("RemoteEvent") or d:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
				local low = d.Name:lower()
				for _, k in ipairs(KEYS) do
					if low:find(k, 1, true) then
						table.insert(out, d.ClassName .. ": " .. d:GetFullName())
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	remotesRow.Set(tostring(#out))
	if #out == 0 then notify("No matching remotes found", T.Warn) return end
	copyText(table.concat(out, "\n"))
	notify(#out .. " remotes copied to clipboard", T.Good)
end, nil, "Copies every shop / chest / claim style remote name to your clipboard")

task.spawn(function()
	while Live.Running do
		local g = findGold()
		if shopGoldRow then shopGoldRow.Set(g and commas(g.Value) or "not found", g and T.Text or T.Sub) end
		task.wait(1)
	end
end)

-- ---------------------------------------------------- HALLOWEEN: Trick Or Treat houses (UNTESTED - structure guessed)
-- Looks for models / parts named like "TrickOrTreatHouse", finds the door (a part named *door*, or wherever a
-- ProximityPrompt / ClickDetector lives), stands in front of it on a small platform (so you can't fall through the map),
-- knocks, and leaves. If your health drops while you stand there (a "trick" house) it instantly teleports you to the next
-- house, or out of the way if there is none. Visited houses are revisited after a wait (default 10s).
local HWclean
do
	local HW = {
		houses = {}, last = {}, danger = {}, my = nil, onceSet = {},
		doorWait = 1.0, settle = 0.3, knock = 0.6, stand = 3, hurtPct = 1, escapeOnHurt = true, skipDanger = true,
		revisit = 10, dangerWait = 60, order = 1, endAt = 1, guard = false, once = false, finished = false,
		houses_done = 0, hurts = 0, deaths = 0, rounds = 0, markers = {},
	}
	local hwStatus, hwDone, hwHurt, hwDeaths, hwRounds, hwCandy, hwFound, hwDanger
	local houseDd, autoToggle

	local function nameHas(inst, s) return inst.Name:lower():find(s, 1, true) ~= nil end
	local function running() return HW.my ~= nil and HW.my == B.token and Live.Running end

	-- ---- finding houses
	local function houseInfo(h)
		local prompts, clicks, door = {}, {}, nil
		for _, d in ipairs(h:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then table.insert(prompts, d)
			elseif d:IsA("ClickDetector") then table.insert(clicks, d) end
			if not door and nameHas(d, "door") then
				if d:IsA("BasePart") then door = d
				elseif d:IsA("Model") then door = d:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) end
			end
		end
		local function partFor(inst)
			if not inst then return nil end
			local p = inst.Parent
			if p and p:IsA("Attachment") then p = p.Parent end
			if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then return p end
			return nil
		end
		local target = partFor(prompts[1]) or partFor(clicks[1]) or door
		if not target then
			target = h:IsA("BasePart") and h or h.PrimaryPart or h:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
		end
		return { model = h, target = target, prompts = prompts, clicks = clicks, hasDoor = (door ~= nil) }
	end
	local function scanHouses(deep)
		local found, set = {}, {}
		local function consider(inst)
			if (inst:IsA("Model") or inst:IsA("BasePart")) and nameHas(inst, "trickortreathouse") then
				local p = inst.Parent
				while p and p ~= Workspace do
					if set[p] then return end
					p = p.Parent
				end
				set[inst] = true
				table.insert(found, inst)
			end
		end
		for _, a in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
			consider(a)
			for _, b in ipairs(a:GetChildren()) do consider(b) end
		end
		if #found == 0 and deep then
			local n = 0
			for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				n = n + 1
				if n % 3000 == 0 then task.wait() end
				consider(d)
			end
		end
		HW.houses = {}
		for _, h in ipairs(found) do
			local info = houseInfo(h)
			if info.target then table.insert(HW.houses, info) end
		end
		return #HW.houses
	end

	local function centerOf(info)
		if info.model:IsA("Model") then
			local ok, p = pcall(function() return info.model:GetPivot().Position end)
			if ok then return p end
		end
		return info.target.Position
	end
	local function touchBased(info) return #info.prompts == 0 and #info.clicks == 0 end
	local function standPos(info)
		local tp = info.target.Position
		local c = centerOf(info)
		local dir = Vector3.new(tp.X - c.X, 0, tp.Z - c.Z)
		if dir.Magnitude < 1 then dir = info.target.CFrame.LookVector * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1) end
		if dir.Magnitude < 0.1 then dir = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
		return tp + dir.Unit * (touchBased(info) and 1 or HW.stand)
	end
	-- put the position on the real ground so you never spawn inside / under the floor
	local function landing(info)
		local p = standPos(info)
		local rp = RaycastParams.new()
		rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		rp.IgnoreWater = true
		rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = { getChar() }
		local res = Workspace:Raycast(p + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), rp)
		if res then return Vector3.new(p.X, res.Position.Y + 3.2, p.Z) end
		return p
	end
	local function interact(info)
		local env = API.env
		local r = getRoot()
		for _, p in ipairs(info.prompts) do
			if p.Parent then
				pcall(function()
					p.HoldDuration = 0
					p.RequiresLineOfSight = false
					p.MaxActivationDistance = math.max(p.MaxActivationDistance, 25)
				end)
				if type(env.fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
					pcall(env.fireproximityprompt, p)
				else
					pcall(function() p:InputHoldBegin() task.wait(0.05) p:InputHoldEnd() end)
				end
			end
		end
		for _, c in ipairs(info.clicks) do
			if c.Parent and type(env.fireclickdetector) == "function" then pcall(env.fireclickdetector, c) end
		end
		if r and info.target and type(env.firetouchinterest) == "function" then
			pcall(env.firetouchinterest, r, info.target, 0)
			pcall(env.firetouchinterest, r, info.target, 1)
		end
	end

	local function goSafe()
		local r = getRoot()
		if not r then return end
		if not (safePart and safePart.Parent) then
			safePart = new("Part", { Anchored = true, Size = Vector3.new(30, 2, 30), Position = Vector3.new(r.Position.X, 2500, r.Position.Z), Transparency = 0.5, Color = T.Accent, Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic }, Workspace)
		end
		r.CFrame = safePart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
		r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	end
	local function goEnd()
		if HW.endAt == 1 then
			goSafe()
		elseif HW.endAt == 2 then
			local z = teamZone()
			if z then pin({ Position = z.Position + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0) }) else goSafe() end
		end
	end

	local function candyValue()
		for _, n in ipairs({ "Candy", "Candies", "Candys", "Treats" }) do
			local ok, v = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild(n, true) end)
			if ok and v and v:IsA("ValueBase") then return v end
		end
		return nil
	end
	local function refreshStats()
		if not hwStatus then return end
		hwDone.Set(tostring(HW.houses_done))
		hwHurt.Set(tostring(HW.hurts))
		hwDeaths.Set(tostring(HW.deaths))
		hwRounds.Set(tostring(HW.rounds))
		local c = candyValue()
		hwCandy.Set(c and tostring(c.Value) or "not found", c and T.Text or T.Sub)
		local n = 0
		for _ in pairs(HW.danger) do n = n + 1 end
		hwDanger.Set(tostring(n), n > 0 and T.Warn or T.Text)
	end

	-- ---- picking the next house: new houses first, then old ones whose wait is over
	local function waitFor(info)
		if HW.danger[info.model] and HW.skipDanger then return HW.dangerWait end
		return HW.revisit
	end
	local function remaining(info)
		local last = HW.last[info.model]
		if not last then return 0 end
		return waitFor(info) - (os.clock() - last)
	end
	local function nextHouses(exclude)
		local list = {}
		for _, info in ipairs(HW.houses) do
			if info.model.Parent and info.target.Parent and not (exclude and info.model == exclude.model)
				and not (HW.once and HW.onceSet[info.model]) and remaining(info) <= 0 then
				table.insert(list, info)
			end
		end
		local r = getRoot()
		local here = r and r.Position or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		table.sort(list, function(a, b)
			local av, bv = HW.last[a.model] ~= nil, HW.last[b.model] ~= nil
			if av ~= bv then return not av end -- never-visited houses first
			if HW.order == 1 then return (a.target.Position - here).Magnitude < (b.target.Position - here).Magnitude end
			if HW.order == 2 then return a.target.Position.X < b.target.Position.X end
			return a.target.Position.Z < b.target.Position.Z
		end)
		return list
	end
	local function soonest()
		local best
		for _, info in ipairs(HW.houses) do
			if info.model.Parent and not (HW.once and HW.onceSet[info.model]) then
				local rem = remaining(info)
				if not best or rem < best then best = rem end
			end
		end
		return best
	end

	-- stand at the door on a platform, knock, watch your health. Returns "ok", "hurt", "died" or "stopped"
	local function visit(info, my)
		local fresh = houseInfo(info.model) -- prompts can appear later, so look again right before knocking
		if fresh.target then info = fresh end
		local pos = landing(info)
		local plat = new("Part", { Anchored = true, Size = Vector3.new(8, 1, 8), Transparency = 1, CanCollide = true, CFrame = CFrame.new(pos.X, pos.Y - 3.6, pos.Z) }, Workspace)
		local function finish(res) plat:Destroy() return res end
		if not moveTo({ Position = pos }, my) then
			if not mine(my) then return finish("stopped") end
			return finish("died")
		end
		local hum = getHum()
		local hp0 = hum and hum.Health or 100
		local t0 = os.clock()
		local knockAt = t0 + HW.settle -- let the teleport settle / replicate before knocking (more on laggy servers)
		local last = nil
		local untilT = t0 + HW.settle + HW.doorWait
		while mine(my) and os.clock() < untilT do
			hum = getHum()
			local r = getRoot()
			if not (hum and hum.Health > 0 and r) then return finish("died") end
			local lost = hp0 - hum.Health
			if HW.escapeOnHurt and lost >= math.max(hum.MaxHealth, 1) * HW.hurtPct / 100 then return finish("hurt") end
			if r.Position.Y < pos.Y - 8 or (r.Position - pos).Magnitude > 12 then pin({ Position = pos }) end -- fell / got pushed away
			pin({ Position = pos })
			if os.clock() >= knockAt and (not last or os.clock() - last >= HW.knock) then
				interact(info)
				last = os.clock()
			end
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
		if not mine(my) then return finish("stopped") end
		return finish("ok")
	end

	-- hurt / trick house: jump (instantly) to the next house, or out of the way if there is none
	local function escape(my, from)
		local nxt = nextHouses(from)[1]
		if nxt then
			moveTo({ Position = landing(nxt) }, my, 1)
		else
			goSafe()
		end
	end

	local function handleResult(res, info, my)
		HW.last[info.model] = os.clock()
		HW.onceSet[info.model] = true
		if res == "ok" then
			HW.houses_done = HW.houses_done + 1
		elseif res == "hurt" then
			HW.hurts = HW.hurts + 1
			HW.danger[info.model] = true
			setStatus("Trick house - escaping", T.Warn)
			escape(my, info)
		elseif res == "died" then
			HW.deaths = HW.deaths + 1
			HW.danger[info.model] = true
			setStatus("Died - respawning", T.Bad)
			waitAlive(my)
		end
		refreshStats()
	end

	-- keeps running until YOU turn it off. Every spawned house gets visited; when none is ready it parks you out of the way,
	-- keeps scanning, and goes back to a house once its wait is over (default 10s) or as soon as a brand new one appears.
	-- ("Run One Round" is the only mode that finishes on its own.)
	local function runRounds(my)
		local parked, lastDeep = false, 0
		HW.onceSet = {}
		while mine(my) do
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				local deep = os.clock() - lastDeep > 10
				if deep then lastDeep = os.clock() end
				local n = scanHouses(deep)
				hwFound.Set(tostring(n))
				local info = nextHouses()[1]
				if info then
					parked = false
					setStatus("Knocking (" .. #nextHouses() .. " ready)", T.Good)
					local res = visit(info, my)
					if res == "stopped" then return end
					handleResult(res, info, my)
					return
				end
				if HW.once then
					HW.finished = true
					return
				end
				if not parked then
					parked = true
					HW.rounds = HW.rounds + 1
					setStatus("No house ready - moving out of the way", T.Sub)
					goEnd()
					refreshStats()
				end
				local s = soonest()
				if n == 0 then
					setStatus("Waiting for a house to appear", T.Sub)
				elseif s then
					setStatus("Next house in " .. math.max(math.ceil(s), 0) .. "s", T.Sub)
				else
					setStatus("Waiting for the next house", T.Sub)
				end
				task.wait(0.5)
			end)
			if not ok then
				warn("[SolosHub] Halloween step error: " .. tostring(err))
				task.wait(1)
			end
			if HW.finished then
				HW.finished = false
				goEnd()
				HW.rounds = HW.rounds + 1
				refreshStats()
				setStatus("Round done", T.Sub)
				return
			end
		end
	end

	local function startJob(fn)
		stopFarm()
		B.token = B.token + 1
		local my = B.token
		HW.my = my
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, err = pcall(fn, my)
			if not ok then
				warn("[SolosHub] Halloween error: " .. tostring(err))
				setStatus("Error", T.Bad)
			end
			if B.token == my then
				HW.my = nil
				setStatus("Idle", T.Sub)
				if autoToggle then autoToggle.Set(false) end
			end
		end)
	end

	-- ---- UI
	local Hw = Section(HwTab, "Trick Or Treat", "Knocks on every house for candy and leaves if a house hurts you", "night")
	autoToggle = Toggle(Hw, "Auto Trick Or Treat", false, function(v)
		if v then
			HW.once = false
			startJob(function(my) runRounds(my) end)
		else
			stopFarm()
		end
	end, nil, nil, "Runs until you turn it off: visits every house, waits out of the way, returns when one is ready")
	Button(Hw, "Run One Round", "Run", function()
		HW.once = true
		startJob(function(my) runRounds(my) end)
		autoToggle.Set(true)
	end, nil, "Visit every house once, then leave (the only mode that stops by itself)")
	Button(Hw, "Stop", "Stop", function() stopFarm() autoToggle.Set(false) end)
	Button(Hw, "Escape Now (out of the way)", "Go", function() stopFarm() autoToggle.Set(false) goSafe() end, nil, "Instantly teleports you to the safe spot platform")

	local Hs = Section(HwTab, "Session", "Numbers for this event run", "info")
	hwStatus = statusRow
	hwFound = Hub.Stat(Hs, "Houses found", "-")
	hwDone = Hub.Stat(Hs, "Houses visited", "0")
	hwHurt = Hub.Stat(Hs, "Times hurt (escaped)", "0")
	hwDeaths = Hub.Stat(Hs, "Deaths", "0")
	hwDanger = Hub.Stat(Hs, "Dangerous houses remembered", "0")
	hwRounds = Hub.Stat(Hs, "Times parked (no house ready)", "0")
	hwCandy = Hub.Stat(Hs, "Candy", "-")
	Button(Hs, "Clear Danger List", "Clear", function() HW.danger = {} refreshStats() end, nil, "Forget which houses hurt you")
	Button(Hs, "Reset Counters", "Reset", function()
		HW.houses_done, HW.hurts, HW.deaths, HW.rounds = 0, 0, 0, 0
		refreshStats()
	end)

	local Hh = Section(HwTab, "Houses", "Find the houses and try one by hand", "teleports")
	houseDd = Dropdown(Hh, "House", { "Press Scan Houses" }, 1)
	local function rebuildList()
		local names = {}
		local r = getRoot()
		for i, info in ipairs(HW.houses) do
			local d = r and math.floor((info.target.Position - r.Position).Magnitude) or 0
			local flags = (#info.prompts > 0 and "prompt") or (#info.clicks > 0 and "click") or (info.hasDoor and "door only" or "no door")
			names[i] = "House " .. i .. "  -  " .. d .. "m  (" .. flags .. ")" .. (HW.danger[info.model] and "  [hurts]" or "")
		end
		if #names == 0 then names = { "No houses found" } end
		houseDd.SetOptions(names)
	end
	Button(Hh, "Scan Houses", "Scan", function()
		local n = scanHouses(true)
		hwFound.Set(tostring(n))
		rebuildList()
		notify(n > 0 and (n .. " house(s) found") or "No TrickOrTreatHouse found", n > 0 and T.Good or T.Warn)
	end, nil, "Looks for anything named TrickOrTreatHouse")
	Button(Hh, "Teleport To Selected House", "Go", function()
		local info = HW.houses[houseDd.Get()]
		if not info then notify("Scan houses first", T.Warn) return end
		pin({ Position = landing(info) })
	end)
	Button(Hh, "Knock Selected (with escape)", "Knock", function()
		local info = HW.houses[houseDd.Get()]
		if not info then notify("Scan houses first", T.Warn) return end
		startJob(function(my)
			setStatus("Knocking", T.Good)
			handleResult(visit(info, my), info, my)
		end)
	end, nil, "Goes to the door, knocks once, escapes if it hurts")
	Toggle(Hh, "House Markers", false, function(v)
		for _, g in ipairs(HW.markers) do g:Destroy() end
		table.clear(HW.markers)
		if not v then return end
		if #HW.houses == 0 then scanHouses(true) end
		for i, info in ipairs(HW.houses) do
			local gui = new("BillboardGui", { Adornee = info.target, AlwaysOnTop = true, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(110, 24), StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 6, 0), ResetOnSpawn = false }, EspGui)
			new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 36), BackgroundTransparency = 0.25, Text = "House " .. i, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 13 }, gui)
			table.insert(HW.markers, gui)
		end
	end, nil, nil, "Labels every door")

	-- ---- guard for knocking by hand: if you get hurt next to a house, jump to the next one / out of the way
	local Hg = Section(HwTab, "Danger Guard", "Protects you even when you knock by hand", "godmode")
	local lastHp
	Toggle(Hg, "Danger Guard (manual knocking)", false, function(v) HW.guard = v lastHp = nil end, nil, nil, "Teleports you away the moment a house hurts you")
	bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
		if not HW.guard or running() then return end
		local hum, r = getHum(), getRoot()
		if not (hum and r and hum.Health > 0) then lastHp = nil return end
		local lost = lastHp and (lastHp - hum.Health) or 0
		lastHp = hum.Health
		if lost >= math.max(hum.MaxHealth, 1) * HW.hurtPct / 100 then
			local near, nd
			for _, info in ipairs(HW.houses) do
				if info.target.Parent then
					local d = (info.target.Position - r.Position).Magnitude
					if d < 60 and (not nd or d < nd) then near, nd = info, d end
				end
			end
			if near then
				HW.danger[near.model] = true
				HW.last[near.model] = os.clock()
				HW.hurts = HW.hurts + 1
				local nxt = nextHouses(near)[1]
				if nxt then pin({ Position = landing(nxt) }) else goSafe() end
				notify("A house hurt you - moved away", T.Warn)
				refreshStats()
			end
		end
	end)

	-- ---- settings
	local Ho = Section(HwTab, "Settings", "How the event farm behaves", "settings")
	local doorSl, settleSl, knockSl, revisitSl
	local PRESETS = {
		{ 8, 1, 4, 8 },    -- fast server
		{ 10, 3, 6, 10 },  -- normal
		{ 20, 8, 10, 15 }, -- laggy
		{ 30, 15, 12, 20 }, -- very laggy
	}
	Dropdown(Ho, "Lag Preset", { "Fast server", "Normal", "Laggy server", "Very laggy server" }, 2, function(i)
		local p = PRESETS[i]
		if not p then return end
		doorSl.Set(p[1])
		settleSl.Set(p[2])
		knockSl.Set(p[3])
		revisitSl.Set(p[4])
		notify("Timing preset applied - fine tune with the sliders below", T.Good)
	end, nil, "Sets the four waits below in one go")
	doorSl = Slider(Ho, "Time At Each Door (x0.1s)", 2, 100, 10, function(v) HW.doorWait = v / 10 end, "bhwdoorwait")
	settleSl = Slider(Ho, "Settle Time Before Knocking (x0.1s)", 0, 50, 3, function(v) HW.settle = v / 10 end, "bhwsettle")
	knockSl = Slider(Ho, "Time Between Knocks (x0.1s)", 2, 50, 6, function(v) HW.knock = v / 10 end, "bhwknock")
	revisitSl = Slider(Ho, "Wait Before Returning To A House (s)", 1, 300, 10, function(v) HW.revisit = v end, "bhwrevisit")
	Input(Ho, "Exact Return Wait (seconds)", "e.g. 7.5", "Set", function(text)
		local n = tonumber(text)
		if not n or n < 0 then notify("Type a number of seconds", T.Warn) return end
		HW.revisit = n
		revisitSl.SetSilent(math.clamp(math.floor(n + 0.5), 1, 300))
		notify("Return wait set to " .. n .. "s", T.Good)
	end)
	Slider(Ho, "Stand Distance From Door (studs)", 0, 10, 3, function(v) HW.stand = v end, "bhwstand")
	Slider(Ho, "Escape When Health Drops By (% of max)", 1, 50, 1, function(v) HW.hurtPct = v end, "bhwhurt")
	Toggle(Ho, "Escape When Hurt", true, function(v) HW.escapeOnHurt = v end, nil, "bhwescape", "Off = stay at the door no matter what (not recommended)")
	Toggle(Ho, "Wait Longer On Houses That Hurt Me", true, function(v) HW.skipDanger = v end, nil, "bhwskip", "Houses that hurt or killed you are retried after the longer wait below")
	Slider(Ho, "Wait After A House Hurt Me (s)", 5, 900, 60, function(v) HW.dangerWait = v end, "bhwdangerwait")
	Dropdown(Ho, "House Order", { "Nearest first", "West to east (X)", "North to south (Z)" }, 1, function(i) HW.order = i end)
	Dropdown(Ho, "When No House Is Ready", { "Safe spot (high platform)", "My team zone", "Stay where I am" }, 1, function(i) HW.endAt = i end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while Live.Running do
			pcall(refreshStats)
			task.wait(2)
		end
	end)
	HWclean = function()
		for _, g in ipairs(HW.markers) do g:Destroy() end
		HW.my = nil
	end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------- LEVERS / BUTTONS / SWITCHES: all on / all off + ESP (UNTESTED - guessed)
-- Finds anything named like "lever", "button" or "switch" in the Workspace, reads its on/off state from a BoolValue /
-- attribute (names like On, State, Active, Toggled ...), and only clicks the ones that are NOT already in the state you want.
-- Ones whose state can't be read are counted as "unknown" and skipped unless you allow clicking them.
local LVclean
do
	local LV = {
		scope = 1, radius = 150, levers = true, buttons = true, switches = true,
		clickUnknown = false, walk = false, delay = 0.2,
		espOn = false, espRange = 300, espDist = true, espItems = {}, cands = {}, scanned = 0,
	}
	local KINDS = { { "lever", "Lever" }, { "button", "Button" }, { "switch", "Switch" } }
	local STATE_NAMES = { on = true, state = true, active = true, activated = true, toggled = true, enabled = true, ison = true, powered = true, pressed = true, status = true }
	local lvRow

	local function posOf(inst)
		if inst:IsA("Model") then
			local ok, p = pcall(function() return inst:GetPivot().Position end)
			if ok then return p end
		end
		return inst.Position
	end
	local function partOfInst(inst)
		if inst:IsA("BasePart") then return inst end
		return inst.PrimaryPart or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
	end
	local function kindOf(inst)
		local n = inst.Name:lower()
		for _, k in ipairs(KINDS) do
			if n:find(k[1], 1, true) then return k[1], k[2] end
		end
		return nil
	end
	local function kindEnabled(k)
		return (k == "lever" and LV.levers) or (k == "button" and LV.buttons) or (k == "switch" and LV.switches)
	end

	-- every lever-like thing in the map (yields now and then so big boats don't freeze you)
	local function scan()
		local out, set, n = {}, {}, 0
		local ch = getChar()
		for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			n = n + 1
			if n % 3000 == 0 then task.wait() end
			if (d:IsA("Model") or d:IsA("BasePart")) and not (ch and d:IsDescendantOf(ch)) then
				local k, label = kindOf(d)
				if k then
					local p, inside = d.Parent, false
					while p and p ~= Workspace do
						if set[p] then inside = true break end
						p = p.Parent
					end
					if not inside then
						set[d] = true
						table.insert(out, { inst = d, kind = k, label = label })
					end
				end
			end
		end
		LV.cands = out
		LV.scanned = os.clock()
		return out
	end

	local function readState(inst)
		for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BoolValue") and STATE_NAMES[d.Name:lower()] then return d.Value end
		end
		if inst:IsA("BoolValue") and STATE_NAMES[inst.Name:lower()] then return inst.Value end
		for k, v in pairs(inst:GetAttributes()) do
			if type(v) == "boolean" and STATE_NAMES[k:lower()] then return v end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function inScope(c)
		if not c.inst.Parent then return false end
		if LV.scope == 3 then return true end
		if LV.scope == 2 then
			local team = LocalPlayer.Team
			if not team then return true end
			local tn = team.Name:lower()
			local p = c.inst.Parent
			while p and p ~= Workspace do
				if p.Name:lower():find(tn, 1, true) then return true end
				p = p.Parent
			end
			return false
		end
		local r = getRoot()
		return r ~= nil and (posOf(c.inst) - r.Position).Magnitude <= LV.radius
	end

	-- press / click / touch it using whatever the block offers
	local function activate(inst)
		local env = API.env
		local did = false
		for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("ClickDetector") and type(env.fireclickdetector) == "function" then
				pcall(function() d.MaxActivationDistance = 100000 end)
				pcall(env.fireclickdetector, d)
				did = true
			elseif d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
				pcall(function() d.HoldDuration = 0 d.RequiresLineOfSight = false d.MaxActivationDistance = 100000 end)
				if type(env.fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
					pcall(env.fireproximityprompt, d)
				else
					pcall(function() d:InputHoldBegin() task.wait(0.05) d:InputHoldEnd() end)
				end
				did = true
			end
		end
		if not did then
			for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("RemoteEvent") or d:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
					local n = d.Name:lower()
					if n:find("click") or n:find("toggle") or n:find("press") or n:find("use") or n:find("activ") or n:find("interact") then
						if d:IsA("RemoteEvent") then pcall(d.FireServer, d) else task.spawn(function() pcall(d.InvokeServer, d) end) end
						did = true
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if not did then
			local part, r = partOfInst(inst), getRoot()
			if part and r and type(env.firetouchinterest) == "function" then
				pcall(env.firetouchinterest, r, part, 0)
				pcall(env.firetouchinterest, r, part, 1)
				did = true
			end
		end
		return did
	end

	local function startApply(want)
		stopFarm()
		B.token = B.token + 1
		local my = B.token
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				if lvRow then lvRow.Text = "Scanning..." end
				scan()
				local list = {}
				for _, c in ipairs(LV.cands) do
					if kindEnabled(c.kind) and inScope(c) then table.insert(list, c) end
				end
				local r0 = getRoot()
				local here = r0 and r0.Position or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				table.sort(list, function(a, b) return (posOf(a.inst) - here).Magnitude < (posOf(b.inst) - here).Magnitude end)
				if #list == 0 then
					if lvRow then lvRow.Text = "Nothing found in range - raise the radius or change the scope" end
					return
				end
				local home = r0 and r0.CFrame
				local changed, skipped, unknown, failed = 0, 0, 0, 0
				for i, c in ipairs(list) do
					if not mine(my) then break end
					if c.inst.Parent then
						local st = readState(c.inst)
						local act = false
						if st == nil then
							unknown = unknown + 1
							act = LV.clickUnknown
						elseif st == want then
							skipped = skipped + 1 -- already how it needs to be
						else
							act = true
						end
						if act then
							if LV.walk then moveTo({ Position = posOf(c.inst) + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0) }, my, 1) task.wait(0.15) end
							local did = activate(c.inst)
							if not did then
								failed = failed + 1
							elseif st ~= nil then
								local t = os.clock()
								local now = st
								while os.clock() - t < 0.5 do
									now = readState(c.inst)
									if now == want then break end
									task.wait(0.05)
								end
								if now == want then changed = changed + 1 else failed = failed + 1 end
							else
								changed = changed + 1
							end
							task.wait(LV.delay)
						end
						if lvRow then lvRow.Text = (want and "Turning ON" or "Turning OFF") .. "  " .. i .. " / " .. #list end
					end
				end
				if LV.walk and home then
					local rr = getRoot()
					if rr then rr.CFrame = home end
				end
				if lvRow then
					lvRow.Text = string.format("Done: %d changed, %d already right (skipped), %d unknown%s, %d failed", changed, skipped, unknown, LV.clickUnknown and " (clicked)" or " (skipped)", failed)
				end
			end)
			if not ok then
				warn("[SolosHub] lever error: " .. tostring(err))
				if lvRow then lvRow.Text = "Error: " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 60) end
			end
		end)
	end

	-- ---- UI
	local Lv = Section(ToolsTab, "Levers, Buttons And Switches", "Flip everything on or off in one click - ones already how you want are skipped", "active")
	local scopeDd = Dropdown(Lv, "Which Ones", { "Near me (radius)", "My team's blocks (guess)", "Everything in the map" }, 1, function(i) LV.scope = i end)
	Slider(Lv, "Radius (studs)", 10, 1000, 150, function(v) LV.radius = v end, "blvradius")
	Toggle(Lv, "Levers", true, function(v) LV.levers = v end, nil, "blvlevers")
	Toggle(Lv, "Buttons", true, function(v) LV.buttons = v end, nil, "blvbuttons")
	Toggle(Lv, "Switches", true, function(v) LV.switches = v end, nil, "blvswitches")
	Button(Lv, "Turn Everything ON", "ON", function() startApply(true) end, nil, "Only clicks the ones that are currently off")
	Button(Lv, "Turn Everything OFF", "OFF", function() startApply(false) end, nil, "Only clicks the ones that are currently on")
	Button(Lv, "Stop", "Stop", function() stopFarm() if lvRow then lvRow.Text = "Stopped" end end)
	lvRow = InfoRow(Lv, "Ready")
	local LvOpt = Collapse(Lv, "Options")
	Toggle(LvOpt, "Click Ones With Unknown State", false, function(v) LV.clickUnknown = v end, nil, "blvunknown", "If a block's on/off state can't be read it is normally skipped. On = click it anyway (may flip it the wrong way)")
	Toggle(LvOpt, "Walk To Each One", false, function(v) LV.walk = v end, nil, "blvwalk", "Teleports next to each one first, then back to where you were")
	Slider(LvOpt, "Delay Between Clicks (x0.1s)", 0, 30, 2, function(v) LV.delay = v / 10 end, "blvdelay")
	Button(Lv, "Scan And Count", "Scan", function()
		task.spawn(function()
			lvRow.Text = "Scanning..."
			scan()
			local counts, inRange = { lever = 0, button = 0, switch = 0 }, 0
			local on, off, unk = 0, 0, 0
			for _, c in ipairs(LV.cands) do
				counts[c.kind] = counts[c.kind] + 1
				if inScope(c) and kindEnabled(c.kind) then
					inRange = inRange + 1
					local s = readState(c.inst)
					if s == nil then unk = unk + 1 elseif s then on = on + 1 else off = off + 1 end
				end
			end
			lvRow.Text = string.format("Map: %d levers, %d buttons, %d switches. In range: %d (%d on, %d off, %d unknown)", counts.lever, counts.button, counts.switch, inRange, on, off, unk)
		end)
	end, nil, "Counts them and reads their state without clicking anything")
	Button(Lv, "Copy Nearest One's Structure", "Copy", function()
		task.spawn(function()
			scan()
			local r = getRoot()
			if not r then return end
			local best, bd
			for _, c in ipairs(LV.cands) do
				local d = (posOf(c.inst) - r.Position).Magnitude
				if not bd or d < bd then best, bd = c, d end
			end
			if not best then notify("No lever / button / switch found", T.Warn) return end
			local out = { best.inst:GetFullName() .. "   (" .. math.floor(bd) .. " studs away)" }
			local function dump(inst, depth)
				if #out > 120 then return end
				local line = string.rep("  ", depth) .. inst.ClassName .. " " .. inst.Name
				if inst:IsA("ValueBase") then line = line .. " = " .. tostring(inst.Value) end
				for k, v in pairs(inst:GetAttributes()) do line = line .. "  [" .. k .. "=" .. tostring(v) .. "]" end
				table.insert(out, line)
				for _, c in ipairs(inst:GetChildren()) do dump(c, depth + 1) end
			end
			dump(best.inst, 0)
			copyText(table.concat(out, "\n"))
			notify("Copied the nearest one's structure - paste it to me so I can fix the detection", T.Good)
		end)
	end, nil, "Stand next to a lever and press this, then send me what it copied")

	-- ---- ESP
	local Le = Section(ToolsTab, "Lever ESP", "Labels every lever, button and switch - green = on, red = off, yellow = unknown", "esp")
	local function clearEsp()
		for inst, e in pairs(LV.espItems) do
			if e.hl then e.hl:Destroy() end
			if e.gui then e.gui:Destroy() end
			LV.espItems[inst] = nil
		end
	end
	Toggle(Le, "Lever / Button / Switch ESP", false, function(v)
		LV.espOn = v
		if not v then clearEsp() end
	end, nil, nil, "Outline + label on every one of them")
	Slider(Le, "ESP Range (studs, 0 = unlimited)", 0, 2000, 300, function(v) LV.espRange = v end, "blvesprange")
	Toggle(Le, "Show Distance", true, function(v) LV.espDist = v end, nil, "blvespdist")
	task.spawn(function()
		while Live.Running do
			if LV.espOn then
				pcall(function()
					if os.clock() - LV.scanned > 5 then scan() end
					local r = getRoot()
					local keep, shown = {}, 0
					local sorted = {}
					for _, c in ipairs(LV.cands) do
						if c.inst.Parent and kindEnabled(c.kind) then
							local d = r and (posOf(c.inst) - r.Position).Magnitude or 0
							if LV.espRange == 0 or d <= LV.espRange then table.insert(sorted, { c = c, d = d }) end
						end
					end
					table.sort(sorted, function(a, b) return a.d < b.d end)
					for _, e in ipairs(sorted) do
						if shown >= 120 then break end
						shown = shown + 1
						local c = e.c
						keep[c.inst] = true
						local st = readState(c.inst)
						local col = (st == nil and T.Warn) or (st and T.Good or T.Bad)
						local item = LV.espItems[c.inst]
						if not item then
							local part = partOfInst(c.inst)
							if part then
								item = {}
								item.hl = new("Highlight", { Adornee = c.inst, FillTransparency = 0.6, OutlineColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }, EspGui)
								item.gui = new("BillboardGui", { Adornee = part, AlwaysOnTop = true, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(150, 22), StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), ResetOnSpawn = false }, EspGui)
								item.lbl = new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 36), BackgroundTransparency = 0.25, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 12 }, item.gui)
								LV.espItems[c.inst] = item
							end
						end
						if item then
							item.hl.FillColor = col
							item.lbl.TextColor3 = col
							item.lbl.Text = c.label .. (st == nil and "" or (st and "  [ON]" or "  [OFF]")) .. (LV.espDist and ("  " .. math.floor(e.d) .. "m") or "")
						end
					end
					for inst, item in pairs(LV.espItems) do
						if not keep[inst] then
							if item.hl then item.hl:Destroy() end
							if item.gui then item.gui:Destroy() end
							LV.espItems[inst] = nil
						end
					end
				end)
			end
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)
	LVclean = function() clearEsp() end
end

-- BABFT BUILD LIBRARY BEGIN
-- Local data only: saved JSON is never evaluated as Lua.
do
	local Http = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local dir = Hub.dir .. "/Babft Builds"
	local MAX_PARTS, MAX_BYTES = 3000, 8 * 1024 * 1024
	local function finite(n, limit)
		return type(n) == "number" and n == n and math.abs(n) <= limit
	end
	local function vector(v) return { v.X, v.Y, v.Z } end
	local function frame(cf) return { cf:GetComponents() } end
	local function array(t, count, limit)
		if type(t) ~= "table" or #t ~= count then return false end
		for i = 1, count do if not finite(t[i], limit) then return false end end
		return true
	end
	local function validFrame(t)
		if not array(t, 12, 100000) then return false end
		-- Reject malformed rotations instead of drawing distorted/out-of-bounds previews.
		for row = 0, 2 do
			local sum = 0
			for col = 1, 3 do sum = sum + t[3 + row * 3 + col]^2 end
			if math.abs(sum - 1) > 0.001 then return false end
		end
		for a = 0, 1 do
			for b = a + 1, 2 do
				local dot = 0
				for col = 1, 3 do dot = dot + t[3 + a * 3 + col] * t[3 + b * 3 + col] end
				if math.abs(dot) > 0.001 then return false end
			end
		end
		local det = t[4]*(t[8]*t[12]-t[9]*t[11]) - t[5]*(t[7]*t[12]-t[9]*t[10]) + t[6]*(t[7]*t[11]-t[8]*t[10])
		return math.abs(det - 1) <= 0.001
	end
	local function safeName(name)
		if type(name) ~= "string" then return nil end
		name = name:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if #name < 1 or #name > 60 or name:find("[^%w _%-]") then return nil end
		local reserved = name:upper():match("^([^ .]+)")
		if reserved == "CON" or reserved == "PRN" or reserved == "AUX" or reserved == "NUL" or reserved:match("^COM%d$") or reserved:match("^LPT%d$") then return nil end
		return name
	end
	local classes = { Part = true, WedgePart = true, CornerWedgePart = true }
	local shapes = { Block = true, Ball = true, Cylinder = true }
	local function validate(data)
		if type(data) ~= "table" or data.format ~= "SolosHub.Babft" or data.version ~= 1 then return false, "Unsupported build file" end
		if type(data.blocks) ~= "table" or #data.blocks == 0 or #data.blocks > MAX_PARTS then return false, "Invalid block count" end
		local count = 0
		for _, block in ipairs(data.blocks) do
			if type(block) ~= "table" or type(block.kind) ~= "string" or #block.kind < 1 or #block.kind > 100 or not validFrame(block.cf) then return false, "Invalid block record" end
			if type(block.parts) ~= "table" or #block.parts == 0 then return false, "Block has no geometry" end
			for _, p in ipairs(block.parts) do
				count = count + 1
				if count > MAX_PARTS then return false, "Build exceeds 3000 preview parts" end
				if type(p) ~= "table" or not classes[p.class] or not shapes[p.shape] or not validFrame(p.cf) or not array(p.size, 3, 2048) or not array(p.color, 3, 1) then return false, "Unsupported part geometry" end
				for i = 1, 3 do if p.size[i] <= 0 or p.color[i] < 0 then return false, "Invalid size/color" end end
				if type(p.material) ~= "string" or not Enum.Material[p.material] or not finite(p.alpha, 1) or p.alpha < 0 then return false, "Invalid appearance" end
			end
		end
		return true, count
	end
	local function plotFrame(zone)
		return zone.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, zone.Size.Y / 2, 0)
	end
	local function placement(zone, x, y, z, angle)
		return plotFrame(zone) * CFrame.new(x, y, z) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(angle), 0)
	end
	local function partFrame(origin, block, part)
		return origin * CFrame.new(table.unpack(block.cf)) * CFrame.new(table.unpack(part.cf))
	end
	local function onPlot(zone, cf, size)
		-- All eight corners: rotated blocks near plot edges must fit too.
		for _, x in ipairs({-1, 1}) do
			for _, y in ipairs({-1, 1}) do
				for _, z in ipairs({-1, 1}) do
					local point = zone.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(cf:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(x * size.X / 2, y * size.Y / 2, z * size.Z / 2)))
					if math.abs(point.X) > zone.Size.X / 2 + 0.01 or math.abs(point.Z) > zone.Size.Z / 2 + 0.01 or point.Y < zone.Size.Y / 2 - 0.01 then return false end
				end
			end
		end
		return true
	end
	-- BABFT BUILD CORE END
	local state = { data = nil, preview = nil, ghosts = {}, x = 0, y = 0, z = 0, angle = 0, shown = false, files = {}, players = {}, busy = false }
	local page = Panel.Sub("Build Library", "loot", "Save builds locally and preview their placement on your plot.")
	local library = Section(page, "Babft Builds", "Local files in SolosHub / Babft Builds", "loot")
	local status = InfoRow(library, "Choose a player to capture, or load a saved build")
	status.TextWrapped = true
	status.Parent.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 64)
	local function say(text, bad) status.Text = text; status.TextColor3 = bad and T.Warn or T.Text end
	local function guarded(fn)
		return function(...)
			if state.busy then say("Stop the current build before changing it", true); return end
			local ok, err = pcall(fn, ...)
			if not ok then say(tostring(err):gsub("^.-:%d+: ", ""), true) end
		end
	end
	local function storage()
		if not (type(makefolder) == "function" and type(isfolder) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function" and type(writefile) == "function" and type(isfile) == "function" and type(listfiles) == "function") then error("This executor does not support local build files") end
		if not isfolder(Hub.dir) then makefolder(Hub.dir) end
		if not isfolder(dir) then makefolder(dir) end
	end
	local function zoneFor(team)
		if not team then return nil end
		local key = team.Name:lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
		for _, object in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if object.Name:lower():gsub("[^%w]", "") == key .. "zone" then
				if object:IsA("BasePart") then return object end
				return object:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
			end
		end
	end
	local function clearPreview()
		if state.preview then state.preview:Destroy() end
		state.preview, state.ghosts, state.shown = nil, {}, false
	end
	local positionRow, previewToggle
	local function updatePreview()
		if not state.preview then return end
		local zone = zoneFor(LocalPlayer.Team)
		if not zone then clearPreview(); if previewToggle then previewToggle.Set(false) end; say("Your team plot is not available", true); return end
		local origin = placement(zone, state.x, state.y, state.z, state.angle)
		local outside = 0
		for _, ghost in ipairs(state.ghosts) do
			local cf = partFrame(origin, ghost.block, ghost.part)
			ghost.object.CFrame = cf
			local valid = onPlot(zone, cf, ghost.object.Size)
			if not valid then outside = outside + 1 end
			ghost.object.Color = valid and Color3.new(table.unpack(ghost.part.color)) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 75, 90)
		end
		state.origin, state.zone, state.zoneCF, state.inside = origin, zone, zone.CFrame, outside == 0
		if positionRow then positionRow.Text = string.format("X %.1f   Y %.1f   Z %.1f   Turn %.0f°  |  %s", state.x, state.y, state.z, state.angle, outside == 0 and "Inside your plot" or (outside .. " parts outside plot / below ground")) end
	end
	local function showPreview()
		if not state.data then error("Capture or load a build first") end
		if not zoneFor(LocalPlayer.Team) then error("Join a team with a plot first") end
		clearPreview()
		local folder = Instance.new("Folder"); folder.Name = "SolosBabftPreview"; folder.Parent = Workspace
		state.preview, state.shown = folder, true
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			for _, block in ipairs(state.data.blocks) do
				for _, p in ipairs(block.parts) do
					local object = Instance.new(p.class)
					object.Name = "Preview"; object.Anchored = true
					object.CanCollide, object.CanTouch, object.CanQuery = false, false, false
					object.CastShadow = false; object.Size = Vector3.new(table.unpack(p.size))
					if p.class == "Part" then object.Shape = Enum.PartType[p.shape] end
					object.Material = Enum.Material[p.material]
					object.Transparency = math.max(p.alpha, 0.45)
					object.Parent = folder
					table.insert(state.ghosts, {object = object, block = block, part = p})
				end
			end
			updatePreview()
		end)
		if not ok then clearPreview(); error(err) end
		if previewToggle then previewToggle.Set(true) end
	end
	local syncControls
	local function install(data)
		local ok, why = validate(data)
		if not ok then error(why) end
		clearPreview(); state.data = data
		state.x, state.y, state.z, state.angle = 0, 0, 0, 0
		if syncControls then syncControls() end
		if previewToggle then previewToggle.Set(false) end
		say(#data.blocks .. " blocks loaded; show the preview to position them")
	end
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local OWNER_KEYS = { owner = true, ownerid = true, owneruserid = true, ownername = true,
		player = true, playerid = true, playername = true, userid = true, creator = true,
		creatorid = true, creatoruserid = true, createdby = true, tag = true }
	local function playerFrom(value, players)
		if typeof(value) == "Instance" then
			if value:IsA("Player") then
				for _, player in ipairs(players) do if value == player then return player end end
			end
			return nil
		end
		for _, player in ipairs(players) do
			if (type(value) == "number" and value == player.UserId) or
				(type(value) == "string" and (value:lower() == player.Name:lower() or value == tostring(player.UserId))) then return player end
		end
	end
	local function readOwner(objects, players)
		local found, tagged, conflict = nil, false, false
		local function consider(value)
			tagged = true
			local player = playerFrom(value, players)
			if not player or (found and found ~= player) then conflict = true else found = player end
		end
		for _, object in ipairs(objects) do
			for key, value in pairs(object:GetAttributes()) do
				if OWNER_KEYS[key:lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")] then consider(value) end
			end
			for _, child in ipairs(object:GetChildren()) do
				if OWNER_KEYS[child.Name:lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")] and
					(child:IsA("ObjectValue") or child:IsA("StringValue") or child:IsA("IntValue") or child:IsA("NumberValue")) then consider(child.Value) end
			end
		end
		return not conflict and found or nil, tagged
	end
	local function blockOwner(model, primary, players)
		-- Direct block/PPart tags win over container names. Conflicting or unresolved
		-- tags are never overridden by a teammate's plot or a guessed owner.
		local owner, tagged = readOwner({model, primary}, players)
		if tagged then return owner end
		local parent = model.Parent
		while parent and parent ~= Workspace do
			owner, tagged = readOwner({parent}, players)
			if tagged then return owner end
			if parent:IsA("Folder") or parent:IsA("Model") then
				owner = playerFrom(parent.Name, players)
				if owner then return owner end
			end
			parent = parent.Parent
		end
	end
	local sourceDd
	local function refreshPlayers()
		local keep = state.sourceUserId or LocalPlayer.UserId
		local players = Players:GetPlayers()
		table.sort(players, function(a, b) return a.Name:lower() < b.Name:lower() end)
		local names, selected, entries = {}, nil, {}
		for i, player in ipairs(players) do
			entries[i] = {player = player, userId = player.UserId}
			names[i] = player.DisplayName == player.Name and ("@" .. player.Name) or (player.DisplayName .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ")")
			if player == LocalPlayer then names[i] = names[i] .. " (you)" end
			if player.UserId == keep then selected = i end
		end
		if not selected then
			table.insert(entries, 1, {userId = keep})
			table.insert(names, 1, "Selected player left - choose a player")
			selected = 1
		end
		state.players, state.sourceUserId = entries, keep
		sourceDd.SetOptions(names); sourceDd.Set(selected, true)
	end
	sourceDd = Dropdown(library, "Source player", {}, 1, function(i)
		local entry = state.players[i]
		state.sourceUserId = entry and entry.userId or nil
	end)
	Button(library, "Refresh Players", "Refresh", guarded(refreshPlayers))
	local function playersChanged()
		task.defer(function() if Live.Running then guarded(refreshPlayers)() end end)
	end
	bind(Players.PlayerAdded, playersChanged)
	bind(Players.PlayerRemoving, playersChanged)
	-- Captures only blocks attributable to the selected player, including multiple
	-- players on one plot. Source position is not used as an ownership signal.
	Button(library, "Capture Player Build", "Capture", guarded(function()
		local entry = state.players[sourceDd.Get()]
		local player = entry and entry.player
		if not player or Players:GetPlayerByUserId(player.UserId) ~= player then error("Selected player left; choose a player from the list") end
		local players = Players:GetPlayers()
		local owned, unknown = {}, 0
		for _, model in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if model:IsA("Model") and not (state.preview and model:IsDescendantOf(state.preview)) then
				local primary = model:FindFirstChild("PPart")
				if primary and primary:IsA("BasePart") then
					local owner = blockOwner(model, primary, players)
					if owner == player then table.insert(owned, {model = model, primary = primary})
					elseif not owner then unknown = unknown + 1 end
				end
			end
		end
		if #owned == 0 then error("No blocks linked to @" .. player.Name .. ". " .. unknown .. " blocks have unreadable ownership; their team's blocks were not included.") end
		local zone = zoneFor(player.Team)
		local origin = zone and plotFrame(zone) or owned[1].primary.CFrame
		local data = {format = "SolosHub.Babft", version = 1, blocks = {}, source = {userId = player.UserId, username = player.Name}}
		local total = 0
		for _, item in ipairs(owned) do
			local model, primary = item.model, item.primary
			local block = {kind = model.Name, cf = frame(origin:ToObjectSpace(primary.CFrame)), parts = {}}
			for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
				if p:IsA("BasePart") then
					if not classes[p.ClassName] or p:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("DataModelMesh") then error("Cannot accurately save " .. model.Name .. ": mesh geometry needs game templates") end
					total = total + 1
					if total > MAX_PARTS then error("Build exceeds 3000 parts") end
					table.insert(block.parts, {class = p.ClassName, shape = p:IsA("Part") and p.Shape.Name or "Block", cf = frame(primary.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(p.CFrame)), size = vector(p.Size), color = {p.Color.R, p.Color.G, p.Color.B}, material = p.Material.Name, alpha = p.Transparency})
				end
			end
			table.insert(data.blocks, block)
		end
		install(data)
		say("Captured " .. #data.blocks .. " blocks from @" .. player.Name .. ". Save a file or show the preview." .. (unknown > 0 and (" Skipped " .. unknown .. " blocks with unreadable ownership.") or ""))
	end), nil, "Capture blocks owned by the selected player, even when players share a team")
	local savedDd = Dropdown(library, "Saved builds", {}, 1)
	local function refreshFiles(preferred)
		local selectedName = preferred or state.files[savedDd.Get()]
		storage()
		local names, seen = {}, {}
		for _, path in ipairs(listfiles(dir)) do
			local name = path:gsub("\\", "/"):match("([^/]+)%.json$")
			if safeName(name) and not seen[name] then seen[name] = true; table.insert(names, name) end
		end
		table.sort(names); state.files = names; savedDd.SetOptions(names)
		for i, name in ipairs(names) do if name == selectedName then savedDd.Set(i, true); break end end
	end
	Input(library, "Save as", "Build name (new file)", "Save", guarded(function(name)
		name = safeName(name)
		if not name then error("Use 1-60 letters, numbers, spaces, underscores or hyphens") end
		if not state.data then error("Capture or load a build first") end
		storage()
		local path = dir .. "/" .. name .. ".json"
		if isfile(path) then error("That build name already exists; use a new name") end
		local text = Http:JSONEncode(state.data)
		if #text > MAX_BYTES then error("Build file is too large") end
		writefile(path, text)
		if readfile(path) ~= text then error("Could not verify the saved file") end
		refreshFiles(name); say("Saved " .. name .. " locally. Click Load to open its preview.")
	end))
	Button(library, "Refresh Saved Builds", "Refresh", guarded(refreshFiles))
	Button(library, "Load Selected Build", "Load", guarded(function()
		storage()
		local name = state.files[savedDd.Get()]
		if not name then error("Select a saved build first") end
		local text = readfile(dir .. "/" .. name .. ".json")
		if #text > MAX_BYTES then error("Build file exceeds 8 MB") end
		local ok, data = pcall(function() return Http:JSONDecode(text) end)
		if not ok then error("Cannot read " .. name .. ": invalid JSON") end
		install(data)
		local shown, reason = pcall(showPreview)
		if shown then
			say("Loaded " .. name .. " (" .. #data.blocks .. " blocks). Preview is on your plot; adjust it, then Build At Preview.")
		else
			say("Loaded " .. name .. ", but preview is unavailable: " .. tostring(reason):gsub("^.-:%d+: ", ""), true)
		end
	end))
	local preview = Section(page, "Placement Preview", "A translucent, non-colliding copy on your current team's plot", "magnet")
	previewToggle = Toggle(preview, "Show Preview", false, function(value)
		if state.busy then previewToggle.Set(state.shown); say("Stop building before changing the preview", true); return end
		if value then
			local ok, err = pcall(showPreview)
			if not ok then clearPreview(); previewToggle.Set(false); say(tostring(err), true) end
		else clearPreview() end
	end)
	positionRow = InfoRow(preview, "Load opens a preview here; capture a player build to create a new file")
	local controls = {}
	local function sync()
		for key, control in pairs(controls) do control.SetSilent(key == "angle" and state[key] or state[key] * 10) end
		updatePreview()
	end
	syncControls = sync
	for _, axis in ipairs({"x", "y", "z"}) do
		controls[axis] = Slider(preview, "Offset " .. axis:upper() .. " (x0.1 studs)", axis == "y" and 0 or -1000, 1000, 0, function(v) if state.busy then sync(); return end; state[axis] = v / 10; updatePreview() end)
	end
	controls.angle = Slider(preview, "Rotation (degrees)", -180, 180, 0, function(v) if state.busy then sync(); return end; state.angle = v; updatePreview() end)
	Input(preview, "Exact offset", "X, Y, Z in studs", "Set", guarded(function(text)
		local x, y, z = text:match("^%s*([^,]+),%s*([^,]+),%s*([^,]+)%s*$")
		x, y, z = tonumber(x), tonumber(y), tonumber(z)
		if not finite(x, 100) or not finite(y, 100) or not finite(z, 100) or y < 0 then error("Use X, Y, Z from -100 to 100; height must be 0 to 100") end
		state.x, state.y, state.z = x, y, z; sync()
	end))
	Button(preview, "Reset Placement", "Reset", guarded(function() state.x, state.y, state.z, state.angle = 0, 0, 0, 0; sync() end))
	Button(preview, "Clear Preview", "Clear", guarded(function() clearPreview(); previewToggle.Set(false); positionRow.Text = "Preview cleared" end))
	-- Learn the native placement call instead of guessing a game's remote contract.
	-- Only a BuildingTool RemoteFunction, seven typed arguments, the local plot and
	-- a newly replicated block can establish a recipe. No arbitrary remote replay.
	local building = Section(page, "Auto Build", "Learn a block with the normal building tool, then build from the preview", "tools")
	local buildRow = InfoRow(building, "No block types learned in this session")
	local recipes, learning, hooked, hookActive, replaying = {}, false, false, true, false
	local learnToken = 0
	local placementReport = "No placement observed yet. Click Learn, then place one normal block."
	local function describePlacement(remote, args)
		local lines = {"BABFT placement report v1", "remote=" .. remote.Name, "arguments=" .. args.n}
		for i = 1, args.n do
			local value = args[i]
			local kind = typeof(value)
			local detail = ""
			if kind == "CFrame" then detail = table.concat(frame(value), ", ")
			elseif kind == "Vector3" then detail = table.concat(vector(value), ", ")
			elseif kind == "Instance" then detail = value.ClassName .. ":" .. value.Name
			elseif kind == "string" or kind == "number" or kind == "boolean" then detail = tostring(value):sub(1, 120) end
			lines[#lines + 1] = i .. ": " .. kind .. " " .. detail
		end
		placementReport = table.concat(lines, "\n")
	end
	local job = 0
	local function buildSay(text) buildRow.Text = text end
	local function inventory(kind)
		local match
		for _, v in ipairs(LocalPlayer:GetDescendants()) do
			if v.Name == kind and (v:IsA("IntValue") or v:IsA("NumberValue")) and finite(v.Value, 10000000) and v.Value >= 0 then
				if match and match ~= v then return nil end
				match = v
			end
		end
		return match
	end
	local function buildingTool(remote)
		if not remote or not remote:IsA("RemoteFunction") then return nil end
		local tool = remote:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool")
		local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
		if tool and tool.Name == "BuildingTool" and (tool.Parent == LocalPlayer.Character or tool.Parent == backpack) then return tool end
	end
	local function cfNear(a, b)
		local aa, bb = frame(a), frame(b)
		for i = 1, 12 do if math.abs(aa[i] - bb[i]) > 0.025 then return false end end
		return true
	end
	local function sameParts(a, b)
		if #a ~= #b then return false end
		local used = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(a) do
			local found
			for i, q in ipairs(b) do
				if not used[i] and p.class == q.class and p.shape == q.shape and p.material == q.material and math.abs(p.alpha - q.alpha) < 0.02 and cfNear(CFrame.new(table.unpack(p.cf)), CFrame.new(table.unpack(q.cf))) then
					local equal = true
					for axis = 1, 3 do if math.abs(p.size[axis] - q.size[axis]) > 0.025 or math.abs(p.color[axis] - q.color[axis]) > 0.01 then equal = false end end
					if equal then found = i; break end
				end
			end
			if not found then return false end
			used[found] = true
		end
		return true
	end
	local function geometry(model)
		local primary = model:FindFirstChild("PPart")
		if not primary or not primary:IsA("BasePart") then return nil end
		local parts = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if p:IsA("BasePart") then
				if not classes[p.ClassName] or p:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("DataModelMesh") then return nil end
				table.insert(parts, {class = p.ClassName, shape = p:IsA("Part") and p.Shape.Name or "Block", cf = frame(primary.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(p.CFrame)), size = vector(p.Size), color = {p.Color.R, p.Color.G, p.Color.B}, material = p.Material.Name, alpha = p.Transparency})
			end
		end
		return parts, primary
	end
	local function models()
		local set = {}
		for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("Model") and d:FindFirstChild("PPart") then set[d] = true end
		end
		return set
	end
	local function arrived(before, kind, target, parts, still)
		local deadline = os.clock() + 6
		repeat
			if not still() then return nil end
			for model in pairs(models()) do
				if not before[model] and (not kind or model.Name == kind) then
					local actual, primary = geometry(model)
					if actual and cfNear(primary.CFrame, target) and (not parts or sameParts(parts, actual)) then return model, actual end
				end
			end
			if not still() then return nil end
			task.wait(0.15)
		until os.clock() > deadline
	end
	local function learnCall(remote, args)
		if not buildingTool(remote) or args.n ~= 7 or not zoneFor(LocalPlayer.Team) then return nil end
		if type(args[1]) ~= "string" or type(args[2]) ~= "number" or args[3] ~= zoneFor(LocalPlayer.Team) or typeof(args[4]) ~= "CFrame" or args[5] ~= true or args[6] ~= 1 or typeof(args[7]) ~= "CFrame" then return nil end
		if not cfNear(args[4], args[7]) then return nil end
		local count = inventory(args[1])
		if not count or args[2] ~= count.Value then return nil end
		return {item = args[1], flag = args[5], remote = remote, target = args[4], before = models(), zone = args[3]}
	end
	local function hook()
		if hooked then return true end
		local hookFn = hookmetamethod or API.env.hookmetamethod
		local methodFn = getnamecallmethod or API.env.getnamecallmethod
		local wrap = newcclosure or API.env.newcclosure or function(f) return f end
		if type(hookFn) ~= "function" or type(methodFn) ~= "function" then error("Learning needs hookmetamethod and getnamecallmethod support in your executor") end
		local previous
		previous = hookFn(game, "__namecall", wrap(function(remote, ...)
			local sample
			if hookActive and learning and not replaying and methodFn() == "InvokeServer" then
				local args = table.pack(...)
				if buildingTool(remote) then pcall(describePlacement, remote, args) end
				local ok, result = pcall(learnCall, remote, args)
				if ok then sample = result end
				if not sample and buildingTool(remote) then task.defer(function() if hookActive then buildSay("Placement format not supported yet. Copy Placement Report for the developer.") end end) end
				if sample then learning = false; sample.token = learnToken end
			end
			-- Preserve the native call and its returns exactly. The observer cannot break it.
			local result
			if sample then
				local call = table.pack(pcall(previous, remote, ...))
				if not call[1] then task.defer(function() if hookActive then buildSay("Native placement failed; learn again") end end); error(call[2], 0) end
				result = {n = call.n - 1}; for i = 2, call.n do result[i-1] = call[i] end
			else result = table.pack(previous(remote, ...)) end
			if sample and hookActive and result[1] == false then
				task.defer(function() if hookActive then buildSay("The game rejected that placement; learn again") end end)
			elseif sample and hookActive then
				task.defer(function()
					if not hookActive or sample.token ~= learnToken then return end
					local ok, err = pcall(function()
						buildSay("Waiting for the block you placed...")
						local model, parts = arrived(sample.before, nil, sample.target, nil, function() return sample.token == learnToken and hookActive and Live.Running and zoneFor(LocalPlayer.Team) == sample.zone end)
						if not hookActive or sample.token ~= learnToken then return end
						if not model then buildSay("Could not verify that placement; learn again with one plain block") return end
						recipes[model.Name] = {item = sample.item, flag = sample.flag, remoteName = sample.remote.Name, parts = parts}
						buildSay("Learned " .. model.Name .. ". Repeat for other block types you need.")
					end)
					if not ok and hookActive then buildSay("Learning failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
				end)
			end
			return table.unpack(result, 1, result.n)
		end))
		hooked = true
	end
	Button(building, "Learn Building Tool", "Learn", guarded(function()
		if state.busy then error("Stop the current build first") end
		hook(); learnToken = learnToken + 1; learning = true
		buildSay("Place ONE plain block normally on your plot. Keep it anchored.")
	end), nil, "Observe your normal tool once per required block type; no building requests are sent while learning")
	Button(building, "Copy Placement Report", "Copy", function() copyText(placementReport); buildSay("Placement report copied. This setup step is temporary while the native builder is developed.") end)
	Button(building, "Cancel Learning", "Cancel", function() learning = false; learnToken = learnToken + 1; buildSay("Learning cancelled") end)
	local function remoteFor(recipe)
		for _, container in pairs({LocalPlayer.Character, LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")}) do
			if container then
				local tool = container:FindFirstChild("BuildingTool")
				local remote = tool and tool:FindFirstChild(recipe.remoteName, true)
				if remote and buildingTool(remote) then return remote end
			end
		end
	end
	local function preflight()
		if not state.data or not state.shown or not state.preview then error("Show and position the preview first") end
		updatePreview()
		if not state.preview or not state.inside then error("Keep every preview part inside your plot and above the ground") end
		local counts = {}
		for _, block in ipairs(state.data.blocks) do
			local recipe = recipes[block.kind]
			if not recipe then error("Learn " .. block.kind .. " using the normal building tool first") end
			if not sameParts(recipe.parts, block.parts) then error(block.kind .. " differs from the learned block. Scaled, repainted and custom blocks need additional tool support.") end
			if not remoteFor(recipe) then error("Equip your building tool first") end
			counts[recipe.item] = (counts[recipe.item] or 0) + 1
		end
		for item, need in pairs(counts) do
			local count = inventory(item)
			if not count then error("Could not read inventory for " .. item) end
			if count.Value < need then error("Not enough " .. item .. ": need " .. need .. ", available " .. count.Value) end
		end
		return state.origin, state.zone, state.data
	end
	Button(building, "Check Build", "Check", guarded(function()
		preflight(); buildSay("Ready: inventory and learned block shapes match the preview")
	end))
	Button(building, "Build At Preview", "Build", guarded(function()
		if state.busy then error("A build is already running") end
		local origin, zone, data = preflight()
		local zoneStart, teamStart = zone.CFrame, LocalPlayer.Team
		state.busy, learning = true, false; learnToken = learnToken + 1
		job = job + 1; local current = job
		local function running()
			return current == job and hookActive and Live.Running and LocalPlayer.Team == teamStart and zone.Parent and zoneFor(LocalPlayer.Team) == zone and state.preview and cfNear(zone.CFrame, zoneStart) and getHum() and getHum().Health > 0
		end
		task.spawn(function()
			local completed = 0
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				for _, block in ipairs(data.blocks) do
					if not running() then error("Stopped") end
					local recipe = recipes[block.kind]
					local remote, count = remoteFor(recipe), inventory(recipe.item)
					if not remote or not count or count.Value < 1 then error("Building tool or inventory became unavailable") end
					local target = origin * CFrame.new(table.unpack(block.cf))
					local before = models()
					buildSay("Building " .. (completed + 1) .. " / " .. #data.blocks)
					replaying = true
					local sent, reason = pcall(function() return remote:InvokeServer(recipe.item, count.Value, zone, target, recipe.flag, 1, target) end)
					replaying = false
					if not sent or reason == false then error("Game rejected placement; stopped without retrying") end
					local model = arrived(before, block.kind, target, block.parts, running)
					if not model then error("Placement did not match the preview; stopped without retrying") end
					completed = completed + 1
					if not running() then error("Stopped") end
					task.wait(0.25)
				end
			end)
			state.busy, replaying = false, false
			if hookActive then buildSay((ok and "Finished" or tostring(err):gsub("^.-:%d+: ", "")) .. " | verified " .. completed .. " / " .. #data.blocks .. " blocks") end
		end)
	end))
	Button(building, "Stop Building", "Stop", function()
		job = job + 1; learning = false; learnToken = learnToken + 1
		buildSay(state.busy and "Stopping after the in-flight placement returns; existing blocks stay" or "Stopped")
	end)
	InfoRow(building, "Standard learned blocks only. Scaling, painting, meshes and wiring are not supported yet.")
	onUnload(function() hookActive = false; learning = false; job = job + 1 end)
	-- A team switch, removed zone or respawn must never leave a preview on the old plot.
	bind(LocalPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"), function() job = job + 1; learning = false; learnToken = learnToken + 1; clearPreview(); previewToggle.Set(false); say("Team changed; show preview again on your new plot") end)
	bind(LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving, function() job = job + 1; learning = false; learnToken = learnToken + 1; clearPreview(); previewToggle.Set(false) end)
	local elapsed = 0
	bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
		if not state.preview then return end
		elapsed = elapsed + dt
		if elapsed < 0.25 then return end
		elapsed = 0
		if not state.zone or not state.zone.Parent or zoneFor(LocalPlayer.Team) ~= state.zone or not cfNear(state.zone.CFrame, state.zoneCF) then job = job + 1; clearPreview(); previewToggle.Set(false); say("Plot changed; show preview again", true) end
	end)
	onUnload(clearPreview)
	guarded(refreshPlayers)()
	guarded(refreshFiles)()
end
-- BABFT BUILD LIBRARY END

-- ---------------------------------------------------- Teleports (shared Universal tab)
local dest = { "Golden Chest", "My Team Zone" }
for i = 10, 1, -1 do table.insert(dest, 1, "Stage " .. i) end
local function destPart(i)
	if i <= 10 then return stagePart(i) end
	if i == 11 then return chestPart() end
	return teamZone()
end
local Tp = Section(Tabs["Teleports"], "Build A Boat Teleports", "Jump to any stage or the chest", "teleports")
local tpDd = Dropdown(Tp, "Destination", dest, 1)
Button(Tp, "Teleport", "Go", function()
	local i = tpDd.Get()
	local ok
	if i <= 10 or i == 11 then
		ok = tpTo(destPart(i))
	else
		ok = tpTo(teamZone(), 10)
	end
	if not ok then notify("Couldn't find " .. tpDd.Name() .. " (or you have no character)", T.Warn) end
end)
Button(Tp, "Tween There", "Tween", function()
	local p = destPart(tpDd.Get())
	if not p then notify("Couldn't find " .. tpDd.Name(), T.Warn) return end
	task.spawn(function() moveTo(p, B.token, 2) end)
end, nil, "Glides there at the Tween speed from the Settings tab")

onUnload(function()
	B.token = B.token + 1
	SH.buyTok = SH.buyTok + 1
	SH.openTok = SH.openTok + 1
	clearMarkers()
	for _, g in ipairs(zoneMarkers) do g:Destroy() end
	if floatPart then floatPart:Destroy() end
	if safePart then safePart:Destroy() end
	if HWclean then HWclean() end
	if LVclean then LVclean() end
end)]==],
	}

	-- everything a game script can use from this file (each script reads it as API.<name>)
	Hub.API = {
		AddTab = AddTab, Button = Button, Esp = Esp, EspGui = EspGui,
		ICONS = ICONS, InfoRow = InfoRow, Input = Input, Keybind = Keybind,
		LocalPlayer = LocalPlayer, PlayerGui = PlayerGui, Players = Players, RunService = RunService,
		Section = Section, Slider = Slider, TabButtons = TabButtons, Tabs = Tabs,
		Toggle = Toggle, TweenService = TweenService, UIS = UIS, Workspace = Workspace,
		bind = bind, cam = cam, copyText = copyText, env = env,
		filterTabs = filterTabs, getChar = getChar, getHum = getHum, getRoot = getRoot,
		nearestPlayer = nearestPlayer, new = new, notify = notify, onUnload = onUnload,
		round = round, spectate = spectate, stopSpectate = stopSpectate,
		PlayerList = PlayerList, Dropdown = Dropdown, Collapse = Collapse,
	}
	-- values that change while the hub runs are read live: Live.Running, Live.Unlocked, Live.BindingActive
	Hub.API.Live = setmetatable({}, { __index = function(_, k)
		if k == "Running" then return Running end
		if k == "Unlocked" then return Unlocked end
		if k == "BindingActive" then return BindingActive end
	end })

	-- downloads + runs a game script. returns true, or false + reason
	function Hub.loadGameModule(g)
		local function failed(stage, reason)
			local message = stage .. ": " .. tostring(reason)
			Hub.LastModuleError = { game = g.name or g.id, id = g.id, url = Hub.ModuleURLs[g.id], message = message }
			warn("[Solos Hub module " .. tostring(g.id) .. "] " .. message)
			return false, message
		end
		local src = Hub.BundledModules and Hub.BundledModules[g.id]
		local url = Hub.ModuleURLs[g.id]
		if not src and type(url) == "string" and url ~= "" then
			local req = type(request) == "function" and request or type(http_request) == "function" and http_request or (syn and syn.request)
			local requestError = "request API unavailable"
			if type(req) == "function" then
				local okq, response = pcall(req, { Url = url, Method = "GET" })
				if okq and type(response) == "table" then
					local status = tonumber(response.StatusCode or response.Status)
					if (status == nil or status == 200) and response.Success ~= false and type(response.Body) == "string" and response.Body:find("%S") then
						src = response.Body
					else
						requestError = "HTTP " .. tostring(status or "unknown") .. " or empty response"
					end
				else
					requestError = okq and "invalid response from request API" or tostring(response)
				end
			end
			if not src then
				local okh, response = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url, true) end)
				if okh and type(response) == "string" and response:find("%S") then
					src = response
				else
					local f = Hub.path("mod_" .. g.id .. ".lua")
					local okf, localSource = pcall(function()
						if canFile and isfile(f) then return readfile(f) end
					end)
					if okf and type(localSource) == "string" and localSource:find("%S") then
						src = localSource
					else
						local httpError = type(response) == "string" and response:find("%S") and response or "empty response"
						return failed("download failed", "request: " .. requestError .. "; HttpGet: " .. tostring(httpError) .. "; no local module at " .. f .. ". Use SoloshubStandalone.txt if the repository is private.")
					end
				end
			end
		elseif not src then
			local f = Hub.path("mod_" .. g.id .. ".lua")
			local okf, result = pcall(function() if canFile and isfile(f) then return readfile(f) end end)
			if not okf then return failed("local file failed", result) end
			src = result
		end
		if type(src) ~= "string" or not src:find("%S") then return failed("source missing", "no script URL or local file for " .. g.id) end
		if type(loadstring) ~= "function" then return failed("compile unavailable", "this executor has no loadstring") end
		local okc, fn, cerr = pcall(loadstring, src, "=" .. g.id)
		if not okc then return failed("compile failed", fn) end
		if type(fn) ~= "function" then return failed("compile failed", cerr) end
		local okr, rerr = pcall(fn, Hub, S, T, C)
		if not okr then return failed("module initialization failed", rerr) end
		Hub.LastModuleError = nil
		return true
	end

	-- ------------------------------------------------------------ game list (shown after the loading screen)
	-- Add a game: append an entry. ready = false shows "Coming Soon" and blocks selecting it.
	-- remote = true: the game's script is downloaded from Hub.ModuleURLs[id] when the game is picked.
	Hub.Games = {
		{ id = "universal", name = "Universal", desc = "You're here for a reason. Let's make it a good one.", tag = "Universal", ready = true },
		{ id = "mm2", name = "Murder Mystery 2", desc = "Survive the murderer, collect coins, and be the last one standing.", tag = "MM2", placeId = 142823291, universeId = 66654135, ready = true, wip = true, remote = true, home = "Murder Mystery 2" },
	}
	table.insert(Hub.Games, { id = "arsenal", name = "Arsenal", desc = "Fast-paced FPS action with a variety of weapons.", tag = "Shooting", placeId = 286090429, universeId = 111958650, ready = true, wip = true, remote = true, home = "Arsenal" })
	table.insert(Hub.Games, { id = "pf", name = "Phantom Forces", desc = "Realistic FPS with tons of weapons and maps.", tag = "Shooting", placeId = 292439477, universeId = 113491250, ready = true, wip = true, remote = true, home = "Phantom Forces" })
	table.insert(Hub.Games, { id = "lt2", name = "Lumber Tycoon 2", desc = "Chop trees, haul logs and build your dream base.", tag = "Tycoon", placeId = 13822889, ready = true, wip = true, remote = true, home = "Lumber Tycoon 2" })
	table.insert(Hub.Games, { id = "doomspire", name = "No Cooldown Doomspire Brickbattle", desc = "Build a tower, bomb your rivals and outlast everyone.", tag = "Brickbattle", placeId = 79602128065352, ready = true, wip = true, remote = true, home = "Doomspire" })
