	-- Every game except Universal is still a work in progress (lots of known bugs). Games carry wip = true and
	-- the picker, the sidebar header and a toast all say so. Set wip = false on a game once it is finished.
	function Hub.gameHeader(title, wip)
		if wip == nil then wip = true end
		return new("TextLabel", {
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, wip and 34 or 22), BackgroundTransparency = 1, RichText = true,
			Text = "  " .. title .. (wip and '\n<font color="#f1c40f">  WORK IN PROGRESS</font>' or ""),
			TextColor3 = T.Sub, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, LayoutOrder = -20,
		}, NavScroll)
	end
	Hub.currentGame = "universal"

	-- ------------------------------------------------------------ game picker
	local function pickGame()
		local GW, GH = 560, 548
		local gc = makeCard(GW, GH)
		logo(gc, UDim2.fromOffset(24, 22), 72)
		UI.brandTitle(gc, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 32), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(112, 28) }, 26)
		Label(gc, { Text = "Select a game to load", Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(112, 62), TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = UI.MUTED })
		local closeG = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28), Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 12), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "✕", TextColor3 = T.Sub, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 13 }, gc)
		Hub.Icons.button(closeG, "close_l", "X", 16, UI.RED)
		bind(closeG.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("click") Unload() end)

		-- search bar
		local sRow = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 44), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 112), BackgroundColor3 = UI.FIELD, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, gc)
		round(sRow, 10)
		local sStroke = new("UIStroke", { Color = UI.FIELD_STROKE, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, sRow)
		UI.iconImg(sRow, "search_l", 20, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -10))
		local sBox = new("TextBox", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", PlaceholderText = "Search games...", PlaceholderColor3 = UI.MUTED, TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 14, ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left }, sRow)
		local sClear = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26), Position = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0.5, -13), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "✕", TextColor3 = T.Sub, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, Visible = false }, sRow)
		Hub.Icons.button(sClear, "close_l", "X", 14, UI.RED)
		bind(sBox.Focused, function() sStroke.Color = UI.BRAND end)
		bind(sBox.FocusLost, function() sStroke.Color = UI.FIELD_STROKE end)
		bind(sClear.MouseButton1Click, function() sBox.Text = "" end)
		bind(sBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function() sClear.Visible = sBox.Text ~= "" end)

		-- list
		local list = new("ScrollingFrame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 1, -214), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 170), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = UI.FIELD_STROKE, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new() }, gc)
		new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, list)
		new("UIPadding", { PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8) }, list)
		local empty = Label(gc, { Text = "No games match your search", Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 190), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = UI.MUTED, Visible = false })
		local footer = Label(gc, { Text = "", Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 1, -32), TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = UI.MUTED })

		local chosen
		local entries = {}
		local function choose(g)
			if chosen then return end
			if not g.ready then
				Hub.play("error")
				notify(g.name .. " is coming soon", T.Warn)
				return
			end
			Hub.play("click")
			chosen = g
		end
		for i, g in ipairs(Hub.Games) do
			local here = g.placeId ~= nil and g.placeId == game.PlaceId
			local row = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 88), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 32), Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false }, list)
			round(row, 12)
			local stroke = new("UIStroke", { Color = here and UI.BRAND or UI.FIELD_STROKE, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, row)
			local icon = new("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(68, 68), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 28, 60), BorderSizePixel = 0, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop, ImageTransparency = g.ready and 0 or 0.45 }, row)
			round(icon, 10)
			if g.id == "universal" then
				task.spawn(function() local a2 = Hub.logoAsset() if a2 and icon.Parent then icon.Image = a2 end end)
			elseif g.universeId or g.placeId then
				if g.universeId then icon.Image = "rbxthumb://type=GameIcon&id=" .. g.universeId .. "&w=150&h=150" end
				task.spawn(function()
					local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request)
					if not (req and g.placeId) then return end
					local ok, res = pcall(req, { Url = "https://apis.roblox.com/universes/v1/places/" .. g.placeId .. "/universe", Method = "GET" })
					local okd, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(res.Body) end)
					if ok and okd and type(data) == "table" and tonumber(data.universeId) and icon.Parent then
						icon.Image = "rbxthumb://type=GameIcon&id=" .. tostring(data.universeId) .. "&w=150&h=150"
					end
				end)
			end
			Label(row, { Text = g.name, Size = UDim2.new(1, -250, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(92, 11), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 16, TextColor3 = g.ready and WHITE or Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 175), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
			Label(row, { Text = g.ready and g.desc or "Not available yet", Size = UDim2.new(1, -250, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(92, 35), TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = UI.MUTED, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
			-- tag pills (game type + WIP)
			local tags = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -250, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(92, 58), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, row)
			new("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, tags)
			local function tagPill(text, col, order)
				local p = new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 20), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, BackgroundColor3 = col, BackgroundTransparency = 0.78, Text = text, TextColor3 = col:Lerp(WHITE, 0.45), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11, LayoutOrder = order }, tags)
				round(p, 6)
				new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 9), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 9) }, p)
			end
			if g.tag then tagPill(g.tag, UI.BRAND, 1) end
			if g.wip then tagPill("WIP", T.Warn, 2) end
			-- Select / Coming Soon / Current Game button
			local bText, bBg, bTrans, bStroke = "Select", Color3.fromRGB(52, 44, 150), 0, UI.BRAND
			if not g.ready then bText, bBg, bTrans, bStroke = "Coming Soon", T.Warn, 0.82, T.Warn elseif here then bText, bBg, bTrans, bStroke = "Current Game", T.Good, 0.82, T.Good end
			local badge = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(116, 38), Position = UDim2.new(1, -130, 0.5, -19), BackgroundColor3 = bBg, BackgroundTransparency = bTrans, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, row)
			round(badge, 19)
			new("UIStroke", { Color = bStroke, Transparency = 0.45, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, badge)
			local bl, bi = UI.iconText(badge, g.ready and not here and "arrow" or nil, bText, 13, (g.ready and not here) and WHITE or bStroke, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
			if bi then bi.LayoutOrder = 3 end
			if g.ready then
				bind(row.MouseEnter, function() Hub.play("hover") row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 22, 48) stroke.Color = UI.BRAND end)
				bind(row.MouseLeave, function() row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 32) stroke.Color = here and UI.BRAND or UI.FIELD_STROKE end)
			end
			bind(row.MouseButton1Click, function() choose(g) end)
			-- order: Universal first, then the game you're standing in, then ready games, then coming soon
			local rankN = (g.id == "universal" and 0) or (here and 1) or (g.ready and 2) or 3
			table.insert(entries, { g = g, row = row, order = rankN * 1000 + i })
		end

		local function filter()
			local q = sBox.Text:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
			local shown, firstReady = 0, nil
			table.sort(entries, function(x, y) return x.order < y.order end)
			for n, e in ipairs(entries) do
				local hit = q == "" or e.g.name:lower():find(q, 1, true) ~= nil or (e.g.desc or ""):lower():find(q, 1, true) ~= nil or (e.g.tag or ""):lower():find(q, 1, true) ~= nil or tostring(e.g.placeId or ""):find(q, 1, true) ~= nil or (e.g.wip == true and ("wip work in progress"):find(q, 1, true) ~= nil)
				e.row.Visible = hit
				e.row.LayoutOrder = n
				if hit then
					shown = shown + 1
					if e.g.ready and not firstReady then firstReady = e.g end
				end
			end
			empty.Visible = shown == 0
			footer.Text = (shown == #entries) and (#entries .. " games") or (shown .. " of " .. #entries .. " games")
			return firstReady
		end
		bind(sBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), filter)
		bind(sBox.FocusLost, function(enter)
			if enter then
				local g = filter()
				if g then choose(g) end
			end
		end)
		filter()
		appear(gc)
		while not chosen and Running do task.wait(0.05) end
		if not Running then return nil end
		vanish(gc)
		gc:Destroy()
		return chosen
	end

		local card = makeCard(540, 236)

		local tc = tierColor(rank)
		local pill = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(134, 38), Position = UDim2.new(1, -164, 0, 44), BackgroundColor3 = tc, BackgroundTransparency = 0.88, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, card)
		round(pill, 19)
		new("UIStroke", { Color = tc, Transparency = 0.45, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, pill)
		UI.iconText(pill, "user", rank, 13, tc, Enum.Font.GothamSemibold)
		local status = Label(card, { Text = "Starting...", Size = UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 142), TextSize = 16, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextColor3 = WHITE })
		local pctLbl = Label(card, { Text = "0%", Size = UDim2.fromOffset(80, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 142), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 16, TextColor3 = WHITE })
		local track = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 10), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 172), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(34, 32, 62), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, card)
		round(track, 5)
		local fill = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, track)
		round(fill, 5)
		new("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(UI.BRAND, UI.BRAND_LIGHT) }, fill)
		UI.iconImg(card, "bulb", 18, UDim2.fromOffset(30, 199))
		local tip = Label(card, { Text = "", RichText = true, Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 198), TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = UI.MUTED, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
		appear(card)

		local tips = {
			"Press " .. S.MenuKey.Name .. " to hide or show the menu",
			"The Players tab has a search bar and profile pictures",
			"Type 'cmds' in the Commands tab to see every command",
			"Join any server by Job ID from the Tools tab",
			"The Panic button turns every feature off at once",
			"Drop .mp3 files in workspace/SolosHub/sounds to change the UI sounds",
			"The X button fully unloads the hub and restores your game",
		}
		task.spawn(function()
			local i = 0
			while Running and card.Parent do
				i = i % #tips + 1
				tip.Text = '<font color="#ffffff">Tip:</font> ' .. tips[i]
				task.wait(1.4)
			end
		end)

		local steps = {
			{ "Verifying license...", 25, 0.15 }, { "Loading modules...", 55, 0.15 },
			{ "Applying settings...", 80, 0.15 }, { "Building interface...", 95, 0.15 }, { "Ready.", 100, 0.35 },
		}
		local shown = 0
		for _, s in ipairs(steps) do
			if not Running then return end
			status.Text = s[1]
			TweenService:Create(fill, TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Size = UDim2.new(s[2] / 100, 0, 1, 0) }):Play()
			while shown < s[2] and Running do
				shown = shown + 1
				pctLbl.Text = shown .. "%"
				task.wait(0.012)
			end
			task.wait(s[3])
		end
		if not Running then return end
		vanish(card)
		if not Running then return end
		card:Destroy()
		local pick = pickGame()
		if not pick or not Running then return end
		Hub.currentGame = pick.id
		if pick.remote then
			notify("Loading " .. pick.name .. "...", T.Sub)
			local okm, errm = Hub.loadGameModule(pick)

				notify("Couldn't load " .. pick.name .. " - using Universal. Try again shortly.", T.Bad)
				Hub.currentGame = "universal"
				pick = Hub.Games[1]
			end
		end

		if pick.wip then task.delay(1.5, function() notify(pick.name .. " is a work in progress - expect bugs", T.Warn) end) end
		task.delay(3, function() notify("Menu key hides it  |  X unloads everything") end)

	-- ------------------------------------------------------------ key window
	local card = makeCard(380, 514)

	local keyBox, boxStroke, pasteBtn
	do
	local boxRow = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 48), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 228), BackgroundColor3 = UI.FIELD, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, card)
	round(boxRow, 10)
	boxStroke = new("UIStroke", { Color = UI.FIELD_STROKE, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, boxRow)
	UI.iconImg(boxRow, "lock_l", 20, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -10))
	keyBox = new("TextBox", { Size = UDim2.new(1, getClip and -138 or -90, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, PlaceholderText = "License key...", PlaceholderColor3 = UI.MUTED, Text = "", TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 14, ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left }, boxRow)
	if getClip then
		pasteBtn = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 26), Position = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0.5, -13), BackgroundColor3 = T.Btn, Text = "Paste", TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11, AutoButtonColor = false }, boxRow)
		round(pasteBtn, 6)
	end

	-- hide / show the key. The choice is saved (keyprefs.json) and defaults to hidden, so a recording or
	-- stream of the key window never shows your key. The real text stays in the box (invisible) so
	-- pasting, the saved key and Verify all work exactly as before.
	local PREF_FILE = Hub.path("keyprefs.json")
	local hideKey = true
	if canFile and isfile(PREF_FILE) then
		local okp, dp = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(PREF_FILE)) end)
		if okp and type(dp) == "table" and type(dp.hide) == "boolean" then hideKey = dp.hide end
	end
	local maskLbl = new("TextLabel", { Size = keyBox.Size, Position = keyBox.Position, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 14, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Visible = false }, boxRow)
	local eyeBtn = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34), Position = UDim2.new(1, -44, 0.5, -17), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Show", TextColor3 = T.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11 }, boxRow)
	local eyeImg = Hub.Icons.button(eyeBtn, "show_l", "Show", 22)
	local function refreshMask()
		if hideKey then
			keyBox.TextTransparency = 1
			maskLbl.Visible = true
			maskLbl.Text = string.rep("•", math.min(#keyBox.Text, 24))
		else
			keyBox.TextTransparency = 0
			maskLbl.Visible = false
		end
		UI.setIcon(eyeImg, hideKey and "show_l" or "hide_l", function() eyeBtn.Text = "" end)
		if not eyeImg.Visible then eyeBtn.Text = hideKey and "Show" or "Hide" end
	end
	bind(keyBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), refreshMask)
	bind(eyeBtn.MouseButton1Click, function()
		Hub.play("click")
		hideKey = not hideKey
		refreshMask()
		if canFile then pcall(function() writefile(PREF_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode({ hide = hideKey })) end) end
	end)
	refreshMask()
	end

	local remember = canFile
	local chk = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 292), BackgroundColor3 = remember and UI.BRAND or UI.OFFC, Text = remember and "✓" or "", TextColor3 = WHITE, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 13, AutoButtonColor = false }, card)
	round(chk, 5)
	Label(card, { Text = canFile and "Remember my key" or "Saving keys isn't supported here", Size = UDim2.new(1, -86, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 291), TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 200, 235) })

	local status = Label(card, { Text = "Free, Premium and Owner keys are supported", Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 322), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = UI.MUTED })
	local submitBtn = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 50), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 348), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false }, card)
	round(submitBtn, 12)
	local paintSubmit
	do
	local subGrad = new("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(UI.BRAND, UI.BRAND_LIGHT), Rotation = 0 }, submitBtn)
	local subText, subArrow = UI.iconText(submitBtn, "arrow", "Verify License", 16, WHITE, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
	subArrow.LayoutOrder = 3
	paintSubmit = function(text, col)
		subText.Text = text
		subGrad.Enabled = col == nil
		submitBtn.BackgroundColor3 = col or WHITE
	end
	end
	new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -118, 0, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 429), BackgroundColor3 = UI.FIELD_STROKE, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, card)
	new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -118, 0, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 90, 0, 429), BackgroundColor3 = UI.FIELD_STROKE, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, card)
	local freeBtn = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -80, 0, 418), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Continue as Free User", TextColor3 = UI.MUTED, Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, TextSize = 13 }, card)
	local discBtn = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 48), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 448), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(36, 38, 80), Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false }, card)
	round(discBtn, 12)
	new("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 74, 140), Transparency = 0.3, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, discBtn)
	UI.iconText(discBtn, "discord_l", "Join our Discord", 14, WHITE, Enum.Font.GothamSemibold)
	bind(discBtn.MouseEnter, function() discBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(46, 48, 98) end)
	bind(discBtn.MouseLeave, function() discBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(36, 38, 80) end)
	bind(discBtn.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("click") Hub.joinDiscord() end)
	appear(card)

	if canFile and isfile(KEY_FILE) then
		local ok, savedKey = pcall(readfile, KEY_FILE)
		if ok and type(savedKey) == "string" and savedKey ~= "" then
			keyBox.Text = savedKey:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
			status.Text = "Saved key loaded - press Verify"
		end
	end

	local function shake()
		local base = card.Position
		for i = 1, 6 do
			if not card.Parent then return end
			card.Position = base + UDim2.fromOffset((i % 2 == 0 and 8 or -8) * (1 - i / 7), 0)
			task.wait(0.04)
		end
		card.Position = base
	end
	local function setStatus(text, color)
		status.Text = text
		status.TextColor3 = color or T.Sub
	end

	-- ------------------------------------------------------------ submit (the server check lives here)
	local busy, fails, lockUntil = false, 0, 0
	local function submit(override)
		if busy then return end
		local left = lockUntil - os.clock()
		if left > 0 then setStatus(("Too many attempts - wait %ds"):format(math.ceil(left)), T.Warn) return end
		local key = (override or keyBox.Text):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if key == "" then setStatus("Enter a key first", T.Warn) return end
		busy = true -- stops a double click from starting two checks / two loading screens
		setStatus("Checking key...", T.Sub)
		paintSubmit("Checking...")
		local role, err, exp, netFail = verifyKey(key)
		if role then
			rank = role
			keyExpiresAt = exp
			currentKey = (role ~= "Free User") and key or nil
			TagLabel.Text = role
			RankLabel.Text = "Rank: " .. role
			TagLabel.TextColor3 = tierColor(role)
			if canFile and not override then
				pcall(function()
					if remember then writefile(KEY_FILE, key)
					elseif isfile(KEY_FILE) and delfile then delfile(KEY_FILE) end
				end)
			end
			paintSubmit("Access granted", T.Good)
			setStatus("Key accepted - " .. role, T.Good)
			Hub.play("notify")
			task.wait(0.35)
			if not Running then return end
			vanish(card)
			card:Destroy()
			runLoading()
		else
			fails = fails + 1
			paintSubmit("Invalid Key", T.Bad)
			setStatus(err or "That key isn't valid", T.Bad)
			Hub.play("error")
			shake()
			-- a server outage should not count against you
			if netFail then fails = math.max(0, fails - 1) end
			if fails >= 5 then
				fails = 0
				lockUntil = os.clock() + 10
				setStatus("Too many attempts - locked for 10s", T.Warn)
			end
			task.wait(0.6)
			if Running then paintSubmit("Verify License") end
			busy = false
		end
	end

	-- ------------------------------------------------------------ expiry + revoke handling (runs after login)
	local function downgrade(reason)
		rank = "Free User"
		keyExpiresAt, currentKey = nil, nil
		TagLabel.Text = rank
		RankLabel.Text = "Rank: " .. rank
		TagLabel.TextColor3 = tierColor(rank)
		if S.Panic then S.Panic() end -- turn off anything that needed the higher rank
		notify(reason .. " - back to Free", T.Warn)
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		local sinceCheck = 0
		while Running do
			task.wait(30)
			sinceCheck = sinceCheck + 30
			if rank ~= "Free User" then
				if keyExpiresAt then
					local left = keyExpiresAt - os.time()
					if left <= 0 then
						downgrade("Your key expired")
					else
						RankLabel.Text = ("Rank: %s  (expires in %dh %dm)"):format(rank, left // 3600, (left % 3600) // 60)
					end
				end
				-- every 5 minutes ask the server again, so a deleted key stops working
				if rank ~= "Free User" and currentKey and sinceCheck >= 300 then
					sinceCheck = 0
					local role, err, _, netFail = verifyKey(currentKey)
					if not role and not netFail then downgrade(err or "Your key is no longer valid") end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	bind(submitBtn.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("click") submit() end)
	bind(freeBtn.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("click") submit("FREE") end)
	bind(keyBox.Focused, function() boxStroke.Color = UI.BRAND end)
	bind(keyBox.FocusLost, function(enter)
		boxStroke.Color = UI.FIELD_STROKE
		if enter then submit() end
	end)
	if pasteBtn then
		bind(pasteBtn.MouseButton1Click, function()
			Hub.play("click")
			local ok, text = pcall(getClip)
			if ok and type(text) == "string" then keyBox.Text = text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") setStatus("Pasted from clipboard") end
		end)
	end
	bind(chk.MouseButton1Click, function()
		if not canFile then return end
		Hub.play("click")
		remember = not remember
		chk.BackgroundColor3 = remember and UI.BRAND or UI.OFFC
		chk.Text = remember and "✓" or ""
	end)
	bind(closeBtn.MouseButton1Click, function() Hub.play("click") Unload() end)

	-- drag the key window by its header
	local dragging, dragStart, startPos
	bind(handle.InputBegan, function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging, dragStart, startPos = true, input.Position, card.Position
			input.Changed:Connect(function() if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end end)
		end
	end)
	bind(UIS.InputChanged, function(input)
		if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			local d = input.Position - dragStart
			card.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
		end
	end)
end

-- ====================================================
-- UNLOAD (kills everything)
-- ====================================================
Unload = function()
	if Unloading then return end
	Unloading = true
	Running = false
	for _, c in ipairs(Connections) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
	for i = #Cleanups, 1, -1 do pcall(Cleanups[i]) end
	pcall(function() UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default end)
	pcall(function() Gui:Destroy() end)
	pcall(function() EspGui:Destroy() end)
	env.SolosHubUnload = nil
end
env.SolosHubUnload = Unload
